        │                       ├── AntiAim.java
        │                       ├── Config.java
        │                       ├── Legit.java
        │                       ├── Misc.java
        │                       ├── Players.java
        │                       ├── Rage.java
        │                       ├── Skins.java
        │                       └── Visuals.java
        └── test
            └── java
                └── studio
                    └── dreamys
                        └── test
                            └── snapshot
                                ├── RasterRenderer.java
                                └── Snapshot.java

```

`CLAUDE.md`:

```md
# Icarus

GUI and config library for Minecraft mods. A Minecraft-free core plus one thin adapter per Minecraft version and loader. User docs live in the [wiki](https://github.com/DxxxxY/Icarus/wiki); the README is kept short on purpose.

## Layout

- `core/`: everything that isn't Minecraft-specific (API, settings, configs, menu, themes), plain Java 8. Most changes belong here.
- `testmod/`: the example mod shared by every target, with one page set per theme (`test/skeet`, `test/fatality`).
- `platforms/`: one adapter per target, each implementing `Platform` and `Renderer`. There are 11 targets:
  - Forge 1.7.10, 1.8.9 and 1.12.2;
  - Fabric and Forge 1.16.5 and 1.20.1;
  - Fabric and NeoForge 1.21.1 and 26.3.
- `platforms/mc-26.3`: a separate included build on Unimined 2.0.2. Every other target uses Unimined 1.4.1. Its `buildSrc` workarounds are each marked for deletion once fixed upstream.

## Commands

- `./gradlew build`: builds and tests everything. Needs JDK 17+ to run Gradle; toolchains fetch JDK 8, 17, 21 and 25.
- `./gradlew :core:test`: the fast check for core changes.
- `./gradlew :testmod:snapshot -Pargs="<theme> <scale> <tab> <out.png>"`: renders a menu headlessly.
  - Optional trailing args: `[mx my] [clicks]`.
  - Env vars: `SNAPSHOT_TYPE` (text to type after the clicks) and `SNAPSHOT_TOAST="title|message"`.
- Dev clients: run from the target's directory.
  - Forge and NeoForge: `runClient`.
  - Fabric 1.16.5–1.21.1: `testmodRunClient`.
  - Fabric 26.3: `runTestmodClient`.
  - `ICARUS_TEST_THEME=fatality` picks the fatality theme; `ICARUS_TEST_OPEN=1` opens the menu on the title screen with a test toast.

## Traps

- **Forge dev runs can hang at startup in a log4j rollover.** The cause is a 0-byte `<date>-1.log.gz`. Move `run/**/logs` aside first.
- **The NeoForge 26.3 dev client doesn't start.** FML fails with "version is null", a Unimined 2.0.2 bug. The jar still builds; the target is untested in game.
- **26.x input is SDL-based.** Typed characters only arrive while `textInputManager().startTextInput(...)` is active. `IcarusScreen` toggles it from `menu.isTyping()`.
- **26.x rendering is retained-mode.** Each submit needs fresh `GuiElementRenderState` objects. Several draw lists are submitted per frame: HUD, menu, and toasts over the menu.
- **Testmod state persists** in `run/.../config/testmod`. Rename that folder aside when you need reproducible screen coordinates.
- **Fonts are system fonts:**
  - skeet uses Verdana;
  - fatality uses Segoe UI Semibold, Segoe UI and Bahnschrift.

  Snapshots and pixel comparisons are only meaningful where these are installed (Windows).

## Conventions

- Keep core on Java 8 and free of Minecraft classes.
- Use `studio.dreamys.icarus.input.Keys`, which are LWJGL2 codes; adapters translate GLFW and SDL codes to them.
- Themes do all the layout and drawing. Keep a steady frame allocation-free and the menu a single draw call from the shared atlas.
- The theme recreations are measured from reference footage (local `references/`, gitignored). Don't change geometry or colours by eye.
- Releases: pushing a `vX.Y.Z` tag runs `.github/workflows/release.yml`.
  - Release notes go in `.github/release-notes/<tag>.md`.
  - It needs the `MAVEN_REPO_DEPLOY_KEY` secret.

```

`README.md`:

```md
# Icarus

A lightweight GUI and config library for Minecraft mods:

- You write your settings as annotated static fields.
- Icarus builds the menu, saves configs and handles keybinds.
- The menus recreate two popular CS:GO menus, skeet (gamesense) and fatality.
- The same mod code runs on Forge, Fabric and NeoForge, from 1.7.10 to 26.3.

| `Themes.SKEET` | `Themes.FATALITY` |
|---|---|
| ![skeet](.github/skeet.gif) | ![fatality](.github/fatality.gif) |

## Supported versions

| Minecraft | Fabric | Forge | NeoForge |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1.7.10, 1.8.9, 1.12.2 | | ✅ | |
| 1.16.5, 1.20.1 | ✅ | ✅ | |
| 1.21.1 | ✅ | | ✅ |
| 26.3 | ✅ | | ⚠️ builds, untested |

## Setup

```groovy
repositories {
    maven { url "https://repo.dreamys.studio/" }
}

dependencies {
    // studio.dreamys:icarus-<loader>-<minecraft version>:<icarus version>
    implementation "studio.dreamys:icarus-fabric-1.21.1:3.0.0"
}
```

How the jar ships with your mod:

- **Forge 1.7.10 to 1.12.2:** shade the jar into your mod.
- **Everything else:** ship the jar inside your mod with Jar-in-Jar (mod id `icarus`). Fabric API isn't needed.

The jars are also attached to each [release](https://github.com/DxxxxY/Icarus/releases).

**Fonts must be installed beforehand:**

- skeet uses Verdana;
- fatality uses Segoe UI Semibold, Segoe UI and Bahnschrift.

Windows 10 and 11 ship them. Elsewhere Icarus falls back to other fonts, and the menus won't match exactly.

## Usage

```java
@IPage(icon = "rage")
public class Combat {
    @IGroup(column = 0)
    public static class KillAura {
        @IKeybind(Keys.KEY_R)
        public static boolean Enabled = false;

        @SliderOptions(min = 1, max = 6, units = "m")
        public static double Range = 3.5;

        @DropdownOptions({"Head", "Chest", "Legs"})
        public static String[] Hitboxes = {"Head"};
    }
}
```

```java
IcarusMod mod = Icarus.create("yourmodid", Themes.SKEET); // once, after the game window exists
mod.onChange(Combat.KillAura.class, "Enabled", (Boolean on) -> mod.toasts().send("KillAura", on ? "On" : "Off"));
```

Right Shift opens the menu. Settings are saved to `config/yourmodid/`.

The **[wiki](https://github.com/DxxxxY/Icarus/wiki)** covers:

- [where to call `create`](https://github.com/DxxxxY/Icarus/wiki/Getting-Started) on each loader;
- [every setting type and annotation](https://github.com/DxxxxY/Icarus/wiki/Pages-and-Settings);
- [keybinds](https://github.com/DxxxxY/Icarus/wiki/Keybinds) and [configs](https://github.com/DxxxxY/Icarus/wiki/Configs);
- [toasts and the HUD](https://github.com/DxxxxY/Icarus/wiki/Callbacks-and-HUD);
- [themes](https://github.com/DxxxxY/Icarus/wiki/Themes);
- [the example mod](https://github.com/DxxxxY/Icarus/wiki/Example-Mod) and [building](https://github.com/DxxxxY/Icarus/wiki/Building);
- [upgrading from v2](https://github.com/DxxxxY/Icarus/wiki/Upgrading-from-v2).

## Credits

- The menu designs belong to [gamesense / skeet](https://gamesense.pub) and [fatality](https://fatality.win). Icarus isn't affiliated with either.
- Measurements, colours and icons were taken from the YouTube reviews "My Brutally Honest Skeet.cc Review" and "My Brutally Honest Fatality.win Review".
- `undefeated.ttf` is the "undefeated" CS:GO icon font from the CS:GO community.

## Disclaimer
This is for educational purposes only. I am not responsible for any damage caused by this tool.

## License
GPLv3 © dxxxxy

`build.gradle.kts`:

```kts
plugins {
    // unimined 1.x for every target; 2.x can't build FG2-era forge yet and has several windows bugs.
    // Revisit once 2.x matures.
    id("xyz.wagyourtail.unimined") version "1.4.1" apply false
}

allprojects {
    group = "studio.dreamys"
    version = providers.gradleProperty("icarus_version").get()
}

subprojects {
    // declared per project rather than in settings: unimined adds its own project repositories,
    // which would make gradle ignore the settings ones
    repositories {
        mavenCentral()
    }
}

// IntelliJ sync workaround. During an IDE sync (idea.sync.active), unimined 1.x adds a mappings jar to Fabric runtime
// classpaths as a java.nio.file.Path. IntelliJ walks classpath structure and treats any Iterable as a container; a
// one-segment Path iterates to itself, so the sync dies with StackOverflowErrors ("Multiple build operations failed.
// null null null"). Swap those Paths for Files. Remove once unimined or IntelliJ fixes it.
fun replacePaths(collection: Any?, seen: MutableSet<Any> = HashSet()) {
    if (collection == null || !seen.add(collection)) return
    when (collection) {
        is ConfigurableFileCollection -> {
            val from = collection.from.toList()
            if (from.any { it is java.nio.file.Path }) collection.setFrom(from.map { if (it is java.nio.file.Path) it.toFile() else it })
            from.forEach { replacePaths(it, seen) }
        }
        is org.gradle.api.internal.file.UnionFileCollection -> collection.sources.forEach { replacePaths(it, seen) }
    }
}

gradle.projectsEvaluated {
    rootProject.allprojects {
        extensions.findByType<SourceSetContainer>()?.forEach {
            replacePaths(it.compileClasspath)
            replacePaths(it.runtimeClasspath)
        }
    }
}

// IntelliJ run configurations. unimined's genIntellijRuns (run on sync) names the module after the Gradle path, e.g.
// Icarus.platforms.mc-1.21.1.fabric.main, but IntelliJ turns dots inside a project name into underscores
// (Icarus.platforms.mc-1_21_1.fabric.main), so every generated config says "Module not specified". Fix the names, and
// add a config for Fabric 26.3, whose unimined 2.x build generates none. That one runs the 26.3 build on its own
// (-p): run through the root build, Gradle would first configure every other target, about a minute.
fun fixIdeaRunConfigs() {
    val dir = rootDir.resolve(".idea/runConfigurations")
    if (!dir.isDirectory) return
    val renames = rootProject.allprojects.filter { "." in it.path }.map {
        val gradleName = rootProject.name + it.path.replace(":", ".")
        val ideaName = rootProject.name + it.path.split(":").joinToString(".") { part -> part.replace(".", "_") }
        "name=\"$gradleName." to "name=\"$ideaName."
    }
    dir.listFiles { f -> f.name.endsWith(".xml") }?.forEach { file ->
        val text = file.readText()
        val fixed = renames.fold(text) { acc, (from, to) -> acc.replace(from, to) }
        if (fixed != text) file.writeText(fixed)
    }
    dir.resolve("Icarus_26_3_Fabric_Client.xml").writeText("""
        <component name="ProjectRunConfigurationManager">
          <configuration default="false" name=":platforms:mc-26.3:fabric Minecraft Client (testmod)" type="GradleRunConfiguration" factoryName="Gradle">
            <ExternalSystemSettings>
              <option name="externalProjectPath" value="${'$'}PROJECT_DIR${'$'}" />
              <option name="externalSystemIdString" value="GRADLE" />
              <option name="scriptParameters" value="-p platforms/mc-26.3" />
              <option name="taskNames">
                <list>
                  <option value=":fabric:runTestmodClient" />
                </list>
              </option>
            </ExternalSystemSettings>
            <ExternalSystemDebugServerProcess>true</ExternalSystemDebugServerProcess>
            <ExternalSystemReattachDebugProcess>true</ExternalSystemReattachDebugProcess>
            <DebugAllEnabled>false</DebugAllEnabled>
            <RunAsTest>false</RunAsTest>
            <method v="2" />
          </configuration>
        </component>
    """.trimIndent() + "\n")
}

// a finalizer, not doLast: unimined adds its own actions after ours, and there is one generator per source set
val fixIdeaRunConfigsTask = tasks.register("fixIdeaRunConfigs") {
    doLast { fixIdeaRunConfigs() }
}

subprojects {
    tasks.matching { it.name == "genIntellijRuns" || it.name.endsWith("GenIntellijRuns") }.configureEach {
        finalizedBy(fixIdeaRunConfigsTask)
    }
}

// `./gradlew build` also builds the separate Minecraft 26.x build (see settings.gradle.kts)
tasks.register("build") {
    group = "build"
    description = "Builds every target, including the included Minecraft 26.x build."
    dependsOn(gradle.includedBuild("mc-26.3").task(":fabric:build"))
    dependsOn(gradle.includedBuild("mc-26.3").task(":neoforge:build"))
}

```

`core/build.gradle.kts`:

```kts
plugins {
    `java-library`
}

base {
    archivesName.set("icarus-core")
}

java {
    toolchain.languageVersion.set(JavaLanguageVersion.of(8))
    withSourcesJar()
}

tasks.withType<JavaCompile>().configureEach {
    options.encoding = "UTF-8"
}

dependencies {
    // every supported Minecraft version ships gson; 2.2.4 is the oldest (1.7.10/1.8.9)
    compileOnly("com.google.code.gson:gson:2.2.4")

    val lombok = "org.projectlombok:lombok:${providers.gradleProperty("lombok_version").get()}"
    compileOnly(lombok)
    annotationProcessor(lombok)

    testImplementation(platform("org.junit:junit-bom:5.13.4"))
    testImplementation("org.junit.jupiter:junit-jupiter")
    testRuntimeOnly("org.junit.platform:junit-platform-launcher")
    testImplementation("com.google.code.gson:gson:2.2.4")
}

tasks.test {
    useJUnitPlatform()
    systemProperty("java.awt.headless", "true")
}

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/Icarus.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.input.Keys;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.annotation.type.IPage;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.config.ClassScanner;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Platform;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.FontAtlas;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.theme.Theme;

import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.concurrent.CopyOnWriteArrayList;

/**
 * Entry point. Create one {@link IcarusMod} per mod with {@link #create}; the platform (Minecraft version and mod
 * loader adapter) is found automatically and feeds key presses, HUD rendering and ticks to every mod.
 */
public final class Icarus {
    private static Platform platform;
    private static boolean installed;
    private static FontAtlas atlas;
    private static final List<IcarusMod> mods = new CopyOnWriteArrayList<>();

    private Icarus() {
    }

    public static IcarusMod create(String modid, Theme theme) {
        return create(modid, theme, IcarusOptions.defaults());
    }

    /**
     * Creates the Icarus instance of a mod: finds its {@code @IPage} classes in the mod's own jar, loads its config
     * from {@code config/<modid>/} (migrating Icarus v2 files) and builds its menu. Call it once, on the client
     * thread, after the game window exists.
     */
    public static IcarusMod create(String modid, Theme theme, IcarusOptions options) {
        for (IcarusMod mod : mods) {
            if (mod.getModid().equals(modid)) throw new IllegalStateException("Icarus was already created for " + modid);
        }
        Platform p = getPlatform();
        if (!installed) {
            p.install();
            installed = true;
        }
        List<Class<?>> pages = ClassScanner.findAnnotated(p.getModRoots(modid), p.getModClassLoader(modid), IPage.class);
        if (options.pagesPackage != null) pages.removeIf(type -> !type.getName().startsWith(options.pagesPackage + "."));
        Path game = p.getGameDir().toPath();
        IcarusMod mod = new IcarusMod(modid, theme, options, pages, game.resolve("config").resolve(modid), game.resolve(modid));
        mods.add(mod);
        return mod;
    }

    /** Builds an instance from explicit page classes and directories, without touching the platform (for tests). */
    public static IcarusMod createDetached(String modid, Theme theme, IcarusOptions options, List<Class<?>> pages, Path configDir, Path legacyDir) {
        return new IcarusMod(modid, theme, options, pages, configDir, legacyDir);
    }

    /** The implementation for the running Minecraft version / mod loader. */
    public static Platform getPlatform() {
        if (platform == null) {
            try {
                platform = (Platform) Class.forName(Platform.IMPLEMENTATION, true, Icarus.class.getClassLoader()).getConstructor().newInstance();
            } catch (ReflectiveOperationException e) {
                throw new IllegalStateException("No Icarus platform found (" + Platform.IMPLEMENTATION + "). Make sure the Icarus build for your Minecraft version and mod loader is on the classpath.", e);
            }
        }
        return platform;
    }

    /** Supplies the platform explicitly instead of looking it up (tests, custom environments). */
    public static void setPlatform(Platform platform) {
        Icarus.platform = platform;
    }

    /** The texture atlas shared by every mod (fonts and generated images). */
    public static FontAtlas getAtlas() {
        if (atlas == null) atlas = new FontAtlas();
        return atlas;
    }

    public static List<IcarusMod> getMods() {
        return Collections.unmodifiableList(mods);
    }

    /* ---- called by the platform ---- */

    /** A key was pressed in game while no screen was open ({@link studio.dreamys.icarus.input.Keys} code). */
    public static void onKeyPressed(int key) {
        if (key <= 0 || !getPlatform().isInGame()) return;
        for (IcarusMod mod : mods) mod.onKeyPressed(key);
    }

    /** A key was released in game while no screen was open (drives "on hotkey" / "off hotkey" checkboxes). */
    public static void onKeyReleased(int key) {
        if (key <= 0 || platform == null) return;
        for (IcarusMod mod : mods) mod.onKeyReleased(key);
    }

    /** A mouse button (0 left, 1 right, 2 middle, 3/4 side) was pressed in game while no screen was open. */
    public static void onMousePressed(int button) {
        onKeyPressed(Keys.mouse(button));
    }

    public static void onMouseReleased(int button) {
        onKeyReleased(Keys.mouse(button));
    }

    /** The HUD is being drawn. */
    public static void renderHud(float partialTick) {
        for (IcarusMod mod : mods) mod.renderHud(partialTick);
    }

    /** Start of a client tick. */
    public static void clientTick() {
        for (IcarusMod mod : mods) mod.tick();
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/IcarusMod.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.config.ConfigStore;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.hud.HudContext;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.hud.HudElement;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.hud.Toasts;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.input.Keys;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Platform;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.FrameRenderer;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.KeybindMode;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.Keybinds;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.ModelBuilder;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.Setting;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.SettingListener;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.SettingType;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.SettingTypes;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.SettingsModel;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.theme.Theme;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.theme.Themes;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.ConfigPanel;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.Menu;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.WidgetFactory;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.Widget;

import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.IdentityHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.CopyOnWriteArrayList;
import java.util.function.Consumer;
import java.util.function.IntConsumer;
import java.util.logging.Level;
import java.util.logging.Logger;

/**
 * One mod's Icarus: its settings, configs, keybinds, menu, theme, toasts and HUD. Create it with
 * {@link Icarus#create}. Several mods can each have their own instance in the same game.
 */
public final class IcarusMod implements SettingListener, Keybinds.Listener {
    private static final Logger LOG = Logger.getLogger("Icarus");

    private final String modid;
    private final IcarusOptions options;
    private final SettingsModel model;
    private final Keybinds keybinds;
    private final ConfigStore store;
    private final Map<SettingType<?>, WidgetFactory<?>> factories = WidgetFactory.defaults();
    private final Map<Setting<?>, List<Consumer<Object>>> callbacks = new IdentityHashMap<>();
    private final List<IntConsumer> keyListeners = new CopyOnWriteArrayList<>();
    private final List<HudElement> hud = new CopyOnWriteArrayList<>();
    private final Toasts toasts = new Toasts();
    /** Bound non-checkbox settings whose key is down, and those toggled on (see {@link #isKeybindActive(Setting)}). */
    private final java.util.Set<Setting<?>> held = java.util.Collections.newSetFromMap(new IdentityHashMap<>());
    private final java.util.Set<Setting<?>> toggled = java.util.Collections.newSetFromMap(new IdentityHashMap<>());

    private Theme theme;
    private ConfigPanel configPanel;
    private Menu menu;
    private int menuKey;

    private final FrameRenderer hudFrame = new FrameRenderer();
    private final HudContext hudContext = new HudContext();
    private final Runnable hudPaint = this::paintHud;
    private final FrameRenderer overlayFrame = new FrameRenderer();
    private final Runnable overlayPaint = () -> toasts.render(overlayFrame.getCanvas(), hudContext);
    private volatile boolean menuOpen;

    IcarusMod(String modid, Theme theme, IcarusOptions options, List<Class<?>> pageClasses, Path configDir, Path legacyDir) {
        this.modid = modid;
        this.options = options;
        this.menuKey = options.menuKey;
        this.model = new ModelBuilder(SettingTypes.builtIn()).build(pageClasses);
        this.keybinds = new Keybinds(model);
        this.store = new ConfigStore(configDir, legacyDir, model, keybinds, options.configUi);
        store.init();

        for (Setting<?> setting : model.getSettings()) setting.setListener(this);
        keybinds.setListener(this);

        Theme preferred = options.themeSelector ? Themes.byId(store.getPrefs().getTheme()) : null;
        this.theme = preferred != null ? preferred : theme;
        hud.add(toasts);
    }

    private Menu menu() {
        if (menu == null) {
            if (options.configUi || options.themeSelector) configPanel = new ConfigPanel(this, options.configUi, options.themeSelector);
            menu = new Menu(this, theme, configPanel);
        }
        return menu;
    }

    public String getModid() {
        return modid;
    }

    public SettingsModel getModel() {
        return model;
    }

    public Keybinds getKeybinds() {
        return keybinds;
    }

    public ConfigStore getConfigStore() {
        return store;
    }

    /** The menu window (created on first use). */
    public Menu getMenu() {
        return menu();
    }

    /* ---- theme and look ---- */

    public Theme getTheme() {
        return theme;
    }

    public void setTheme(Theme theme) {
        this.theme = theme;
        if (menu != null) menu.setTheme(theme);
        store.setTheme(theme.getId());
    }

    /** Accent colour (ARGB): the one from the options, or the theme's. */
    public int getAccent() {
        return options.accent != null ? options.accent : theme.getDefaultAccent();
    }

    /** Changes the accent colour (ARGB) at runtime, e.g. from a "Menu color" setting. */
    public void setAccent(int argb) {
        options.accent = argb;
    }

    /** Changes the menu scale at runtime (1 = the theme's natural size), e.g. from a "DPI scale" setting. */
    public void setMenuScale(double scale) {
        if (!(scale > 0)) throw new IllegalArgumentException("menu scale must be positive");
        options.menuScale = scale;
    }

    /** Design units to framebuffer pixels: 1 at Minecraft GUI scale 2, times the menu scale option. */
    public double renderScale(double guiScale) {
        return guiScale / 2 * options.menuScale;
    }

    /** Footer text of the menu window (empty by default). */
    public void setFooter(String left, String center, String right) {
        menu().setFooter(left, center, right);
    }

    /** Window title, for themes that show one (the mod id in capitals by default). */
    public void setTitle(String title) {
        menu().setTitle(title);
    }

    public int getMenuKey() {
        return menuKey;
    }

    public void setMenuKey(int key) {
        menuKey = key;
    }

    /* ---- callbacks and widgets ---- */

    /** Runs {@code callback} with the new value whenever the setting changes (by the user, a config load or a reset). */
    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    public <T> void onChange(Setting<T> setting, Consumer<T> callback) {
        callbacks.computeIfAbsent(setting, s -> new ArrayList<>()).add((Consumer<Object>) callback);
    }

    /** {@link #onChange(Setting, Consumer)} for the setting declared as {@code field} in the {@code @IGroup} class {@code group}. */
    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    public <T> void onChange(Class<?> group, String field, Consumer<T> callback) {
        Setting<?> setting = model.find(group, field);
        if (setting == null) throw new IllegalArgumentException("No setting " + group.getName() + "." + field);
        onChange((Setting<T>) setting, callback);
    }

    /** Uses {@code factory} to create the widgets of one setting type (e.g. a custom checkbox). */
    public <T> void replaceWidget(SettingType<T> type, WidgetFactory<T> factory) {
        factories.put(type, factory);
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    public <T> Widget createWidget(Menu menu, Setting<T> setting) {
        WidgetFactory<T> factory = (WidgetFactory<T>) factories.get(setting.getType());
        if (factory == null) {
            LOG.warning("[Icarus] No widget for setting type " + setting.getType() + " (" + setting + ")");
            return null;
        }
        return factory.create(menu, setting);
    }

    @Override
    public void changed(Setting<?> setting, boolean byUser) {
        if (byUser && setting.isPersistent()) store.markDirty();
        List<Consumer<Object>> list = callbacks.get(setting);
        if (list == null) return;
        Object value = setting.get();
        for (int i = 0; i < list.size(); i++) {
            try {
                list.get(i).accept(value);
            } catch (RuntimeException e) {
                LOG.log(Level.WARNING, "[Icarus] Callback for " + setting + " failed", e);
            }
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void keybindChanged(Setting<?> setting, boolean byUser) {
        if (byUser) {
            store.markDirty();
            applyRestingState(setting);
        }
    }

    /* ---- HUD, keys, ticks ---- */

    /** Adds an element to this mod's HUD. */
    public void addHud(HudElement element) {
        hud.add(element);
    }

    public void removeHud(HudElement element) {
        hud.remove(element);
    }

    public Toasts toasts() {
        return toasts;
    }

    /** Listens for key presses in game while no screen is open ({@link studio.dreamys.icarus.input.Keys} codes). */
    public void addKeyListener(IntConsumer listener) {
        keyListeners.add(listener);
    }

    /** Opens the menu. */
    public void openMenu() {
        Icarus.getPlatform().openScreen(menu());
    }

    void onKeyPressed(int key) {
        if (key == menuKey) {
            openMenu();
            return;
        }
        for (Map.Entry<Setting<?>, Integer> binding : keybinds.asMap().entrySet()) {
            if (binding.getValue() != key) continue;
            Setting<?> setting = binding.getKey();
            if (setting.getType() == SettingTypes.BUTTON) {
                Runnable action = (Runnable) setting.get();
                if (action != null) action.run();
            } else if (setting.getType() != SettingTypes.CHECKBOX) {
                held.add(setting);
                if (keybinds.getMode(setting) == KeybindMode.TOGGLE && !toggled.remove(setting)) toggled.add(setting);
            } else {
                @SuppressWarnings("unchecked") Setting<Boolean> toggle = (Setting<Boolean>) setting;
                switch (keybinds.getMode(setting)) {
                    case TOGGLE:
                        toggle.set(!Boolean.TRUE.equals(toggle.get()));
                        break;
                    case ON_HOTKEY:
                        toggle.set(true);
                        break;
                    case OFF_HOTKEY:
                        toggle.set(false);
                        break;
                    default:
                        break;
                }
            }
        }
        for (IntConsumer listener : keyListeners) listener.accept(key);
    }

    void onKeyReleased(int key) {
        for (Map.Entry<Setting<?>, Integer> binding : keybinds.asMap().entrySet()) {
            if (binding.getValue() != key) continue;
            held.remove(binding.getKey());
            if (binding.getKey().getType() != SettingTypes.CHECKBOX) continue;
            @SuppressWarnings("unchecked") Setting<Boolean> toggle = (Setting<Boolean>) binding.getKey();
            KeybindMode mode = keybinds.getMode(toggle);
            if (mode == KeybindMode.ON_HOTKEY) toggle.set(false);
            else if (mode == KeybindMode.OFF_HOTKEY) toggle.set(true);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Whether a bound setting is active by its keybind mode: always on, held (on hotkey), not held (off hotkey) or
     * toggled by presses. For checkboxes that's simply their value; for other rows (e.g. an "Activation type" label)
     * the mod asks here. A setting without a key counts as active.
     */
    public boolean isKeybindActive(Setting<?> setting) {
        if (setting.getType() == SettingTypes.CHECKBOX) return Boolean.TRUE.equals(setting.get());
        if (keybinds.get(setting) == Keys.KEY_NONE) return true;
        switch (keybinds.getMode(setting)) {
            case ALWAYS_ON:
                return true;
            case ON_HOTKEY:
                return held.contains(setting);
            case OFF_HOTKEY:
                return !held.contains(setting);
            default:
                return toggled.contains(setting);
        }
    }

    /** {@link #isKeybindActive(Setting)} for the field {@code name} in a page or group class. */
    public boolean isKeybindActive(Class<?> owner, String name) {
        Setting<?> setting = model.find(owner, name);
        if (setting == null) throw new IllegalArgumentException("No setting " + name + " in " + owner.getName());
        return isKeybindActive(setting);
    }

    /** Puts a bound checkbox in the resting state of its mode (after the user picked a mode). */
    private void applyRestingState(Setting<?> setting) {
        if (setting.getType() != SettingTypes.CHECKBOX) return;
        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked") Setting<Boolean> toggle = (Setting<Boolean>) setting;
        KeybindMode mode = keybinds.getMode(setting);
        if (mode == KeybindMode.ALWAYS_ON || mode == KeybindMode.OFF_HOTKEY) toggle.set(true);
        else if (mode == KeybindMode.ON_HOTKEY) toggle.set(false);
    }

    void tick() {
        for (HudElement element : hud) element.tick();
        store.tick(menu != null && menu.isInteracting());
    }

    void renderHud(float partialTick) {
        if (hud.isEmpty()) return;
        Platform platform = Icarus.getPlatform();
        double scale = renderScale(platform.getGuiScale());
        int fbWidth = platform.getFramebufferWidth(), fbHeight = platform.getFramebufferHeight();
        hudContext.set(theme, getAccent(), fbWidth / scale, fbHeight / scale, partialTick);
        hudFrame.render(platform.getRenderer(), Icarus.getAtlas(), scale, fbWidth, fbHeight, hudPaint);
    }

    private void paintHud() {
        for (HudElement element : hud) {
            //while the menu is open its own frame draws the toasts on top of it
            if (element == toasts && menuOpen) continue;
            element.render(hudFrame.getCanvas(), hudContext);
        }
    }

    /** Draws the toasts over the open menu (called by the menu after it paints). */
    public void renderOverMenu(float partialTick) {
        if (!toasts.hasActive()) return;
        Platform platform = Icarus.getPlatform();
        double scale = renderScale(platform.getGuiScale());
        int fbWidth = platform.getFramebufferWidth(), fbHeight = platform.getFramebufferHeight();
        hudContext.set(theme, getAccent(), fbWidth / scale, fbHeight / scale, partialTick);
        overlayFrame.render(platform.getRenderer(), Icarus.getAtlas(), scale, fbWidth, fbHeight, overlayPaint);
    }

    /** Whether this mod's menu is on screen (set by the menu). */
    public void setMenuOpen(boolean open) {
        menuOpen = open;
    }

    /* ---- called by the menu ---- */

    public void interactionEnded() {
        store.interactionEnded();
    }

    public void menuClosed() {
        store.closed();
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/IcarusOptions.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.input.Keys;

import java.awt.Color;

/** Optional settings for {@link Icarus#create(String, studio.dreamys.icarus.theme.Theme, IcarusOptions)}. */
public final class IcarusOptions {
    boolean configUi = true;
    boolean themeSelector = true;
    int menuKey = Keys.KEY_RSHIFT;
    Integer accent;
    double menuScale = 1;
    String pagesPackage;

    public static IcarusOptions defaults() {
        return new IcarusOptions();
    }

    public static IcarusOptions builder() {
        return new IcarusOptions();
    }

    /**
     * The built-in config UI (named configs with load/save/delete/reset/import/export), on by default. While it's
     * on, changes are only written when the user presses Save. Turn it off for a single config that auto-saves.
     */
    public IcarusOptions configUi(boolean enabled) {
        configUi = enabled;
        return this;
    }

    /** Lets players switch the menu theme (on by default). */
    public IcarusOptions themeSelector(boolean enabled) {
        themeSelector = enabled;
        return this;
    }

    /** Key that opens the menu (a {@link Keys} code), Right Shift by default. */
    public IcarusOptions menuKey(int key) {
        menuKey = key;
        return this;
    }

    /** Accent colour; the theme's own accent is used if unset. */
    public IcarusOptions accent(Color color) {
        accent = color == null ? null : color.getRGB();
        return this;
    }

    /**
     * Only use {@code @IPage} classes in this package (and its sub-packages). Useful when a mod ships page sets for
     * different themes, since themes differ in the controls they offer.
     */
    public IcarusOptions pagesPackage(String packageName) {
        pagesPackage = packageName == null || packageName.isEmpty() ? null : packageName;
        return this;
    }

    /** Extra menu scale on top of Minecraft's GUI scale (1 = the theme's natural size). */
    public IcarusOptions menuScale(double scale) {
        if (!(scale > 0)) throw new IllegalArgumentException("menu scale must be positive");
        menuScale = scale;
        return this;
    }
}

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.annotation;

import java.lang.annotation.ElementType;
import java.lang.annotation.Retention;
import java.lang.annotation.RetentionPolicy;
import java.lang.annotation.Target;

/**
 * The text shown for a page, sub-page, group or setting, when its Java name can't spell it (e.g.
 * "Auto-accept matchmaking"). Without it, the name is shown with underscores as spaces. Config keys always use
 * the Java name, so changing a label never loses saved values.
 */
@Retention(RetentionPolicy.RUNTIME)
@Target({ElementType.TYPE, ElementType.FIELD})
public @interface ILabel {
    String value();
}

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/annotation/type/IGroup.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.annotation.type;

import java.lang.annotation.ElementType;
import java.lang.annotation.Retention;
import java.lang.annotation.RetentionPolicy;
import java.lang.annotation.Target;

/**
 * Marks a class nested in an {@link IPage} or {@link ISubPage} as a group box. Its static fields become settings.
 * Groups are placed by the theme; {@link #column()} and {@link #order()} are hints it follows.
 */
@Retention(RetentionPolicy.RUNTIME)
@Target(ElementType.TYPE)
public @interface IGroup {
    int AUTO = -1;

    /** Preferred column (0-based), or {@link #AUTO} to let the theme balance columns. */
    int column() default AUTO;

    /** Position within its column; lower comes first. Ties are ordered by class name. */
    int order() default Integer.MAX_VALUE;

    /** Keep the group at the bottom of its column. */
    boolean pinBottom() default false;

    /** Fixed height in design units; 0 lets the theme size it (stretching to fill the column). */
    double height() default 0;
}

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/annotation/type/IPage.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.annotation.type;

import java.lang.annotation.ElementType;
import java.lang.annotation.Retention;
import java.lang.annotation.RetentionPolicy;
import java.lang.annotation.Target;

/**
 * Marks a class as a menu page (a tab). Its nested {@link IGroup} classes (or {@link ISubPage} classes holding
 * groups) become the page's content.
 */
@Retention(RetentionPolicy.RUNTIME)
@Target(ElementType.TYPE)
public @interface IPage {
    /** Tab icon: a character of the icon font (used by themes with icon tabs). */
    char value() default ' ';

    /**
     * Named tab icon provided by the theme (e.g. the skeet theme's "rage", "antiaim", "legit", "visuals", "misc",
     * "skins", "players", "config"); takes precedence over {@link #value()} when the theme has it.
     */
    String icon() default "";

    /** Tab position; lower comes first. Ties are ordered by class name. */
    int order() default Integer.MAX_VALUE;
}

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/annotation/type/ISubPage.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.annotation.type;

import java.lang.annotation.ElementType;
import java.lang.annotation.Retention;
import java.lang.annotation.RetentionPolicy;
import java.lang.annotation.Target;

/**
 * Marks a class nested in an {@link IPage} as a sub-tab. Its nested {@link IGroup} classes are its content.
 * A page either has sub-tabs or groups directly, not both.
 */
@Retention(RetentionPolicy.RUNTIME)
@Target(ElementType.TYPE)
public @interface ISubPage {
    /** Sub-tab position; lower comes first. Ties are ordered by class name. */
    int order() default Integer.MAX_VALUE;

    /** Icon character for themes that show sub-tabs as icons (the weapon rows of both built-in themes use the weapon icon font). */
    char icon() default ' ';
}

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/config/ClassScanner.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.config;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.lang.annotation.Annotation;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.FileSystem;
import java.nio.file.FileSystems;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.stream.Stream;

/**
 * Finds classes carrying a runtime annotation inside a mod's own directories/jars.
 * <p>
 * Replaces the Reflections library, whose classpath scan sees nothing on Fabric or modern (Neo)Forge
 * class loaders. Only classes whose bytes mention the annotation's descriptor are loaded, so unrelated
 * classes (mixins in particular) are never touched.
 */
public final class ClassScanner {
    private ClassScanner() {
    }

    public static List<Class<?>> findAnnotated(Collection<Path> roots, ClassLoader loader, Class<? extends Annotation> annotation) {
        byte[] descriptor = ("L" + annotation.getName().replace('.', '/') + ";").getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        List<Class<?>> found = new ArrayList<>();

        for (Path root : roots) {
            try {
                if (Files.isDirectory(root)) {
                    scan(root, descriptor, loader, annotation, found);
                } else if (Files.isRegularFile(root)) {
                    try (FileSystem fs = FileSystems.newFileSystem(root, (ClassLoader) null)) {
                        scan(fs.getPath("/"), descriptor, loader, annotation, found);
                    }
                }
            } catch (IOException e) {
                throw new RuntimeException("Failed to scan " + root + " for @" + annotation.getSimpleName(), e);
            }
        }

        return found;
    }

    private static void scan(Path root, byte[] descriptor, ClassLoader loader, Class<? extends Annotation> annotation, List<Class<?>> found) throws IOException {
        List<Path> classFiles = new ArrayList<>();
        try (Stream<Path> files = Files.walk(root)) {
            files.filter(p -> p.toString().endsWith(".class") && Files.isRegularFile(p)).forEach(classFiles::add);
        }

        for (Path file : classFiles) {
            String relative = root.relativize(file).toString().replace('\\', '/');
            if (relative.startsWith("META-INF/") || relative.endsWith("module-info.class") || relative.endsWith("package-info.class")) continue;
            if (!contains(Files.readAllBytes(file), descriptor)) continue;

            String name = relative.substring(0, relative.length() - ".class".length()).replace('/', '.');
            try {
                Class<?> type = Class.forName(name, false, loader);
                if (type.isAnnotationPresent(annotation)) found.add(type);
            } catch (ClassNotFoundException | LinkageError e) {
                throw new RuntimeException("Failed to load " + name + " while scanning for @" + annotation.getSimpleName(), e);
            }
        }
    }

    private static boolean contains(byte[] haystack, byte[] needle) {
        outer:
        for (int i = 0; i <= haystack.length - needle.length; i++) {
            for (int j = 0; j < needle.length; j++) {
                if (haystack[i + j] != needle[j]) continue outer;
            }
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/config/ConfigStore.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.config;

import com.google.gson.JsonObject;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.Keybinds;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.Setting;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.SettingsModel;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.file.DirectoryStream;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.logging.Level;
import java.util.logging.Logger;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;

/**
 * Persists one mod's settings under {@code config/<modid>/}:
 * <ul>
 *     <li>{@code icarus.json}: UI preferences, always auto-saved</li>
 *     <li>{@code configs/<name>.json}: config profiles (settings + keybinds)</li>
 * </ul>
 * In <b>profile mode</b> (the built-in config UI is on) changes stay in memory until {@link #save()}.
 * Otherwise there's one profile ("default") that auto-saves: changes are written when an interaction ends
 * ({@link #interactionEnded()}) or on the next {@link #tick(boolean)} without an interaction in progress.
 */
public final class ConfigStore {
    private static final Logger LOG = Logger.getLogger("Icarus");
    public static final String DEFAULT_PROFILE = "default";
    private static final Pattern PROFILE_NAME = Pattern.compile("[\\p{L}\\p{N} _.\\-]{1,32}");

    private final Path dir;
    private final Path profilesDir;
    private final Path prefsFile;
    private final Path legacyDir;
    private final SettingsModel model;
    private final Keybinds keybinds;
    private final boolean profileMode;
    private final UiPrefs prefs = new UiPrefs();

    private String profile = DEFAULT_PROFILE;
    private boolean dirty;
    private boolean prefsDirty;
    private int writeCount;

    /**
     * @param dir       {@code <game>/config/<modid>}
     * @param legacyDir v2's {@code <game>/<modid>}, migrated on first start
     */
    public ConfigStore(Path dir, Path legacyDir, SettingsModel model, Keybinds keybinds, boolean profileMode) {
        this.dir = dir;
        this.profilesDir = dir.resolve("configs");
        this.prefsFile = dir.resolve("icarus.json");
        this.legacyDir = legacyDir;
        this.model = model;
        this.keybinds = keybinds;
        this.profileMode = profileMode;
    }

    /** Loads preferences and the active profile. Nothing is written unless v2 files were migrated. */
    public void init() {
        boolean fresh = !Files.exists(prefsFile) && listProfiles().isEmpty();

        JsonObject prefsJson = JsonFiles.read(prefsFile);
        if (prefsJson != null) prefs.read(prefsJson);

        if (fresh && legacyDir != null && LegacyMigration.migrate(legacyDir, model, keybinds)) {
            write(profileFile(DEFAULT_PROFILE), ProfileCodec.encode(model, keybinds));
        }

        String wanted = profileMode && prefs.getProfile() != null && isValidName(prefs.getProfile()) ? prefs.getProfile() : DEFAULT_PROFILE;
        profile = wanted;
        JsonObject json = JsonFiles.read(profileFile(profile));
        if (json != null) ProfileCodec.apply(json, model, keybinds);
        dirty = false;
    }

    public boolean isProfileMode() {
        return profileMode;
    }

    public UiPrefs getPrefs() {
        return prefs;
    }

    /** A setting or keybind was changed by the user. */
    public void markDirty() {
        dirty = true;
    }

    /** Whether there are changes not written to the active profile (shown by themes in profile mode). */
    public boolean hasUnsavedChanges() {
        return dirty;
    }

    /** Auto-save mode: writes pending changes now (end of a drag, field unfocus, toggle, menu close). */
    public void interactionEnded() {
        if (!profileMode && dirty) save();
    }

    /** Called once per client tick: writes pending auto-save changes (unless the user is mid-interaction) and preferences. */
    public void tick(boolean interacting) {
        if (!interacting) interactionEnded();
        if (prefsDirty) {
            prefsDirty = false;
            write(prefsFile, prefs.toJson());
        }
    }

    /** Menu closed: flush everything that's due. */
    public void closed() {
        tick(false);
    }

    /* ---- UI preferences ---- */

    public void setTheme(String theme) {
        if (theme.equals(prefs.getTheme())) return;
        prefs.setTheme(theme);
        prefsDirty = true;
    }

    public void setPosition(double x, double y) {
        if (x == prefs.getX() && y == prefs.getY()) return;
        prefs.setPosition(x, y);
        prefsDirty = true;
    }

    public void setPage(String page, String subPage) {
        prefs.setPage(page, subPage);
        prefsDirty = true;
    }

    /* ---- profiles ---- */

    /** The active profile's name. */
    public String getProfile() {
        return profile;
    }

    public static boolean isValidName(String name) {
        return name != null && PROFILE_NAME.matcher(name).matches() && !name.trim().isEmpty() && !name.equals(".") && !name.equals("..");
    }

    /** Saved profiles, sorted by name. */
    public List<String> listProfiles() {
        List<String> names = new ArrayList<>();
        if (!Files.isDirectory(profilesDir)) return names;
        try (DirectoryStream<Path> files = Files.newDirectoryStream(profilesDir, "*.json")) {
            for (Path file : files) {
                String fileName = file.getFileName().toString();
                names.add(fileName.substring(0, fileName.length() - ".json".length()));
            }
        } catch (IOException e) {
            LOG.log(Level.WARNING, "[Icarus] Couldn't list " + profilesDir, e);
        }
        Collections.sort(names, String.CASE_INSENSITIVE_ORDER);
        return names;
    }

    /** Writes the current settings to the active profile. */
    public void save() {
        if (write(profileFile(profile), ProfileCodec.encode(model, keybinds))) dirty = false;
    }

    /** Writes the current settings to {@code name} and makes it the active profile. */
    public boolean saveAs(String name) {
        if (!isValidName(name)) return false;
        profile = name;
        rememberProfile();
        save();
        return true;
    }

    /**
     * Switches to a saved profile, discarding unsaved changes. Settings it doesn't mention keep their defaults, and a
     * profile that was never saved (an empty slot) loads as all defaults. Returns false for an unreadable file.
     */
    public boolean load(String name) {
        if (!isValidName(name)) return false;
        Path file = profileFile(name);
        JsonObject json = Files.exists(file) ? JsonFiles.read(file) : new JsonObject();
        if (json == null) return false;
        resetValues();
        ProfileCodec.apply(json, model, keybinds);
        profile = name;
        rememberProfile();
        dirty = false;
        return true;
    }

    /** Deletes a saved profile. Deleting the active one keeps the current values as unsaved changes. */
    public boolean delete(String name) {
        if (!isValidName(name)) return false;
        try {
            boolean deleted = Files.deleteIfExists(profileFile(name));
            if (deleted && name.equals(profile)) dirty = true;
            return deleted;
        } catch (IOException e) {
            LOG.log(Level.WARNING, "[Icarus] Couldn't delete profile " + name, e);
            return false;
        }
    }

    /** Resets every setting and keybind to its default (a pending change until saved). */
    public void resetToDefaults() {
        resetValues();
        dirty = true;
        interactionEnded();
    }

    /** The current settings as JSON text (for the clipboard). */
    public String exportCurrent() {
        return JsonFiles.toString(ProfileCodec.encode(model, keybinds));
    }

    /** Applies settings from JSON text (from the clipboard). Returns false if it isn't a profile. */
    public boolean importJson(String text) {
        JsonObject json = text == null ? null : JsonFiles.parse(text);
        if (json == null || (json.get("settings") == null && json.get("keybinds") == null)) return false;
        ProfileCodec.apply(json, model, keybinds);
        dirty = true;
        interactionEnded();
        return true;
    }

    private void resetValues() {
        for (Setting<?> setting : model.getSettings()) {
            if (setting.isPersistent()) setting.resetToDefault();
        }
        keybinds.resetToDefaults();
    }

    private void rememberProfile() {
        if (profileMode) {
            prefs.setProfile(profile);
            prefsDirty = true;
        }
    }

    Path profileFile(String name) {
        return profilesDir.resolve(name + ".json");
    }

    /** Number of files written so far (for tests and diagnostics). */
    public int getWriteCount() {
        return writeCount;
    }

    public Path getDirectory() {
        return dir;
    }

    private boolean write(Path file, JsonObject json) {
        try {
            JsonFiles.writeAtomic(file, json);
            writeCount++;
            return true;
        } catch (IOException e) {
            LOG.log(Level.WARNING, "[Icarus] Couldn't write " + file, e);
            return false;
        }
    }
}

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/config/JsonFiles.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.config;

import com.google.gson.Gson;
import com.google.gson.GsonBuilder;
import com.google.gson.JsonElement;
import com.google.gson.JsonObject;
import com.google.gson.JsonParseException;
import com.google.gson.JsonParser;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.Reader;
import java.io.StringReader;
import java.io.Writer;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.AtomicMoveNotSupportedException;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.nio.file.StandardCopyOption;
import java.util.logging.Level;
import java.util.logging.Logger;

/** UTF-8 JSON files: tolerant reads and atomic writes. */
public final class JsonFiles {
    private static final Logger LOG = Logger.getLogger("Icarus");
    static final Gson GSON = new GsonBuilder().setPrettyPrinting().disableHtmlEscaping().create();

    private JsonFiles() {
    }

    /**
     * Reads a JSON object. Returns null if the file is missing or empty. A file that isn't a valid JSON object is
     * moved aside to {@code <name>.broken-<millis>} (so it's never overwritten) and null is returned.
     */
    public static JsonObject read(Path file) {
        if (!Files.isRegularFile(file)) return null;
        String text;
        try {
            text = new String(Files.readAllBytes(file), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            LOG.log(Level.WARNING, "[Icarus] Couldn't read " + file, e);
            return null;
        }
        if (text.trim().isEmpty()) return null;

        JsonObject object = parse(text);
        if (object != null) return object;

        Path broken = file.resolveSibling(file.getFileName() + ".broken-" + System.currentTimeMillis());
        try {
            Files.move(file, broken);
            LOG.warning("[Icarus] " + file + " isn't valid JSON; kept it as " + broken.getFileName() + " and continued with defaults");
        } catch (IOException e) {
            LOG.log(Level.WARNING, "[Icarus] " + file + " isn't valid JSON and couldn't be moved aside", e);
        }
        return null;
    }

    /** Parses a JSON object, or returns null if the text isn't one. */
    public static JsonObject parse(String text) {
        try (Reader reader = new StringReader(text)) {
            JsonElement element = new JsonParser().parse(reader);
            return element.isJsonObject() ? element.getAsJsonObject() : null;
        } catch (JsonParseException | IOException | IllegalStateException e) {
            return null;
        }
    }

    public static String toString(JsonElement json) {
        return GSON.toJson(json);
    }

    /** Writes through a temp file and an atomic move, so a crash never leaves a half-written file. */
    public static void writeAtomic(Path file, JsonElement json) throws IOException {
        Files.createDirectories(file.getParent());
        Path temp = file.resolveSibling(file.getFileName() + ".tmp");
        try (Writer writer = Files.newBufferedWriter(temp, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)) {
            GSON.toJson(json, writer);
        }
        try {
            Files.move(temp, file, StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING, StandardCopyOption.ATOMIC_MOVE);
        } catch (AtomicMoveNotSupportedException e) {
            Files.move(temp, file, StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING);
        }
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/config/LegacyMigration.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.config;

import com.google.gson.JsonElement;
import com.google.gson.JsonObject;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.GroupNode;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.Keybinds;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.Setting;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.SettingsModel;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.logging.Level;
import java.util.logging.Logger;

/**
 * Reads Icarus v2's {@code <game>/<modid>/config.json} and {@code keybind.json}.
 * <p>
 * v2 keyed config.json by class/field names ({@code {"Combat": {"KillAura": {"Checkbox_Haha": true}}}}), but
 * keybind.json by display labels for the page and group (underscores shown as spaces), so both spellings are tried.
 */
final class LegacyMigration {
    private static final Logger LOG = Logger.getLogger("Icarus");

    private LegacyMigration() {
    }

    /** Applies the v2 files, if any, and renames them to {@code *.migrated}. Returns whether anything was found. */
    static boolean migrate(Path legacyDir, SettingsModel model, Keybinds keybinds) {
        Path configFile = legacyDir.resolve("config.json");
        Path keybindFile = legacyDir.resolve("keybind.json");
        JsonObject config = JsonFiles.read(configFile);
        JsonObject keys = JsonFiles.read(keybindFile);
        if (config == null && keys == null) return false;

        for (Setting<?> setting : model.getSettings()) {
            if (config != null && setting.isPersistent()) {
                JsonObject group = find(config, setting.getGroup());
                if (group != null) ProfileCodec.applyValue(setting, group.get(setting.getName()));
            }
            if (keys != null && setting.isBindable()) {
                JsonObject group = find(keys, setting.getGroup());
                if (group != null) {
                    JsonElement key = group.get(setting.getName());
                    if (key != null && key.isJsonPrimitive() && key.getAsJsonPrimitive().isNumber()) keybinds.load(setting, key.getAsInt());
                }
            }
        }

        markMigrated(configFile);
        markMigrated(keybindFile);
        LOG.info("[Icarus] Migrated v2 settings from " + legacyDir);
        return true;
    }

    private static JsonObject find(JsonObject root, GroupNode group) {
        if (group.getSubPage() == null) return null;
        String page = group.getSubPage().getPage().getName();
        JsonObject pageObject = either(root, page);
        return pageObject == null ? null : either(pageObject, group.getName());
    }

    private static JsonObject either(JsonObject parent, String name) {
        JsonObject found = ProfileCodec.object(parent, name);
        return found != null ? found : ProfileCodec.object(parent, name.replace('_', ' '));
    }

    private static void markMigrated(Path file) {
        if (!Files.isRegularFile(file)) return;
        try {
            Files.move(file, file.resolveSibling(file.getFileName() + ".migrated"));
        } catch (IOException e) {
            LOG.log(Level.WARNING, "[Icarus] Couldn't rename migrated " + file, e);
        }
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/config/ProfileCodec.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.config;

import com.google.gson.JsonElement;
import com.google.gson.JsonObject;
import com.google.gson.JsonPrimitive;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.GroupNode;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.KeybindMode;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.Keybinds;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.Setting;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.SettingsModel;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.SubPageNode;

/**
 * Converts settings and keybinds to and from a config profile:
 * {@code {"version": 1, "settings": {page: {[subPage:] {group: {field: value}}}}, "keybinds": {same path: key}}}.
 */
public final class ProfileCodec {
    public static final int VERSION = 1;

    private ProfileCodec() {
    }

    public static JsonObject encode(SettingsModel model, Keybinds keybinds) {
        JsonObject root = new JsonObject();
        root.addProperty("version", VERSION);
        JsonObject settings = new JsonObject();
        JsonObject keys = new JsonObject();
        for (Setting<?> setting : model.getSettings()) {
            if (setting.isPersistent()) groupObject(settings, setting.getGroup()).add(setting.getName(), toJson(setting));
            int key = keybinds.get(setting);
            if (key < 0) continue;
            KeybindMode mode = keybinds.getMode(setting);
            if (mode == null || mode == setting.getDefaultMode()) {
                groupObject(keys, setting.getGroup()).addProperty(setting.getName(), key);
            } else {
                //{"key": 7, "mode": "on_hotkey"}; a plain number (older configs) means the default mode
                JsonObject binding = new JsonObject();
                binding.addProperty("key", key);
                binding.addProperty("mode", mode.name().toLowerCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT));
                groupObject(keys, setting.getGroup()).add(setting.getName(), binding);
            }
        }
        root.add("settings", settings);
        root.add("keybinds", keys);
        return root;
    }

    private static <T> JsonElement toJson(Setting<T> setting) {
        return setting.getType().toJson(setting.get());
    }

    /** Applies a profile; values that are missing or invalid keep their current value. */
    public static void apply(JsonObject root, SettingsModel model, Keybinds keybinds) {
        JsonObject settings = object(root, "settings");
        JsonObject keys = object(root, "keybinds");
        for (Setting<?> setting : model.getSettings()) {
            if (settings != null && setting.isPersistent()) {
                JsonObject group = findGroup(settings, setting.getGroup());
                if (group != null && group.has(setting.getName())) applyValue(setting, group.get(setting.getName()));
            }
            if (keys != null && setting.isBindable()) {
                JsonObject group = findGroup(keys, setting.getGroup());
                if (group != null) {
                    JsonElement key = group.get(setting.getName());
                    if (key != null && key.isJsonPrimitive() && key.getAsJsonPrimitive().isNumber()) {
                        keybinds.load(setting, key.getAsInt());
                        keybinds.loadMode(setting, setting.getDefaultMode());
                    } else if (key != null && key.isJsonObject()) {
                        JsonObject binding = key.getAsJsonObject();
                        JsonElement code = binding.get("key"), mode = binding.get("mode");
                        if (code != null && code.isJsonPrimitive() && code.getAsJsonPrimitive().isNumber()) keybinds.load(setting, code.getAsInt());
                        KeybindMode parsed = mode != null && mode.isJsonPrimitive() ? KeybindMode.byName(mode.getAsString()) : null;
                        keybinds.loadMode(setting, parsed != null ? parsed : setting.getDefaultMode());
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    static <T> void applyValue(Setting<T> setting, JsonElement json) {
        if (json == null || json.isJsonNull()) return;
        T value = setting.getType().fromJson(json, setting);
        if (value != null) setting.load(value);
    }

    private static JsonObject groupObject(JsonObject root, GroupNode group) {
        SubPageNode sub = group.getSubPage();
        JsonObject parent = root;
        if (sub != null) {
            parent = child(parent, sub.getPage().getName());
            if (!sub.isImplicit()) parent = child(parent, sub.getName());
        }
        return child(parent, group.getName());
    }

    private static JsonObject findGroup(JsonObject root, GroupNode group) {
        SubPageNode sub = group.getSubPage();
        JsonObject parent = root;
        if (sub != null) {
            parent = object(parent, sub.getPage().getName());
            if (parent != null && !sub.isImplicit()) parent = object(parent, sub.getName());
        }
        return parent == null ? null : object(parent, group.getName());
    }

    private static JsonObject child(JsonObject parent, String name) {
        JsonObject child = object(parent, name);
        if (child == null) {
            child = new JsonObject();
            parent.add(name, child);
        }
        return child;
    }

    static JsonObject object(JsonObject parent, String name) {
        JsonElement element = parent.get(name);
        return element != null && element.isJsonObject() ? element.getAsJsonObject() : null;
    }

    /** Reads {@code "version"} (0 if absent). */
    static int version(JsonObject root) {
        JsonElement v = root.get("version");
        return v instanceof JsonPrimitive && ((JsonPrimitive) v).isNumber() ? v.getAsInt() : 0;
    }
}

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/config/UiPrefs.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.config;

import com.google.gson.JsonElement;
import com.google.gson.JsonObject;

/** Per-mod UI preferences ({@code icarus.json}): always auto-saved, independent of config profiles. */
public final class UiPrefs {
    private String theme;
    private double x = Double.NaN, y = Double.NaN;
    private String page, subPage;
    private String profile;

    public String getTheme() {
        return theme;
    }

    public double getX() {
        return x;
    }

    public double getY() {
        return y;
    }

    public boolean hasPosition() {
        return !Double.isNaN(x) && !Double.isNaN(y);
    }

    public String getPage() {
        return page;
    }

    public String getSubPage() {
        return subPage;
    }

    public String getProfile() {
        return profile;
    }

    void setTheme(String theme) {
        this.theme = theme;
    }

    void setPosition(double x, double y) {
        this.x = x;
        this.y = y;
    }

    void setPage(String page, String subPage) {
        this.page = page;
        this.subPage = subPage;
    }

    void setProfile(String profile) {
        this.profile = profile;
    }

    JsonObject toJson() {
        JsonObject json = new JsonObject();
        json.addProperty("version", 1);
        if (theme != null) json.addProperty("theme", theme);
        if (hasPosition()) {
            json.addProperty("x", x);
            json.addProperty("y", y);
        }
        if (page != null) json.addProperty("page", page);
        if (subPage != null) json.addProperty("subPage", subPage);
        if (profile != null) json.addProperty("profile", profile);
        return json;
    }

    void read(JsonObject json) {
        theme = string(json, "theme");
        page = string(json, "page");
        subPage = string(json, "subPage");
        profile = string(json, "profile");
        Double px = number(json, "x"), py = number(json, "y");
        if (px != null && py != null) setPosition(px, py);
    }

    private static String string(JsonObject json, String key) {
        JsonElement e = json.get(key);
        return e != null && e.isJsonPrimitive() ? e.getAsString() : null;
    }

    private static Double number(JsonObject json, String key) {
        JsonElement e = json.get(key);
        return e != null && e.isJsonPrimitive() && e.getAsJsonPrimitive().isNumber() ? e.getAsDouble() : null;
    }
}

package studio.dreamys.icarus.hud;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.theme.Theme;

/** Per-frame information for HUD elements (reused, don't keep a reference). */
public final class HudContext {
    Theme theme;
    int accent;
    double width, height;
    float partialTick;

    public Theme getTheme() {
        return theme;
    }

    /** Accent colour of the owning mod (ARGB). */
    public int getAccent() {
        return accent;
    }

    /** Screen width in design units. */
    public double getWidth() {
        return width;
    }

    public double getHeight() {
        return height;
    }

    /** Fraction of the current tick that has passed (for smooth motion between ticks). */
    public float getPartialTick() {
        return partialTick;
    }

    public void set(Theme theme, int accent, double width, double height, float partialTick) {
        this.theme = theme;
        this.accent = accent;
        this.width = width;
        this.height = height;
        this.partialTick = partialTick;
    }
}

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/hud/HudElement.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.hud;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.Canvas;

/** Something drawn on the in-game HUD by a mod (see {@code IcarusMod#addHud}). */
public interface HudElement {
    /** Draws the element. Coordinates are design units; {@code hud} gives the theme, screen size and partial tick. */
    void render(Canvas canvas, HudContext hud);

    /** Start of every client tick. */
    default void tick() {
    }
}

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/hud/Position.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.hud;

/** Anchors for HUD elements. Stateless: {@link #x}/{@link #y} place a box of a given size on a screen of a given size. */
public enum Position {
    TOP_LEFT(0, 0), TOP_CENTER(1, 0), TOP_RIGHT(2, 0),
    CENTER_LEFT(0, 1), CENTER(1, 1), CENTER_RIGHT(2, 1),
    BOTTOM_LEFT(0, 2), BOTTOM_CENTER(1, 2), BOTTOM_RIGHT(2, 2);

    private final int column, row;

    Position(int column, int row) {
        this.column = column;
        this.row = row;
    }

    /** Left edge of a {@code width}-wide box on a {@code screenWidth}-wide screen, {@code margin} from the edge. */
    public double x(double width, double screenWidth, double margin) {
        switch (column) {
            case 0:
                return margin;
            case 1:
                return Math.round((screenWidth - width) / 2);
            default:
                return screenWidth - width - margin;
        }
    }

    /** Top edge of a {@code height}-tall box on a {@code screenHeight}-tall screen, {@code margin} from the edge. */
    public double y(double height, double screenHeight, double margin) {
        switch (row) {
            case 0:
                return margin;
            case 1:
                return Math.round((screenHeight - height) / 2);
            default:
                return screenHeight - height - margin;
        }
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/hud/Toast.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.hud;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.Canvas;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.Font;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * A toast that slides in, stays for a while and slides out. Send it with {@code IcarusMod#toasts()}.
 * Timing is in client ticks; drawing interpolates between ticks.
 */
public final class Toast {
    public static final int SLIDE_TICKS = 6;

    private final String title;
    private final String message;
    private final int stayTicks;
    int age;

    private List<String> lines;
    private double linesWidth = -1;
    private Font linesFont;

    public Toast(String title, String message) {
        this(title, message, 40);
    }

    public Toast(String title, String message, int stayTicks) {
        this.title = title == null ? "" : title;
        this.message = message == null ? "" : message;
        this.stayTicks = stayTicks;
    }

    public String getTitle() {
        return title;
    }

    public String getMessage() {
        return message;
    }

    public int getStayTicks() {
        return stayTicks;
    }

    boolean isFinished() {
        return age >= SLIDE_TICKS * 2 + stayTicks;
    }

    /** Slide progress at a point in time: 0 hidden → 1 shown → 0 hidden, eased. */
    public double visibility(float partialTick) {
        double t = age + partialTick;
        double v;
        if (t < SLIDE_TICKS) v = t / SLIDE_TICKS;
        else if (t < SLIDE_TICKS + stayTicks) v = 1;
        else v = 1 - (t - SLIDE_TICKS - stayTicks) / SLIDE_TICKS;
        v = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v));
        return 1 - Math.pow(1 - v, 3); //ease out
    }

    /** The message word-wrapped to {@code width} design units (cached until the width or font changes). */
    public List<String> lines(Canvas canvas, Font font, double width) {
        if (lines == null || width != linesWidth || font != linesFont) {
            linesWidth = width;
            linesFont = font;
            List<String> out = new ArrayList<>();
            StringBuilder line = new StringBuilder();
            for (String word : message.split(" ")) {
                String candidate = line.length() == 0 ? word : line + " " + word;
                if (line.length() > 0 && canvas.measure(font, candidate) > width) {
                    out.add(line.toString());
                    line.setLength(0);
                    line.append(word);
                } else {
                    line.setLength(0);
                    line.append(candidate);
                }
            }
            if (line.length() > 0) out.add(line.toString());
            lines = Collections.unmodifiableList(out);
        }
        return lines;
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/hud/Toasts.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.hud;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.Canvas;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Iterator;
import java.util.List;

/** A mod's toast stack: newest at the bottom, sliding in from the right edge. */
public final class Toasts implements HudElement {
    public static final double MARGIN = 10;
    public static final double GAP = 4;

    private final List<Toast> active = new ArrayList<>();
    private double offsetY;

    /** Queues a toast. */
    public void send(Toast toast) {
        active.add(toast);
    }

    public void send(String title, String message) {
        send(new Toast(title, message));
    }

    /** Extra space above the stack (e.g. to start below a watermark), in design units. */
    public void setOffsetY(double offsetY) {
        this.offsetY = offsetY;
    }

    public List<Toast> getActive() {
        return active;
    }

    public boolean hasActive() {
        return !active.isEmpty();
    }

    @Override
    public void tick() {
        Iterator<Toast> it = active.iterator();
        while (it.hasNext()) {
            Toast n = it.next();
            n.age++;
            if (n.isFinished()) it.remove();
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void render(Canvas canvas, HudContext hud) {
        double y = MARGIN + offsetY;
        for (int i = 0; i < active.size(); i++) {
            Toast n = active.get(i);
            double visibility = n.visibility(hud.getPartialTick());
            double w = hud.getTheme().toastWidth(canvas, n);
            double h = hud.getTheme().toastHeight(canvas, n);
            double x = hud.getWidth() - MARGIN - w * visibility;
            hud.getTheme().paintToast(canvas, n, x, y, w, h, visibility, hud.getAccent());
            y += (h + GAP) * visibility;
        }
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/hud/Watermark.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.hud;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.Canvas;

/** A text box in a corner of the HUD, drawn in the theme's style. Add it with {@code IcarusMod#addHud}. */
public final class Watermark implements HudElement {
    public static final double MARGIN = 10;

    private String text;
    private Position position;
    private boolean enabled = true;

    public Watermark(String text) {
        this(text, Position.TOP_RIGHT);
    }

    public Watermark(String text, Position position) {
        this.text = text;
        this.position = position;
    }

    public String getText() {
        return text;
    }

    public void setText(String text) {
        this.text = text;
    }

    public Position getPosition() {
        return position;
    }

    public void setPosition(Position position) {
        this.position = position;
    }

    public boolean isEnabled() {
        return enabled;
    }

    public void setEnabled(boolean enabled) {
        this.enabled = enabled;
    }

    /** Height of the watermark with the given context's theme (for placing things below it). */
    public double height(Canvas canvas, HudContext hud) {
        return hud.getTheme().watermarkHeight(canvas);
    }

    @Override
    public void render(Canvas canvas, HudContext hud) {
        if (!enabled || text == null) return;
        double h = hud.getTheme().watermarkHeight(canvas);
        double w = hud.getTheme().watermarkWidth(canvas, this);
        double x = position.x(w, hud.getWidth(), MARGIN);
        double y = position.y(h, hud.getHeight(), MARGIN);
        hud.getTheme().paintWatermark(canvas, this, x, y, w, h, hud.getAccent());
    }
}

```
