```java
        sdl(225, KEY_LSHIFT);
        sdl(226, KEY_LMENU);
        sdl(227, KEY_LMETA);
        sdl(228, KEY_RCONTROL);
        sdl(229, KEY_RSHIFT);
        sdl(230, KEY_RMENU);
        sdl(231, KEY_RMETA);
    }

    private Keys() {
    }

    private static void glfw(int glfw, int key) {
        GLFW_TO_KEYS.put(glfw, key);
    }

    private static void sdl(int scancode, int key) {
        SDL_TO_KEYS.put(scancode, key);
    }

    /** First mouse button code: mouse buttons are bindable as {@code MOUSE_BASE + button} (0 left, 1 right, 2 middle, 3/4 side). */
    public static final int MOUSE_BASE = 0x100;
    public static final int MOUSE_1 = MOUSE_BASE, MOUSE_2 = MOUSE_BASE + 1, MOUSE_3 = MOUSE_BASE + 2, MOUSE_4 = MOUSE_BASE + 3, MOUSE_5 = MOUSE_BASE + 4;

    /** The code of a mouse button (LWJGL numbering: 0 left, 1 right, 2 middle, 3 and 4 side buttons). */
    public static int mouse(int button) {
        return MOUSE_BASE + button;
    }

    public static boolean isMouse(int key) {
        return key >= MOUSE_BASE && key < MOUSE_BASE + 16;
    }

    /** Same as LWJGL2's {@code Keyboard.getKeyName}: e.g. "RSHIFT", "6", "M5" for mouse 5, or null for unknown codes. */
    public static String getKeyName(int key) {
        if (isMouse(key)) return "M" + (key - MOUSE_BASE + 1);
        return key >= 0 && key < NAMES.length ? NAMES[key] : null;
    }

    /** Translates a GLFW key code to the matching code in this class, or {@link #KEY_NONE}. */
    public static int fromGlfw(int glfwKey) {
        Integer key = GLFW_TO_KEYS.get(glfwKey);
        return key == null ? KEY_NONE : key;
    }

    /** Translates an SDL3 scancode (Minecraft 26.x key codes) to the matching code in this class, or {@link #KEY_NONE}. */
    public static int fromSdl(int scancode) {
        Integer key = SDL_TO_KEYS.get(scancode);
        return key == null ? KEY_NONE : key;
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/Platform.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.MenuController;

import java.io.File;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.Collection;

/**
 * Everything Icarus needs from the running Minecraft version and mod loader. Each Icarus build ships exactly one
 * implementation, named {@link #IMPLEMENTATION}.
 */
public interface Platform {
    String IMPLEMENTATION = "studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.PlatformImpl";

    /**
     * Called once, before the first mod is created. Registers the loader hooks that feed
     * {@link studio.dreamys.icarus.Icarus#onKeyPressed(int)}, {@link studio.dreamys.icarus.Icarus#renderHud(float)}
     * and {@link studio.dreamys.icarus.Icarus#clientTick()}.
     */
    void install();

    Renderer getRenderer();

    /** Minecraft's GUI scale (GUI units to framebuffer pixels). */
    double getGuiScale();

    int getFramebufferWidth();

    int getFramebufferHeight();

    /** Whether a world is loaded and the player exists. */
    boolean isInGame();

    /** Opens a Minecraft screen showing the menu; it forwards input and rendering to {@code menu}. */
    void openScreen(MenuController menu);

    File getGameDir();

    /** The roots (directories or jars) holding the classes of the given mod. */
    Collection<Path> getModRoots(String modid);

    ClassLoader getModClassLoader(String modid);

    String getClipboard();

    void setClipboard(String text);
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/Renderer.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.DrawList;

/**
 * The drawing a platform has to provide: RGBA textures and rendering a {@link DrawList} (indexed triangles in
 * framebuffer pixels, one texture per command). All methods are called on the render thread.
 */
public interface Renderer {
    /** A new RGBA texture with nearest filtering and undefined contents. */
    Texture createTexture(int width, int height);

    /**
     * Replaces a region of a texture. {@code argb[offset + row * stride + column]} is the pixel at
     * ({@code x + column}, {@code y + row}).
     */
    void upload(Texture texture, int x, int y, int width, int height, int[] argb, int offset, int stride);

    void deleteTexture(Texture texture);

    /** Draws the list over whatever is on screen, with alpha blending, in the current GUI pass. */
    void render(DrawList list);
}
```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/render/AtlasImage.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.render;

/** An image drawn from the shared atlas (e.g. a generated texture). It's (re)added to the atlas when needed. */
public final class AtlasImage {
    private final int[] argb;
    private final int width, height;

    private FontAtlas atlas;
    private int generation = -1;
    private int x, y;
    private boolean placed;

    public AtlasImage(int[] argb, int width, int height) {
        if (argb.length != width * height) throw new IllegalArgumentException("argb has " + argb.length + " pixels, expected " + width * height);
        this.argb = argb.clone();
        this.width = width;
        this.height = height;
    }

    public int getWidth() {
        return width;
    }

    public int getHeight() {
        return height;
    }

    /** Makes sure the image is in {@code atlas}; returns false if it doesn't fit. */
    boolean ensure(FontAtlas atlas) {
        if (this.atlas != atlas || generation != atlas.getGeneration()) {
            int[] at = atlas.add(argb, width, height);
            this.atlas = atlas;
            generation = atlas.getGeneration();
            placed = at != null;
            if (placed) {
                x = at[0];
                y = at[1];
            }
        }
        return placed;
    }

    int getX() {
        return x;
    }

    int getY() {
        return y;
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/render/Canvas.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.render;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Texture;

/**
 * Drawing in design units (1 unit = 1 screen pixel at Minecraft GUI scale 2), written to a {@link DrawList} in
 * framebuffer pixels. Every edge is snapped to whole pixels the same way, so shapes and text line up exactly at any
 * scale. All primitives sample the shared {@link FontAtlas}, so a frame is normally a single draw call.
 */
public final class Canvas {
    private DrawList list;
    private FontAtlas atlas;
    private double scale = 1;
    private float whiteU, whiteV;
    private int alphaMul = 255;
    private int clipDepth;

    /** Starts drawing a frame. {@code scale} is pixels per design unit; the list is reset to the framebuffer size. */
    public void begin(DrawList list, FontAtlas atlas, Texture atlasTexture, double scale, int framebufferWidth, int framebufferHeight) {
        this.list = list;
        this.atlas = atlas;
        this.scale = scale;
        this.alphaMul = 255;
        this.clipDepth = 0;
        list.reset(framebufferWidth, framebufferHeight);
        list.setTexture(atlasTexture);
        refreshWhite();
    }

    /** Prepares measuring (text widths, line heights) for a scale without starting a frame. */
    public void setup(FontAtlas atlas, double scale) {
        this.atlas = atlas;
        this.scale = scale;
    }

    private void refreshWhite() {
        whiteU = atlas.whiteU();
        whiteV = atlas.whiteV();
    }

    public DrawList getList() {
        return list;
    }

    public FontAtlas getAtlas() {
        return atlas;
    }

    /** Pixels per design unit. */
    public double getScale() {
        return scale;
    }

    /** A design coordinate in whole framebuffer pixels. */
    public int px(double design) {
        return (int) Math.round(design * scale);
    }

    /** Thickness of a 1-unit line in pixels (at least 1). */
    public int hairline() {
        return Math.max(1, (int) Math.round(scale));
    }

    /** Multiplies the alpha of everything drawn until reset (0..1), for fades. */
    public void setAlpha(double alpha) {
        alphaMul = (int) Math.round(Math.max(0, Math.min(1, alpha)) * 255);
    }

    private int a(int argb) {
        if (alphaMul == 255) return argb;
        int alpha = (argb >>> 24) * alphaMul / 255;
        return alpha << 24 | argb & 0xFFFFFF;
    }

    /* ---- clipping ---- */

    public void pushClip(double x, double y, double w, double h) {
        list.pushClip(px(x), px(y), px(x + w), px(y + h));
        clipDepth++;
    }

    public void popClip() {
        list.popClip();
        clipDepth--;
    }

    /* ---- shapes ---- */

    private void pixelRect(int x0, int y0, int x1, int y1, int tl, int tr, int br, int bl) {
        list.quad(x0, y0, x1, y1, whiteU, whiteV, whiteU, whiteV, a(tl), a(tr), a(br), a(bl));
    }

    /** End pixel of a span that is at least one pixel wide when {@code size > 0}. */
    private int end(double start, double size) {
        int s = px(start), e = px(start + size);
        return size > 0 && e <= s ? s + 1 : e;
    }

    public void rect(double x, double y, double w, double h, int color) {
        if (w <= 0 || h <= 0) return;
        pixelRect(px(x), px(y), end(x, w), end(y, h), color, color, color, color);
    }

    /** Vertical gradient. */
    public void gradientV(double x, double y, double w, double h, int top, int bottom) {
        if (w <= 0 || h <= 0) return;
        pixelRect(px(x), px(y), end(x, w), end(y, h), top, top, bottom, bottom);
    }

    /** Horizontal gradient. */
    public void gradientH(double x, double y, double w, double h, int left, int right) {
        if (w <= 0 || h <= 0) return;
        pixelRect(px(x), px(y), end(x, w), end(y, h), left, right, right, left);
    }

    /** A rectangle with a colour per corner. */
    public void gradient4(double x, double y, double w, double h, int topLeft, int topRight, int bottomRight, int bottomLeft) {
        if (w <= 0 || h <= 0) return;
        pixelRect(px(x), px(y), end(x, w), end(y, h), topLeft, topRight, bottomRight, bottomLeft);
    }

    /** A border of {@link #hairline()} pixels drawn inside the rectangle. */
    public void outline(double x, double y, double w, double h, int color) {
        if (w <= 0 || h <= 0) return;
        int x0 = px(x), y0 = px(y), x1 = end(x, w), y1 = end(y, h), t = hairline();
        if (x1 - x0 <= 2 * t || y1 - y0 <= 2 * t) {
            pixelRect(x0, y0, x1, y1, color, color, color, color);
            return;
        }
        pixelRect(x0, y0, x1, y0 + t, color, color, color, color);
        pixelRect(x0, y1 - t, x1, y1, color, color, color, color);
        pixelRect(x0, y0 + t, x0 + t, y1 - t, color, color, color, color);
        pixelRect(x1 - t, y0 + t, x1, y1 - t, color, color, color, color);
    }

    /** A horizontal line of {@link #hairline()} pixels whose top edge is at {@code y}. */
    public void hline(double x, double y, double w, int color) {
        if (w <= 0) return;
        int y0 = px(y);
        pixelRect(px(x), y0, end(x, w), y0 + hairline(), color, color, color, color);
    }

    /** A vertical line of {@link #hairline()} pixels whose left edge is at {@code x}. */
    public void vline(double x, double y, double h, int color) {
        if (h <= 0) return;
        int x0 = px(x);
        pixelRect(x0, px(y), x0 + hairline(), end(y, h), color, color, color, color);
    }

    /** A horizontal gradient line (e.g. an accent strip). */
    public void hlineGradient(double x, double y, double w, int left, int right) {
        if (w <= 0) return;
        int y0 = px(y);
        pixelRect(px(x), y0, end(x, w), y0 + hairline(), left, right, right, left);
    }

    public void triangle(double x0, double y0, double x1, double y1, double x2, double y2, int color) {
        list.triangle((float) (x0 * scale), (float) (y0 * scale), (float) (x1 * scale), (float) (y1 * scale),
                (float) (x2 * scale), (float) (y2 * scale), whiteU, whiteV, a(color));
    }

    /* ---- images ---- */

    /** Draws an atlas image stretched over the rectangle, tinted. */
    public void image(AtlasImage image, double x, double y, double w, double h, int tint) {
        if (!image.ensure(atlas)) return;
        refreshWhite();
        float s = atlas.getSize();
        list.quad(px(x), px(y), end(x, w), end(y, h), image.getX() / s, image.getY() / s,
                (image.getX() + image.getWidth()) / s, (image.getY() + image.getHeight()) / s, a(tint), a(tint), a(tint), a(tint));
    }

    /** Tiles an atlas image over the rectangle at one image pixel per screen pixel (e.g. a noise background). */
    public void tile(AtlasImage image, double x, double y, double w, double h, int tint) {
        if (w <= 0 || h <= 0 || !image.ensure(atlas)) return;
        refreshWhite();
        int x0 = px(x), y0 = px(y), x1 = end(x, w), y1 = end(y, h);
        list.pushClip(x0, y0, x1, y1);
        float s = atlas.getSize();
        float u0 = image.getX() / s, v0 = image.getY() / s;
        float u1 = (image.getX() + image.getWidth()) / s, v1 = (image.getY() + image.getHeight()) / s;
        int c = a(tint);
        for (int ty = y0; ty < y1; ty += image.getHeight()) {
            for (int tx = x0; tx < x1; tx += image.getWidth()) {
                list.quad(tx, ty, tx + image.getWidth(), ty + image.getHeight(), u0, v0, u1, v1, c, c, c, c);
            }
        }
        list.popClip();
    }

    /* ---- text ---- */

    /** Line height of a font in design units. */
    public double lineHeight(Font font) {
        return font.face(scale).getLineHeight() / scale;
    }

    /** Distance from the top of a line to the baseline, in design units. */
    public double ascent(Font font) {
        return font.face(scale).getAscent() / scale;
    }

    /** Width of text in design units (formatting codes aren't counted). */
    public double measure(Font font, CharSequence text) {
        if (text == null) return 0;
        boolean bold = false, italic = false;
        float pen = 0;
        int length = text.length();
        for (int i = 0; i < length; i++) {
            char c = text.charAt(i);
            if (c == Formatting.PREFIX) {
                if (i + 1 < length) {
                    int code = Formatting.code(text.charAt(++i));
                    if (code >= 0 && code < 16 || code == Formatting.RESET) {
                        bold = false;
                        italic = false;
                    } else if (code == Formatting.BOLD) {
                        bold = true;
                    } else if (code == Formatting.ITALIC) {
                        italic = true;
                    }
                }
                continue;
            }
            int codePoint = c;
            if (Character.isHighSurrogate(c) && i + 1 < length && Character.isLowSurrogate(text.charAt(i + 1))) {
                codePoint = Character.toCodePoint(c, text.charAt(++i));
            }
            pen += font.variant(bold, italic).face(scale).glyph(codePoint).advance;
        }
        return pen / scale;
    }

    /**
     * Draws text with its line top at {@code y}. Supports {@code §} formatting codes; colour codes keep the base
     * colour's alpha. Returns the width in design units.
     */
    public double text(Font font, CharSequence text, double x, double y, int color) {
        if (text == null) return 0;
        refreshWhite();
        FontFace baseFace = font.face(scale);
        float start = px(x);
        float pen = start;
        int baseline = px(y) + Math.round(baseFace.getAscent());
        int current = a(color);
        int alpha = current & 0xFF000000;
        boolean bold = false, italic = false, underline = false, strike = false;
        float s = atlas.getSize();
        int length = text.length();
        for (int i = 0; i < length; i++) {
            char c = text.charAt(i);
            if (c == Formatting.PREFIX) {
                if (i + 1 < length) {
                    int code = Formatting.code(text.charAt(++i));
                    if (code >= 0 && code < 16) {
                        current = alpha | Formatting.rgb(code);
                        bold = italic = underline = strike = false;
                    } else if (code == Formatting.RESET) {
                        current = a(color);
                        bold = italic = underline = strike = false;
                    } else if (code == Formatting.BOLD) {
                        bold = true;
                    } else if (code == Formatting.ITALIC) {
                        italic = true;
                    } else if (code == Formatting.UNDERLINE) {
                        underline = true;
                    } else if (code == Formatting.STRIKETHROUGH) {
                        strike = true;
                    }
                }
                continue;
            }
            int codePoint = c;
            if (Character.isHighSurrogate(c) && i + 1 < length && Character.isLowSurrogate(text.charAt(i + 1))) {
                codePoint = Character.toCodePoint(c, text.charAt(++i));
            }
            FontFace face = font.variant(bold, italic).face(scale);
            FontFace.Glyph glyph = face.glyph(codePoint);
            if (glyph.hasImage()) {
                int gx = Math.round(pen) + glyph.offsetX, gy = baseline + glyph.offsetY;
                list.quad(gx, gy, gx + glyph.width, gy + glyph.height,
                        glyph.x / s, glyph.y / s, (glyph.x + glyph.width) / s, (glyph.y + glyph.height) / s,
                        current, current, current, current);
            }
            float next = pen + glyph.advance;
            int t = hairline();
            if (underline) {
                int uy = baseline + Math.max(1, Math.round(face.getDescent() / 2));
                list.quad(Math.round(pen), uy, Math.round(next), uy + t, whiteU, whiteV, whiteU, whiteV, current, current, current, current);
            }
            if (strike) {
                int sy = baseline - Math.round(face.getAscent() * 0.3f);
                list.quad(Math.round(pen), sy, Math.round(next), sy + t, whiteU, whiteV, whiteU, whiteV, current, current, current, current);
            }
            pen = next;
        }
        return (pen - start) / scale;
    }

    /** Text with a 1-pixel drop shadow (down-right). */
    public double textShadow(Font font, CharSequence text, double x, double y, int color, int shadow) {
        double offset = 1 / scale;
        text(font, text, x + offset, y + offset, shadow);
        return text(font, text, x, y, color);
    }

    /** Text with a 1-pixel outline on all sides. */
    public double textOutline(Font font, CharSequence text, double x, double y, int color, int outline) {
        double o = 1 / scale;
        text(font, text, x - o, y, outline);
        text(font, text, x + o, y, outline);
        text(font, text, x, y - o, outline);
        text(font, text, x, y + o, outline);
        return text(font, text, x, y, color);
    }

    /** {@code text}, shortened with "…" so it fits {@code maxWidth}. Returns the same instance when it fits. */
    public String fit(Font font, String text, double maxWidth) {
        if (text == null || measure(font, text) <= maxWidth) return text;
        String ellipsis = "…";
        double room = maxWidth - measure(font, ellipsis);
        int end = text.length();
        while (end > 0 && measure(font, text.substring(0, end)) > room) end--;
        if (end > 0 && Character.isHighSurrogate(text.charAt(end - 1))) end--;
        return text.substring(0, end) + ellipsis;
    }

    /** Depth of {@link #pushClip} nesting (for checks). */
    public int getClipDepth() {
        return clipDepth;
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/render/DrawList.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.render;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Texture;

/**
 * One frame of Icarus drawing: indexed triangles in framebuffer pixels with per-vertex colour and UVs, split into
 * commands only when the texture changes. Clipping is done on the CPU while primitives are added (axis-aligned
 * quads are cut exactly, triangles are kept or dropped), so platforms never need a scissor. Arrays grow as needed
 * and are reused across frames, so steady-state frames don't allocate.
 */
public final class DrawList {
    private float[] xy = new float[2 * 1024];
    private float[] uv = new float[2 * 1024];
    private int[] colors = new int[1024];
    private int vertexCount;

    private int[] indices = new int[1536];
    private int indexCount;

    private Texture[] cmdTexture = new Texture[8];
    private int[] cmdStart = new int[8];
    private int[] cmdCount = new int[8];
    private int commandCount;
    private Texture texture;

    private float[] clips = new float[4 * 16];
    private int clipDepth;
    private float clipX0, clipY0, clipX1, clipY1;

    public DrawList() {
        reset(Float.MAX_VALUE, Float.MAX_VALUE);
    }

    /** Starts a new frame; {@code width}/{@code height} bound the default clip (the framebuffer). */
    public void reset(float width, float height) {
        vertexCount = 0;
        indexCount = 0;
        commandCount = 0;
        texture = null;
        clipDepth = 0;
        clipX0 = 0;
        clipY0 = 0;
        clipX1 = width;
        clipY1 = height;
    }

    /* ---- state ---- */

    public void setTexture(Texture texture) {
        this.texture = texture;
    }

    /** Intersects the clip rectangle (pixels) with the current one. */
    public void pushClip(float x0, float y0, float x1, float y1) {
        if (clipDepth * 4 + 4 > clips.length) clips = grow(clips, clips.length * 2);
        clips[clipDepth * 4] = clipX0;
        clips[clipDepth * 4 + 1] = clipY0;
        clips[clipDepth * 4 + 2] = clipX1;
        clips[clipDepth * 4 + 3] = clipY1;
        clipDepth++;
        clipX0 = Math.max(clipX0, x0);
        clipY0 = Math.max(clipY0, y0);
        clipX1 = Math.min(clipX1, x1);
        clipY1 = Math.min(clipY1, y1);
    }

    public void popClip() {
        if (clipDepth == 0) throw new IllegalStateException("popClip without pushClip");
        clipDepth--;
        clipX0 = clips[clipDepth * 4];
        clipY0 = clips[clipDepth * 4 + 1];
        clipX1 = clips[clipDepth * 4 + 2];
        clipY1 = clips[clipDepth * 4 + 3];
    }

    public boolean isClippedOut(float x0, float y0, float x1, float y1) {
        return x1 <= clipX0 || x0 >= clipX1 || y1 <= clipY0 || y0 >= clipY1 || clipX1 <= clipX0 || clipY1 <= clipY0;
    }

    /* ---- primitives ---- */

    /**
     * An axis-aligned quad with a colour per corner (top-left, top-right, bottom-right, bottom-left), cut to the clip
     * rectangle with UVs and colours interpolated.
     */
    public void quad(float x0, float y0, float x1, float y1, float u0, float v0, float u1, float v1,
                     int topLeft, int topRight, int bottomRight, int bottomLeft) {
        if (x1 < x0 || y1 < y0 || isClippedOut(x0, y0, x1, y1)) return;

        float cx0 = Math.max(x0, clipX0), cy0 = Math.max(y0, clipY0);
        float cx1 = Math.min(x1, clipX1), cy1 = Math.min(y1, clipY1);
        if (cx0 != x0 || cy0 != y0 || cx1 != x1 || cy1 != y1) {
            float w = x1 - x0, h = y1 - y0;
            float fx0 = (cx0 - x0) / w, fx1 = (cx1 - x0) / w, fy0 = (cy0 - y0) / h, fy1 = (cy1 - y0) / h;
            float nu0 = u0 + (u1 - u0) * fx0, nu1 = u0 + (u1 - u0) * fx1;
            float nv0 = v0 + (v1 - v0) * fy0, nv1 = v0 + (v1 - v0) * fy1;
            int tl = bilerp(topLeft, topRight, bottomRight, bottomLeft, fx0, fy0);
            int tr = bilerp(topLeft, topRight, bottomRight, bottomLeft, fx1, fy0);
            int br = bilerp(topLeft, topRight, bottomRight, bottomLeft, fx1, fy1);
            int bl = bilerp(topLeft, topRight, bottomRight, bottomLeft, fx0, fy1);
            x0 = cx0; y0 = cy0; x1 = cx1; y1 = cy1;
            u0 = nu0; u1 = nu1; v0 = nv0; v1 = nv1;
            topLeft = tl; topRight = tr; bottomRight = br; bottomLeft = bl;
        }

        ensureCommand();
        ensureVertices(4);
        ensureIndices(6);
        int base = vertexCount;
        vertex(x0, y0, u0, v0, topLeft);
        vertex(x1, y0, u1, v0, topRight);
        vertex(x1, y1, u1, v1, bottomRight);
        vertex(x0, y1, u0, v1, bottomLeft);
        //counter-clockwise on screen (y down), the winding GUI renderers treat as front-facing
        index(base, base + 3, base + 2);
        index(base, base + 2, base + 1);
    }

    /** A triangle; kept whole if its bounds are inside the clip, dropped otherwise. */
    public void triangle(float x0, float y0, float x1, float y1, float x2, float y2, float u, float v, int color) {
        float minX = Math.min(x0, Math.min(x1, x2)), maxX = Math.max(x0, Math.max(x1, x2));
        float minY = Math.min(y0, Math.min(y1, y2)), maxY = Math.max(y0, Math.max(y1, y2));
        if (minX < clipX0 || minY < clipY0 || maxX > clipX1 || maxY > clipY1) return;
        //same winding as quads (counter-clockwise on screen), so renderers that cull back faces keep it
        if ((x1 - x0) * (y2 - y0) - (y1 - y0) * (x2 - x0) > 0) {
            float tx = x1, ty = y1;
            x1 = x2;
            y1 = y2;
            x2 = tx;
            y2 = ty;
        }
        ensureCommand();
        ensureVertices(3);
        ensureIndices(3);
        int base = vertexCount;
        vertex(x0, y0, u, v, color);
        vertex(x1, y1, u, v, color);
        vertex(x2, y2, u, v, color);
        index(base, base + 1, base + 2);
    }

    private void vertex(float x, float y, float u, float v, int color) {
        int i = vertexCount++;
        xy[i * 2] = x;
        xy[i * 2 + 1] = y;
        uv[i * 2] = u;
        uv[i * 2 + 1] = v;
        colors[i] = color;
    }

    private void index(int a, int b, int c) {
        indices[indexCount++] = a;
        indices[indexCount++] = b;
        indices[indexCount++] = c;
        cmdCount[commandCount - 1] += 3;
    }

    private void ensureCommand() {
        if (texture == null) throw new IllegalStateException("No texture set");
        if (commandCount > 0 && cmdTexture[commandCount - 1] == texture) return;
        if (commandCount > 0 && cmdCount[commandCount - 1] == 0) {
            cmdTexture[commandCount - 1] = texture;
            return;
        }
        if (commandCount == cmdTexture.length) {
            Texture[] t = new Texture[commandCount * 2];
            System.arraycopy(cmdTexture, 0, t, 0, commandCount);
            cmdTexture = t;
            cmdStart = grow(cmdStart, commandCount * 2);
            cmdCount = grow(cmdCount, commandCount * 2);
        }
        cmdTexture[commandCount] = texture;
        cmdStart[commandCount] = indexCount;
        cmdCount[commandCount] = 0;
        commandCount++;
    }

    private void ensureVertices(int n) {
        if (vertexCount + n <= colors.length) return;
        int capacity = Math.max(colors.length * 2, vertexCount + n);
        xy = grow(xy, capacity * 2);
        uv = grow(uv, capacity * 2);
        colors = grow(colors, capacity);
    }

    private void ensureIndices(int n) {
        if (indexCount + n > indices.length) indices = grow(indices, Math.max(indices.length * 2, indexCount + n));
    }

    private static float[] grow(float[] a, int size) {
        float[] b = new float[size];
        System.arraycopy(a, 0, b, 0, a.length);
        return b;
    }

    private static int[] grow(int[] a, int size) {
        int[] b = new int[size];
        System.arraycopy(a, 0, b, 0, a.length);
        return b;
    }

    /** Bilinear interpolation of four ARGB corner colours (fx, fy in 0..1). */
    static int bilerp(int tl, int tr, int br, int bl, float fx, float fy) {
        if (tl == tr && tr == br && br == bl) return tl;
        return lerpColor(lerpColor(tl, tr, fx), lerpColor(bl, br, fx), fy);
    }

    public static int lerpColor(int a, int b, float t) {
        if (a == b) return a;
        int aa = a >>> 24, ar = a >> 16 & 255, ag = a >> 8 & 255, ab = a & 255;
        int ba = b >>> 24, br = b >> 16 & 255, bg = b >> 8 & 255, bb = b & 255;
        return Math.round(aa + (ba - aa) * t) << 24 | Math.round(ar + (br - ar) * t) << 16
                | Math.round(ag + (bg - ag) * t) << 8 | Math.round(ab + (bb - ab) * t);
    }

    /* ---- read access for renderers ---- */

    public boolean isEmpty() {
        return indexCount == 0;
    }

    public int getVertexCount() {
        return vertexCount;
    }

    /** Interleaved x, y pixel coordinates (2 floats per vertex). */
    public float[] getPositions() {
        return xy;
    }

    /** Interleaved u, v texture coordinates (2 floats per vertex). */
    public float[] getUvs() {
        return uv;
    }

    /** ARGB colour per vertex. */
    public int[] getColors() {
        return colors;
    }

    public int[] getIndices() {
        return indices;
    }

    public int getCommandCount() {
        return commandCount;
    }

    public Texture getCommandTexture(int command) {
        return cmdTexture[command];
    }

    public int getCommandIndexStart(int command) {
        return cmdStart[command];
    }

    public int getCommandIndexCount(int command) {
        return cmdCount[command];
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/render/Font.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.render;

/**
 * A font family/style/size in design units (1 design unit = 1 screen pixel at Minecraft GUI scale 2). Text is
 * rasterised at the real pixel size for the current render scale, so it stays sharp at every scale.
 */
public final class Font {
    private final FontAtlas atlas;
    private final String family;
    private final int style;
    private final double size;

    private double lastScale = -1;
    private FontFace lastFace;
    private Font bold, italic, boldItalic;

    public Font(FontAtlas atlas, String family, int style, double size) {
        this.atlas = atlas;
        this.family = family;
        this.style = style;
        this.size = size;
    }

    public String getFamily() {
        return family;
    }

    /** {@link java.awt.Font} style bits. */
    public int getStyle() {
        return style;
    }

    /** Size in design units. */
    public double getSize() {
        return size;
    }

    /** The rasterised face for a render scale (pixels per design unit). */
    public FontFace face(double scale) {
        if (scale != lastScale) {
            lastFace = atlas.face(family, style, Math.max(1, (int) Math.round(size * scale)));
            lastScale = scale;
        }
        return lastFace;
    }

    /** This font with bold and/or italic added (for formatting codes). */
    public Font variant(boolean wantBold, boolean wantItalic) {
        if (!wantBold && !wantItalic) return this;
        if (wantBold && wantItalic) {
            if (boldItalic == null) boldItalic = new Font(atlas, family, style | java.awt.Font.BOLD | java.awt.Font.ITALIC, size);
            return boldItalic;
        }
        if (wantBold) {
            if (bold == null) bold = new Font(atlas, family, style | java.awt.Font.BOLD, size);
            return bold;
        }
        if (italic == null) italic = new Font(atlas, family, style | java.awt.Font.ITALIC, size);
        return italic;
    }
}
```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/render/FontAtlas.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.render;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Renderer;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Texture;

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * The single texture everything Icarus draws comes from: glyphs (rasterised on demand), generated images, and a
 * white block used for solid shapes. Because all primitives share it, a frame is normally one draw call.
 * <p>
 * Regions are packed in shelves. When the atlas fills up it doubles (up to {@link #MAX_SIZE}); beyond that it's
 * cleared and {@link #getGeneration()} increments so caches re-add what they need. The CPU copy is uploaded in one
 * dirty rectangle per frame by {@link #sync(Renderer)}.
 */
public final class FontAtlas {
    static final int INITIAL_SIZE = 1024;
    static final int MAX_SIZE = 2048;
    private static final int PADDING = 1;

    private int size;
    private int[] pixels;
    private int shelfX, shelfY, shelfHeight;
    private int dirtyX0, dirtyY0, dirtyX1, dirtyY1;
    private int generation;

    private Texture texture;
    private int textureSize;

    private final Map<String, FontFace> faces = new HashMap<>();

    public FontAtlas() {
        this(INITIAL_SIZE);
    }

    FontAtlas(int initialSize) {
        allocate(initialSize);
    }

    private void allocate(int newSize) {
        size = newSize;
        pixels = new int[size * size];
        shelfX = 0;
        shelfY = 0;
        shelfHeight = 0;
        //white block at the origin for solid shapes (sampled at its centre)
        for (int y = 0; y < 4; y++) for (int x = 0; x < 4; x++) pixels[y * size + x] = 0xFFFFFFFF;
        shelfX = 4 + PADDING;
        shelfHeight = 4;
        markDirty(0, 0, size, size);
    }

    public int getSize() {
        return size;
    }

    /** Increments whenever the atlas is cleared; cached regions from an older generation are gone. */
    public int getGeneration() {
        return generation;
    }

    /** U/V of a pixel inside the white block. */
    public float whiteU() {
        return 2f / size;
    }

    public float whiteV() {
        return 2f / size;
    }

    /**
     * Reserves a {@code w}×{@code h} region and copies {@code argb} into it. Returns {x, y} or null if the image
     * can't fit even in a cleared atlas. May grow or clear the atlas (check {@link #getGeneration()}).
     */
    public int[] add(int[] argb, int w, int h) {
        if (w + PADDING > MAX_SIZE || h + PADDING > MAX_SIZE) return null;
        int[] at = place(w, h);
        if (at == null) {
            if (size < MAX_SIZE) {
                grow();
            } else {
                clear();
            }
            at = place(w, h);
            if (at == null) return null;
        }
        for (int row = 0; row < h; row++) System.arraycopy(argb, row * w, pixels, (at[1] + row) * size + at[0], w);
        markDirty(at[0], at[1], at[0] + w, at[1] + h);
        return at;
    }

    private int[] place(int w, int h) {
        if (shelfX + w + PADDING > size) {
            shelfY += shelfHeight + PADDING;
            shelfX = 0;
            shelfHeight = 0;
        }
        if (shelfY + h + PADDING > size) return null;
        int[] at = {shelfX, shelfY};
        shelfX += w + PADDING;
        shelfHeight = Math.max(shelfHeight, h);
        return at;
    }

    private void grow() {
        int oldSize = size;
        int[] old = pixels;
        int oldShelfX = shelfX, oldShelfY = shelfY, oldShelfHeight = shelfHeight;
        size = oldSize * 2;
        pixels = new int[size * size];
        for (int row = 0; row < oldSize; row++) System.arraycopy(old, row * oldSize, pixels, row * size, oldSize);
        //keep packing below the old content; the old shelves' right side stays unused
        shelfX = 0;
        shelfY = oldShelfY + oldShelfHeight + PADDING;
        shelfHeight = 0;
        if (oldShelfX == 0 && oldShelfHeight == 0) shelfY = oldShelfY;
        markDirty(0, 0, size, size);
    }

    private void clear() {
        allocate(size);
        generation++;
        for (FontFace face : faces.values()) face.clearGlyphs();
    }

    private void markDirty(int x0, int y0, int x1, int y1) {
        if (dirtyX1 <= dirtyX0 || dirtyY1 <= dirtyY0) {
            dirtyX0 = x0;
            dirtyY0 = y0;
            dirtyX1 = x1;
            dirtyY1 = y1;
        } else {
            dirtyX0 = Math.min(dirtyX0, x0);
            dirtyY0 = Math.min(dirtyY0, y0);
            dirtyX1 = Math.max(dirtyX1, x1);
            dirtyY1 = Math.max(dirtyY1, y1);
        }
    }

    /** Creates/recreates the GPU texture and uploads pending changes. Call on the render thread before drawing. */
    public Texture sync(Renderer renderer) {
        if (texture == null || textureSize != size) {
            if (texture != null) renderer.deleteTexture(texture);
            texture = renderer.createTexture(size, size);
            textureSize = size;
            markDirty(0, 0, size, size);
        }
        if (dirtyX1 > dirtyX0 && dirtyY1 > dirtyY0) {
            renderer.upload(texture, dirtyX0, dirtyY0, dirtyX1 - dirtyX0, dirtyY1 - dirtyY0, pixels, dirtyY0 * size + dirtyX0, size);
            dirtyX0 = dirtyY0 = dirtyX1 = dirtyY1 = 0;
        }
        return texture;
    }

    /** The current texture (after {@link #sync}). */
    public Texture getTexture() {
        return texture;
    }

    /** Drops the GPU texture (e.g. after the platform lost its GL context); it's recreated on the next sync. */
    public void invalidateTexture() {
        texture = null;
    }

    /** A rasterised face of {@code family}/{@code style} at {@code pixelSize} real pixels (cached). */
    public FontFace face(String family, int style, int pixelSize) {
        String key = family + '\u0000' + style + '\u0000' + pixelSize;
        FontFace face = faces.get(key);
        if (face == null) {
            face = new FontFace(this, family, style, pixelSize);
            faces.put(key, face);
        }
        return face;
    }

    /** Pixel at (x, y) of the CPU copy (tests). */
    int pixel(int x, int y) {
        return pixels[y * size + x];
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/render/FontFace.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.render;

import java.awt.Font;
import java.awt.Graphics2D;
import java.awt.Rectangle;
import java.awt.RenderingHints;
import java.awt.font.FontRenderContext;
import java.awt.font.GlyphVector;
import java.awt.font.LineMetrics;
import java.awt.geom.AffineTransform;
import java.awt.image.BufferedImage;

/**
 * A font at one real pixel size. Glyphs are hinted with whole-pixel advances, like Windows (GDI) text, so small
 * sizes stay crisp; they're rasterised with AWT the first time a code point is used and packed into the shared
 * {@link FontAtlas}; afterwards lookups and measuring don't allocate.
 */
public final class FontFace {
    private final FontAtlas atlas;
    private final Font font;
    private final FontRenderContext frc;
    private final float ascent, descent, lineHeight;
    private final IntMap<Glyph> glyphs = new IntMap<>();

    FontFace(FontAtlas atlas, String family, int style, int pixelSize) {
        this.atlas = atlas;
        this.font = new Font(family, style, 1).deriveFont((float) pixelSize);
        this.frc = new FontRenderContext(new AffineTransform(), true, false);
        LineMetrics metrics = font.getLineMetrics("Hg", frc);
        this.ascent = metrics.getAscent();
        this.descent = metrics.getDescent();
        this.lineHeight = (float) Math.ceil(metrics.getAscent() + metrics.getDescent());
    }

    /** Pixels from the top of a line to the baseline. */
    public float getAscent() {
        return ascent;
    }

    public float getDescent() {
        return descent;
    }

    public float getLineHeight() {
        return lineHeight;
    }

    /** Whether the font has a real glyph for the code point (otherwise its missing-glyph box is drawn). */
    public boolean canDisplay(int codePoint) {
        return font.canDisplay(codePoint);
    }

    /** The glyph for a code point, rasterising it on first use. */
    public Glyph glyph(int codePoint) {
        Glyph glyph = glyphs.get(codePoint);
        if (glyph == null || glyph.generation != atlas.getGeneration()) {
            glyph = rasterise(codePoint);
            glyphs.put(codePoint, glyph);
        }
        return glyph;
    }

    void clearGlyphs() {
        glyphs.clear();
    }

    private Glyph rasterise(int codePoint) {
        GlyphVector vector = font.createGlyphVector(frc, new String(Character.toChars(codePoint)));
        float advance = (float) vector.getGlyphMetrics(0).getAdvanceX();
        Rectangle bounds = vector.getPixelBounds(frc, 0, 0);

        //a thin stem hinted flush against its advance (Segoe UI Bold "I" at 11px) would touch the next glyph
        if (bounds.width > 0 && bounds.width <= 2 && bounds.x >= 1 && bounds.x + bounds.width >= advance) advance += 1;

        Glyph glyph = new Glyph();
        glyph.advance = advance;
        glyph.generation = atlas.getGeneration();
        if (bounds.width <= 0 || bounds.height <= 0) return glyph; //whitespace

        int w = bounds.width + 2, h = bounds.height + 2;
        BufferedImage image = new BufferedImage(w, h, BufferedImage.TYPE_INT_ARGB);
        Graphics2D g = image.createGraphics();
        g.setRenderingHint(RenderingHints.KEY_TEXT_ANTIALIASING, RenderingHints.VALUE_TEXT_ANTIALIAS_ON);
        g.setRenderingHint(RenderingHints.KEY_ANTIALIASING, RenderingHints.VALUE_ANTIALIAS_ON);
        g.setRenderingHint(RenderingHints.KEY_FRACTIONALMETRICS, RenderingHints.VALUE_FRACTIONALMETRICS_OFF);
        g.setRenderingHint(RenderingHints.KEY_STROKE_CONTROL, RenderingHints.VALUE_STROKE_PURE);
        g.setColor(java.awt.Color.WHITE);
        g.drawGlyphVector(vector, 1 - bounds.x, 1 - bounds.y);
        g.dispose();

        int[] argb = image.getRGB(0, 0, w, h, null, 0, w);
        for (int i = 0; i < argb.length; i++) argb[i] = (argb[i] & 0xFF000000) | 0x00FFFFFF; //white, coverage in alpha

        int generation = atlas.getGeneration();
        int[] at = atlas.add(argb, w, h);
        if (atlas.getGeneration() != generation) {
            //the atlas was cleared to make room; this glyph made it into the new generation
            glyph.generation = atlas.getGeneration();
        }
        if (at == null) return glyph;
        glyph.x = at[0];
        glyph.y = at[1];
        glyph.width = w;
        glyph.height = h;
        glyph.offsetX = bounds.x - 1;
        glyph.offsetY = bounds.y - 1;
        return glyph;
    }

    /** A rasterised glyph: atlas region plus placement relative to the pen position on the baseline. */
    public static final class Glyph {
        int x, y, width, height;
        int offsetX, offsetY;
        float advance;
        int generation;

        public float getAdvance() {
            return advance;
        }

        public boolean hasImage() {
            return width > 0;
        }
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/render/Formatting.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.render;

/**
 * Minecraft-style {@code §} formatting codes, shared by text drawing and measuring so both always agree.
 * A {@code §} and the character after it are never drawn; unknown codes are ignored.
 */
public final class Formatting {
    public static final char PREFIX = '§';

    public static final int BOLD = 17, STRIKETHROUGH = 18, UNDERLINE = 19, ITALIC = 20, RESET = 21, OBFUSCATED = 16;
    public static final int NONE = -1;

    private static final int[] PALETTE = {
            0x000000, 0x0000AA, 0x00AA00, 0x00AAAA, 0xAA0000, 0xAA00AA, 0xFFAA00, 0xAAAAAA,
            0x555555, 0x5555FF, 0x55FF55, 0x55FFFF, 0xFF5555, 0xFF55FF, 0xFFFF55, 0xFFFFFF
    };

    private Formatting() {
    }

    /**
     * The meaning of the character after {@code §} (case-insensitive): 0–15 colours, {@link #OBFUSCATED},
     * {@link #BOLD}, {@link #STRIKETHROUGH}, {@link #UNDERLINE}, {@link #ITALIC}, {@link #RESET}, or {@link #NONE}.
     */
    public static int code(char c) {
        if (c >= '0' && c <= '9') return c - '0';
        if (c >= 'a' && c <= 'f') return c - 'a' + 10;
        if (c >= 'A' && c <= 'F') return c - 'A' + 10;
        switch (Character.toLowerCase(c)) {
            case 'k':
                return OBFUSCATED;
            case 'l':
                return BOLD;
            case 'm':
                return STRIKETHROUGH;
            case 'n':
                return UNDERLINE;
            case 'o':
                return ITALIC;
            case 'r':
                return RESET;
            default:
                return NONE;
        }
    }

    /** RGB of a colour code (0–15). */
    public static int rgb(int colorCode) {
        return PALETTE[colorCode];
    }

    /** The text without formatting codes. */
    public static String strip(String text) {
        if (text.indexOf(PREFIX) < 0) return text;
        StringBuilder out = new StringBuilder(text.length());
        for (int i = 0; i < text.length(); i++) {
            char c = text.charAt(i);
            if (c == PREFIX) {
                i++;
                continue;
            }
            out.append(c);
        }
        return out.toString();
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/render/FrameRenderer.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.render;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Renderer;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Texture;

/**
 * Runs a paint pass into a {@link DrawList} and hands it to the platform renderer. New glyphs can be added to the
 * atlas while painting; if that made the atlas grow or clear, UVs painted earlier in the pass are stale, so the
 * pass is repeated once (cheap, and only happens while new text first appears).
 */
public final class FrameRenderer {
    private final DrawList list = new DrawList();
    private final Canvas canvas = new Canvas();

    public Canvas getCanvas() {
        return canvas;
    }

    public DrawList getList() {
        return list;
    }

    public void render(Renderer renderer, FontAtlas atlas, double scale, int framebufferWidth, int framebufferHeight, Runnable paint) {
        for (int attempt = 0; attempt < 2; attempt++) {
            Texture texture = atlas.sync(renderer);
            int size = atlas.getSize(), generation = atlas.getGeneration();
            canvas.begin(list, atlas, texture, scale, framebufferWidth, framebufferHeight);
            paint.run();
            if (atlas.getSize() == size && atlas.getGeneration() == generation) break;
        }
        atlas.sync(renderer);
        if (!list.isEmpty()) renderer.render(list);
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/render/IntMap.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.render;

import java.util.Arrays;

/** Open-addressing int → object map, so glyph lookups don't box code points. */
final class IntMap<V> {
    private static final int EMPTY = Integer.MIN_VALUE;

    private int[] keys = new int[256];
    private Object[] values = new Object[256];
    private int size;

    IntMap() {
        Arrays.fill(keys, EMPTY);
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    V get(int key) {
        int mask = keys.length - 1;
        for (int i = mix(key) & mask; ; i = (i + 1) & mask) {
            int k = keys[i];
            if (k == key) return (V) values[i];
            if (k == EMPTY) return null;
        }
    }

    void put(int key, V value) {
        if ((size + 1) * 2 > keys.length) rehash(keys.length * 2);
        int mask = keys.length - 1;
        for (int i = mix(key) & mask; ; i = (i + 1) & mask) {
            if (keys[i] == key) {
                values[i] = value;
                return;
            }
            if (keys[i] == EMPTY) {
                keys[i] = key;
                values[i] = value;
                size++;
                return;
            }
        }
    }

    void clear() {
        Arrays.fill(keys, EMPTY);
        Arrays.fill(values, null);
        size = 0;
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    private void rehash(int capacity) {
        int[] oldKeys = keys;
        Object[] oldValues = values;
        keys = new int[capacity];
        values = new Object[capacity];
        Arrays.fill(keys, EMPTY);
        size = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < oldKeys.length; i++) if (oldKeys[i] != EMPTY) put(oldKeys[i], (V) oldValues[i]);
    }

    private static int mix(int key) {
        int h = key * 0x9E3779B9;
        return h ^ (h >>> 16);
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/settings/GroupNode.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.settings;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;

/** A group box holding settings. */
public final class GroupNode {
    private final SubPageNode subPage;
    private final Class<?> type;
    private final String name;
    private int column;
    private final int order;
    private final boolean pinBottom;
    private final String label;
    double height;
    final List<Setting<?>> settings = new ArrayList<>();

    GroupNode(SubPageNode subPage, Class<?> type, String name, int column, int order, boolean pinBottom) {
        this.subPage = subPage;
        this.type = type;
        this.name = name;
        this.column = column;
        this.order = order;
        this.pinBottom = pinBottom;
        this.label = Labels.of(type, name);
    }

    /** The sub-page holding this group, or null for a free-standing group created in code. */
    public SubPageNode getSubPage() {
        return subPage;
    }

    public Class<?> getType() {
        return type;
    }

    /** Config key: the class's simple name. */
    public String getName() {
        return name;
    }

    public String getLabel() {
        return label;
    }

    /** Fixed height in design units from {@code @IGroup(height)}, or 0 for automatic. */
    public double getHeight() {
        return height;
    }

    public GroupNode height(double height) {
        this.height = height;
        return this;
    }

    /** Changes the preferred column (for groups created in code). */
    public GroupNode column(int column) {
        this.column = column;
        return this;
    }

    /** Preferred column, or -1 for automatic. */
    public int getColumn() {
        return column;
    }

    public int getOrder() {
        return order;
    }

    public boolean isPinBottom() {
        return pinBottom;
    }

    public List<Setting<?>> getSettings() {
        return Collections.unmodifiableList(settings);
    }

    /** Readable path for messages, e.g. "Combat/KillAura". */
    public String getPath() {
        if (subPage == null) return name;
        StringBuilder path = new StringBuilder(subPage.getPage().getName());
        if (!subPage.isImplicit()) path.append('/').append(subPage.getName());
        return path.append('/').append(name).toString();
    }

    /** Adds a virtual setting backed by {@code holder} (used for the built-in config UI and similar). */
    public <T> Setting<T> addVirtual(String name, SettingType<T> type, Setting.Holder<T> holder, List<String> options, Setting.SliderSpec slider) {
        Setting<T> setting = new Setting<>(this, name, type, holder, null, false, options, slider, 64, -1);
        setting.captureDefault();
        settings.add(setting);
        return setting;
    }

    /** Creates a free-standing group (not part of the annotated model). */
    public static GroupNode standalone(String name, int column, int order, boolean pinBottom) {
        return new GroupNode(null, null, name, column, order, pinBottom);
    }
}
```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/settings/KeybindMode.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.settings;

/** How a bound checkbox follows its key. */
public enum KeybindMode {
    /** Always on; the key is ignored. */
    ALWAYS_ON("Always on"),
    /** On while the key is held. */
    ON_HOTKEY("On hotkey"),
    /** Each press flips it. */
    TOGGLE("Toggle"),
    /** Off while the key is held. */
    OFF_HOTKEY("Off hotkey");

    private final String label;

    KeybindMode(String label) {
        this.label = label;
    }

    /** Name shown in the menu. */
    public String getLabel() {
        return label;
    }

    /** Lenient lookup for config files; null if unknown. */
    public static KeybindMode byName(String name) {
        for (KeybindMode mode : values()) if (mode.name().equalsIgnoreCase(name)) return mode;
        return null;
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/settings/Keybinds.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.settings;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.input.Keys;

import java.util.IdentityHashMap;
import java.util.Map;

/** Current key and {@link KeybindMode} of each bindable setting ({@link Keys} codes). */
public final class Keybinds {
    /** Notified when a binding changes. */
    public interface Listener {
        void keybindChanged(Setting<?> setting, boolean byUser);
    }

    private final Map<Setting<?>, Integer> keys = new IdentityHashMap<>();
    private final Map<Setting<?>, KeybindMode> modes = new IdentityHashMap<>();
    private Listener listener;

    public Keybinds(SettingsModel model) {
        for (Setting<?> setting : model.getSettings()) {
            if (!setting.isBindable()) continue;
            keys.put(setting, setting.getDefaultKey());
            modes.put(setting, setting.getDefaultMode());
        }
    }

    public void setListener(Listener listener) {
        this.listener = listener;
    }

    public Map<Setting<?>, Integer> asMap() {
        return keys;
    }

    /** The bound key, {@link Keys#KEY_NONE}, or -1 if the setting isn't bindable. */
    public int get(Setting<?> setting) {
        Integer key = keys.get(setting);
        return key == null ? -1 : key;
    }

    /** The binding's mode (meaningful for checkboxes), or null if the setting isn't bindable. */
    public KeybindMode getMode(Setting<?> setting) {
        return modes.get(setting);
    }

    /** A user change (e.g. rebinding in the menu). */
    public void set(Setting<?> setting, int key) {
        put(setting, key, true);
    }

    /** A user change of the mode. */
    public void setMode(Setting<?> setting, KeybindMode mode) {
        putMode(setting, mode, true);
    }

    /** A binding applied from a config file. */
    public void load(Setting<?> setting, int key) {
        put(setting, key, false);
    }

    /** A mode applied from a config file. */
    public void loadMode(Setting<?> setting, KeybindMode mode) {
        putMode(setting, mode, false);
    }

    private void put(Setting<?> setting, int key, boolean byUser) {
        if (!keys.containsKey(setting)) throw new IllegalArgumentException(setting + " is not bindable");
        if (keys.get(setting) == key) return;
        keys.put(setting, key);
        if (listener != null) listener.keybindChanged(setting, byUser);
    }

    private void putMode(Setting<?> setting, KeybindMode mode, boolean byUser) {
        if (!modes.containsKey(setting)) throw new IllegalArgumentException(setting + " is not bindable");
        if (mode == null || modes.get(setting) == mode) return;
        modes.put(setting, mode);
        if (listener != null) listener.keybindChanged(setting, byUser);
    }

    public void resetToDefaults() {
        for (Setting<?> setting : keys.keySet()) {
            load(setting, setting.getDefaultKey());
            loadMode(setting, setting.getDefaultMode());
        }
    }
}

```

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.settings;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.annotation.ILabel;

import java.lang.reflect.AnnotatedElement;

/** Display labels: {@code @ILabel} if present, otherwise the Java name with underscores as spaces. */
final class Labels {
    private Labels() {
    }

    static String of(AnnotatedElement element, String name) {
        ILabel label = element == null ? null : element.getAnnotation(ILabel.class);
        if (label != null) return label.value();
        return name == null ? null : name.replace('_', ' ');
    }
}
```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/settings/ListModel.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.settings;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * Backing value of a list box: a list of items plus the selected one. Declare it as a static field in a group;
 * the selection is saved in configs, the items are supplied by the mod (and may change at runtime).
 */
public final class ListModel {
    private final List<String> items = new ArrayList<>();
    private final List<String> view = Collections.unmodifiableList(items);
    private String selected;
    private int version;

    public ListModel(String... items) {
        this.items.addAll(Arrays.asList(items));
    }

    private boolean searchable;
    private double height;

    /** Height of the list box in design units (0 lets the theme choose). */
    public ListModel height(double height) {
        this.height = height;
        return this;
    }

    public double getHeight() {
        return height;
    }

    /** Shows a search box above the list that filters it as the user types. */
    public ListModel searchable(boolean searchable) {
        this.searchable = searchable;
        return this;
    }

    public boolean isSearchable() {
        return searchable;
    }

    public List<String> getItems() {
        return view;
    }

    public void setItems(Collection<String> newItems) {
        items.clear();
        items.addAll(newItems);
        if (selected != null && !items.contains(selected)) selected = null;
        version++;
    }

    public void add(String item) {
        items.add(item);
        version++;
    }

    public void remove(String item) {
        items.remove(item);
        if (item.equals(selected)) selected = null;
        version++;
    }

    /** The selected item, or null. */
    public String getSelected() {
        return selected;
    }

    /** Selects an item (null clears). A value that isn't in the list is kept, so it can be restored once it appears. */
    public void select(String item) {
        selected = item;
        version++;
    }

    /** Incremented on every change, so views can cache derived data. */
    public int getVersion() {
        return version;
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/settings/ModelBuilder.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.settings;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.annotation.field.DropdownOptions;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.annotation.field.FieldOptions;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.annotation.field.IAttach;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.annotation.field.IKeybind;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.annotation.field.IDisabled;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.annotation.field.IShowIf;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.annotation.field.IWarning;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.annotation.field.SliderOptions;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.annotation.type.IGroup;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.annotation.type.IPage;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.annotation.type.ISubPage;

import java.lang.reflect.Field;
import java.lang.reflect.Modifier;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.logging.Logger;

/** Builds a {@link SettingsModel} from {@code @IPage} classes, validating as it goes. */
public final class ModelBuilder {
    private static final Logger LOG = Logger.getLogger("Icarus");

    private final List<SettingType<?>> types;

    public ModelBuilder(List<SettingType<?>> types) {
        this.types = types;
    }

    public SettingsModel build(List<Class<?>> pageClasses) {
        List<PageNode> pages = new ArrayList<>();
        for (Class<?> pageClass : pageClasses) {
            IPage annotation = pageClass.getAnnotation(IPage.class);
            if (annotation == null) throw new IllegalArgumentException(pageClass.getName() + " is not annotated with @IPage");
            PageNode page = new PageNode(pageClass, pageClass.getSimpleName(), annotation.value(), annotation.icon(), annotation.order());

            List<Class<?>> subPageClasses = new ArrayList<>();
            List<Class<?>> groupClasses = new ArrayList<>();
            for (Class<?> nested : pageClass.getDeclaredClasses()) {
                if (nested.isAnnotationPresent(ISubPage.class)) subPageClasses.add(nested);
                else if (nested.isAnnotationPresent(IGroup.class)) groupClasses.add(nested);
                else LOG.warning("[Icarus] " + nested.getName() + " is nested in an @IPage but has no @IGroup/@ISubPage; skipped");
            }
            if (!subPageClasses.isEmpty() && !groupClasses.isEmpty()) {
                throw new IllegalStateException("@IPage " + pageClass.getName() + " mixes @ISubPage and @IGroup classes; put its groups inside sub-pages");
            }

            if (subPageClasses.isEmpty()) {
                SubPageNode implicit = new SubPageNode(page, null, null, 0);
                addGroups(implicit, groupClasses);
                page.subPages.add(implicit);
            } else {
                sortByOrder(subPageClasses, c -> c.getAnnotation(ISubPage.class).order());
                for (Class<?> subClass : subPageClasses) {
                    SubPageNode sub = new SubPageNode(page, subClass, subClass.getSimpleName(), subClass.getAnnotation(ISubPage.class).order());
                    List<Class<?>> subGroups = new ArrayList<>();
                    for (Class<?> nested : subClass.getDeclaredClasses()) {
                        if (nested.isAnnotationPresent(IGroup.class)) subGroups.add(nested);
                        else LOG.warning("[Icarus] " + nested.getName() + " is nested in an @ISubPage but has no @IGroup; skipped");
                    }
                    addGroups(sub, subGroups);
                    page.subPages.add(sub);
                }
            }
            pages.add(page);
        }
        pages.sort(Comparator.comparingInt(PageNode::getOrder).thenComparing(PageNode::getName));

        SettingsModel model = new SettingsModel(pages);
        for (Setting<?> setting : model.getSettings()) setting.captureDefault();
        return model;
    }

    private void addGroups(SubPageNode sub, List<Class<?>> groupClasses) {
        sortByOrder(groupClasses, c -> c.getAnnotation(IGroup.class).order());
        for (Class<?> groupClass : groupClasses) {
            IGroup annotation = groupClass.getAnnotation(IGroup.class);
            GroupNode group = new GroupNode(sub, groupClass, groupClass.getSimpleName(), annotation.column(), annotation.order(), annotation.pinBottom());
            group.height = annotation.height();
            for (Field field : groupClass.getDeclaredFields()) {
                if (field.isSynthetic()) continue;
                Setting<?> setting = createSetting(group, field);
                if (setting != null) group.settings.add(setting);
            }
            resolveShowIf(group);
            sub.groups.add(group);
        }
    }

    /** Links {@code @IShowIf} settings to the checkbox they follow. */
    private static void resolveShowIf(GroupNode group) {
        for (Setting<?> setting : group.settings) {
            Field field = setting.getField();
            IShowIf showIf = field == null ? null : field.getAnnotation(IShowIf.class);
            if (showIf == null) continue;
            Setting<?> condition = null;
            for (Setting<?> other : group.settings) if (other.getName().equals(showIf.value())) condition = other;
            if (condition == null || condition.getType() != SettingTypes.CHECKBOX) {
                LOG.warning("[Icarus] @IShowIf(\"" + showIf.value() + "\") on " + field + " ignored: no checkbox of that name in the group");
                continue;
            }
            setting.showIf = condition;
            setting.showIfNot = showIf.not();
        }
    }

    private Setting<?> createSetting(GroupNode group, Field field) {
        SettingType<?> type = null;
        for (SettingType<?> candidate : types) {
            if (candidate.matches(field)) {
                type = candidate;
                break;
            }
        }
        if (type == null) {
            LOG.warning("[Icarus] " + field + " has no supported setting type (" + field.getType().getSimpleName() + "); skipped");
            return null;
        }
        if (!Modifier.isStatic(field.getModifiers())) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Setting " + field + " must be static");
        }
        field.setAccessible(true);
        return create(group, field, type);
    }

    private <T> Setting<T> create(GroupNode group, Field field, SettingType<T> type) {
        DropdownOptions dropdown = field.getAnnotation(DropdownOptions.class);
        SliderOptions sliderOptions = field.getAnnotation(SliderOptions.class);
        FieldOptions fieldOptions = field.getAnnotation(FieldOptions.class);
        IKeybind keybind = field.getAnnotation(IKeybind.class);

        Setting.SliderSpec slider = null;
        if (type == SettingTypes.SLIDER) {
            try {
                slider = new Setting.SliderSpec(sliderOptions.min(), sliderOptions.max(), sliderOptions.onlyInt(), sliderOptions.units()).step(sliderOptions.step()).endTexts(sliderOptions.minText(), sliderOptions.maxText());
            } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
                throw new IllegalStateException("Invalid @SliderOptions on " + field + ": " + e.getMessage(), e);
            }
        }

        //any setting can show a key; buttons run, checkboxes follow the mode, others just store it for the mod to read
        boolean bindable = keybind != null;

        if (type == SettingTypes.CHOICE || type == SettingTypes.COMBO) {
            if (dropdown.value().length == 0) throw new IllegalStateException("@DropdownOptions on " + field + " has no options");
        }

        Setting.Holder<T> holder = new FieldHolder<>(field, type);
        Setting<T> setting = new Setting<>(group, field.getName(), type, holder, field, true,
                dropdown == null ? null : Arrays.asList(dropdown.value()), slider,
                fieldOptions == null ? 64 : fieldOptions.maxLength(), bindable ? keybind.value() : -1);
        if (bindable) setting.defaultMode = keybind.mode();
        setting.warning = field.isAnnotationPresent(IWarning.class);
        setting.disabled = field.isAnnotationPresent(IDisabled.class);
        if (field.isAnnotationPresent(IAttach.class)) {
            if (type == SettingTypes.COLOR) setting.attached = true;
            else LOG.warning("[Icarus] @IAttach on " + field + " ignored: only colours can share a row");
        }
        if (type == SettingTypes.LIST) {
            try {
                Object model = field.get(null);
                if (model == null) throw new IllegalStateException("ListModel field " + field + " must be initialised");
                setting.setExtra(model);
            } catch (IllegalAccessException e) {
                throw new IllegalStateException(e);
            }
        }
        return setting;
    }

    private interface OrderOf {
        int order(Class<?> c);
    }

    private static void sortByOrder(List<Class<?>> classes, OrderOf order) {
        classes.sort(Comparator.<Class<?>>comparingInt(order::order).thenComparing(Class::getName));
    }

    private static final class FieldHolder<T> implements Setting.Holder<T> {
        private final Field field;
        private final SettingType<T> type;

        FieldHolder(Field field, SettingType<T> type) {
            this.field = field;
            this.type = type;
        }

        @Override
        public T get() {
            try {
                return type.load(field);
            } catch (IllegalAccessException e) {
                throw new IllegalStateException(e);
            }
        }

        @Override
        public void set(T value) {
            try {
                type.store(field, value);
            } catch (IllegalAccessException e) {
                throw new IllegalStateException(e);
            }
        }
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/settings/PageNode.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.settings;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;

/** A menu page (tab). Always has at least one sub-page; pages without {@code @ISubPage} get one implicit sub-page. */
public final class PageNode {
    private final Class<?> type;
    private final String name;
    private final char icon;
    private final String iconName;
    private final String label;
    private final int order;
    final List<SubPageNode> subPages = new ArrayList<>();

    PageNode(Class<?> type, String name, char icon, String iconName, int order) {
        this.type = type;
        this.name = name;
        this.icon = icon;
        this.iconName = iconName == null ? "" : iconName;
        this.label = Labels.of(type, name);
        this.order = order;
    }

    /** The annotated class, or null for a page created in code. */
    public Class<?> getType() {
        return type;
    }

    /** Config key: the class's simple name. */
    public String getName() {
        return name;
    }

    public String getLabel() {
        return label;
    }

    public char getIcon() {
        return icon;
    }

    /** Named theme icon from {@code @IPage(icon)}, or empty. */
    public String getIconName() {
        return iconName;
    }

    public int getOrder() {
        return order;
    }

    public List<SubPageNode> getSubPages() {
        return Collections.unmodifiableList(subPages);
    }

    /** Whether this page shows a sub-tab row (it declared {@code @ISubPage} classes). */
    public boolean hasSubTabs() {
        return !(subPages.size() == 1 && subPages.get(0).isImplicit());
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/settings/Setting.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.settings;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.input.Keys;

import java.lang.annotation.Annotation;
import java.lang.reflect.Field;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Objects;

/**
 * One configurable value: a static field of an {@code @IGroup} class, or a virtual value created in code
 * (e.g. the built-in config UI). All reads and writes go through here so every change marks the config dirty and
 * fires callbacks, whatever widget made it.
 */
public final class Setting<T> {
    /** Storage behind a setting. */
    public interface Holder<T> {
        T get();

        void set(T value);
    }

    private final GroupNode group;
    private final String name;
    private String label;
    private final SettingType<T> type;
    private final Holder<T> holder;
    private final Field field;
    private final boolean persistent;

    private final List<String> options;
    private final SliderSpec slider;
    private final int maxLength;
    private final int defaultKey;

    private T defaultValue;
    boolean attached;
    boolean warning;
    boolean disabled;
    /** {@code @IShowIf}: the checkbox this setting follows, or null to always show. */
    Setting<?> showIf;
    boolean showIfNot;
    KeybindMode defaultMode = KeybindMode.TOGGLE;
    private SettingListener listener;
    private Object extra;

    Setting(GroupNode group, String name, SettingType<T> type, Holder<T> holder, Field field, boolean persistent,
            List<String> options, SliderSpec slider, int maxLength, int defaultKey) {
        this.group = group;
        this.name = name;
        this.label = Labels.of(field, name);
        this.type = type;
        this.holder = holder;
        this.field = field;
        this.persistent = persistent;
        this.options = options == null ? Collections.<String>emptyList() : Collections.unmodifiableList(options);
        this.slider = slider;
        this.maxLength = maxLength;
        this.defaultKey = defaultKey;
    }

    void captureDefault() {
        defaultValue = type.copy(holder.get());
    }

    public void setListener(SettingListener listener) {
        this.listener = listener;
    }

    /** An object attached to the setting, e.g. the {@link ListModel} behind a list setting. */
    public Object getExtra() {
        return extra;
    }

    public Setting<T> setExtra(Object extra) {
        this.extra = extra;
        return this;
    }

    public T get() {
        return holder.get();
    }

    /** A change made by the user or the mod: stores it, marks the config dirty and fires callbacks. */
    public void set(T value) {
        T old = holder.get();
        if (type.equal(old, value)) return;
        holder.set(value);
        if (listener != null) listener.changed(this, true);
    }

    /** A value applied from a config file or a reset: stores it and fires callbacks, but isn't a pending change. */
    public void load(T value) {
        T old = holder.get();
        if (type.equal(old, value)) return;
        holder.set(value);
        if (listener != null) listener.changed(this, false);
    }

    /** Notifies listeners that a mutable value (e.g. a {@link ListModel}'s items) changed in place. */
    public void touched() {
        if (listener != null) listener.changed(this, true);
    }

    public void resetToDefault() {
        load(type.copy(defaultValue));
    }

    public T getDefaultValue() {
        return defaultValue;
    }

    public GroupNode getGroup() {
        return group;
    }

    /** The Java field name; config files are keyed by it. */
    public String getName() {
        return name;
    }

    /** Display label: the name with underscores as spaces. */
    public String getLabel() {
        return label;
    }

    /** Replaces the shown label (for settings created in code); empty hides it in themes that allow that. */
    public Setting<T> labelled(String label) {
        this.label = label == null ? "" : label;
        return this;
    }

    public SettingType<T> getType() {
        return type;
    }

    /** The backing field, or null for a virtual setting. */
    public Field getField() {
        return field;
    }

    public <A extends Annotation> A getAnnotation(Class<A> annotation) {
        return field == null ? null : field.getAnnotation(annotation);
    }

    /** Whether the value is written to config files (buttons and the config UI's own controls are not). */
    public boolean isPersistent() {
        return persistent && type.isPersistent();
    }

    /** Dropdown options (Choice / Combo). */
    public List<String> getOptions() {
        return options;
    }

    /** Slider range, or null. */
    public SliderSpec getSlider() {
        return slider;
    }

    /** Text field length limit. */
    public int getMaxLength() {
        return maxLength;
    }

    /** Whether the setting can be bound to a key (has {@code @IKeybind}). */
    public boolean isBindable() {
        return defaultKey >= 0;
    }

    /** Whether the setting is drawn on the previous setting's row ({@code @IAttach}). */
    public boolean isAttached() {
        return attached;
    }

    /** Whether the setting is shown greyed out and ignores input ({@code @IDisabled}). */
    public boolean isDisabled() {
        return disabled;
    }

    /** Whether the setting is shown right now ({@code @IShowIf}). */
    public boolean isVisible() {
        if (showIf == null) return true;
        return Boolean.TRUE.equals(showIf.get()) != showIfNot;
    }

    /** Whether the setting is marked risky ({@code @IWarning}). */
    public boolean isWarning() {
        return warning;
    }

    /** Keybind mode from {@code @IKeybind} (checkboxes). */
    public KeybindMode getDefaultMode() {
        return defaultMode;
    }

    /** The {@link Keys} code from {@code @IKeybind}, {@link Keys#KEY_NONE} for none, or -1 if not bindable. */
    public int getDefaultKey() {
        return defaultKey;
    }

    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return group.getPath() + "/" + name;
    }

    /** Slider range and formatting. */
    public static final class SliderSpec {
        private final double min, max;
        private final boolean onlyInt;
        private final String units;
        private double step;
        private String minText = "", maxText = "";

        public SliderSpec(double min, double max, boolean onlyInt, String units) {
            if (!(max > min)) throw new IllegalArgumentException("slider max (" + max + ") must be greater than min (" + min + ")");
            this.min = min;
            this.max = max;
            this.onlyInt = onlyInt;
            this.units = Objects.requireNonNull(units);
        }

        public double getMin() {
            return min;
        }

        public double getMax() {
            return max;
        }

        public boolean isOnlyInt() {
            return onlyInt;
        }

        public String getUnits() {
            return units;
        }

        /** Step for -/+ buttons and nudging. */
        public double getStep() {
            if (step > 0) return step;
            return onlyInt ? 1 : (max - min) / 100;
        }

        public SliderSpec step(double step) {
            this.step = step;
            return this;
        }

        /** Texts shown at the ends of the range instead of the value (empty to show the value). */
        public SliderSpec endTexts(String minText, String maxText) {
            this.minText = minText == null ? "" : minText;
            this.maxText = maxText == null ? "" : maxText;
            return this;
        }

        /** Clamps and rounds a value to what this slider can hold. */
        public double sanitize(double value) {
            if (Double.isNaN(value)) value = min;
            value = Math.max(min, Math.min(max, value));
            if (onlyInt) return Math.round(value);
            if (step > 0) {
                value = Math.max(min, Math.min(max, min + Math.round((value - min) / step) * step));
                int decimals = Math.max(2, java.math.BigDecimal.valueOf(step).stripTrailingZeros().scale());
                return java.math.BigDecimal.valueOf(value).setScale(decimals, java.math.RoundingMode.HALF_UP).doubleValue();
            }
            return java.math.BigDecimal.valueOf(value).setScale(2, java.math.RoundingMode.HALF_UP).doubleValue();
        }

        public double fraction(double value) {
            return (Math.max(min, Math.min(max, value)) - min) / (max - min);
        }

        public double valueAt(double fraction) {
            return sanitize(min + (max - min) * Math.max(0, Math.min(1, fraction)));
        }

        public String format(double value) {
            if (!minText.isEmpty() && value <= min) return minText;
            if (!maxText.isEmpty() && value >= max) return maxText;
            String number;
            if (onlyInt) number = Long.toString(Math.round(value));
            else if (step > 0) {
                //as many decimals as the step has (0.1 -> "10.0", 0.05 -> "0.65")
                int decimals = Math.max(0, java.math.BigDecimal.valueOf(step).stripTrailingZeros().scale());
                number = java.math.BigDecimal.valueOf(value).setScale(decimals, java.math.RoundingMode.HALF_UP).toPlainString();
            } else number = java.math.BigDecimal.valueOf(value).stripTrailingZeros().toPlainString();
            return number + units;
        }
    }
}

```

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.settings;

/** Receives every setting change (implemented by the owning {@code IcarusMod}). */
public interface SettingListener {
    /**
     * @param byUser true for a change by the user or mod code (a pending config change), false when a value was
     *               applied from a config file or a reset
     */
    void changed(Setting<?> setting, boolean byUser);
}
```
