`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/settings/SettingType.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.settings;

import com.google.gson.JsonElement;

import java.lang.reflect.Field;
import java.util.Objects;

/**
 * Everything Icarus needs to know about one kind of setting: which fields it applies to, how the value is read
 * from / written to the field, and how it's stored in config files. Widgets are looked up by type (see
 * {@code IcarusMod#replaceWidget}). The built-in types are in {@link SettingTypes}.
 */
public abstract class SettingType<T> {
    private final String id;

    protected SettingType(String id) {
        this.id = id;
    }

    /** Stable id, e.g. "checkbox". */
    public final String getId() {
        return id;
    }

    /** Whether a static field declares a setting of this type. */
    public abstract boolean matches(Field field);

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    public T load(Field field) throws IllegalAccessException {
        return (T) field.get(null);
    }

    public void store(Field field, T value) throws IllegalAccessException {
        field.set(null, value);
    }

    /** A copy safe to keep as a default (for mutable values such as arrays). */
    public T copy(T value) {
        return value;
    }

    public boolean equal(T a, T b) {
        return Objects.equals(a, b);
    }

    /** Whether values are saved in config files. */
    public boolean isPersistent() {
        return true;
    }

    public abstract JsonElement toJson(T value);

    /** Parses a stored value; returns null if it isn't valid for this setting (the current value is then kept). */
    public abstract T fromJson(JsonElement json, Setting<T> setting);

    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return id;
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/settings/SettingTypes.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.settings;

import com.google.gson.JsonArray;
import com.google.gson.JsonElement;
import com.google.gson.JsonNull;
import com.google.gson.JsonPrimitive;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.annotation.field.DropdownOptions;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.annotation.field.SliderOptions;

import java.awt.Color;
import java.lang.reflect.Field;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.List;

/** The built-in setting types, in matching order. */
public final class SettingTypes {
    private SettingTypes() {
    }

    /** {@code Runnable} field: a button (not saved). */
    public static final SettingType<Runnable> BUTTON = new SettingType<Runnable>("button") {
        @Override
        public boolean matches(Field field) {
            return field.getType() == Runnable.class;
        }

        @Override
        public boolean isPersistent() {
            return false;
        }

        @Override
        public JsonElement toJson(Runnable value) {
            return JsonNull.INSTANCE;
        }

        @Override
        public Runnable fromJson(JsonElement json, Setting<Runnable> setting) {
            return null;
        }
    };

    /** {@code boolean} field: a checkbox. */
    public static final SettingType<Boolean> CHECKBOX = new SettingType<Boolean>("checkbox") {
        @Override
        public boolean matches(Field field) {
            return field.getType() == boolean.class || field.getType() == Boolean.class;
        }

        @Override
        public JsonElement toJson(Boolean value) {
            return new JsonPrimitive(value);
        }

        @Override
        public Boolean fromJson(JsonElement json, Setting<Boolean> setting) {
            return json.isJsonPrimitive() && json.getAsJsonPrimitive().isBoolean() ? json.getAsBoolean() : null;
        }
    };

    /** {@code String} field with {@link DropdownOptions}: a single-choice dropdown. */
    public static final SettingType<String> CHOICE = new SettingType<String>("choice") {
        @Override
        public boolean matches(Field field) {
            return field.getType() == String.class && field.isAnnotationPresent(DropdownOptions.class);
        }

        @Override
        public JsonElement toJson(String value) {
            return value == null ? JsonNull.INSTANCE : new JsonPrimitive(value);
        }

        @Override
        public String fromJson(JsonElement json, Setting<String> setting) {
            if (!json.isJsonPrimitive()) return null;
            String value = json.getAsString();
            return setting.getOptions().contains(value) ? value : null;
        }
    };

    /** {@code String[]} field with {@link DropdownOptions}: a multi-choice dropdown. */
    public static final SettingType<String[]> COMBO = new SettingType<String[]>("combo") {
        @Override
        public boolean matches(Field field) {
            return field.getType() == String[].class && field.isAnnotationPresent(DropdownOptions.class);
        }

        @Override
        public String[] copy(String[] value) {
            return value == null ? null : value.clone();
        }

        @Override
        public boolean equal(String[] a, String[] b) {
            return Arrays.equals(a, b);
        }

        @Override
        public JsonElement toJson(String[] value) {
            JsonArray array = new JsonArray();
            if (value != null) for (String s : value) array.add(new JsonPrimitive(s));
            return array;
        }

        @Override
        public String[] fromJson(JsonElement json, Setting<String[]> setting) {
            if (!json.isJsonArray()) return null;
            List<String> values = new ArrayList<>();
            for (JsonElement e : json.getAsJsonArray()) {
                if (e.isJsonPrimitive() && setting.getOptions().contains(e.getAsString()) && !values.contains(e.getAsString())) values.add(e.getAsString());
            }
            //keep the declared option order regardless of the order things were ticked in
            List<String> ordered = new ArrayList<>();
            for (String option : setting.getOptions()) if (values.contains(option)) ordered.add(option);
            return ordered.toArray(new String[0]);
        }
    };

    /** {@code String} field without options: a text field. */
    public static final SettingType<String> FIELD = new SettingType<String>("field") {
        @Override
        public boolean matches(Field field) {
            return field.getType() == String.class;
        }

        @Override
        public JsonElement toJson(String value) {
            return value == null ? JsonNull.INSTANCE : new JsonPrimitive(value);
        }

        @Override
        public String fromJson(JsonElement json, Setting<String> setting) {
            if (!json.isJsonPrimitive()) return null;
            String value = json.getAsString();
            return value.length() > setting.getMaxLength() ? value.substring(0, setting.getMaxLength()) : value;
        }
    };

    /** {@code double}/{@code float}/{@code int}/{@code long} field with {@link SliderOptions}: a slider. */
    public static final SettingType<Double> SLIDER = new SettingType<Double>("slider") {
        @Override
        public boolean matches(Field field) {
            Class<?> t = field.getType();
            return field.isAnnotationPresent(SliderOptions.class)
                    && (t == double.class || t == float.class || t == int.class || t == long.class
                    || t == Double.class || t == Float.class || t == Integer.class || t == Long.class);
        }

        @Override
        public Double load(Field field) throws IllegalAccessException {
            Object value = field.get(null);
            return value == null ? 0 : ((Number) value).doubleValue();
        }

        @Override
        public void store(Field field, Double value) throws IllegalAccessException {
            Class<?> t = field.getType();
            if (t == double.class || t == Double.class) field.set(null, value);
            else if (t == float.class || t == Float.class) field.set(null, value.floatValue());
            else if (t == int.class || t == Integer.class) field.set(null, (int) Math.round(value));
            else field.set(null, Math.round(value));
        }

        @Override
        public JsonElement toJson(Double value) {
            return new JsonPrimitive(value);
        }

        @Override
        public Double fromJson(JsonElement json, Setting<Double> setting) {
            if (!json.isJsonPrimitive() || !json.getAsJsonPrimitive().isNumber()) return null;
            return setting.getSlider().sanitize(json.getAsDouble());
        }
    };

    /** {@link Color} field: a colour picker. Stored as {@code "#AARRGGBB"}. */
    public static final SettingType<Color> COLOR = new SettingType<Color>("color") {
        @Override
        public boolean matches(Field field) {
            return field.getType() == Color.class;
        }

        @Override
        public JsonElement toJson(Color value) {
            return value == null ? JsonNull.INSTANCE : new JsonPrimitive(String.format("#%08X", value.getRGB()));
        }

        @Override
        public Color fromJson(JsonElement json, Setting<Color> setting) {
            if (!json.isJsonPrimitive()) return null;
            String s = json.getAsString();
            if (!s.matches("#[0-9a-fA-F]{8}")) return null;
            return new Color((int) Long.parseLong(s.substring(1), 16), true);
        }
    };

    /** {@link ListModel} field: a list box. The selected item is saved. */
    public static final SettingType<String> LIST = new SettingType<String>("list") {
        @Override
        public boolean matches(Field field) {
            return field.getType() == ListModel.class;
        }

        @Override
        public String load(Field field) throws IllegalAccessException {
            return model(field).getSelected();
        }

        @Override
        public void store(Field field, String value) throws IllegalAccessException {
            model(field).select(value);
        }

        private ListModel model(Field field) throws IllegalAccessException {
            ListModel model = (ListModel) field.get(null);
            if (model == null) throw new IllegalStateException("ListModel field " + field + " is null");
            return model;
        }

        @Override
        public JsonElement toJson(String value) {
            return value == null ? JsonNull.INSTANCE : new JsonPrimitive(value);
        }

        @Override
        public String fromJson(JsonElement json, Setting<String> setting) {
            return json.isJsonPrimitive() ? json.getAsString() : null;
        }
    };

    /** In matching order: the more specific types first. */
    /** {@link Label} field: a text-only row (optionally with a keybind). Not saved. */
    public static final SettingType<Label> LABEL = new SettingType<Label>("label") {
        @Override
        public boolean matches(Field field) {
            return field.getType() == Label.class;
        }

        @Override
        public boolean isPersistent() {
            return false;
        }

        @Override
        public JsonElement toJson(Label value) {
            return JsonNull.INSTANCE;
        }

        @Override
        public Label fromJson(JsonElement json, Setting<Label> setting) {
            return null;
        }
    };

    public static List<SettingType<?>> builtIn() {
        return Arrays.<SettingType<?>>asList(BUTTON, CHECKBOX, CHOICE, COMBO, FIELD, SLIDER, COLOR, LIST, LABEL);
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/settings/SettingsModel.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.settings;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;

/** All pages, sub-pages, groups and settings of one mod. */
public final class SettingsModel {
    private final List<PageNode> pages;
    private final List<Setting<?>> settings = new ArrayList<>();

    SettingsModel(List<PageNode> pages) {
        this.pages = Collections.unmodifiableList(pages);
        for (PageNode page : pages)
            for (SubPageNode sub : page.subPages)
                for (GroupNode group : sub.groups) settings.addAll(group.settings);
    }

    public List<PageNode> getPages() {
        return pages;
    }

    /** Every setting in menu order. */
    public List<Setting<?>> getSettings() {
        return Collections.unmodifiableList(settings);
    }

    /** Finds a setting by its group class and field name, or null. */
    public Setting<?> find(Class<?> group, String field) {
        for (Setting<?> setting : settings) {
            if (setting.getGroup().getType() == group && setting.getName().equals(field)) return setting;
        }
        return null;
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/settings/SubPageNode.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.settings;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.annotation.type.ISubPage;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;

/** A sub-tab of a page, or the implicit single sub-page of a page without sub-tabs. */
public final class SubPageNode {
    private final PageNode page;
    private final Class<?> type;
    private final String name;
    private final int order;
    private final char icon;
    final List<GroupNode> groups = new ArrayList<>();

    SubPageNode(PageNode page, Class<?> type, String name, int order) {
        this.page = page;
        this.type = type;
        this.name = name;
        this.order = order;
        ISubPage annotation = type == null ? null : type.getAnnotation(ISubPage.class);
        this.icon = annotation == null ? ' ' : annotation.icon();
    }

    /** Icon from {@code @ISubPage(icon)}, or a space. */
    public char getIcon() {
        return icon;
    }

    public PageNode getPage() {
        return page;
    }

    public Class<?> getType() {
        return type;
    }

    /** Config key, or null for the implicit sub-page. */
    public String getName() {
        return name;
    }

    public String getLabel() {
        return name == null ? page.getLabel() : Labels.of(type, name);
    }

    public int getOrder() {
        return order;
    }

    public boolean isImplicit() {
        return name == null;
    }

    public List<GroupNode> getGroups() {
        return Collections.unmodifiableList(groups);
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/theme/BaseTheme.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.theme;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.input.Keys;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.AtlasImage;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.Canvas;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.Font;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.FontAtlas;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.GroupView;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.Menu;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.Rect;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.popup.ColorPopup;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.popup.ContextPopup;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.popup.DropdownPopup;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.ButtonWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.CheckboxWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.ChoiceWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.ColorWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.ComboWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.FieldWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.KeybindWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.LabelWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.ListWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.SliderWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.Widget;

/**
 * Shared layout for themes that use the common "label, then control" row structure: themes set the metrics in
 * their constructor and implement painting. Fonts are created lazily from the shared atlas.
 */
public abstract class BaseTheme implements Theme {
    /** An image from the classpath as a white alpha mask for tinting (e.g. icons cut from a reference), or null. */
    protected static AtlasImage resourceImage(String path) {
        try (java.io.InputStream in = BaseTheme.class.getResourceAsStream(path)) {
            if (in == null) return null;
            java.awt.image.BufferedImage img = javax.imageio.ImageIO.read(in);
            if (img == null) return null;
            int[] argb = img.getRGB(0, 0, img.getWidth(), img.getHeight(), null, 0, img.getWidth());
            return new AtlasImage(argb, img.getWidth(), img.getHeight());
        } catch (java.io.IOException e) {
            return null;
        }
    }

    /** Row metrics in design units. */
    protected double textRow = 13;
    /** Height of the label line above sliders, dropdowns and fields. */
    protected double labelRow = 13;
    protected double checkboxSize = 8;
    /** Offset of labels and controls from the row's left edge (checkbox column width). */
    protected double indent = 20;
    protected double controlWidth = 162;
    protected double controlRightReserve = 30;
    protected double boxHeight = 20;
    protected double labelGap = 2;
    protected double buttonHeight = 25;
    protected double sliderTrackHeight = 6;
    protected double sliderValueOverhang = 6;
    protected double swatchWidth = 16, swatchHeight = 8;
    protected double listItemHeight = 16;
    protected int listRows = 8;
    protected double popupItemHeight = 20;
    protected int popupMaxRows = 10;
    protected double scrollbarWidth = 3;

    private FontAtlas fontsFor;
    private Font text, bold, small, icon, title, tab;

    /** Creates the theme's fonts (called once per atlas). */
    protected abstract void createFonts(FontAtlas atlas);

    protected final void setFonts(Font text, Font bold, Font small, Font icon, Font title, Font tab) {
        this.text = text;
        this.bold = bold;
        this.small = small;
        this.icon = icon;
        this.title = title;
        this.tab = tab;
    }

    private void ensureFonts(Canvas canvas) {
        if (fontsFor != canvas.getAtlas()) {
            fontsFor = canvas.getAtlas();
            createFonts(fontsFor);
        }
    }

    protected Font text(Canvas c) {
        ensureFonts(c);
        return text;
    }

    protected Font bold(Canvas c) {
        ensureFonts(c);
        return bold;
    }

    protected Font small(Canvas c) {
        ensureFonts(c);
        return small;
    }

    protected Font icon(Canvas c) {
        ensureFonts(c);
        return icon;
    }

    protected Font title(Canvas c) {
        ensureFonts(c);
        return title;
    }

    protected Font tab(Canvas c) {
        ensureFonts(c);
        return tab;
    }

    /** Top of text vertically centred on {@code centerY}. */
    protected double centeredTextY(Canvas c, Font font, double centerY) {
        return Math.round(centerY - c.lineHeight(font) / 2);
    }

    protected double controlWidth(double rowWidth) {
        return Math.max(40, Math.min(controlWidth, rowWidth - indent - controlRightReserve));
    }

    /** Places a widget's keybind label right-aligned in a row. */
    protected void layoutKeybind(Canvas c, Widget widget, double rowRight, double y, double height) {
        KeybindWidget keybind = widget.getKeybind();
        if (keybind == null) return;
        double w = c.measure(small(c), keybind.getText(this));
        keybind.bounds.set(rowRight - w, y, w, height);
        keybind.label.set(rowRight - w - 2, y, w + 4, height);
    }

    /* ---- row layout ---- */

    @Override
    public double layoutCheckbox(Canvas c, CheckboxWidget w, double x, double y, double width) {
        double h = Math.max(textRow, checkboxSize);
        w.bounds.set(x, y, width, h);
        w.box.set(x, Math.round(y + (h - checkboxSize) / 2), checkboxSize, checkboxSize);
        layoutKeybind(c, w, x + width, y, h);
        return h;
    }

    @Override
    public double layoutButton(Canvas c, ButtonWidget w, double x, double y, double width) {
        w.bounds.set(x, y, width, buttonHeight);
        w.button.set(x + indent, y, controlWidth(width), buttonHeight);
        layoutKeybind(c, w, x + width, y, buttonHeight);
        return buttonHeight;
    }

    @Override
    public double layoutSlider(Canvas c, SliderWidget w, double x, double y, double width) {
        double h = labelRow + labelGap + sliderTrackHeight + sliderValueOverhang;
        w.bounds.set(x, y, width, h);
        w.track.set(x + indent, y + labelRow + labelGap, controlWidth(width), sliderTrackHeight);
        return h;
    }

    protected double labelledBox(Rect box, Widget w, double x, double y, double width) {
        double h = labelRow + labelGap + boxHeight;
        w.bounds.set(x, y, width, h);
        box.set(x + indent, y + labelRow + labelGap, controlWidth(width), boxHeight);
        return h;
    }

    @Override
    public double layoutChoice(Canvas c, ChoiceWidget w, double x, double y, double width) {
        return labelledBox(w.box, w, x, y, width);
    }

    @Override
    public double layoutCombo(Canvas c, ComboWidget w, double x, double y, double width) {
        return labelledBox(w.box, w, x, y, width);
    }

    @Override
    public double layoutField(Canvas c, FieldWidget w, double x, double y, double width) {
        return labelledBox(w.box, w, x, y, width);
    }

    @Override
    public double layoutColor(Canvas c, ColorWidget w, double x, double y, double width) {
        double h = Math.max(textRow, swatchHeight);
        w.bounds.set(x, y, width, h);
        w.swatch.set(x + width - swatchWidth, Math.round(y + (h - swatchHeight) / 2), swatchWidth, swatchHeight);
        return h;
    }

    @Override
    public void layoutAttachedColor(Canvas c, ColorWidget w, Widget row, int index) {
        Rect r = row.bounds;
        double h = Math.max(textRow, swatchHeight);
        w.bounds.set(r.x, r.y, r.w, h);
        w.swatch.set(r.right() - swatchWidth - index * (swatchWidth + 4), Math.round(r.y + (h - swatchHeight) / 2), swatchWidth, swatchHeight);
        if (index > 0) return;
        //a keybind on the same row moves left of the swatch
        KeybindWidget keybind = row.getKeybind();
        if (keybind != null) {
            double shift = swatchWidth + 6;
            keybind.bounds.x -= shift;
            keybind.label.x -= shift;
        }
    }

    @Override
    public double layoutLabel(Canvas c, LabelWidget w, double x, double y, double width) {
        w.bounds.set(x, y, width, textRow);
        layoutKeybind(c, w, x + width, y, textRow);
        return textRow;
    }

    @Override
    public void layoutContext(Canvas c, ContextPopup popup) {
        popup.itemHeight = popupItemHeight;
        double w = 0;
        for (String item : popup.getItems()) w = Math.max(w, c.measure(text(c), item));
        w += 20;
        double h = popup.getItems().size() * popupItemHeight;
        Menu menu = popup.getOwner().getMenu();
        double x = Math.min(popup.anchorX, menu.getViewWidth() - w), y = Math.min(popup.anchorY, menu.getViewHeight() - h);
        popup.bounds.set(Math.max(0, x), Math.max(0, y), w, h);
    }

    @Override
    public double layoutList(Canvas c, ListWidget w, double x, double y, double width) {
        int rows = Math.max(1, listRows);
        double h = rows * listItemHeight + 2;
        w.bounds.set(x, y, width, h);
        w.box.set(x + indent, y, controlWidth(width), h);
        w.itemHeight = listItemHeight;
        w.visibleRows = rows;
        return h;
    }

    /* ---- popups ---- */

    @Override
    public void layoutDropdown(Canvas c, DropdownPopup popup) {
        Rect box = popup.getOwner() instanceof ChoiceWidget ? ((ChoiceWidget) popup.getOwner()).box : ((ComboWidget) popup.getOwner()).box;
        int rows = Math.min(popupMaxRows, popup.getOptions().size());
        popup.itemHeight = popupItemHeight;
        popup.visibleRows = rows;
        double h = rows * popupItemHeight;
        double y = box.bottom() + 1;
        Menu menu = popup.getOwner().getMenu();
        if (y + h > menu.getViewHeight() && box.y - h - 1 >= 0) y = box.y - h - 1;
        popup.bounds.set(box.x, y, box.w, h);
    }

    @Override
    public void layoutColorPicker(Canvas c, ColorPopup popup) {
        Rect swatch = popup.getWidget().swatch;
        double pad = 6, square = 150, bar = 10;
        double w = pad + square + pad + bar + pad, h = pad + square + pad + bar + pad;
        Menu menu = popup.getOwner().getMenu();
        double x = swatch.right() + 6;
        if (x + w > menu.getViewWidth()) x = swatch.x - w - 6;
        double y = Math.min(swatch.y, menu.getViewHeight() - h);
        popup.bounds.set(x, Math.max(0, y), w, h);
        popup.svSquare.set(popup.bounds.x + pad, popup.bounds.y + pad, square, square);
        popup.hueBar.set(popup.svSquare.right() + pad, popup.svSquare.y, bar, square);
        popup.alphaBar.set(popup.svSquare.x, popup.svSquare.bottom() + pad, square, bar);
    }

    /** Draws the colour picker's gradients (shared by themes; frames are theme-specific). */
    protected void paintPickerParts(Canvas c, ColorPopup p, int border) {
        Rect sv = p.svSquare;
        //saturation: white → hue horizontally, then value: transparent → black vertically
        c.gradientH(sv.x, sv.y, sv.w, sv.h, 0xFFFFFFFF, p.hueColor());
        c.gradientV(sv.x, sv.y, sv.w, sv.h, 0x00000000, 0xFF000000);
        c.outline(sv.x - 1, sv.y - 1, sv.w + 2, sv.h + 2, border);
        double mx = sv.x + p.getSaturation() * sv.w, my = sv.y + (1 - p.getBrightness()) * sv.h;
        c.outline(mx - 2, my - 2, 5, 5, 0xFFFFFFFF);

        Rect hue = p.hueBar;
        int segments = 6;
        for (int i = 0; i < segments; i++) {
            int top = 0xFF000000 | java.awt.Color.HSBtoRGB(i / (float) segments, 1, 1);
            int bottom = 0xFF000000 | java.awt.Color.HSBtoRGB((i + 1) / (float) segments, 1, 1);
            c.gradientV(hue.x, hue.y + hue.h * i / segments, hue.w, hue.h / segments, top, bottom);
        }
        c.outline(hue.x - 1, hue.y - 1, hue.w + 2, hue.h + 2, border);
        double hy = hue.y + p.getHue() * hue.h;
        c.rect(hue.x - 1, hy - 1, hue.w + 2, 2, 0xFFFFFFFF);

        Rect alpha = p.alphaBar;
        paintChecker(c, alpha.x, alpha.y, alpha.w, alpha.h);
        int rgb = p.getOwner() instanceof ColorWidget ? ((ColorWidget) p.getOwner()).getColor().getRGB() & 0xFFFFFF : 0xFFFFFF;
        c.gradientH(alpha.x, alpha.y, alpha.w, alpha.h, rgb, 0xFF000000 | rgb);
        c.outline(alpha.x - 1, alpha.y - 1, alpha.w + 2, alpha.h + 2, border);
        double ax = alpha.x + p.getAlpha() / 255.0 * alpha.w;
        c.rect(ax - 1, alpha.y - 1, 2, alpha.h + 2, 0xFFFFFFFF);
    }

    /** A grey checkerboard (behind translucent colours). */
    protected void paintChecker(Canvas c, double x, double y, double w, double h) {
        c.rect(x, y, w, h, 0xFFBFBFBF);
        double cell = 4;
        c.pushClip(x, y, w, h);
        for (double cy = 0; cy < h; cy += cell) {
            for (double cx = ((int) (cy / cell) % 2) * cell; cx < w; cx += cell * 2) c.rect(x + cx, y + cy, cell, cell, 0xFFFFFFFF);
        }
        c.popClip();
    }

    /* ---- keybinds ---- */

    protected String keyName(int key) {
        String name = Keys.getKeyName(key);
        return name == null ? "#" + key : name;
    }

    /* ---- scroll bars ---- */

    @Override
    public void paintGroupForeground(Canvas c, Menu menu, GroupView g) {
        if (!g.isScrollable()) return;
        Rect content = g.content;
        double trackX = content.right() + 2;
        double thumbH = Math.max(12, content.h * content.h / g.getContentHeight());
        double thumbY = content.y + (content.h - thumbH) * g.getScroll() / (g.getContentHeight() - content.h);
        c.rect(trackX, content.y, scrollbarWidth, content.h, scrollbarTrack());
        c.rect(trackX, thumbY, scrollbarWidth, thumbH, scrollbarThumb());
    }

    protected abstract int scrollbarTrack();

    protected abstract int scrollbarThumb();
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/theme/Colors.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.theme;

/** ARGB helpers for themes. */
public final class Colors {
    private Colors() {
    }

    public static int rgb(int rgb) {
        return 0xFF000000 | rgb;
    }

    public static int withAlpha(int argb, int alpha) {
        return (alpha & 255) << 24 | argb & 0xFFFFFF;
    }

    /** Scales the RGB channels by {@code factor} (0 = black, 1 = unchanged, >1 brighter, clamped). */
    public static int shade(int argb, double factor) {
        int r = clamp((int) Math.round((argb >> 16 & 255) * factor));
        int g = clamp((int) Math.round((argb >> 8 & 255) * factor));
        int b = clamp((int) Math.round((argb & 255) * factor));
        return argb & 0xFF000000 | r << 16 | g << 8 | b;
    }

    /** Mixes two colours ({@code t} = 0 → a, 1 → b). */
    public static int mix(int a, int b, double t) {
        t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, t));
        int aa = a >>> 24, ar = a >> 16 & 255, ag = a >> 8 & 255, ab = a & 255;
        int ba = b >>> 24, br = b >> 16 & 255, bg = b >> 8 & 255, bb = b & 255;
        return (int) Math.round(aa + (ba - aa) * t) << 24 | (int) Math.round(ar + (br - ar) * t) << 16
                | (int) Math.round(ag + (bg - ag) * t) << 8 | (int) Math.round(ab + (bb - ab) * t);
    }

    private static int clamp(int v) {
        return Math.max(0, Math.min(255, v));
    }
}
```

```java
/** How a theme presents the built-in config UI. */
public enum ConfigUiStyle {
    /** A dedicated last tab with a config list, name field and load/save/delete/reset/import/export buttons. */
    PAGE,
    /** A compact "Config" group pinned to the bottom of the last column of every page: slot dropdown + load/save. */

}
```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/theme/FontFamilies.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.theme;

import java.awt.Font;
import java.awt.FontFormatException;
import java.awt.GraphicsEnvironment;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.logging.Logger;

/** Picks installed font families and registers bundled ones. */
public final class FontFamilies {
    private static final Logger LOG = Logger.getLogger("Icarus");
    private static Set<String> installed;

    private FontFamilies() {
    }

    private static synchronized Set<String> installed() {
        if (installed == null) {
            installed = new HashSet<>();
            for (String family : GraphicsEnvironment.getLocalGraphicsEnvironment().getAvailableFontFamilyNames(Locale.ROOT)) {
                installed.add(family.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT));
            }
        }
        return installed;
    }

    /** The first installed family of {@code candidates}, or Java's logical "SansSerif" (with a warning). */
    public static String first(String... candidates) {
        for (String candidate : candidates) {
            if (installed().contains(candidate.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT))) return candidate;
        }
        LOG.warning("[Icarus] None of the fonts " + Arrays.toString(candidates) + " is installed; using SansSerif");
        return Font.SANS_SERIF;
    }

    /** Registers a TrueType font shipped as a resource; returns its family name (or SansSerif if it fails). */
    public static synchronized String register(Class<?> owner, String resource) {
        try (InputStream in = owner.getResourceAsStream(resource)) {
            if (in == null) throw new IOException("missing resource " + resource);
            Font font = Font.createFont(Font.TRUETYPE_FONT, in);
            GraphicsEnvironment.getLocalGraphicsEnvironment().registerFont(font);
            if (installed != null) installed.add(font.getFamily().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT));
            return font.getFamily();
        } catch (IOException | FontFormatException e) {
            LOG.warning("[Icarus] Couldn't load font " + resource + ": " + e);
            return Font.SANS_SERIF;
        }
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/theme/Theme.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.theme;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.hud.Toast;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.hud.Watermark;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.Canvas;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.GroupView;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.Menu;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.popup.ColorPopup;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.popup.ContextPopup;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.popup.DropdownPopup;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.ButtonWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.CheckboxWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.ChoiceWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.ColorWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.ComboWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.FieldWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.KeybindWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.LabelWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.ListWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.SliderWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.Widget;

/**
 * A complete look for the menu and HUD: window chrome, layout and every widget. Widgets and the menu only hold
 * state; everything visual comes from here, so a new style is one implementation of this interface.
 * <p>
 * All coordinates are design units (1 unit = 1 screen pixel at Minecraft GUI scale 2). Themes must be stateless
 * with respect to a particular menu (one instance can serve several mods); per-menu state lives in the menu and
 * widgets. The built-in themes are in {@link Themes}.
 */
public interface Theme {
    /** Stable id, saved in UI preferences. */
    String getId();

    /** Name shown in the theme selector. */
    String getDisplayName();

    /** Accent colour used when the mod doesn't set one (ARGB). */
    int getDefaultAccent();

    /** Where the built-in config UI goes. */
    ConfigUiStyle getConfigUiStyle();

    /** Icon of the config tab for {@link ConfigUiStyle#PAGE} (a character of the icon font). */
    char getConfigIcon();

    /* ---- menu ---- */

    /**
     * Lays out the window: sets {@code menu.window} size, {@code menu.dragArea}, each tab's and sub-tab's bounds,
     * and the groups of the current sub-tab (normally via {@link Menu#layoutColumns}).
     */
    void layout(Menu menu, Canvas canvas);

    /** Window background, chrome, tabs, sub-tabs and footer. */
    void paintWindow(Canvas canvas, Menu menu);

    /** A group's frame and title (before its widgets). */
    void paintGroup(Canvas canvas, Menu menu, GroupView group);

    /** Anything over a group's widgets (e.g. its scrollbar). */
    void paintGroupForeground(Canvas canvas, Menu menu, GroupView group);

    /* ---- widgets: layout returns the row height and fills the widget's part rectangles ---- */

    double layoutCheckbox(Canvas canvas, CheckboxWidget widget, double x, double y, double width);

    void paintCheckbox(Canvas canvas, CheckboxWidget widget);

    double layoutButton(Canvas canvas, ButtonWidget widget, double x, double y, double width);

    void paintButton(Canvas canvas, ButtonWidget widget);

    double layoutSlider(Canvas canvas, SliderWidget widget, double x, double y, double width);

    void paintSlider(Canvas canvas, SliderWidget widget);

    double layoutChoice(Canvas canvas, ChoiceWidget widget, double x, double y, double width);

    void paintChoice(Canvas canvas, ChoiceWidget widget);

    double layoutCombo(Canvas canvas, ComboWidget widget, double x, double y, double width);

    void paintCombo(Canvas canvas, ComboWidget widget);

    double layoutField(Canvas canvas, FieldWidget widget, double x, double y, double width);

    void paintField(Canvas canvas, FieldWidget widget);

    double layoutColor(Canvas canvas, ColorWidget widget, double x, double y, double width);

    void paintColor(Canvas canvas, ColorWidget widget);

    double layoutList(Canvas canvas, ListWidget widget, double x, double y, double width);

    void paintList(Canvas canvas, ListWidget widget);

    /**
     * A swatch shown on another widget's row ({@code @IAttach}); sets the swatch inside {@code row}'s bounds.
     * {@code index} counts the swatches already on that row (0 for the first), so several can line up.
     */
    void layoutAttachedColor(Canvas canvas, ColorWidget widget, Widget row, int index);

    double layoutLabel(Canvas canvas, LabelWidget widget, double x, double y, double width);

    void paintLabel(Canvas canvas, LabelWidget widget);

    void paintKeybind(Canvas canvas, KeybindWidget widget);

    /** Keybind label text, e.g. "[DEL]". {@code refused} is true briefly after the menu key was pressed. */
    String formatKeybind(int key, boolean capturing, boolean refused);

    /* ---- popups ---- */

    void layoutDropdown(Canvas canvas, DropdownPopup popup);

    void paintDropdown(Canvas canvas, DropdownPopup popup);

    /** Right-click menu at {@code popup.anchorX/anchorY}: sets its bounds and item height. */
    void layoutContext(Canvas canvas, ContextPopup popup);

    void paintContext(Canvas canvas, ContextPopup popup);

    void layoutColorPicker(Canvas canvas, ColorPopup popup);

    void paintColorPicker(Canvas canvas, ColorPopup popup);

    /* ---- HUD ---- */

    double watermarkHeight(Canvas canvas);

    double watermarkWidth(Canvas canvas, Watermark watermark);

    void paintWatermark(Canvas canvas, Watermark watermark, double x, double y, double width, double height, int accent);

    double toastWidth(Canvas canvas, Toast toast);

    double toastHeight(Canvas canvas, Toast toast);

    /** {@code visibility} is the slide-in/out progress, 0 (hidden) to 1 (fully shown). */
    void paintToast(Canvas canvas, Toast toast, double x, double y, double width, double height, double visibility, int accent);
}
```
