```java
        c.rect(b.x + p, b.y + p, b.w - 2 * p, b.h - 2 * p, w.isFocused() ? 0xFF232323 : Colors.mix(0xFF1D1D1D, 0xFF222222, w.getHover()));
        Font font = text(c);
        String value = w.getText();
        double tw = c.measure(font, value);
        double offset = Math.min(0, b.w - P(22) - tw);
        double ty = capsTop(c, font, b.y + P(6));
        c.pushClip(b.x + p, b.y + p, b.w - 2 * p, b.h - 2 * p);
        c.text(font, value, b.x + P(7) + offset, ty, TEXT);
        if (!w.isFocused() || w.isCaretVisible()) c.text(font, "_", b.x + P(7) + offset + tw, ty, TEXT);
        c.popClip();
    }

    @Override
    public void paintColor(Canvas c, ColorWidget w) {
        if (!w.isAttached()) label(c, w, w.bounds.x + P(LABEL_X), w.bounds.y);
        Rect s = w.swatch;
        double p = px(c, 1);
        c.rect(s.x, s.y, s.w, s.h, BLACK);
        Color color = w.getColor();
        if (color.getAlpha() < 255) paintChecker(c, s.x + p, s.y + p, s.w - 2 * p, s.h - 2 * p);
        int argb = color.getRGB();
        c.gradientV(s.x + p, s.y + p, s.w - 2 * p, s.h - 2 * p, argb, Colors.shade(argb, 0.85));
    }

    @Override
    public void paintList(Canvas c, ListWidget w) {
        Rect b = w.box;
        double p = px(c, 1);
        boolean search = w.isSearchable();
        if (search) {
            //a text field (black, grey and dark rings) sitting on top of the list
            Rect s = w.search;
            c.rect(s.x, s.y, s.w, s.h, BLACK);
            c.rect(s.x + p, s.y + p, s.w - 2 * p, s.h - p, 0xFF2F2F2F);
            c.rect(s.x + 2 * p, s.y + 2 * p, s.w - 4 * p, s.h - 3 * p, 0xFF111111);
            c.rect(s.x + 3 * p, s.y + 3 * p, s.w - 6 * p, s.h - 5 * p, w.isSearchFocused() ? 0xFF1D1D1D : 0xFF181818);
            Font sf = text(c);
            double qw = c.measure(sf, w.getQuery());
            double ty = capsTop(c, sf, s.y + P(8));
            c.text(sf, w.getQuery(), s.x + P(7), ty, TEXT);
            c.text(sf, "_", s.x + P(7) + qw, ty, TEXT);
        }
        //the list: black sides and bottom (and top without a search field), dark grey inside
        c.rect(b.x, b.y - (search ? 0 : p), b.w, b.h + (search ? 0 : p), BLACK);
        c.rect(b.x + p, b.y, b.w - 2 * p, b.h - p, 0xFF222222);
        List<String> items = w.getItems();
        boolean scrolls = items.size() > w.visibleRows;
        double textW = b.w - P(20) - (scrolls ? P(6) : 0);
        Font font = text(c);
        c.pushClip(b.x + p, b.y, b.w - 2 * p, b.h - p);
        for (int row = 0; row < w.visibleRows; row++) {
            int index = w.getScroll() + row;
            if (index >= items.size()) break;
            String item = items.get(index);
            double y = b.y + row * w.itemHeight;
            boolean selected = item.equals(w.getSelected());
            boolean hovered = index == w.getHoveredIndex();
            if (hovered || selected) c.rect(b.x + p, y, b.w - 2 * p, w.itemHeight - 2 * p, 0xFF191919);
            Font f = selected || hovered ? bold(c) : font;
            c.text(f, c.fit(f, item, textW), b.x + P(10), capsTop(c, f, y + P(LIST_TEXT_Y)), selected ? w.getMenu().getAccent() : TEXT);
        }
        c.popClip();
        if (scrolls) {
            //scrollbar track along the right edge, a small thumb, and the "more below" arrow
            double tx = b.right() - p - P(6);
            c.rect(tx, b.y, P(6), b.h - p, 0xFF2D2D2D);
            double thumbH = Math.max(P(20), (b.h - p) * w.visibleRows / items.size());
            double thumbY = b.y + (b.h - p - thumbH) * w.getScroll() / (items.size() - w.visibleRows);
            c.rect(tx + p, snap(c, thumbY), P(4), thumbH, 0xFF404040);
            if (w.getScroll() + w.visibleRows < items.size()) {
                double ax = snap(c, b.right() - P(17)), ay = snap(c, b.bottom() - P(9));
                c.rect(ax - P(2), ay, P(5), p, BLACK);
                c.triangle(ax - P(2), ay + p, ax + P(3), ay + p, ax + P(0.5), ay + p + P(2.5), 0xFFCCCCCC);
            }
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void paintKeybind(Canvas c, KeybindWidget w) {
        int color = w.isRefused() ? 0xFFD04040 : w.isCapturing() ? TEXT : TEXT_KEYBIND;
        PixelFont.draw(c, w.getText(this), w.bounds.x, w.bounds.y, color);
    }

    private static final Map<String, String> SHORT_KEYS = new HashMap<>();

    static {
        SHORT_KEYS.put("DELETE", "DEL");
        SHORT_KEYS.put("INSERT", "INS");
        SHORT_KEYS.put("LSHIFT", "SHF");
        SHORT_KEYS.put("RSHIFT", "SHF");
        SHORT_KEYS.put("LCONTROL", "CTR");
        SHORT_KEYS.put("RCONTROL", "CTR");
        SHORT_KEYS.put("LMENU", "ALT");
        SHORT_KEYS.put("RMENU", "ALT");
        SHORT_KEYS.put("SPACE", "SPC");
        SHORT_KEYS.put("RETURN", "RET");
        SHORT_KEYS.put("ESCAPE", "ESC");
        SHORT_KEYS.put("CAPITAL", "CAP");
        SHORT_KEYS.put("BACK", "BCK");
        SHORT_KEYS.put("PRIOR", "PGU");
        SHORT_KEYS.put("NEXT", "PGD");
        SHORT_KEYS.put("HOME", "HOM");
        SHORT_KEYS.put("END", "END");
        SHORT_KEYS.put("TAB", "TAB");
        SHORT_KEYS.put("GRAVE", "~");
    }

    @Override
    public String formatKeybind(int key, boolean capturing, boolean refused) {
        if (refused) return "[-]";
        if (capturing) return "[...]";
        if (key <= 0) return "[-]";
        String name = keyName(key);
        String shortName = SHORT_KEYS.get(name);
        if (shortName != null) name = shortName;
        else if (name.startsWith("NUMPAD")) name = "N" + name.substring(6);
        return "[" + name + "]";
    }

    /* ---- popups ---- */

    @Override
    public void layoutDropdown(Canvas c, DropdownPopup popup) {
        Rect box = popup.getOwner() instanceof ChoiceWidget ? ((ChoiceWidget) popup.getOwner()).box : ((ComboWidget) popup.getOwner()).box;
        int rows = Math.min(popupMaxRows, popup.getOptions().size());
        double p = px(c, 1);
        popup.itemHeight = popupItemHeight;
        popup.visibleRows = rows;
        double h = rows * popupItemHeight + 2 * p;
        double y = box.bottom() + p;
        Menu menu = popup.getOwner().getMenu();
        if (y + h > menu.getViewHeight() && box.y - h - p >= 0) y = box.y - h - p;
        popup.bounds.set(box.x, y, box.w, h);
    }

    @Override
    public void paintDropdown(Canvas c, DropdownPopup p) {
        Rect b = p.bounds;
        double px = px(c, 1);
        c.rect(b.x, b.y, b.w, b.h, BLACK);
        c.rect(b.x + px, b.y + px, b.w - 2 * px, b.h - 2 * px, 0xFF232323);
        List<String> options = p.getOptions();
        int accent = p.getOwner().getMenu().getAccent();
        for (int row = 0; row < p.visibleRows; row++) {
            int index = p.getScroll() + row;
            if (index >= options.size()) break;
            double y = b.y + px + row * p.itemHeight;
            boolean hovered = index == p.getHovered();
            boolean selected = p.getSource().isSelected(index);
            if (hovered) c.rect(b.x + px, y, b.w - 2 * px, p.itemHeight, 0xFF1A1A1A);
            Font font = selected || hovered ? bold(c) : text(c);
            c.text(font, c.fit(font, options.get(index), b.w - P(22)), b.x + P(11), capsTop(c, font, y + P(7)), selected ? accent : TEXT);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void layoutContext(Canvas c, ContextPopup popup) {
        double p = px(c, 1);
        popup.itemHeight = P(20);
        double w = 0;
        for (String item : popup.getItems()) w = Math.max(w, c.measure(bold(c), item));
        w = Math.max(P(70), w + P(22));
        double h = popup.getItems().size() * popup.itemHeight + 2 * p;
        Menu menu = popup.getOwner().getMenu();
        double x = Math.min(popup.anchorX, menu.getViewWidth() - w), y = Math.min(popup.anchorY, menu.getViewHeight() - h);
        popup.bounds.set(Math.max(0, x), Math.max(0, y), w, h);
    }

    @Override
    public void paintContext(Canvas c, ContextPopup popup) {
        Rect b = popup.bounds;
        double p = px(c, 1);
        c.rect(b.x, b.y, b.w, b.h, BLACK);
        c.rect(b.x + p, b.y + p, b.w - 2 * p, b.h - 2 * p, 0xFF232323);
        int accent = popup.getOwner().getMenu().getAccent();
        List<String> items = popup.getItems();
        for (int i = 0; i < items.size(); i++) {
            double y = b.y + p + i * popup.itemHeight;
            boolean hovered = i == popup.getHovered(), selected = i == popup.getSelected();
            if (hovered) c.rect(b.x + p, y, b.w - 2 * p, popup.itemHeight, 0xFF1A1A1A);
            Font font = selected || hovered ? bold(c) : text(c);
            c.text(font, items.get(i), b.x + P(10), capsTop(c, font, y + P(6)), selected ? accent : TEXT);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Colour picker (reference pixels): black and grey rings, 2px padding, then the SV square and the hue bar (red at
     * the top, running backwards) side by side and the alpha bar below, each in a dark inset with 3px between them.
     */
    /** Lists (reference pixels): search field height, row pitch, text offset in a row. */
    protected static final double LIST_SEARCH_H = 24, LIST_ITEM_H = 23, LIST_TEXT_Y = 6;
    protected static final double PICKER_SV = 188, PICKER_HUE_W = 19, PICKER_ALPHA_H = 13, PICKER_GAP = 3;

    @Override
    public void layoutColorPicker(Canvas c, ColorPopup popup) {
        Rect swatch = popup.getWidget().swatch;
        double edge = P(4); //black ring, grey ring, 2px padding
        double w = 2 * edge + P(1 + PICKER_SV + 1 + PICKER_GAP + 1 + PICKER_HUE_W + 1);
        double h = 2 * edge + P(1 + PICKER_SV + 1 + PICKER_GAP + 1 + PICKER_ALPHA_H + 1);
        Menu menu = popup.getOwner().getMenu();
        double x = swatch.x - P(2), y = swatch.bottom() + P(2);
        if (x + w > menu.getViewWidth()) x = menu.getViewWidth() - w;
        if (y + h > menu.getViewHeight()) y = swatch.y - h - P(2);
        popup.bounds.set(x, Math.max(0, y), w, h);
        Rect b = popup.bounds;
        popup.svSquare.set(b.x + edge + P(1), b.y + edge + P(1), P(PICKER_SV), P(PICKER_SV));
        popup.hueBar.set(popup.svSquare.right() + P(1 + PICKER_GAP + 1), popup.svSquare.y, P(PICKER_HUE_W), P(PICKER_SV));
        popup.alphaBar.set(popup.svSquare.x, popup.svSquare.bottom() + P(1 + PICKER_GAP + 1), P(PICKER_SV), P(PICKER_ALPHA_H));
        popup.hueReversed = true;
    }

    @Override
    public void paintColorPicker(Canvas c, ColorPopup p) {
        Rect b = p.bounds;
        double px = px(c, 1);
        c.rect(b.x, b.y, b.w, b.h, BLACK);
        c.rect(b.x + px, b.y + px, b.w - 2 * px, b.h - 2 * px, 0xFF393939);
        c.rect(b.x + 2 * px, b.y + 2 * px, b.w - 4 * px, b.h - 4 * px, 0xFF262626);

        Rect sv = p.svSquare;
        c.rect(sv.x - px, sv.y - px, sv.w + 2 * px, sv.h + 2 * px, 0xFF0D0D0D);
        c.gradientH(sv.x, sv.y, sv.w, sv.h, 0xFFFFFFFF, p.hueColor());
        c.gradientV(sv.x, sv.y, sv.w, sv.h, 0x00000000, 0xFF000000);
        double mx = snap(c, sv.x + p.getSaturation() * (sv.w - px)), my = snap(c, sv.y + (1 - p.getBrightness()) * (sv.h - px));
        c.outline(mx - 2 * px, my - 2 * px, 5 * px, 5 * px, BLACK);
        c.outline(mx - px, my - px, 3 * px, 3 * px, 0xFFE6E6E6);

        //hue: red at the top, then magenta, blue, cyan, green, yellow, red again
        Rect hue = p.hueBar;
        c.rect(hue.x - px, hue.y - px, hue.w + 2 * px, hue.h + 2 * px, 0xFF0D0D0D);
        for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
            int top = 0xFF000000 | Color.HSBtoRGB(1 - i / 6f, 1, 1), bottom = 0xFF000000 | Color.HSBtoRGB(1 - (i + 1) / 6f, 1, 1);
            double y0 = snap(c, hue.y + hue.h * i / 6), y1 = snap(c, hue.y + hue.h * (i + 1) / 6);
            c.gradientV(hue.x, y0, hue.w, y1 - y0, top, bottom);
        }
        double hy = snap(c, hue.y + (1 - p.getHue()) * (hue.h - 2 * px));
        c.rect(hue.x, hy, hue.w, 2 * px, 0xFFE6E6E6);

        //alpha: filled with the colour up to its alpha, like a slider, with a small handle at the end
        Rect alpha = p.alphaBar;
        c.rect(alpha.x - px, alpha.y - px, alpha.w + 2 * px, alpha.h + 2 * px, 0xFF0D0D0D);
        c.rect(alpha.x, alpha.y, alpha.w, alpha.h, 0xFF262626);
        int rgb = p.getWidget().getColor().getRGB() | 0xFF000000;
        double fill = snap(c, alpha.w * p.getAlpha() / 255.0);
        c.rect(alpha.x, alpha.y, fill, alpha.h, rgb);
        double hx = Math.min(alpha.right() - 3 * px, alpha.x + fill - 2 * px);
        c.rect(hx, alpha.y, 3 * px, alpha.h, 0xFFB4B4B4);
    }

    /* ---- HUD ---- */

    @Override
    public double watermarkHeight(Canvas c) {
        return P(30);
    }

    @Override
    public double watermarkWidth(Canvas c, Watermark watermark) {
        return Math.round(c.measure(text(c), watermark.getText()) + P(30));
    }

    protected void hudFrame(Canvas c, double x, double y, double w, double h) {
        double p = px(c, 1);
        c.rect(x, y, w, h, BLACK);
        c.rect(x + p, y + p, w - 2 * p, h - 2 * p, BEVEL_LIGHT);
        c.rect(x + 2 * p, y + 2 * p, w - 4 * p, h - 4 * p, BEVEL_MID);
        c.rect(x + 5 * p, y + 5 * p, w - 10 * p, h - 10 * p, BEVEL_LIGHT);
        c.rect(x + 6 * p, y + 6 * p, w - 12 * p, h - 12 * p, BLACK);
        double ix = x + 7 * p, iy = y + 7 * p, iw = w - 14 * p, ih = h - 14 * p;
        c.tile(stripes(), ix, iy, iw, ih, 0xFFFFFFFF);
        double half = iw / 2;
        c.hlineGradient(ix, iy, half, STRIP_LEFT, STRIP_MID);
        c.hlineGradient(ix + half, iy, iw - half, STRIP_MID, STRIP_RIGHT);
        c.hlineGradient(ix, iy + p, half, Colors.shade(STRIP_LEFT, 0.53), Colors.shade(STRIP_MID, 0.53));
        c.hlineGradient(ix + half, iy + p, iw - half, Colors.shade(STRIP_MID, 0.53), Colors.shade(STRIP_RIGHT, 0.53));
    }

    @Override
    public void paintWatermark(Canvas c, Watermark watermark, double x, double y, double w, double h, int accent) {
        hudFrame(c, x, y, w, h);
        c.text(text(c), watermark.getText(), x + P(15), capsTop(c, text(c), y + P(13)), TEXT);
    }

    @Override
    public double toastWidth(Canvas c, Toast n) {
        return P(280);
    }

    @Override
    public double toastHeight(Canvas c, Toast n) {
        return P(24) + P(20) + n.lines(c, text(c), P(280 - 36)).size() * P(17) + P(12);
    }

    @Override
    public void paintToast(Canvas c, Toast n, double x, double y, double w, double h, double visibility, int accent) {
        c.setAlpha(visibility);
        hudFrame(c, x, y, w, h);
        double ty = y + P(18);
        c.text(bold(c), n.getTitle(), x + P(18), capsTop(c, bold(c), ty), TEXT);
        ty += P(20);
        for (String line : n.lines(c, text(c), w - P(36))) {
            c.text(text(c), line, x + P(18), capsTop(c, text(c), ty), TEXT_VALUE);
            ty += P(17);
        }
        c.setAlpha(1);
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/ui/ConfigPanel.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.ui;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.Icarus;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.IcarusMod;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.config.ConfigStore;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.GroupNode;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.ListModel;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.Setting;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.SettingTypes;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.theme.Theme;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.theme.Themes;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * The built-in config UI and theme selector, made of virtual settings so they use the normal widgets:
 * <ul>
 *     <li>full (for {@code ConfigUiStyle.PAGE}): config list, name field, load/save/delete/reset/import/export</li>
 *     <li>compact (for {@code ConfigUiStyle.PINNED}): slot dropdown, load/save</li>
 * </ul>
 * Its controls aren't saved in configs.
 */
public final class ConfigPanel {
    static final List<String> SLOTS = Arrays.asList("Slot 1", "Slot 2", "Slot 3", "Slot 4", "Slot 5");

    private final IcarusMod mod;
    private final ConfigStore store;
    private final GroupNode full;
    private final GroupNode compact;
    private final ListModel profiles = new ListModel();
    private String name = "";
    private String slot = SLOTS.get(0);

    /**
     * @param configUi      include the config controls
     * @param themeSelector include a theme dropdown
     */
    public ConfigPanel(IcarusMod mod, boolean configUi, boolean themeSelector) {
        this.mod = mod;
        this.store = mod.getConfigStore();
        this.full = GroupNode.standalone(configUi ? "Presets" : "Menu", LAST_COLUMN, Integer.MAX_VALUE, false);
        this.compact = GroupNode.standalone(configUi ? "Config" : "Menu", LAST_COLUMN, Integer.MAX_VALUE, true);

        if (configUi) {
            full.addVirtual("Configs", SettingTypes.LIST, new Setting.Holder<String>() {
                @Override
                public String get() {
                    return profiles.getSelected();
                }

                @Override
                public void set(String value) {
                    profiles.select(value);
                    if (value != null) name = value;
                }
            }, null, null).labelled("").setExtra(profiles);
            full.addVirtual("Name", SettingTypes.FIELD, new Setting.Holder<String>() {
                @Override
                public String get() {
                    return name;
                }

                @Override
                public void set(String value) {
                    name = value;
                }
            }, null, null).labelled("");
            button(full, "Load", () -> {
                String selected = profiles.getSelected();
                if (selected != null) store.load(selected);
                refresh();
            });
            button(full, "Save", this::saveFull);
            button(full, "Delete", () -> {
                String selected = profiles.getSelected();
                if (selected != null) store.delete(selected);
                refresh();
            });
            button(full, "Reset", store::resetToDefaults);
            button(full, "Import from clipboard", () -> store.importJson(Icarus.getPlatform().getClipboard()));
            button(full, "Export to clipboard", () -> Icarus.getPlatform().setClipboard(store.exportCurrent()));

            compact.addVirtual("Slot", SettingTypes.CHOICE, new Setting.Holder<String>() {
                @Override
                public String get() {
                    return slot;
                }

                @Override
                public void set(String value) {
                    slot = value;
                }
            }, SLOTS, null);
            button(compact, "Load config", () -> store.load(slot));
            button(compact, "Save config", () -> store.saveAs(slot));
        }

        if (themeSelector) {
            Setting.Holder<String> themeHolder = new Setting.Holder<String>() {
                @Override
                public String get() {
                    return mod.getTheme().getDisplayName();
                }

                @Override
                public void set(String value) {
                    Theme theme = Themes.byDisplayName(value);
                    if (theme != null) mod.setTheme(theme);
                }
            };
            full.addVirtual("Menu theme", SettingTypes.CHOICE, themeHolder, Themes.displayNames(), null);
            compact.addVirtual("Menu theme", SettingTypes.CHOICE, themeHolder, Themes.displayNames(), null);
        }
        refresh();
    }

    /** Marker meaning "last column" for groups created in code. */
    private static final int LAST_COLUMN = Integer.MAX_VALUE;

    private static void button(GroupNode group, String label, Runnable action) {
        group.addVirtual(label, SettingTypes.BUTTON, new Setting.Holder<Runnable>() {
            @Override
            public Runnable get() {
                return action;
            }

            @Override
            public void set(Runnable value) {
            }
        }, null, null);
    }

    private void saveFull() {
        String target = name == null ? "" : name.trim();
        if (ConfigStore.isValidName(target) && !target.equals(store.getProfile())) store.saveAs(target);
        else store.save();
        refresh();
    }

    /** Re-reads the saved configs (after any file operation, and when the menu opens). */
    public void refresh() {
        List<String> names = new ArrayList<>(store.listProfiles());
        if (!names.contains(store.getProfile())) names.add(0, store.getProfile());
        profiles.setItems(names);
        profiles.select(store.getProfile());
        name = store.getProfile();
        if (SLOTS.contains(store.getProfile())) slot = store.getProfile();
    }

    /** The group for a style: full for {@code PAGE}, compact for {@code PINNED}. */
    public GroupNode group(boolean page) {
        return page ? full : compact;
    }

    /** Label for a save button: marked while there are unsaved changes. */
    public String saveLabel(String base) {
        return store.hasUnsavedChanges() ? base + " *" : base;
    }

    /** Whether a group is one of this panel's. */
    public boolean owns(GroupNode group) {
        return group == full || group == compact;
    }

    IcarusMod getMod() {
        return mod;
    }

    /** Column hint used for the panel's groups: the last column. */
    public static boolean isLastColumn(int column) {
        return column == LAST_COLUMN;
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/ui/GroupMetrics.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.ui;

/** Spacing a theme uses inside group boxes, in design units. */
public final class GroupMetrics {
    /** Space from the group's top edge to its first row (includes the title). */
    public double padTop;
    public double padBottom;
    public double padLeft;
    public double padRight;
    /** Vertical gap between rows. */
    public double rowGap;
    /** Minimum height of a group (title only). */
    public double minHeight;
    /**
     * How far below the clipped content top the first row's {@code y} goes, for themes whose rows draw a little
     * above their {@code y} (so the first row isn't clipped).
     */
    public double rowOffset;
    /** How far below the content area rows may still be drawn (themes that fade them out into the frame). */
    public double clipBelow;
    /** Vertical gap between groups stacked in one column; negative means the same as the column gap. */
    public double stackGap = -1;

    public GroupMetrics(double padTop, double padBottom, double padLeft, double padRight, double rowGap, double minHeight) {
        this.padTop = padTop;
        this.padBottom = padBottom;
        this.padLeft = padLeft;
        this.padRight = padRight;
        this.rowGap = rowGap;
        this.minHeight = minHeight;
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/ui/GroupView.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.ui;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.GroupNode;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.Widget;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;

/** A group box on screen: its widgets plus the layout and scroll state the menu keeps for it. */
public final class GroupView {
    private final GroupNode group;
    private final List<Widget> widgets = new ArrayList<>();
    private final List<Widget> widgetsView = Collections.unmodifiableList(widgets);

    /** The whole box, as laid out by the theme. */
    public final Rect frame = new Rect();
    /** The scrollable, clipped area holding the rows. */
    public final Rect content = new Rect();

    double scroll;
    double contentHeight;
    /** Height counted in {@link #contentHeight} that rows don't draw into (the theme's row offset), so it never scrolls. */
    double slack;
    /** Extra height below {@link #content} that is still drawn (see GroupMetrics#clipBelow). */
    double clipBelow;

    GroupView(GroupNode group) {
        this.group = group;
    }

    public GroupNode getGroup() {
        return group;
    }

    public String getLabel() {
        return group.getLabel();
    }

    public List<Widget> getWidgets() {
        return widgetsView;
    }

    void add(Widget widget) {
        widgets.add(widget);
    }

    /** Scroll offset in design units (0 = top). */
    public double getScroll() {
        return scroll;
    }

    /** Height of all rows. */
    public double getContentHeight() {
        return contentHeight;
    }

    public boolean isScrollable() {
        return contentHeight - slack > content.h + 0.5;
    }

    void scrollBy(double amount) {
        scroll = Math.max(0, Math.min(Math.max(0, contentHeight - slack - content.h), scroll + amount));
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/ui/Menu.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.ui;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.Icarus;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.IcarusMod;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.config.UiPrefs;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.input.Keys;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Platform;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.Canvas;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.FrameRenderer;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.GroupNode;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.PageNode;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.Setting;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.SubPageNode;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.theme.ConfigUiStyle;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.theme.Theme;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.popup.ContextPopup;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.popup.Popup;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.ButtonWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.ColorWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.FieldWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.KeybindWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.ListWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.Widget;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.IdentityHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * The menu window of one mod. Each frame runs in a fixed order so nothing ever lags or overlaps wrongly:
 * <ol>
 *     <li>input: window drag and captured widgets (e.g. a slider) update from the mouse</li>
 *     <li>layout: the theme positions the window, tabs, groups and every widget</li>
 *     <li>hover is resolved against that layout</li>
 *     <li>paint: chrome, then each group (frame, clipped widgets, foreground), then the one open popup last</li>
 * </ol>
 * Clicks go to the open popup first, then the widget under the cursor, then tabs, then the drag area.
 * Keyboard input goes only to the focused widget.
 */
public final class Menu implements MenuController {
    /** Design units of the window that must stay on screen when dragging. */
    private static final double KEEP_VISIBLE = 24;
    private static final double SCROLL_STEP = 24;

    private final IcarusMod mod;
    private final ConfigPanel configPanel;
    private Theme theme;

    private final List<TabView> tabs = new ArrayList<>();
    private final List<TabView> tabsView = Collections.unmodifiableList(tabs);
    private final Map<GroupNode, GroupView> groupViews = new IdentityHashMap<>();
    private int tab;
    private int subTab;

    /** Window frame; the theme sets its size, the menu its position. */
    public final Rect window = new Rect();
    /** Area that starts a window drag, set by the theme. */
    public final Rect dragArea = new Rect();
    /** Area the theme gives to groups (for reference by painters). */
    public final Rect content = new Rect();

    private Widget hovered, captured, focused;
    private Popup popup;
    private boolean popupCaptured;
    private boolean dragging;
    private double grabX, grabY;
    private boolean positioned;

    private double mouseX, mouseY;
    private double viewWidth, viewHeight;
    /** Framebuffer pixels kept free around a window that had to shrink to fit. */
    private static final double FIT_MARGIN = 8;
    /** View size the window was last kept on screen for; -1 forces a check (on open). */
    private double clampedViewWidth = -1, clampedViewHeight = -1;
    private long lastFrame;
    private float dt;

    private String footerLeft = "", footerCenter = "", footerRight = "";
    private String title;

    private final FrameRenderer frame = new FrameRenderer();
    private final Canvas layoutCanvas = new Canvas();
    private final Runnable paintPass = this::paint;

    /** Open/close fade: ease-out over {@link #FADE_NANOS}; reversed when closing. */
    private static final long FADE_NANOS = 150_000_000L;
    private long fadeStart;
    private boolean closing;

    public Menu(IcarusMod mod, Theme theme, ConfigPanel configPanel) {
        this.mod = mod;
        this.theme = theme;
        this.configPanel = configPanel;
        this.title = mod.getModid().toUpperCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT);
        rebuild();
        restorePrefs(mod.getConfigStore().getPrefs());
    }

    /* ---- structure ---- */

    private void rebuild() {
        tabs.clear();
        GroupView extra = null;
        boolean pageStyle = theme.getConfigUiStyle() == ConfigUiStyle.PAGE;
        if (configPanel != null) extra = groupView(configPanel.group(pageStyle));

        boolean merged = false;
        for (PageNode page : mod.getModel().getPages()) {
            TabView tabView = new TabView(page, page.getLabel(), page.getIcon());
            //a page with the config icon hosts the built-in config UI (first, in the first column) instead of a tab of its own
            boolean host = extra != null && pageStyle && !merged && "config".equals(page.getIconName());
            for (SubPageNode sub : page.getSubPages()) {
                SubTabView subView = new SubTabView(sub, sub.getLabel());
                if (host) {
                    extra.getGroup().column(0);
                    subView.groups.add(extra);
                    host = false;
                    merged = true;
                }
                for (GroupNode group : sub.getGroups()) subView.groups.add(groupView(group));
                if (extra != null && !pageStyle) subView.groups.add(extra);
                tabView.subTabs.add(subView);
            }
            tabs.add(tabView);
        }
        if (extra != null && pageStyle && !merged) {
            TabView configTab = new TabView(null, extra.getLabel(), theme.getConfigIcon());
            SubTabView sub = new SubTabView(null, extra.getLabel());
            sub.groups.add(extra);
            configTab.subTabs.add(sub);
            tabs.add(configTab);
        }
        if (tabs.isEmpty()) {
            TabView empty = new TabView(null, "", ' ');
            empty.subTabs.add(new SubTabView(null, ""));
            tabs.add(empty);
        }
        tab = Math.min(tab, tabs.size() - 1);
        subTab = Math.min(subTab, tabs.get(tab).subTabs.size() - 1);
    }

    private GroupView groupView(GroupNode group) {
        GroupView view = groupViews.get(group);
        if (view != null) return view;
        view = new GroupView(group);
        for (Setting<?> setting : group.getSettings()) {
            Widget widget = mod.createWidget(this, setting);
            if (widget == null) continue;
            widget.setGroup(view);
            if (setting.isBindable()) {
                KeybindWidget keybind = new KeybindWidget(this, setting, mod.getKeybinds(), widget);
                keybind.setGroup(view);
                widget.setKeybind(keybind);
            }
            if (configPanel != null && configPanel.owns(group) && widget instanceof ButtonWidget && setting.getName().startsWith("Save")) {
                String base = setting.getLabel();
                ((ButtonWidget) widget).setLabelSource(() -> configPanel.saveLabel(base));
            }
            view.add(widget);
        }
        groupViews.put(group, view);
        return view;
    }

    private void restorePrefs(UiPrefs prefs) {
        if (prefs.hasPosition()) {
            window.x = prefs.getX();
            window.y = prefs.getY();
            positioned = true;
        }
        if (prefs.getPage() != null) {
            for (int i = 0; i < tabs.size(); i++) {
                TabView t = tabs.get(i);
                String name = t.getPage() == null ? "" : t.getPage().getName();
                if (!name.equals(prefs.getPage())) continue;
                tab = i;
                for (int j = 0; j < t.subTabs.size(); j++) {
                    SubPageNode sub = t.subTabs.get(j).getSubPage();
                    if (sub != null && sub.getName() != null && sub.getName().equals(prefs.getSubPage())) subTab = j;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    public IcarusMod getMod() {
        return mod;
    }

    public Theme getTheme() {
        return theme;
    }

    /** Switches theme (rebuilds tabs, since themes place the config UI differently). */
    public void setTheme(Theme theme) {
        if (theme == this.theme) return;
        closePopup();
        focus(null);
        this.theme = theme;
        rebuild();
    }

    public List<TabView> getTabs() {
        return tabsView;
    }

    public int getTabIndex() {
        return tab;
    }

    public TabView getCurrentTab() {
        return tabs.get(tab);
    }

    public int getSubTabIndex() {
        return subTab;
    }

    public SubTabView getCurrentSubTab() {
        return getCurrentTab().subTabs.get(subTab);
    }

    public void selectTab(int index) {
        if (index < 0 || index >= tabs.size() || index == tab) return;
        closePopup();
        focus(null);
        tab = index;
        subTab = 0;
        rememberPage();
    }

    public void selectSubTab(int index) {
        if (index < 0 || index >= getCurrentTab().subTabs.size() || index == subTab) return;
        closePopup();
        focus(null);
        subTab = index;
        rememberPage();
    }

    private void rememberPage() {
        TabView t = getCurrentTab();
        SubPageNode sub = getCurrentSubTab().getSubPage();
        mod.getConfigStore().setPage(t.getPage() == null ? "" : t.getPage().getName(), sub == null ? null : sub.getName());
    }

    /* ---- title and footer ---- */

    /** Window title for themes that show one (the mod id in capitals by default). */
    public String getTitle() {
        return title;
    }

    public void setTitle(String title) {
        this.title = title == null ? "" : title;
    }

    public void setFooter(String left, String center, String right) {
        footerLeft = left == null ? "" : left;
        footerCenter = center == null ? "" : center;
        footerRight = right == null ? "" : right;
    }

    public String getFooterLeft() {
        return footerLeft;
    }

    public String getFooterCenter() {
        return footerCenter;
    }

    public String getFooterRight() {
        return footerRight;
    }

    /* ---- state for themes ---- */

    /** Accent colour (ARGB). */
    public int getAccent() {
        return mod.getAccent();
    }

    public double getMouseX() {
        return mouseX;
    }

    public double getMouseY() {
        return mouseY;
    }

    /** Seconds since the last frame (capped), for animations. */
    public float getDeltaTime() {
        return dt;
    }

    public Popup getPopup() {
        return popup;
    }

    public Widget getFocused() {
        return focused;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isTyping() {
        return !closing && (focused instanceof FieldWidget || focused instanceof ListWidget);
    }

    public Widget getHovered() {
        return hovered;
    }

    public boolean isDragging() {
        return dragging;
    }

    /** Whether the user is mid-interaction (mouse held on something, or typing), so auto-save waits. */
    public boolean isInteracting() {
        return captured != null || popupCaptured || dragging || focused != null;
    }

    /** Whether there are unsaved config changes (profile mode). */
    public boolean hasUnsavedChanges() {
        return mod.getConfigStore().isProfileMode() && mod.getConfigStore().hasUnsavedChanges();
    }

    /* ---- popups and focus ---- */

    public void togglePopup(Popup p) {
        if (popup == p) {
            closePopup();
            return;
        }
        closePopup();
        focus(null);
        popup = p;
        p.resetAnimation();
    }

    /** Opens a right-click menu at the given point (design units), replacing any open popup. */
    public void openContext(ContextPopup context, double x, double y) {
        closePopup();
        focus(null);
        context.anchorX = x;
        context.anchorY = y;
        popup = context;
        context.resetAnimation();
    }

    public void closePopup() {
        if (popup == null) return;
        if (popupCaptured) popup.mouseReleased(mouseX, mouseY, 0);
        popupCaptured = false;
        popup = null;
    }

    /** Gives keyboard focus to a widget (null clears it). The previous one is told it lost focus. */
    public void focus(Widget widget) {
        if (focused == widget) return;
        Widget old = focused;
        focused = widget;
        if (old != null) old.focusLost();
    }

    /** An interaction finished (release, unfocus, pick): lets auto-save write. */
    public void interactionEnded() {
        mod.interactionEnded();
    }

    /* ---- layout helpers for themes ---- */

    /**
     * Lays out the current sub-tab's groups in {@code columns} columns inside {@code area}. Groups go to their
     * {@code column} hint (the last column for the config panel) or the shortest column; columns are filled
     * top-down, pinned groups stack at the bottom, and spare height is shared out. Groups that don't fit scroll.
     */
    public void layoutColumns(Canvas canvas, Rect area, int columns, double gap, GroupMetrics metrics) {
        layoutColumns(canvas, area, columns, gap, metrics, null);
    }

    /** Like {@link #layoutColumns(Canvas, Rect, int, double, GroupMetrics)}, with column {@code i} starting {@code topOffsets[i]} lower. */
    public void layoutColumns(Canvas canvas, Rect area, int columns, double gap, GroupMetrics metrics, double[] topOffsets) {
        content.set(area);
        List<GroupView> groups = getCurrentSubTab().groups;
        columns = Math.max(1, columns);
        double columnWidth = (area.w - gap * (columns - 1)) / columns;

        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
        List<GroupView>[] perColumn = new List[columns];
        double[] used = new double[columns];
        for (int c = 0; c < columns; c++) perColumn[c] = new ArrayList<>();

        double innerWidth = columnWidth - metrics.padLeft - metrics.padRight;
        for (GroupView g : groups) {
            g.contentHeight = metrics.rowOffset + measureRows(canvas, g, innerWidth, metrics.rowGap);
            g.slack = metrics.rowOffset; //rows start that far down but also end that far above their height
            g.clipBelow = metrics.clipBelow;
            int hint = g.getGroup().getColumn();
            int column;
            if (ConfigPanel.isLastColumn(hint)) column = columns - 1;
            else if (hint >= 0) column = Math.min(hint, columns - 1);
            else {
                column = 0;
                for (int c = 1; c < columns; c++) if (used[c] < used[column]) column = c;
            }
            perColumn[column].add(g);
            used[column] += natural(g, metrics) + gap;
        }

        for (int c = 0; c < columns; c++) {
            double x = area.x + c * (columnWidth + gap);
            double offset = topOffsets != null && c < topOffsets.length ? topOffsets[c] : 0;
            placeColumn(canvas, perColumn[c], x, area.y + offset, columnWidth, area.h - offset, metrics.stackGap >= 0 ? metrics.stackGap : gap, metrics);
        }
    }

    private double natural(GroupView g, GroupMetrics m) {
        double fixed = g.getGroup().getHeight();
        if (fixed > 0) return fixed;
        return Math.max(m.minHeight, m.padTop + g.contentHeight + m.padBottom);
    }

    private static boolean grows(GroupView g) {
        return !g.getGroup().isPinBottom() && g.getGroup().getHeight() <= 0;
    }

    private void placeColumn(Canvas canvas, List<GroupView> column, double x, double y, double width, double height, double gap, GroupMetrics m) {
        if (column.isEmpty()) return;
        List<GroupView> top = new ArrayList<>(), bottom = new ArrayList<>();
        for (GroupView g : column) (g.getGroup().isPinBottom() ? bottom : top).add(g);

        double gaps = gap * (column.size() - 1);
        double total = 0;
        for (GroupView g : column) total += natural(g, m);
        double available = height - gaps;

        double[] heights = new double[column.size()];
        if (total <= available) {
            double extra = available - total;
            int growers = 0;
            for (GroupView g : column) if (grows(g)) growers++;
            for (int i = 0; i < column.size(); i++) {
                GroupView g = column.get(i);
                double h = natural(g, m);
                if (grows(g) && growers > 0) h += extra / growers;
                heights[i] = h;
            }
        } else {
            //fixed-height groups keep their size; the others share what's left in proportion and scroll
            double fixedTotal = 0, flexTotal = 0;
            for (GroupView g : column) {
                if (g.getGroup().getHeight() > 0) fixedTotal += g.getGroup().getHeight();
                else flexTotal += natural(g, m);
            }
            double flexSpace = Math.max(0, available - fixedTotal);
            for (int i = 0; i < column.size(); i++) {
                GroupView g = column.get(i);
                double fixed = g.getGroup().getHeight();
                heights[i] = fixed > 0 ? fixed : Math.max(m.minHeight, flexTotal > 0 ? natural(g, m) * flexSpace / flexTotal : 0);
            }
        }

        double cursor = y;
        for (int i = 0; i < column.size(); i++) {
            GroupView g = column.get(i);
            if (g.getGroup().isPinBottom()) continue;
            g.frame.set(x, cursor, width, heights[i]);
            cursor += heights[i] + gap;
        }
        double bottomCursor = y + height;
        for (int i = column.size() - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            GroupView g = column.get(i);
            if (!g.getGroup().isPinBottom()) continue;
            bottomCursor -= heights[i];
            g.frame.set(x, bottomCursor, width, heights[i]);
            bottomCursor -= gap;
        }

        for (GroupView g : column) {
            g.content.set(g.frame.x + m.padLeft, g.frame.y + m.padTop, g.frame.w - m.padLeft - m.padRight, g.frame.h - m.padTop - m.padBottom);
            g.scrollBy(0); //clamp to the new size
            positionRows(canvas, g, m);
        }
    }

    private double measureRows(Canvas canvas, GroupView g, double width, double rowGap) {
        return stackRows(canvas, g, 0, 0, width, rowGap);
    }

    private void positionRows(Canvas canvas, GroupView g, GroupMetrics m) {
        stackRows(canvas, g, g.content.x, g.content.y + m.rowOffset - g.scroll, g.content.w, m.rowGap);
    }

    /** Lays rows out top-down from {@code y}; returns the total height. */
    private double stackRows(Canvas canvas, GroupView g, double x, double y, double width, double rowGap) {
        double cursor = y;
        boolean first = true;
        Widget row = null;
        int attached = 0;
        boolean rowHidden = false;
        for (Widget w : g.getWidgets()) {
            if (row != null && w instanceof ColorWidget && ((ColorWidget) w).isAttached()) {
                //an attached swatch follows its row, also when the row is hidden
                w.hidden = rowHidden || !w.getSetting().isVisible();
                if (!w.hidden) theme.layoutAttachedColor(canvas, (ColorWidget) w, row, attached++);
                continue;
            }
            attached = 0;
            rowHidden = w.hidden = !w.getSetting().isVisible();
            if (w.hidden) {
                row = w; //its attached swatches hide with it
                continue;
            }
            double rowY = first ? cursor : cursor + rowGap;
            double h = w.layout(theme, canvas, x, rowY, width);
            if (h <= 0) continue;
            cursor = rowY + h;
            first = false;
            row = w;
        }
        return cursor - y;
    }

    /* ---- MenuController ---- */

    @Override
    public void opened() {
        mod.setMenuOpen(true);
        if (closing || fadeStart == 0) fadeStart = System.nanoTime(); //a resize re-opens without fading again
        closing = false;
        clampedViewWidth = clampedViewHeight = -1;
        if (configPanel != null) configPanel.refresh();
        lastFrame = 0;
    }

    @Override
    public void render(double guiX, double guiY, float partialTick) {
        Platform platform = Icarus.getPlatform();
        double guiScale = platform.getGuiScale();
        int fbWidth = platform.getFramebufferWidth(), fbHeight = platform.getFramebufferHeight();
        double scale = fitScale(guiScale, fbWidth, fbHeight);
        viewWidth = fbWidth / scale;
        viewHeight = fbHeight / scale;
        mouseX = guiX * guiScale / scale;
        mouseY = guiY * guiScale / scale;

        long now = System.nanoTime();
        dt = lastFrame == 0 ? 0 : Math.min(0.1f, (now - lastFrame) / 1e9f);
        lastFrame = now;

        //1. input that moves things
        if (dragging) {
            window.x = mouseX - grabX;
            window.y = mouseY - grabY;
            clampWindow();
        }
        if (captured != null) captured.mouseDragged(mouseX, mouseY);
        if (popup != null && popupCaptured) popup.mouseDragged(mouseX, mouseY);

        //2. layout
        layoutCanvas.setup(Icarus.getAtlas(), scale);
        theme.layout(this, layoutCanvas);
        boolean shrunk = scale < mod.renderScale(guiScale);
        if (!positioned || shrunk) { //a window shrunk to fit fills the screen, so it stays centred
            window.x = Math.round((viewWidth - window.w) / 2);
            window.y = Math.round((viewHeight - window.h) / 2);
            positioned = true;
            theme.layout(this, layoutCanvas);
        } else if (viewWidth != clampedViewWidth || viewHeight != clampedViewHeight ? keepWholeWindowVisible() : clampWindow()) {
            theme.layout(this, layoutCanvas);
        }
        clampedViewWidth = viewWidth;
        clampedViewHeight = viewHeight;
        if (popup != null) popup.layout(theme, layoutCanvas);

        //3. hover
        Widget hit = popup != null && popup.contains(mouseX, mouseY) ? null : hitWidget(mouseX, mouseY);
        if (hovered != hit) {
            if (hovered != null) hovered.setHovered(false);
            hovered = hit;
            if (hovered != null) hovered.setHovered(true);
        }
        for (GroupView g : getCurrentSubTab().groups) {
            for (Widget w : g.getWidgets()) {
                w.animate(dt);
                if (w.getKeybind() != null) w.getKeybind().animate(dt);
                if (w instanceof ListWidget) ((ListWidget) w).hover(mouseX, mouseY);
            }
        }
        if (popup != null) {
            popup.hover(mouseX, mouseY);
            popup.animate(dt);
        }

        //4. paint, then the toasts on top
        frame.render(platform.getRenderer(), Icarus.getAtlas(), scale, fbWidth, fbHeight, paintPass);
        mod.renderOverMenu(partialTick);
    }

    /**
     * On open and after a resize, a window that fits is moved fully on screen (a saved position may come from a
     * bigger screen); dragging may still leave part of it off screen. Returns whether it moved.
     */
    private boolean keepWholeWindowVisible() {
        if (window.w <= 0 || viewWidth <= 0 || window.w > viewWidth || window.h > viewHeight) return clampWindow();
        double x = Math.max(0, Math.min(viewWidth - window.w, window.x));
        double y = Math.max(0, Math.min(viewHeight - window.h, window.y));
        boolean moved = x != window.x || y != window.y;
        window.x = x;
        window.y = y;
        return moved;
    }

    /** Keeps part of the window on screen; returns whether it moved. */
    private boolean clampWindow() {
        if (window.w <= 0 || viewWidth <= 0) return false;
        double x = Math.max(KEEP_VISIBLE - window.w, Math.min(viewWidth - KEEP_VISIBLE, window.x));
        double y = Math.max(0, Math.min(viewHeight - KEEP_VISIBLE, window.y));
        boolean moved = x != window.x || y != window.y;
        window.x = x;
        window.y = y;
        return moved;
    }

    private void paint() {
        Canvas canvas = frame.getCanvas();
        canvas.setAlpha(fadeAlpha());
        theme.paintWindow(canvas, this);
        for (GroupView g : getCurrentSubTab().groups) {
            if (g.frame.isEmpty()) continue;
            theme.paintGroup(canvas, this, g);
            canvas.pushClip(g.content.x, g.content.y, g.content.w, g.content.h + g.clipBelow);
            for (Widget w : g.getWidgets()) {
                if (w.hidden || w.bounds.bottom() < g.content.y || w.bounds.y > g.content.bottom() + g.clipBelow) continue;
                w.paint(theme, canvas);
                if (w.getKeybind() != null) w.getKeybind().paint(theme, canvas);
            }
            canvas.popClip();
            theme.paintGroupForeground(canvas, this, g);
        }
        if (popup != null) {
            canvas.pushClip(0, 0, viewWidth, viewHeight);
            popup.paint(theme, canvas);
            canvas.popClip();
        }
        canvas.setAlpha(1);
    }

    private double fadeProgress() {
        if (fadeStart == 0) return 1;
        return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (System.nanoTime() - fadeStart) / (double) FADE_NANOS));
    }

    /** The whole menu's opacity: eased in after opening, out after {@link #requestClose()}. */
    public double fadeAlpha() {
        double t = fadeProgress();
        double eased = 1 - (1 - t) * (1 - t) * (1 - t);
        return closing ? 1 - eased : eased;
    }

    @Override
    public void requestClose() {
        if (closing) return;
        //reverse from wherever the fade-in got to
        double shown = fadeAlpha();
        closing = true;
        double t = 1 - Math.cbrt(shown); //the closing progress whose eased value leaves "shown" visible
        fadeStart = System.nanoTime() - (long) (t * FADE_NANOS);
        closePopup();
        focus(null);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isFadedOut() {
        return closing && fadeProgress() >= 1;
    }

    /** Whether the menu is fading out (input is ignored). */
    public boolean isClosing() {
        return closing;
    }

    /** Shows the menu fully at once (no fade), e.g. for snapshots. */
    public void skipFade() {
        fadeStart = 0;
        closing = false;
    }

    private Widget hitWidget(double x, double y) {
        for (GroupView g : getCurrentSubTab().groups) {
            if (!g.content.contains(x, y)) continue;
            //last first: a swatch attached to a row sits on top of that row's widget
            List<Widget> widgets = g.getWidgets();
            for (int i = widgets.size() - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
                Widget w = widgets.get(i);
                if (w.hidden || w.getSetting().isDisabled()) continue;
                KeybindWidget keybind = w.getKeybind();
                if (keybind != null && keybind.contains(x, y)) return keybind;
                if (w.contains(x, y)) return w;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    private GroupView groupAt(double x, double y) {
        for (GroupView g : getCurrentSubTab().groups) if (g.frame.contains(x, y)) return g;
        return null;
    }

    @Override
    public void mousePressed(double guiX, double guiY, int button) {
        updateMouse(guiX, guiY);
        if (closing) return;
        double x = mouseX, y = mouseY;

        if (focused instanceof KeybindWidget && ((KeybindWidget) focused).bindMouse(button)) return;

        if (popup != null) {
            if (popup.contains(x, y)) {
                popupCaptured = popup.mousePressed(x, y, button);
            } else {
                closePopup();
            }
            return;
        }

        Widget target = hitWidget(x, y);
        if (focused != null && focused != target) focus(null);

        if (target != null) {
            if (target.mousePressed(x, y, button)) captured = target;
            return;
        }

        if (button == 0) {
            List<TabView> tabList = tabs;
            for (int i = 0; i < tabList.size(); i++) {
                if (tabList.get(i).bounds.contains(x, y)) {
                    selectTab(i);
                    return;
                }
            }
            List<SubTabView> subs = getCurrentTab().subTabs;
            for (int i = 0; i < subs.size(); i++) {
                if (subs.get(i).bounds.contains(x, y)) {
                    selectSubTab(i);
                    return;
                }
            }
            if (dragArea.contains(x, y)) {
                dragging = true;
                grabX = x - window.x;
                grabY = y - window.y;
            }
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void mouseReleased(double guiX, double guiY, int button) {
        updateMouse(guiX, guiY);
        if (dragging) {
            dragging = false;
            mod.getConfigStore().setPosition(window.x, window.y);
        }
        if (captured != null) {
            captured.mouseReleased(mouseX, mouseY, button);
            captured = null;
            interactionEnded();
        }
        if (popup != null && popupCaptured) {
            popup.mouseReleased(mouseX, mouseY, button);
            popupCaptured = false;
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void mouseScrolled(double guiX, double guiY, double amount) {
        updateMouse(guiX, guiY);
        if (popup != null) {
            if (popup.contains(mouseX, mouseY)) popup.scroll(amount);
            return;
        }
        Widget target = hitWidget(mouseX, mouseY);
        if (target != null && target.scroll(amount)) return;
        GroupView g = groupAt(mouseX, mouseY);
        if (g != null) g.scrollBy(-amount * SCROLL_STEP);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean keyPressed(int key) {
        if (closing) return true;
        if (key == Keys.KEY_ESCAPE && popup != null) {
            closePopup();
            return true;
        }
        if (focused != null && focused.keyPressed(key)) return true;
        //the menu key toggles, like Escape; not while it fades in, since the press that opened the menu can reach
        //the new screen too on some versions
        boolean fadedIn = fadeStart == 0 || System.nanoTime() - fadeStart > FADE_NANOS;
        if (key == mod.getMenuKey() && !isTyping() && fadedIn) {
            closePopup();
            requestClose();
            return true;
        }
        return focused != null;
    }

    @Override
    public void charTyped(int codePoint) {
        if (focused != null) focused.charTyped(codePoint);
    }

    @Override
    public void closed() {
        mod.setMenuOpen(false);
        fadeStart = 0; //the next open fades in again
        closing = false;
        closePopup();
        focus(null);
        if (captured != null) {
            captured.mouseReleased(mouseX, mouseY, 0);
            captured = null;
        }
        if (dragging) {
            dragging = false;
            mod.getConfigStore().setPosition(window.x, window.y);
        }
        mod.menuClosed();
    }

    /**
     * The mod's render scale, shrunk when the window wouldn't fit the screen at it (e.g. 720p at the default GUI
     * scale), keeping a small margin. Uses the window size from the previous layout.
     */
    private double fitScale(double guiScale, int fbWidth, int fbHeight) {
        double scale = mod.renderScale(guiScale);
        if (window.w > 0 && window.h > 0) {
            double fit = Math.min((fbWidth - 2 * FIT_MARGIN) / window.w, (fbHeight - 2 * FIT_MARGIN) / window.h);
            if (fit > 0 && fit < scale) scale = fit;
        }
        return scale;
    }

    private void updateMouse(double guiX, double guiY) {
        Platform platform = Icarus.getPlatform();
        double guiScale = platform.getGuiScale();
        double scale = fitScale(guiScale, platform.getFramebufferWidth(), platform.getFramebufferHeight());
        mouseX = guiX * guiScale / scale;
        mouseY = guiY * guiScale / scale;
    }

    /** Visible area in design units (valid after the first frame). */
    public double getViewWidth() {
        return viewWidth;
    }

    public double getViewHeight() {
        return viewHeight;
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/ui/MenuController.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.ui;

/**
 * What a platform's menu screen forwards to Icarus. Coordinates are Minecraft GUI units; key codes are
 * {@link studio.dreamys.icarus.input.Keys} codes; mouse buttons are numbered like LWJGL/GLFW (0 left, 1 right,
 * 2 middle).
 */
public interface MenuController {
    /** The screen was opened (or resized). */
    void opened();

    /** Draws the menu. Called once per frame from the screen's render method. */
    void render(double mouseX, double mouseY, float partialTick);

    void mousePressed(double mouseX, double mouseY, int button);

    void mouseReleased(double mouseX, double mouseY, int button);

    /** {@code amount} > 0 scrolls up. */
    void mouseScrolled(double mouseX, double mouseY, double amount);

    /**
     * A key was pressed. Returns whether the menu used it; for Escape, a screen closes only if this returns false
     * (Escape first closes popups, cancels keybind capture or unfocuses a text field).
     */
    boolean keyPressed(int key);

    /** A character was typed (any Unicode code point). */
    void charTyped(int codePoint);

    /**
     * Escape found nothing left to dismiss: the menu starts fading out, ignoring input meanwhile. The screen closes
     * itself once {@link #isFadedOut()} says so (checked after each {@link #render}).
     */
    default void requestClose() {
    }

    /** Whether a requested close has finished fading, so the screen can go. */
    default boolean isFadedOut() {
        return true;
    }

    /**
     * Whether a text field (or list search) has keyboard focus. Platforms that only deliver typed characters while
     * OS text input is on (26.x, SDL) start it while this is true.
     */
    default boolean isTyping() {
        return false;
    }

    /** The screen was closed. */
    void closed();
}
```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/ui/Rect.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.ui;

/** A mutable rectangle in design units. Containment is half-open: {@code [x, x + w) × [y, y + h)}. */
public final class Rect {
    public double x, y, w, h;

    public Rect set(double x, double y, double w, double h) {
        this.x = x;
        this.y = y;
        this.w = Math.max(0, w);
        this.h = Math.max(0, h);
        return this;
    }

    public Rect set(Rect other) {
        return set(other.x, other.y, other.w, other.h);
    }

    public Rect clear() {
        return set(0, 0, 0, 0);
    }

    public boolean contains(double px, double py) {
        return w > 0 && h > 0 && px >= x && px < x + w && py >= y && py < y + h;
    }

    public boolean isEmpty() {
        return w <= 0 || h <= 0;
    }

    public double right() {
        return x + w;
    }

    public double bottom() {
        return y + h;
    }

    public double centerX() {
        return x + w / 2;
    }

    public double centerY() {
        return y + h / 2;
    }

    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return "Rect[" + x + ", " + y + ", " + w + "x" + h + "]";
    }
}

```

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.ui;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.SubPageNode;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;

/** A sub-tab (or the single implicit one) with the groups shown under it. */
public final class SubTabView {
    private final SubPageNode subPage;
    private final String label;
    final List<GroupView> groups = new ArrayList<>();
    private final List<GroupView> groupsView = Collections.unmodifiableList(groups);
    /** Sub-tab button, laid out by the theme (empty when the page has no sub-tabs). */
    public final Rect bounds = new Rect();

    SubTabView(SubPageNode subPage, String label) {
        this.subPage = subPage;
        this.label = label;
    }

    /** The model sub-page, or null for the config page. */
    public SubPageNode getSubPage() {
        return subPage;
    }

    public String getLabel() {
        return label;
    }

    public List<GroupView> getGroups() {
        return groupsView;
    }
}
```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/ui/TabView.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.ui;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.PageNode;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;

/** A top-level tab: a settings page, or the built-in config page. */
public final class TabView {
    private final PageNode page;
    private final String label;
    private final char icon;
    final List<SubTabView> subTabs = new ArrayList<>();
    private final List<SubTabView> subTabsView = Collections.unmodifiableList(subTabs);
    /** Tab button, laid out by the theme. */
    public final Rect bounds = new Rect();

    TabView(PageNode page, String label, char icon) {
        this.page = page;
        this.label = label;
        this.icon = icon;
    }

    /** The settings page, or null for the built-in config page. */
    public PageNode getPage() {
        return page;
    }

    public String getLabel() {
        return label;
    }

    public char getIcon() {
        return icon;
    }

    public List<SubTabView> getSubTabs() {
        return subTabsView;
    }

    /** Whether the theme should show a sub-tab row. */
    public boolean hasSubTabs() {
        return page != null && page.hasSubTabs();
    }
}
```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/ui/WidgetFactory.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.ui;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.Label;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.ListModel;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.Setting;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.SettingType;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.SettingTypes;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.ButtonWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.CheckboxWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.ChoiceWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.ColorWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.ComboWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.FieldWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.LabelWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.ListWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.SliderWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.Widget;

import java.awt.Color;
import java.util.IdentityHashMap;
import java.util.Map;

/** Creates the widget for a setting. Replace one per setting type with {@code IcarusMod#replaceWidget}. */
public interface WidgetFactory<T> {
    Widget create(Menu menu, Setting<T> setting);

    /** The built-in factory for each built-in type. */
    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    static Map<SettingType<?>, WidgetFactory<?>> defaults() {
        Map<SettingType<?>, WidgetFactory<?>> map = new IdentityHashMap<>();
        map.put(SettingTypes.BUTTON, (WidgetFactory<Runnable>) ButtonWidget::new);
        map.put(SettingTypes.CHECKBOX, (WidgetFactory<Boolean>) CheckboxWidget::new);
        map.put(SettingTypes.CHOICE, (WidgetFactory<String>) ChoiceWidget::new);
        map.put(SettingTypes.COMBO, (WidgetFactory<String[]>) ComboWidget::new);
        map.put(SettingTypes.FIELD, (WidgetFactory<String>) FieldWidget::new);
        map.put(SettingTypes.SLIDER, (WidgetFactory<Double>) SliderWidget::new);
        map.put(SettingTypes.COLOR, (WidgetFactory<Color>) ColorWidget::new);
        map.put(SettingTypes.LABEL, (WidgetFactory<Label>) LabelWidget::new);
        map.put(SettingTypes.LIST, (WidgetFactory<String>) (menu, setting) -> new ListWidget(menu, setting, (ListModel) setting.getExtra()));
        return map;
    }
}
```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/ui/popup/ColorPopup.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.popup;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.Canvas;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.theme.Theme;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.Rect;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.ColorWidget;

import java.awt.Color;

/** Saturation/value square, hue bar, alpha bar (and optional Copy/Paste buttons) for a {@link ColorWidget}. Edits are kept in HSV. */
public final class ColorPopup extends Popup {
    private enum Drag { NONE, SV, HUE, ALPHA }

    private final ColorWidget widget;
    /** Parts, set by the theme. */
    public final Rect svSquare = new Rect();
    public final Rect hueBar = new Rect();
    public final Rect alphaBar = new Rect();
    /** Copy/Paste buttons inside the picker, for themes that have them (empty otherwise). */
    public final Rect copyButton = new Rect();
    public final Rect pasteButton = new Rect();
    /** Set by the theme when its hue bar runs backwards (red, magenta, blue, ... from the start). */
    public boolean hueReversed;

    private float hue, saturation, brightness;
    private int alpha = 255;
    private Drag drag = Drag.NONE;

    public ColorPopup(ColorWidget widget) {
        super(widget);
        this.widget = widget;
    }

    public ColorWidget getWidget() {
        return widget;
    }

    /** Reads the current colour into the HSV state (keeps the hue when the colour is grey). */
    public void syncFromColor() {
        Color color = widget.getColor();
        float[] hsb = Color.RGBtoHSB(color.getRed(), color.getGreen(), color.getBlue(), null);
        if (hsb[1] > 0 && hsb[2] > 0) hue = hsb[0];
        saturation = hsb[1];
        brightness = hsb[2];
        alpha = color.getAlpha();
    }

    public float getHue() {
        return hue;
    }

    public float getSaturation() {
        return saturation;
    }

    public float getBrightness() {
        return brightness;
    }

    public int getAlpha() {
        return alpha;
    }

    /** ARGB of the fully saturated, bright current hue. */
    public int hueColor() {
        return 0xFF000000 | Color.HSBtoRGB(hue, 1, 1);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean mousePressed(double x, double y, int button) {
        if (button != 0) return false;
        if (copyButton.contains(x, y)) {
            widget.copy();
            return false;
        }
        if (pasteButton.contains(x, y)) {
            if (widget.paste()) syncFromColor();
            return false;
        }
        if (svSquare.contains(x, y)) drag = Drag.SV;
        else if (hueBar.contains(x, y)) drag = Drag.HUE;
        else if (alphaBar.contains(x, y)) drag = Drag.ALPHA;
        else return false;
        mouseDragged(x, y);
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public void mouseDragged(double x, double y) {
        switch (drag) {
            case SV:
                saturation = clamp((x - svSquare.x) / svSquare.w);
                brightness = 1 - clamp((y - svSquare.y) / svSquare.h);
                break;
            case HUE:
                //a wide bar runs left to right, a tall one top to bottom
                float along = hueBar.w > hueBar.h ? clamp((x - hueBar.x) / hueBar.w) : clamp((y - hueBar.y) / hueBar.h);
                hue = Math.min(0.9999f, hueReversed ? 1 - along : along);
                break;
            case ALPHA:
                alpha = Math.round(255 * clamp((x - alphaBar.x) / alphaBar.w));
                break;
            default:
                return;
        }
        int rgb = Color.HSBtoRGB(hue, saturation, brightness) & 0xFFFFFF;
        widget.setColor(new Color(alpha << 24 | rgb, true));
    }

    @Override
    public void mouseReleased(double x, double y, int button) {
        if (drag != Drag.NONE) widget.getMenu().interactionEnded();
        drag = Drag.NONE;
    }

    private static float clamp(double v) {
        return (float) Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v));
    }

    @Override
    public void layout(Theme theme, Canvas canvas) {
        theme.layoutColorPicker(canvas, this);
    }

    @Override
    public void paint(Theme theme, Canvas canvas) {
        theme.paintColorPicker(canvas, this);
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/ui/popup/ContextPopup.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.popup;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.Canvas;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.theme.Theme;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.Widget;

import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.function.IntConsumer;
import java.util.function.IntSupplier;

/**
 * A small right-click menu (e.g. a keybind's modes, or Copy/Paste on a colour). Opens where it was clicked;
 * picking an item runs the action and closes it.
 */
public final class ContextPopup extends Popup {
    private final List<String> items;
    private final IntSupplier selected;
    private final IntConsumer action;
    /** Where it was opened (design units); the theme anchors the popup here. */
    public double anchorX, anchorY;
    /** Row height, set by the theme. */
    public double itemHeight;
    private int hovered = -1;

    /**
     * @param selected index shown as current (e.g. the active mode), or a supplier returning -1 for none
     * @param action   receives the picked index
     */
    public ContextPopup(Widget owner, List<String> items, IntSupplier selected, IntConsumer action) {
        super(owner);
        this.items = Collections.unmodifiableList(items);
        this.selected = selected;
        this.action = action;
    }

    public ContextPopup(Widget owner, IntConsumer action, String... items) {
        this(owner, Arrays.asList(items), () -> -1, action);
    }

    public List<String> getItems() {
        return items;
    }

    /** The current item, or -1. */
    public int getSelected() {
        return selected.getAsInt();
    }

    public int getHovered() {
        return hovered;
    }

    private int indexAt(double y) {
        if (itemHeight <= 0) return -1;
        int index = (int) Math.floor((y - bounds.y) / itemHeight);
        return index >= 0 && index < items.size() ? index : -1;
    }

    @Override
    public void hover(double x, double y) {
        hovered = contains(x, y) ? indexAt(y) : -1;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean mousePressed(double x, double y, int button) {
        int index = indexAt(y);
        if (index < 0) return false;
        owner.getMenu().closePopup();
        action.accept(index);
        owner.getMenu().interactionEnded();
        return false;
    }

    @Override
    public void layout(Theme theme, Canvas canvas) {
        theme.layoutContext(canvas, this);
    }

    @Override
    public void paint(Theme theme, Canvas canvas) {
        theme.paintContext(canvas, this);
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/ui/popup/DropdownPopup.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.popup;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.Canvas;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.theme.Theme;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.Widget;

import java.util.List;

/** The option list of a Choice (single selection, closes on pick) or Combo (multi selection, stays open). */
public final class DropdownPopup extends Popup {
    /** What the list shows and how picks are applied. */
    public interface Source {
        List<String> getOptions();

        boolean isSelected(int index);

        /** Applies a pick; returns true if the popup should close. */
        boolean choose(int index);
    }

    private final Source source;
    /** Row height and visible rows, set by the theme. */
    public double itemHeight;
    /** Space above the first option, set by the theme. */
    public double padTop;
    public int visibleRows;
    private int scroll;
    private int hovered = -1;

    public DropdownPopup(Widget owner, Source source) {
        super(owner);
        this.source = source;
    }

    public Source getSource() {
        return source;
    }

    public List<String> getOptions() {
        return source.getOptions();
    }

    /** Index of the first visible option. */
    public int getScroll() {
        return scroll;
    }

    public int getHovered() {
        return hovered;
    }

    private int indexAt(double y) {
        if (itemHeight <= 0) return -1;
        int row = (int) Math.floor((y - bounds.y - padTop) / itemHeight);
        int index = scroll + row;
        return row >= 0 && row < visibleRows && index < getOptions().size() ? index : -1;
    }

    @Override
    public void hover(double x, double y) {
        hovered = contains(x, y) ? indexAt(y) : -1;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean mousePressed(double x, double y, int button) {
        if (button != 0) return false;
        int index = indexAt(y);
        if (index >= 0 && source.choose(index)) owner.getMenu().closePopup();
        return false;
    }

    @Override
    public void scroll(double amount) {
        int max = Math.max(0, getOptions().size() - visibleRows);
        scroll = Math.max(0, Math.min(max, scroll - (int) Math.signum(amount)));
    }

    @Override
    public void layout(Theme theme, Canvas canvas) {
        theme.layoutDropdown(canvas, this);
        scroll = Math.max(0, Math.min(Math.max(0, getOptions().size() - visibleRows), scroll));
    }

    @Override
    public void paint(Theme theme, Canvas canvas) {
        theme.paintDropdown(canvas, this);
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/ui/popup/Popup.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.popup;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.Canvas;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.theme.Theme;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.Rect;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.Widget;

/**
 * A floating panel opened by a widget (dropdown list, colour picker). At most one is open per menu; it's drawn
 * after everything else and gets mouse input first.
 */
public abstract class Popup {
    protected final Widget owner;
    /** Laid out by the theme. */
    public final Rect bounds = new Rect();
    private float open;

    protected Popup(Widget owner) {
        this.owner = owner;
    }

    public Widget getOwner() {
        return owner;
    }

    /** Opening animation, 0..1. */
    public float getOpen() {
        return open;
    }

    public void resetAnimation() {
        open = 0;
    }

    public void animate(float dt) {
        open = Math.min(1, open + dt * 12);
    }

    public boolean contains(double x, double y) {
        return bounds.contains(x, y);
    }

    /** Returns true to capture the mouse until release. */
    public boolean mousePressed(double x, double y, int button) {
        return false;
    }

    public void mouseDragged(double x, double y) {
    }

    public void mouseReleased(double x, double y, int button) {
    }

    public void scroll(double amount) {
    }

    /** Updates hover state from the mouse position (before painting). */
    public void hover(double x, double y) {
    }

    public abstract void layout(Theme theme, Canvas canvas);

    public abstract void paint(Theme theme, Canvas canvas);
}
```

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.Canvas;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.Setting;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.theme.Theme;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.Menu;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.Rect;

/** Runs the setting's {@link Runnable} when clicked. */
public final class ButtonWidget extends Widget {
    /** Supplies a label that can change (e.g. a "Save" button showing unsaved changes). */
    public interface LabelSource {
        String label();
    }

    private final Setting<Runnable> action;
    /** The button face, set during layout. */
    public final Rect button = new Rect();
    private boolean pressed;
    private LabelSource labelSource;

    public ButtonWidget(Menu menu, Setting<Runnable> setting) {
        super(menu, setting);
        this.action = setting;
    }

    public void setLabelSource(LabelSource labelSource) {
        this.labelSource = labelSource;
    }

    @Override
    public String getLabel() {
        return labelSource != null ? labelSource.label() : super.getLabel();
    }

    public boolean isPressed() {
        return pressed;
    }

    public void run() {
        Runnable runnable = action.get();
        if (runnable != null) runnable.run();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean contains(double x, double y) {
        return button.contains(x, y);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean mousePressed(double x, double y, int button) {
        if (button != 0) return false;
        pressed = true;
        run();
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public void mouseReleased(double x, double y, int button) {
        pressed = false;
    }

    @Override
    public double layout(Theme theme, Canvas canvas, double x, double y, double width) {
        return theme.layoutButton(canvas, this, x, y, width);
    }

    @Override
    public void paint(Theme theme, Canvas canvas) {
        theme.paintButton(canvas, this);
    }
}
```

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget;
```

```java
import java.util.List;

/** Picks one of a fixed set of options from a dropdown. */
public final class ChoiceWidget extends Widget implements DropdownPopup.Source {
    private final Setting<String> value;
    private final DropdownPopup popup;
    /** The closed dropdown box, set during layout. */
    public final Rect box = new Rect();

    public ChoiceWidget(Menu menu, Setting<String> setting) {
        super(menu, setting);
        this.value = setting;
        this.popup = new DropdownPopup(this, this);
    }

    public String getSelected() {
        return value.get();
    }

    public boolean isOpen() {
        return menu.getPopup() == popup;
    }

    @Override
    public List<String> getOptions() {
        return setting.getOptions();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isSelected(int index) {
        return getOptions().get(index).equals(value.get());
    }

    @Override
    public boolean choose(int index) {
        value.set(getOptions().get(index));
        menu.interactionEnded();
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean contains(double x, double y) {
        return box.contains(x, y);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean mousePressed(double x, double y, int button) {
        if (button != 0) return false;
        menu.togglePopup(popup);
        return false;
    }

    @Override
    public double layout(Theme theme, Canvas canvas, double x, double y, double width) {
        return theme.layoutChoice(canvas, this, x, y, width);
```
