`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/ui/widget/ColorWidget.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.Canvas;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.Setting;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.theme.Theme;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.Menu;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.Rect;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.Icarus;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.popup.ColorPopup;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.popup.ContextPopup;

import java.awt.Color;

/** A colour swatch that opens a colour picker. */
public final class ColorWidget extends Widget {
    private final Setting<Color> value;
    private final ColorPopup popup;
    private final ContextPopup context;
    /** The swatch, set during layout. */
    public final Rect swatch = new Rect();

    public ColorWidget(Menu menu, Setting<Color> setting) {
        super(menu, setting);
        this.value = setting;
        this.popup = new ColorPopup(this);
        this.context = new ContextPopup(this, index -> {
            if (index == 0) copy();
            else paste();
        }, "Copy", "Paste");
    }

    /** Puts the colour on the clipboard as {@code #RRGGBBAA}. */
    public void copy() {
        Icarus.getPlatform().setClipboard(format(getColor()));
    }

    /** Takes the colour from the clipboard; returns false (keeping the colour) if it isn't one. */
    public boolean paste() {
        Color pasted = parse(Icarus.getPlatform().getClipboard());
        if (pasted == null) return false;
        setColor(pasted);
        menu.interactionEnded();
        return true;
    }

    /** Clipboard form: {@code #RRGGBBAA}. */
    public static String format(Color color) {
        return String.format("#%02X%02X%02X%02X", color.getRed(), color.getGreen(), color.getBlue(), color.getAlpha());
    }

    /** Reads {@code #RRGGBB}, {@code #RRGGBBAA} (with or without '#'); null if it isn't a colour. */
    public static Color parse(String text) {
        if (text == null) return null;
        String hex = text.trim();
        if (hex.startsWith("#")) hex = hex.substring(1);
        if (hex.length() != 6 && hex.length() != 8) return null;
        try {
            long value = Long.parseLong(hex, 16);
            if (hex.length() == 6) return new Color((int) value);
            return new Color((int) (value >> 24 & 255), (int) (value >> 16 & 255), (int) (value >> 8 & 255), (int) (value & 255));
        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
            return null;
        }
    }

    /** Whether this swatch sits on the previous setting's row ({@code @IAttach}). */
    public boolean isAttached() {
        return setting.isAttached();
    }

    public Color getColor() {
        Color color = value.get();
        return color == null ? Color.WHITE : color;
    }

    public void setColor(Color color) {
        value.set(color);
    }

    public boolean isOpen() {
        return menu.getPopup() == popup;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean contains(double x, double y) {
        return swatch.contains(x, y);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean mousePressed(double x, double y, int button) {
        if (button == 1) {
            menu.openContext(context, x, y);
            return false;
        }
        if (button != 0) return false;
        if (!isOpen()) popup.syncFromColor();
        menu.togglePopup(popup);
        return false;
    }

    @Override
    public double layout(Theme theme, Canvas canvas, double x, double y, double width) {
        return theme.layoutColor(canvas, this, x, y, width);
    }

    @Override
    public void paint(Theme theme, Canvas canvas) {
        theme.paintColor(canvas, this);
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/ui/widget/ComboWidget.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.Canvas;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.Setting;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.theme.Theme;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.Menu;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.Rect;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.popup.DropdownPopup;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

/** Picks any number of a fixed set of options from a dropdown that stays open. */
public final class ComboWidget extends Widget implements DropdownPopup.Source {
    private final Setting<String[]> value;
    private final DropdownPopup popup;
    /** The closed dropdown box, set during layout. */
    public final Rect box = new Rect();

    private String[] cachedFor;
    private String summary;

    public ComboWidget(Menu menu, Setting<String[]> setting) {
        super(menu, setting);
        this.value = setting;
        this.popup = new DropdownPopup(this, this);
    }

    public boolean isOpen() {
        return menu.getPopup() == popup;
    }

    /** Selected options joined with ", " in option order, or "-" (cached until the selection changes). */
    public String getSummary() {
        String[] selected = value.get();
        if (selected != cachedFor || summary == null) {
            cachedFor = selected;
            StringBuilder text = new StringBuilder();
            for (String option : getOptions()) {
                if (contains(selected, option)) {
                    if (text.length() > 0) text.append(", ");
                    text.append(option);
                }
            }
            summary = text.length() == 0 ? "-" : text.toString();
        }
        return summary;
    }

    private static boolean contains(String[] values, String option) {
        if (values == null) return false;
        for (String v : values) if (option.equals(v)) return true;
        return false;
    }

    @Override
    public List<String> getOptions() {
        return setting.getOptions();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isSelected(int index) {
        return contains(value.get(), getOptions().get(index));
    }

    @Override
    public boolean choose(int index) {
        String option = getOptions().get(index);
        boolean selected = isSelected(index);
        List<String> next = new ArrayList<>();
        for (String o : getOptions()) {
            if (o.equals(option) ? !selected : contains(value.get(), o)) next.add(o);
        }
        value.set(next.toArray(new String[0]));
        menu.interactionEnded();
        return false;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean contains(double x, double y) {
        return box.contains(x, y);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean mousePressed(double x, double y, int button) {
        if (button != 0) return false;
        menu.togglePopup(popup);
        return false;
    }

    @Override
    public double layout(Theme theme, Canvas canvas, double x, double y, double width) {
        return theme.layoutCombo(canvas, this, x, y, width);
    }

    @Override
    public void paint(Theme theme, Canvas canvas) {
        theme.paintCombo(canvas, this);
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/ui/widget/FieldWidget.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.input.Keys;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.Canvas;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.Setting;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.theme.Theme;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.Menu;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.Rect;

/**
 * A single-line text field. Click to focus; typing appends any printable character up to the setting's maximum
 * length, Backspace deletes, Enter or Escape unfocuses. The text is saved when the field loses focus.
 */
public final class FieldWidget extends Widget {
    private final Setting<String> value;
    /** The text box, set during layout. */
    public final Rect box = new Rect();
    private long focusedAt;

    public FieldWidget(Menu menu, Setting<String> setting) {
        super(menu, setting);
        this.value = setting;
    }

    public String getText() {
        String text = value.get();
        return text == null ? "" : text;
    }

    public boolean isFocused() {
        return menu.getFocused() == this;
    }

    /** Whether the caret is in the visible half of its blink cycle. */
    public boolean isCaretVisible() {
        return isFocused() && ((System.nanoTime() - focusedAt) / 500_000_000L) % 2 == 0;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean contains(double x, double y) {
        return box.contains(x, y);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean mousePressed(double x, double y, int button) {
        if (button != 0) return false;
        menu.focus(this);
        focusedAt = System.nanoTime();
        return false;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean keyPressed(int key) {
        if (key == Keys.KEY_BACK) {
            String text = getText();
            if (!text.isEmpty()) {
                int cut = text.offsetByCodePoints(text.length(), -1);
                value.set(text.substring(0, cut));
            }
            focusedAt = System.nanoTime();
            return true;
        }
        if (key == Keys.KEY_RETURN || key == Keys.KEY_NUMPADENTER || key == Keys.KEY_ESCAPE) {
            menu.focus(null);
            return true;
        }
        return true; //swallow everything else while typing (so it can't trigger keybinds)
    }

    @Override
    public void charTyped(int codePoint) {
        if (Character.isISOControl(codePoint) || !Character.isDefined(codePoint)) return;
        String text = getText();
        if (text.codePointCount(0, text.length()) >= setting.getMaxLength()) return;
        value.set(text + new String(Character.toChars(codePoint)));
        focusedAt = System.nanoTime();
    }

    @Override
    public void focusLost() {
        menu.interactionEnded();
    }

    @Override
    public double layout(Theme theme, Canvas canvas, double x, double y, double width) {
        return theme.layoutField(canvas, this, x, y, width);
    }

    @Override
    public void paint(Theme theme, Canvas canvas) {
        theme.paintField(canvas, this);
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/ui/widget/KeybindWidget.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.input.Keys;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.Canvas;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.KeybindMode;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.Keybinds;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.SettingTypes;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.Setting;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.theme.Theme;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.Menu;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.Rect;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.popup.ContextPopup;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * The key bound to a button or checkbox, shown in its row. Click to capture: the next key binds it,
 * Escape cancels, Backspace/Delete clears, and the menu key is refused.
 */
public final class KeybindWidget extends Widget {
    private final Keybinds keybinds;
    private final Widget owner;
    private final ContextPopup modes;
    /** The clickable label, set during layout. */
    public final Rect label = new Rect();
    private boolean capturing;
    private long refusedAt;

    private int cachedKey = Integer.MIN_VALUE;
    private boolean cachedCapturing;
    private boolean cachedRefused;
    private Theme cachedTheme;
    private String cachedText;

    public KeybindWidget(Menu menu, Setting<?> setting, Keybinds keybinds, Widget owner) {
        super(menu, setting);
        this.keybinds = keybinds;
        this.owner = owner;
        List<String> labels = new ArrayList<>();
        for (KeybindMode mode : KeybindMode.values()) labels.add(mode.getLabel());
        this.modes = new ContextPopup(this, labels, () -> getMode().ordinal(), index -> keybinds.setMode(setting, KeybindMode.values()[index]));
    }

    /** Whether the binding has modes the user can pick (everything but buttons, which just run). */
    public boolean hasModes() {
        return setting.getType() != SettingTypes.BUTTON;
    }

    public KeybindMode getMode() {
        KeybindMode mode = keybinds.getMode(setting);
        return mode == null ? KeybindMode.TOGGLE : mode;
    }

    public Widget getOwner() {
        return owner;
    }

    public int getKey() {
        return keybinds.get(setting);
    }

    public boolean isCapturing() {
        return capturing;
    }

    /** Whether the last key was refused (it's the menu key) within the last second. */
    public boolean isRefused() {
        return System.nanoTime() - refusedAt < 1_000_000_000L;
    }

    /** The label text, formatted by the theme and cached until something changes. */
    public String getText(Theme theme) {
        int key = getKey();
        boolean refused = isRefused();
        if (key != cachedKey || capturing != cachedCapturing || refused != cachedRefused || theme != cachedTheme || cachedText == null) {
            cachedKey = key;
            cachedCapturing = capturing;
            cachedRefused = refused;
            cachedTheme = theme;
            cachedText = theme.formatKeybind(key, capturing, refused);
        }
        return cachedText;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean contains(double x, double y) {
        return label.contains(x, y);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean mousePressed(double x, double y, int button) {
        if (button == 1) {
            if (hasModes()) menu.openContext(modes, x, y);
            return false;
        }
        if (button != 0) return false;
        capturing = true;
        menu.focus(this);
        return false;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean keyPressed(int key) {
        if (!capturing) return false;
        if (key == Keys.KEY_ESCAPE) {
            //cancel, keeping the old binding
        } else if (key == Keys.KEY_BACK || key == Keys.KEY_DELETE) {
            keybinds.set(setting, Keys.KEY_NONE);
        } else if (key == menu.getMod().getMenuKey()) {
            refusedAt = System.nanoTime();
            return true;
        } else if (key > 0) {
            keybinds.set(setting, key);
        }
        menu.focus(null);
        return true;
    }

    /** While capturing: binds a mouse button (right, middle or side). */
    public boolean bindMouse(int button) {
        if (!capturing || button < 1) return false;
        keybinds.set(setting, Keys.mouse(button));
        menu.focus(null);
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public void focusLost() {
        capturing = false;
        menu.interactionEnded();
    }

    @Override
    public double layout(Theme theme, Canvas canvas, double x, double y, double width) {
        return 0; //laid out by its owner's row
    }

    @Override
    public void paint(Theme theme, Canvas canvas) {
        theme.paintKeybind(canvas, this);
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/ui/widget/ListWidget.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.input.Keys;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Locale;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.Canvas;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.ListModel;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.Setting;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.theme.Theme;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.Menu;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.Rect;

import java.util.List;

/** A scrollable list box; clicking an item selects it. */
public final class ListWidget extends Widget {
    private final Setting<String> selection;
    private final ListModel model;
    /** The list box, set during layout. */
    public final Rect box = new Rect();
    /** Row height and visible rows, set during layout. */
    public double itemHeight;
    public int visibleRows;
    private int scroll;
    private int hovered = -1;
    /** The search box when the model is searchable (else empty), set during layout. */
    public final Rect search = new Rect();
    private final StringBuilder query = new StringBuilder();
    private final List<String> filtered = new ArrayList<>();
    private String filteredFor;
    private int filteredVersion = -1;

    public ListWidget(Menu menu, Setting<String> setting, ListModel model) {
        super(menu, setting);
        this.selection = setting;
        this.model = model;
    }

    /** The items shown: all of them, or those matching the search text. */
    public List<String> getItems() {
        if (query.length() == 0) return model.getItems();
        String q = query.toString();
        if (!q.equals(filteredFor) || filteredVersion != model.getVersion()) {
            filtered.clear();
            String lower = q.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
            for (String item : model.getItems()) if (item.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).contains(lower)) filtered.add(item);
            filteredFor = q;
            filteredVersion = model.getVersion();
        }
        return filtered;
    }

    public ListModel getModel() {
        return model;
    }

    public boolean isSearchable() {
        return model.isSearchable();
    }

    /** The search text. */
    public String getQuery() {
        return query.toString();
    }

    public boolean isSearchFocused() {
        return menu.getFocused() == this;
    }

    public String getSelected() {
        return selection.get();
    }

    public int getScroll() {
        return scroll;
    }

    public int getHoveredIndex() {
        return hovered;
    }

    private int indexAt(double y) {
        if (itemHeight <= 0) return -1;
        int row = (int) Math.floor((y - box.y) / itemHeight);
        int index = scroll + row;
        return row >= 0 && row < visibleRows && index < getItems().size() ? index : -1;
    }

    /** Updates the hovered row (called by the menu every frame). */
    public void hover(double x, double y) {
        hovered = box.contains(x, y) ? indexAt(y) : -1;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean contains(double x, double y) {
        return box.contains(x, y) || search.contains(x, y);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean mousePressed(double x, double y, int button) {
        if (button != 0) return false;
        if (search.contains(x, y)) {
            menu.focus(this);
            return false;
        }
        int index = indexAt(y);
        if (index >= 0) {
            selection.set(getItems().get(index));
            menu.interactionEnded();
        }
        return false;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean keyPressed(int key) {
        if (key == Keys.KEY_BACK) {
            if (query.length() > 0) query.setLength(query.length() - 1);
            scroll = 0;
            return true;
        }
        if (key == Keys.KEY_ESCAPE || key == Keys.KEY_RETURN) {
            menu.focus(null);
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    @Override
    public void charTyped(int codePoint) {
        if (codePoint < 32 || query.length() >= 48) return;
        query.appendCodePoint(codePoint);
        scroll = 0;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean scroll(double amount) {
        int max = Math.max(0, getItems().size() - visibleRows);
        if (max == 0) return false;
        scroll = Math.max(0, Math.min(max, scroll - (int) Math.signum(amount)));
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public double layout(Theme theme, Canvas canvas, double x, double y, double width) {
        double height = theme.layoutList(canvas, this, x, y, width);
        scroll = Math.max(0, Math.min(Math.max(0, getItems().size() - visibleRows), scroll));
        return height;
    }

    @Override
    public void paint(Theme theme, Canvas canvas) {
        theme.paintList(canvas, this);
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/ui/widget/SliderWidget.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.Canvas;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.Setting;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.theme.Theme;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.Menu;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.Rect;

/** A numeric slider. Dragging sets the value from the mouse position before the frame is laid out. */
public final class SliderWidget extends Widget {
    /** Extra grab margin above and below the track, in design units. */
    public static final double GRAB_MARGIN = 4;

    private final Setting<Double> value;
    private final Setting.SliderSpec spec;
    /** The track, set during layout. */
    public final Rect track = new Rect();
    /** Optional -/+ buttons that step the value, set by themes that show them (else empty). */
    public final Rect minus = new Rect(), plus = new Rect();
    private boolean dragging;
    private double cachedValue = Double.NaN;
    private String cachedText;

    public SliderWidget(Menu menu, Setting<Double> setting) {
        super(menu, setting);
        this.value = setting;
        this.spec = setting.getSlider();
    }

    public Setting.SliderSpec getSpec() {
        return spec;
    }

    public double getValue() {
        Double v = value.get();
        return v == null ? spec.getMin() : v;
    }

    /** Filled fraction of the track, 0..1. */
    public double getFraction() {
        return spec.fraction(getValue());
    }

    /** The formatted value (cached until it changes). */
    public String getValueText() {
        double v = getValue();
        if (v != cachedValue || cachedText == null) {
            cachedValue = v;
            cachedText = spec.format(v);
        }
        return cachedText;
    }

    public boolean isDragging() {
        return dragging;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean contains(double x, double y) {
        if (minus.contains(x, y) || plus.contains(x, y)) return true;
        return x >= track.x && x < track.right() && y >= track.y - GRAB_MARGIN && y < track.bottom() + GRAB_MARGIN;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean mousePressed(double x, double y, int button) {
        if (button != 0) return false;
        if (minus.contains(x, y) || plus.contains(x, y)) {
            double step = spec.getStep() * (minus.contains(x, y) ? -1 : 1);
            value.set(spec.sanitize(getValue() + step));
            menu.interactionEnded();
            return false;
        }
        dragging = true;
        mouseDragged(x, y);
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public void mouseDragged(double x, double y) {
        if (!dragging || track.w <= 0) return;
        value.set(spec.valueAt((x - track.x) / track.w));
    }

    @Override
    public void mouseReleased(double x, double y, int button) {
        dragging = false;
    }

    @Override
    public double layout(Theme theme, Canvas canvas, double x, double y, double width) {
        return theme.layoutSlider(canvas, this, x, y, width);
    }

    @Override
    public void paint(Theme theme, Canvas canvas) {
        theme.paintSlider(canvas, this);
    }
}

```

`core/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/ui/widget/Widget.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.Canvas;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.Setting;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.theme.Theme;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.GroupView;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.Menu;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.Rect;

/**
 * A control bound to a setting. Widgets hold behaviour and state only; the active {@link Theme} lays them out
 * (filling {@link #bounds} and any part rectangles) and paints them, so swapping themes never touches widget code.
 */
public abstract class Widget {
    protected final Menu menu;
    protected final Setting<?> setting;
    /** The widget's whole row, set during layout. */
    public final Rect bounds = new Rect();

    private KeybindWidget keybind;
    private GroupView group;
    private boolean hovered;
    /** Set by the menu's layout: the row is hidden ({@code @IShowIf}) and takes no space, paint or input. */
    public boolean hidden;
    private float hover;

    protected Widget(Menu menu, Setting<?> setting) {
        this.menu = menu;
        this.setting = setting;
    }

    public Menu getMenu() {
        return menu;
    }

    public Setting<?> getSetting() {
        return setting;
    }

    public String getLabel() {
        return setting.getLabel();
    }

    public GroupView getGroup() {
        return group;
    }

    public void setGroup(GroupView group) {
        this.group = group;
    }

    /** The keybind shown in this widget's row, or null. */
    public KeybindWidget getKeybind() {
        return keybind;
    }

    public void setKeybind(KeybindWidget keybind) {
        this.keybind = keybind;
    }

    /** Whether the mouse is over this widget (topmost under the cursor). */
    public boolean isHovered() {
        return hovered;
    }

    /** Hover animation, 0 (not hovered) to 1. */
    public float getHover() {
        return hover;
    }

    public void setHovered(boolean hovered) {
        this.hovered = hovered;
    }

    /** Advances animations by {@code dt} seconds. */
    public void animate(float dt) {
        float target = hovered ? 1 : 0;
        float step = dt * 10;
        hover = hover < target ? Math.min(target, hover + step) : Math.max(target, hover - step);
    }

    /** Whether a point hits the widget's interactive area (by default the whole row). */
    public boolean contains(double x, double y) {
        return bounds.contains(x, y);
    }

    /** Returns true to capture the mouse until it's released (receiving {@link #mouseDragged}). */
    public boolean mousePressed(double x, double y, int button) {
        return false;
    }

    /** Called every frame while the widget has the mouse captured, before layout. */
    public void mouseDragged(double x, double y) {
    }

    public void mouseReleased(double x, double y, int button) {
    }

    /** Keys while focused. Returns whether the key was used. */
    public boolean keyPressed(int key) {
        return false;
    }

    public void charTyped(int codePoint) {
    }

    public void focusLost() {
    }

    /** Wheel over the widget; return true to stop it scrolling the group. */
    public boolean scroll(double amount) {
        return false;
    }

    /** Lays the widget out at the given row origin and width; returns the row height. */
    public abstract double layout(Theme theme, Canvas canvas, double x, double y, double width);

    public abstract void paint(Theme theme, Canvas canvas);
}
```

`core/src/test/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/KeybindModeTest.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.io.TempDir;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.input.Keys;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.KeybindMode;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.Setting;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.support.FakePlatform;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.support.Fixture;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.theme.Themes;

import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.Collections;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

class KeybindModeTest {
    @TempDir
    Path game;

    private IcarusMod mod;
    private Setting<?> enabled;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        Fixture.reset();
        Icarus.setPlatform(new FakePlatform(game.toFile()));
        mod = Icarus.createDetached("test", Themes.SKEET, IcarusOptions.builder().configUi(false),
                Collections.<Class<?>>singletonList(Fixture.Combat.class), game.resolve("config"), game.resolve("legacy"));
        for (Setting<?> s : mod.getModel().getSettings()) if (s.getName().equals("Enabled")) enabled = s;
    }

    @AfterEach
    void tearDown() {
        Icarus.setPlatform(null);
    }

    @Test
    void toggleFlipsOnEachPress() {
        assertEquals(KeybindMode.TOGGLE, mod.getKeybinds().getMode(enabled));
        mod.onKeyPressed(Keys.KEY_7);
        assertFalse(Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.Enabled);
        mod.onKeyReleased(Keys.KEY_7);
        assertFalse(Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.Enabled);
        mod.onKeyPressed(Keys.KEY_7);
        assertTrue(Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.Enabled);
    }

    @Test
    void onHotkeyFollowsTheKey() {
        mod.getKeybinds().setMode(enabled, KeybindMode.ON_HOTKEY);
        assertFalse(Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.Enabled, "picking the mode puts it at rest (off)");
        mod.onKeyPressed(Keys.KEY_7);
        assertTrue(Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.Enabled);
        mod.onKeyReleased(Keys.KEY_7);
        assertFalse(Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.Enabled);
    }

    @Test
    void offHotkeyAndAlwaysOn() {
        mod.getKeybinds().setMode(enabled, KeybindMode.OFF_HOTKEY);
        assertTrue(Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.Enabled);
        mod.onKeyPressed(Keys.KEY_7);
        assertFalse(Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.Enabled);
        mod.onKeyReleased(Keys.KEY_7);
        assertTrue(Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.Enabled);

        mod.getKeybinds().setMode(enabled, KeybindMode.ALWAYS_ON);
        mod.onKeyPressed(Keys.KEY_7);
        mod.onKeyReleased(Keys.KEY_7);
        assertTrue(Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.Enabled);
    }

    @Test
    void mouseButtonsAreKeys() {
        mod.getKeybinds().set(enabled, Keys.MOUSE_5);
        mod.getKeybinds().setMode(enabled, KeybindMode.ON_HOTKEY);
        mod.onKeyPressed(Keys.mouse(4));
        assertTrue(Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.Enabled);
        assertEquals("M5", Keys.getKeyName(Keys.MOUSE_5));
    }

    @Test
    void labelKeybindsReportTheirMode() {
        IcarusMod tall = Icarus.createDetached("tall", Themes.SKEET, IcarusOptions.builder().configUi(false),
                Collections.<Class<?>>singletonList(Fixture.Tall.class), game.resolve("config-tall"), game.resolve("legacy-tall"));
        Setting<?> label = tall.getModel().find(Fixture.Tall.Fixed.class, "Activation");
        assertFalse(tall.isKeybindActive(label), "toggle starts off");
        tall.onKeyPressed(Keys.KEY_8);
        assertTrue(tall.isKeybindActive(label));
        tall.onKeyPressed(Keys.KEY_8);
        assertFalse(tall.isKeybindActive(label));

        tall.getKeybinds().setMode(label, KeybindMode.ON_HOTKEY);
        tall.onKeyPressed(Keys.KEY_8);
        assertTrue(tall.isKeybindActive(label));
        tall.onKeyReleased(Keys.KEY_8);
        assertFalse(tall.isKeybindActive(label));

        tall.getKeybinds().setMode(label, KeybindMode.ALWAYS_ON);
        assertTrue(tall.isKeybindActive(label));
    }
}
```

`core/src/test/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/config/ClassScannerTest.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.config;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.io.TempDir;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.annotation.type.IPage;

import java.io.OutputStream;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.nio.file.Paths;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.jar.JarEntry;
import java.util.jar.JarOutputStream;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;
import java.util.stream.Stream;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;

class ClassScannerTest {
    @IPage('a')
    public static class FirstPage {
    }

    @IPage('b')
    public static class SecondPage {
    }

    /** Mentions IPage in its constant pool without being annotated with it. */
    public static class NotAPage {
        public static final Class<?> REFERENCE = IPage.class;
    }

    private static Path testClassesDir() throws Exception {
        return Paths.get(ClassScannerTest.class.getProtectionDomain().getCodeSource().getLocation().toURI());
    }

    private static List<String> names(List<Class<?>> classes) {
        //only this test's own fixtures (other tests declare pages too)
        return classes.stream().filter(c -> c.getEnclosingClass() == ClassScannerTest.class).map(Class::getSimpleName).sorted().collect(Collectors.toList());
    }

    @Test
    void findsAnnotatedClassesInDirectory() throws Exception {
        List<Class<?>> found = ClassScanner.findAnnotated(Collections.singletonList(testClassesDir()), getClass().getClassLoader(), IPage.class);
        assertEquals(java.util.Arrays.asList("FirstPage", "SecondPage"), names(found));
    }

    @Test
    void findsAnnotatedClassesInJar(@TempDir Path tmp) throws Exception {
        Path root = testClassesDir();
        Path jar = tmp.resolve("mod.jar");
        try (JarOutputStream out = new JarOutputStream(Files.newOutputStream(jar)); Stream<Path> files = Files.walk(root)) {
            for (Path file : (Iterable<Path>) files.filter(Files::isRegularFile)::iterator) {
                out.putNextEntry(new JarEntry(root.relativize(file).toString().replace('\\', '/')));
                Files.copy(file, (OutputStream) out);
                out.closeEntry();
            }
        }

        List<Class<?>> found = ClassScanner.findAnnotated(Collections.singletonList(jar), getClass().getClassLoader(), IPage.class);
        assertEquals(java.util.Arrays.asList("FirstPage", "SecondPage"), names(found));
    }
}
```

`core/src/test/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/config/ConfigStoreTest.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.config;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.io.TempDir;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.input.Keys;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.Keybinds;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.ModelBuilder;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.Setting;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.SettingTypes;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.SettingsModel;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.support.Fixture;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertArrayEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

class ConfigStoreTest {
    @TempDir
    Path game;

    private SettingsModel model;
    private Keybinds keybinds;

    @BeforeEach
    void reset() {
        Fixture.reset();
        build();
    }

    private void build() {
        List<Class<?>> pages = Arrays.asList(Fixture.Combat.class, Fixture.Visuals.class);
        model = new ModelBuilder(SettingTypes.builtIn()).build(pages);
        keybinds = new Keybinds(model);
    }

    private ConfigStore store(boolean profiles) {
        ConfigStore store = new ConfigStore(game.resolve("config/test"), game.resolve("test"), model, keybinds, profiles);
        for (Setting<?> s : model.getSettings()) s.setListener((setting, byUser) -> {
            if (byUser && setting.isPersistent()) store.markDirty();
        });
        keybinds.setListener((setting, byUser) -> {
            if (byUser) store.markDirty();
        });
        store.init();
        return store;
    }

    private Setting<?> setting(Class<?> group, String field) {
        return model.find(group, field);
    }

    @Test
    void keybindSurvivesRestart() {
        ConfigStore store = store(false);
        Setting<?> enabled = setting(Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.class, "Enabled");
        keybinds.set(enabled, Keys.KEY_A);
        store.interactionEnded();

        Fixture.reset();
        build();
        store(false);
        assertEquals(Keys.KEY_A, keybinds.get(model.find(Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.class, "Enabled")));
    }

    @Test
    void valuesSurviveRestartAndInitWritesNothing() {
        ConfigStore store = store(false);
        assertEquals(0, store.getWriteCount(), "init must not write");
        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked") Setting<Double> range = (Setting<Double>) setting(Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.class, "Range");
        range.set(75.0);
        store.interactionEnded();
        assertEquals(1, store.getWriteCount());

        Fixture.reset();
        build();
        store(false);
        assertEquals(75, Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.Range);
    }

    @Test
    void migratesV2Files() throws Exception {
        Path legacy = Files.createDirectories(game.resolve("test"));
        Files.write(legacy.resolve("config.json"), ("{\"Combat\":{\"Kill_Aura\":{\"Enabled\":false,\"Mode\":\"Option 3\","
                + "\"Targets\":[\"Legs\",\"Head\"],\"Name\":\"migrated\",\"Range\":20.0}}}").getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        //v2 keyed keybinds by display labels (spaces instead of underscores)
        Files.write(legacy.resolve("keybind.json"), "{\"Combat\":{\"Kill Aura\":{\"Enabled\":30}}}".getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));

        store(false);
        assertFalse(Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.Enabled);
        assertEquals("Option 3", Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.Mode);
        assertArrayEquals(new String[]{"Head", "Legs"}, Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.Targets);
        assertEquals("migrated", Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.Name);
        assertEquals(20, Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.Range);
        assertEquals(30, keybinds.get(setting(Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.class, "Enabled")));
        assertTrue(Files.exists(legacy.resolve("config.json.migrated")));
        assertTrue(Files.exists(legacy.resolve("keybind.json.migrated")));
        assertTrue(Files.exists(game.resolve("config/test/configs/default.json")));
    }

    @Test
    void corruptProfileIsKeptAside() throws Exception {
        Path profile = game.resolve("config/test/configs/default.json");
        Files.createDirectories(profile.getParent());
        Files.write(profile, "{ not json".getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));

        ConfigStore store = store(false);
        assertFalse(Files.exists(profile));
        assertEquals(1, Files.list(profile.getParent()).filter(p -> p.getFileName().toString().startsWith("default.json.broken-")).count());
        assertTrue(Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.Enabled, "defaults stay");
        assertEquals(0, store.getWriteCount());
    }

    @Test
    void emptyFileIsQuiet() throws Exception {
        Path profile = game.resolve("config/test/configs/default.json");
        Files.createDirectories(profile.getParent());
        Files.write(profile, new byte[0]);
        store(false);
        assertTrue(Files.exists(profile), "an empty file isn't treated as broken");
    }

    @Test
    void profileModeWritesOnlyOnSave() {
        ConfigStore store = store(true);
        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked") Setting<Boolean> enabled = (Setting<Boolean>) setting(Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.class, "Enabled");
        enabled.set(false);
        store.interactionEnded();
        store.tick(false);
        assertEquals(0, store.getWriteCount());
        assertTrue(store.hasUnsavedChanges());

        store.save();
        assertFalse(store.hasUnsavedChanges());
        assertTrue(Files.exists(game.resolve("config/test/configs/default.json")));
    }

    @Test
    void profilesLoadDeleteResetImportExport() {
        ConfigStore store = store(true);
        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked") Setting<String> name = (Setting<String>) setting(Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.class, "Name");
        name.set("first");
        assertTrue(store.saveAs("one"));
        name.set("second");
        assertTrue(store.saveAs("two"));
        assertEquals(Arrays.asList("one", "two"), store.listProfiles());

        assertTrue(store.load("one"));
        assertEquals("first", Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.Name);
        assertEquals("one", store.getProfile());

        String exported = store.exportCurrent();
        store.resetToDefaults();
        assertEquals("Hello", Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.Name);
        assertTrue(store.importJson(exported));
        assertEquals("first", Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.Name);
        assertFalse(store.importJson("not a config"));

        assertTrue(store.delete("two"));
        assertEquals(Arrays.asList("one"), store.listProfiles());
        assertFalse(store.saveAs("../escape"));
    }

    @Test
    void autoSaveWaitsForInteractionEnd() {
        ConfigStore store = store(false);
        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked") Setting<Double> smooth = (Setting<Double>) setting(Fixture.Combat.Second.class, "Smooth");
        for (int frame = 0; frame < 100; frame++) {
            smooth.set(frame / 100.0);
            store.tick(true); //mid-drag
        }
        assertEquals(0, store.getWriteCount());
        store.interactionEnded();
        assertEquals(1, store.getWriteCount());
        store.tick(false);
        assertEquals(1, store.getWriteCount(), "nothing left to write");
    }
}
```

`core/src/test/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/input/KeysTest.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.input;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNull;

class KeysTest {
    @Test
    void namesMatchLwjgl2() {
        assertEquals("RSHIFT", Keys.getKeyName(Keys.KEY_RSHIFT));
        assertEquals("6", Keys.getKeyName(Keys.KEY_6));
        assertEquals("LMETA", Keys.getKeyName(Keys.KEY_LWIN)); //LWJGL2 skips the *WIN aliases
        assertNull(Keys.getKeyName(84));
    }

    @Test
    void translatesGlfwCodes() {
        assertEquals(Keys.KEY_RSHIFT, Keys.fromGlfw(344));
        assertEquals(Keys.KEY_A, Keys.fromGlfw(65));
        assertEquals(Keys.KEY_0, Keys.fromGlfw(48));
        assertEquals(Keys.KEY_6, Keys.fromGlfw(54));
        assertEquals(Keys.KEY_BACK, Keys.fromGlfw(259));
        assertEquals(Keys.KEY_ESCAPE, Keys.fromGlfw(256));
        assertEquals(Keys.KEY_F12, Keys.fromGlfw(301));
        assertEquals(Keys.KEY_NONE, Keys.fromGlfw(-1));
    }

    @Test
    void translatesSdlScancodes() {
        assertEquals(Keys.KEY_RSHIFT, Keys.fromSdl(229));
        assertEquals(Keys.KEY_A, Keys.fromSdl(4));
        assertEquals(Keys.KEY_0, Keys.fromSdl(39));
        assertEquals(Keys.KEY_6, Keys.fromSdl(35));
        assertEquals(Keys.KEY_BACK, Keys.fromSdl(42));
        assertEquals(Keys.KEY_ESCAPE, Keys.fromSdl(41));
        assertEquals(Keys.KEY_F12, Keys.fromSdl(69));
        assertEquals(Keys.KEY_PRIOR, Keys.fromSdl(75));
        assertEquals(Keys.KEY_NONE, Keys.fromSdl(-1));
    }
}
```

`core/src/test/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/render/TextTest.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.render;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.support.FakePlatform;

import java.time.Duration;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTimeoutPreemptively;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

class TextTest {
    private final FontAtlas atlas = new FontAtlas();
    private final Font font = new Font(atlas, "Dialog", java.awt.Font.PLAIN, 12);
    private final Canvas canvas = new Canvas();

    TextTest() {
        canvas.setup(atlas, 1);
    }

    @Test
    void unknownCodeTerminates() {
        assertTimeoutPreemptively(Duration.ofSeconds(3), () -> {
            canvas.measure(font, "§z");
            canvas.measure(font, "a§");
            canvas.measure(font, "§");
        });
    }

    @Test
    void codesAreNotMeasured() {
        assertEquals(canvas.measure(font, "AB"), canvas.measure(font, "§cAB"), 1e-9);
        assertEquals(canvas.measure(font, "AB"), canvas.measure(font, "§CAB"), 1e-9, "upper-case codes too");
        assertEquals(canvas.measure(font, "AB"), canvas.measure(font, "A§zB"), 1e-9, "unknown codes are skipped");
    }

    @Test
    void anyUnicodeMeasuresAndDraws() {
        String text = "café € Ж 中 😀";
        assertTrue(canvas.measure(font, text) > canvas.measure(font, "caf"));

        FakePlatform.RecordingRenderer renderer = new FakePlatform.RecordingRenderer();
        FrameRenderer frame = new FrameRenderer();
        frame.render(renderer, atlas, 1, 800, 600, () -> frame.getCanvas().text(font, text, 10, 10, 0xFFFFFFFF));
        assertEquals(1, renderer.renders);
        assertEquals(1, renderer.lastCommandCount, "all glyphs share one texture");
    }

    @Test
    void fitAddsEllipsis() {
        String fitted = canvas.fit(font, "a fairly long piece of text", 40);
        assertTrue(fitted.endsWith("…"));
        assertTrue(canvas.measure(font, fitted) <= 40);
        assertEquals("short", canvas.fit(font, "short", 1000));
    }

    @Test
    void atlasGrowsAndKeepsWhitePixel() {
        FontAtlas small = new FontAtlas(64);
        assertEquals(0xFFFFFFFF, small.pixel(1, 1));
        for (int i = 0; i < 40; i++) small.add(new int[20 * 20], 20, 20);
        assertTrue(small.getSize() > 64, "grew instead of overflowing");
        assertEquals(0xFFFFFFFF, small.pixel(1, 1));
    }

    @Test
    void scaleChangesRasterSize() {
        double at1 = font.face(1).getLineHeight();
        double at2 = font.face(2).getLineHeight();
        assertTrue(at2 > at1 * 1.5, "glyphs are rasterised at the real pixel size");
    }
}
```

`core/src/test/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/support/FakePlatform.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.support;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Platform;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Renderer;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Texture;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.DrawList;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.MenuController;

import java.io.File;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;

/** A headless platform that records what Icarus draws. */
public final class FakePlatform implements Platform {
    public final RecordingRenderer renderer = new RecordingRenderer();
    public final File gameDir;
    public double guiScale = 2;
    public int width = 1920, height = 1080;
    public MenuController opened;
    public String clipboard = "";

    public FakePlatform(File gameDir) {
        this.gameDir = gameDir;
    }

    @Override
    public void install() {
    }

    @Override
    public Renderer getRenderer() {
        return renderer;
    }

    @Override
    public double getGuiScale() {
        return guiScale;
    }

    @Override
    public int getFramebufferWidth() {
        return width;
    }

    @Override
    public int getFramebufferHeight() {
        return height;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isInGame() {
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public void openScreen(MenuController menu) {
        opened = menu;
    }

    @Override
    public File getGameDir() {
        return gameDir;
    }

    @Override
    public Collection<Path> getModRoots(String modid) {
        return Collections.emptyList();
    }

    @Override
    public ClassLoader getModClassLoader(String modid) {
        return FakePlatform.class.getClassLoader();
    }

    @Override
    public String getClipboard() {
        return clipboard;
    }

    @Override
    public void setClipboard(String text) {
        clipboard = text;
    }

    /** Records draw lists (as quads) and texture uploads. */
    public static final class RecordingRenderer implements Renderer {
        public int renders;
        public int lastCommandCount;
        public int lastIndexCount;
        /** Quads of the last render: x0, y0, x1, y1 per quad (from each pair of triangles). */
        public final List<float[]> lastQuads = new ArrayList<>();
        public boolean recordQuads = true;
        public int uploads;

        @Override
        public Texture createTexture(int width, int height) {
            return new Texture() {
                @Override
                public int getWidth() {
                    return width;
                }

                @Override
                public int getHeight() {
                    return height;
                }
            };
        }

        @Override
        public void upload(Texture texture, int x, int y, int width, int height, int[] argb, int offset, int stride) {
            uploads++;
        }

        @Override
        public void deleteTexture(Texture texture) {
        }

        @Override
        public void render(DrawList list) {
            renders++;
            lastCommandCount = list.getCommandCount();
            lastIndexCount = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < list.getCommandCount(); i++) lastIndexCount += list.getCommandIndexCount(i);
            if (!recordQuads) return;
            lastQuads.clear();
            float[] xy = list.getPositions();
            int[] indices = list.getIndices();
            for (int i = 0; i + 5 < lastIndexCount; i += 6) {
                int a = indices[i], c = indices[i + 2];
                lastQuads.add(new float[]{xy[a * 2], xy[a * 2 + 1], xy[c * 2], xy[c * 2 + 1]});
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`core/src/test/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/ui/MenuTest.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.ui;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.io.TempDir;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.Icarus;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.IcarusMod;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.IcarusOptions;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.input.Keys;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.Canvas;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.Setting;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.support.FakePlatform;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.support.Fixture;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.theme.skeet.SkeetStyleTheme;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.KeybindMode;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.popup.ContextPopup;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.popup.DropdownPopup;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.ColorWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.CheckboxWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.ChoiceWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.FieldWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.KeybindWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.SliderWidget;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.widget.Widget;

import java.lang.management.ManagementFactory;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNull;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertSame;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

class MenuTest {
    @TempDir
    Path game;

    private FakePlatform platform;
    private final RecordingTheme theme = new RecordingTheme();

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        Fixture.reset();
        platform = new FakePlatform(game.toFile());
        Icarus.setPlatform(platform);
    }

    @AfterEach
    void tearDown() {
        Icarus.setPlatform(null);
    }

    private IcarusMod mod(IcarusOptions options, Class<?>... pages) {
        return Icarus.createDetached("test", theme, options, Arrays.asList(pages), game.resolve("config/test"), game.resolve("test"));
    }

    /** Menu coordinates are design units; at GUI scale 2 the screen passes half of them. */
    private static void frame(Menu menu, double x, double y) {
        menu.render(x / 2, y / 2, 0);
    }

    private static void click(Menu menu, double x, double y) {
        menu.mousePressed(x / 2, y / 2, 0);
        menu.mouseReleased(x / 2, y / 2, 0);
    }

    private static <T extends Widget> T widget(Menu menu, Class<T> type, String name) {
        for (GroupView g : menu.getCurrentSubTab().getGroups()) {
            for (Widget w : g.getWidgets()) {
                if (type.isInstance(w) && w.getSetting().getName().equals(name)) return type.cast(w);
            }
        }
        throw new AssertionError("no " + type.getSimpleName() + " " + name);
    }

    @Test
    void draggingMovesEverythingInTheSameFrame() {
        Menu menu = mod(IcarusOptions.defaults(), Fixture.Combat.class).getMenu();
        frame(menu, 0, 0);
        double wx = menu.window.x, wy = menu.window.y;
        List<double[]> before = snapshot(menu);

        menu.mousePressed((wx + 300) / 2, (wy + 5) / 2, 0);
        frame(menu, wx + 300 + 40, wy + 5 + 20);

        assertEquals(wx + 40, menu.window.x, 1e-9);
        assertEquals(wy + 20, menu.window.y, 1e-9);
        List<double[]> after = snapshot(menu);
        for (int i = 0; i < before.size(); i++) {
            assertEquals(before.get(i)[0] + 40, after.get(i)[0], 1e-9, "rect " + i + " x");
            assertEquals(before.get(i)[1] + 20, after.get(i)[1], 1e-9, "rect " + i + " y");
        }
    }

    private static List<double[]> snapshot(Menu menu) {
        List<double[]> rects = new ArrayList<>();
        for (GroupView g : menu.getCurrentSubTab().getGroups()) {
            rects.add(new double[]{g.frame.x, g.frame.y});
            for (Widget w : g.getWidgets()) {
                rects.add(new double[]{w.bounds.x, w.bounds.y});
                if (w.getKeybind() != null) rects.add(new double[]{w.getKeybind().bounds.x, w.getKeybind().bounds.y});
            }
        }
        for (TabView t : menu.getTabs()) rects.add(new double[]{t.bounds.x, t.bounds.y});
        return rects;
    }

    @Test
    void groupFramesUnderWidgetsAndPopupLast() {
        Menu menu = mod(IcarusOptions.defaults(), Fixture.Combat.class).getMenu();
        frame(menu, 0, 0);
        ChoiceWidget mode = widget(menu, ChoiceWidget.class, "Mode");
        click(menu, mode.box.centerX(), mode.box.centerY());
        theme.calls.clear();
        frame(menu, 0, 0);

        List<String> calls = theme.calls;
        assertEquals("window", calls.get(0));
        assertEquals("dropdown", calls.get(calls.size() - 1), "the open popup is painted last");
        int firstGroup = calls.indexOf("group");
        int firstWidget = calls.indexOf("widget");
        assertTrue(firstGroup >= 0 && firstGroup < firstWidget, "group frame before its widgets");
    }

    @Test
    void clickOnOpenDropdownOnlyHitsTheDropdown() {
        Menu menu = mod(IcarusOptions.defaults(), Fixture.Combat.class).getMenu();
        frame(menu, 0, 0);
        ChoiceWidget mode = widget(menu, ChoiceWidget.class, "Mode");
        click(menu, mode.box.centerX(), mode.box.centerY());
        frame(menu, 0, 0);
        DropdownPopup popup = (DropdownPopup) menu.getPopup();
        double itemY = popup.bounds.y + popup.itemHeight * 2.5;

        click(menu, popup.bounds.centerX(), itemY);
        assertEquals("Option 3", Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.Mode);
        assertNull(menu.getPopup());
        assertNull(menu.getFocused(), "nothing under the dropdown reacted");
        assertTrue(Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.Enabled);
    }

    @Test
    void clickOutsidePopupOnlyClosesIt() {
        Menu menu = mod(IcarusOptions.defaults(), Fixture.Combat.class).getMenu();
        frame(menu, 0, 0);
        ChoiceWidget mode = widget(menu, ChoiceWidget.class, "Mode");
        CheckboxWidget enabled = widget(menu, CheckboxWidget.class, "Enabled");
        click(menu, mode.box.centerX(), mode.box.centerY());
        frame(menu, 0, 0);
        click(menu, enabled.box.centerX(), enabled.box.centerY());
        assertNull(menu.getPopup());
        assertTrue(Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.Enabled, "the click closed the popup and didn't toggle");
    }

    @Test
    void checkboxLabelIsClickable() {
        Menu menu = mod(IcarusOptions.defaults(), Fixture.Combat.class).getMenu();
        frame(menu, 0, 0);
        CheckboxWidget enabled = widget(menu, CheckboxWidget.class, "Enabled");
        click(menu, enabled.box.right() + 30, enabled.box.centerY());
        assertFalse(Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.Enabled);
    }

    @Test
    void keybindCaptureEscapeCancelsAndBackspaceClears() {
        IcarusMod mod = mod(IcarusOptions.defaults(), Fixture.Combat.class);
        Menu menu = mod.getMenu();
        frame(menu, 0, 0);
        KeybindWidget keybind = widget(menu, CheckboxWidget.class, "Enabled").getKeybind();
        Setting<?> setting = keybind.getSetting();

        click(menu, keybind.label.centerX(), keybind.label.centerY());
        assertTrue(keybind.isCapturing());
        assertTrue(menu.keyPressed(Keys.KEY_ESCAPE), "escape is used to cancel, not to close the menu");
        assertFalse(keybind.isCapturing());
        assertEquals(Keys.KEY_7, mod.getKeybinds().get(setting));
        assertFalse(menu.keyPressed(Keys.KEY_ESCAPE), "now escape may close the menu");

        click(menu, keybind.label.centerX(), keybind.label.centerY());
        menu.keyPressed(mod.getMenuKey());
        assertTrue(keybind.isCapturing(), "the menu key is refused");
        menu.keyPressed(Keys.KEY_BACK);
        assertEquals(Keys.KEY_NONE, mod.getKeybinds().get(setting));

        click(menu, keybind.label.centerX(), keybind.label.centerY());
        menu.keyPressed(Keys.KEY_G);
        assertEquals(Keys.KEY_G, mod.getKeybinds().get(setting));
    }

    @Test
    void menuKeyClosesTheMenuUnlessTyping() {
        IcarusMod mod = mod(IcarusOptions.defaults(), Fixture.Combat.class);
        Menu menu = mod.getMenu();
        menu.opened();
        frame(menu, 0, 0);
        menu.keyPressed(mod.getMenuKey());
        assertFalse(menu.isClosing(), "ignored while fading in: the press that opened the menu can reach it too");

        menu.skipFade();
        FieldWidget name = widget(menu, FieldWidget.class, "Name");
        click(menu, name.box.centerX(), name.box.centerY());
        assertTrue(menu.isTyping());
        menu.keyPressed(mod.getMenuKey());
        assertFalse(menu.isClosing(), "typing keeps the menu open");

        menu.focus(null);
        assertTrue(menu.keyPressed(mod.getMenuKey()));
        assertTrue(menu.isClosing(), "the menu key toggles the menu");
    }

    @Test
    void rightClickKeybindPicksAMode() {
        IcarusMod mod = mod(IcarusOptions.defaults(), Fixture.Combat.class);
        Menu menu = mod.getMenu();
        frame(menu, 0, 0);
        KeybindWidget keybind = widget(menu, CheckboxWidget.class, "Enabled").getKeybind();
        menu.mousePressed(keybind.label.centerX() / 2, keybind.label.centerY() / 2, 1);
        assertTrue(menu.getPopup() instanceof ContextPopup, "right click opens the mode menu");
        ContextPopup modes = (ContextPopup) menu.getPopup();
        frame(menu, 0, 0);
        assertEquals(KeybindMode.TOGGLE.ordinal(), modes.getSelected());
        click(menu, modes.bounds.centerX(), modes.bounds.y + modes.itemHeight * 1.5);
        assertNull(menu.getPopup());
        assertEquals(KeybindMode.ON_HOTKEY, mod.getKeybinds().getMode(keybind.getSetting()));
    }

    @Test
    void rightClickLabelKeybindOpensModes() {
        Menu menu = mod(IcarusOptions.builder().configUi(false), Fixture.Tall.class).getMenu();
        frame(menu, 0, 0);
        KeybindWidget keybind = null;
        for (Widget w : menu.getCurrentSubTab().getGroups().get(0).getWidgets()) if (w.getKeybind() != null) keybind = w.getKeybind();
        menu.mousePressed(keybind.label.centerX() / 2, keybind.label.centerY() / 2, 1);
        assertTrue(menu.getPopup() instanceof ContextPopup, "labels with a key have modes too");
    }

    @Test
    void rightClickColourCopiesAndPastes() {
        IcarusMod mod = mod(IcarusOptions.defaults(), Fixture.Combat.class);
        Menu menu = mod.getMenu();
        frame(menu, 0, 0);
        ColorWidget tint = widget(menu, ColorWidget.class, "Tint");
        menu.mousePressed(tint.swatch.centerX() / 2, tint.swatch.centerY() / 2, 1);
        ContextPopup context = (ContextPopup) menu.getPopup();
        frame(menu, 0, 0);
        click(menu, context.bounds.centerX(), context.bounds.y + context.itemHeight * 0.5);
        assertEquals("#FF0000FF", platform.clipboard);

        platform.clipboard = "#00FF0080";
        menu.mousePressed(tint.swatch.centerX() / 2, tint.swatch.centerY() / 2, 1);
        context = (ContextPopup) menu.getPopup();
        frame(menu, 0, 0);
        click(menu, context.bounds.centerX(), context.bounds.y + context.itemHeight * 1.5);
        assertEquals(new java.awt.Color(0, 255, 0, 128), Fixture.Combat.Second.Tint);
    }

    @Test
    void sliderDragWritesOnceOnRelease() {
        IcarusMod mod = mod(IcarusOptions.builder().configUi(false), Fixture.Combat.class);
        Menu menu = mod.getMenu();
        frame(menu, 0, 0);
        SliderWidget range = widget(menu, SliderWidget.class, "Range");
        double y = range.track.centerY();
        menu.mousePressed(range.track.x / 2, y / 2, 0);
        for (int i = 0; i <= 100; i++) {
            frame(menu, range.track.x + range.track.w * i / 100.0, y);
            mod.getConfigStore().tick(menu.isInteracting());
        }
        assertEquals(0, mod.getConfigStore().getWriteCount());
        menu.mouseReleased(range.track.right() / 2, y / 2, 0);
        assertEquals(100, Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.Range);
        assertEquals(1, mod.getConfigStore().getWriteCount());
    }

    @Test
    void subTabsSwitch() {
        Menu menu = mod(IcarusOptions.defaults(), Fixture.Visuals.class).getMenu();
        frame(menu, 0, 0);
        assertEquals("Players", menu.getCurrentSubTab().getGroups().get(0).getLabel());
        Rect second = menu.getCurrentTab().getSubTabs().get(1).bounds;
        click(menu, second.centerX(), second.centerY());
        frame(menu, 0, 0);
        assertEquals("Chams", menu.getCurrentSubTab().getGroups().get(0).getLabel());
    }

    @Test
    void twoModsAreIsolated() {
        IcarusMod a = Icarus.createDetached("a", theme, IcarusOptions.builder().configUi(false), Collections.singletonList(Fixture.Combat.class),
                game.resolve("config/a"), game.resolve("a"));
        IcarusMod b = Icarus.createDetached("b", theme, IcarusOptions.builder().configUi(false), Collections.singletonList(Fixture.Visuals.class),
                game.resolve("config/b"), game.resolve("b"));
        Setting<?> enabled = a.getModel().find(Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.class, "Enabled");
        a.getKeybinds().set(enabled, Keys.KEY_H);
        a.interactionEnded();
        assertEquals(1, a.getConfigStore().getWriteCount());
        assertEquals(0, b.getConfigStore().getWriteCount());
        assertSame(null, b.getModel().find(Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.class, "Enabled"));
        assertTrue(game.resolve("config/a/configs/default.json").toFile().exists());
        assertFalse(game.resolve("config/b/configs/default.json").toFile().exists());
    }

    @Test
    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    void loadingAnEmptySlotResetsToDefaults() {
        IcarusMod mod = mod(IcarusOptions.defaults(), Fixture.Combat.class);
        Setting<?> enabled = mod.getModel().find(Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.class, "Enabled");
        ((Setting<Boolean>) enabled).set(false);
        assertTrue(mod.getConfigStore().load("Slot 4"), "a never-saved slot loads");
        assertTrue(Fixture.Combat.Kill_Aura.Enabled, "back to its default");
        assertEquals("Slot 4", mod.getConfigStore().getProfile());
    }

    @Test
    void showIfHidesRowsUntilTheCheckboxIsTicked() {
        Menu menu = mod(IcarusOptions.builder().configUi(false), Fixture.Tall.class).getMenu();
        frame(menu, 0, 0);
        CheckboxWidget a = widget(menu, CheckboxWidget.class, "A"), child = widget(menu, CheckboxWidget.class, "Child");
        assertTrue(child.hidden, "hidden while A is off");
        double below = widget(menu, CheckboxWidget.class, "C").bounds.y;
        click(menu, a.box.centerX(), a.box.centerY());
        frame(menu, 0, 0);
        assertFalse(child.hidden);
        assertTrue(child.bounds.y > below, "takes a row once shown");
    }

    @Test
    void groupsNeverLeaveTheirColumn() {
        Menu menu = mod(IcarusOptions.builder().configUi(false), Fixture.Tall.class).getMenu();
        frame(menu, 0, 0);
        Rect area = menu.content;
        for (GroupView g : menu.getCurrentSubTab().getGroups()) {
            assertTrue(g.frame.y + g.frame.h <= area.y + area.h + 0.01, g.getLabel() + " ends at " + (g.frame.y + g.frame.h) + ", past " + (area.y + area.h));
        }
        GroupView fixed = menu.getCurrentSubTab().getGroups().get(0), tall = menu.getCurrentSubTab().getGroups().get(1);
        assertEquals(300, fixed.frame.h, 0.01);
        assertTrue(tall.isScrollable());
    }

    @Test
    void steadyFramesAreOneDrawCallAndBarelyAllocate() {
        Menu menu = Icarus.createDetached("perf", new SkeetStyleTheme(), IcarusOptions.defaults(), Collections.singletonList(Fixture.Combat.class),
                game.resolve("config/perf"), game.resolve("perf")).getMenu();
        platform.renderer.recordQuads = false;
        for (int i = 0; i < 200; i++) frame(menu, 0, 0); //warm up: glyphs, JIT
        assertEquals(1, platform.renderer.lastCommandCount, "everything comes from one atlas texture");

        com.sun.management.ThreadMXBean bean = (com.sun.management.ThreadMXBean) ManagementFactory.getThreadMXBean();
        long tid = Thread.currentThread().getId();
        long before = bean.getThreadAllocatedBytes(tid);
        for (int i = 0; i < 100; i++) frame(menu, 0, 0);
        long perFrame = (bean.getThreadAllocatedBytes(tid) - before) / 100;
        assertTrue(perFrame < 2048, "allocated " + perFrame + " bytes per frame");
    }

    /** Records the order of paint calls. */
    static final class RecordingTheme extends SkeetStyleTheme {
        final List<String> calls = new ArrayList<>();

        @Override
        public void paintWindow(Canvas c, Menu menu) {
            calls.add("window");
            super.paintWindow(c, menu);
        }

        @Override
        public void paintGroup(Canvas c, Menu menu, GroupView g) {
            calls.add("group");
            super.paintGroup(c, menu, g);
        }

        @Override
        public void paintCheckbox(Canvas c, CheckboxWidget w) {
            calls.add("widget");
            super.paintCheckbox(c, w);
        }

        @Override
        public void paintChoice(Canvas c, ChoiceWidget w) {
            calls.add("widget");
            super.paintChoice(c, w);
        }

        @Override
        public void paintDropdown(Canvas c, DropdownPopup p) {
            calls.add("dropdown");
            super.paintDropdown(c, p);
        }
    }
}
```

`gradlew`:

```
#!/bin/sh

#
# Copyright © 2015 the original authors.
#
# Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the "License");
# you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.
# You may obtain a copy of the License at
#
#      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
#
# Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software
# distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS,
# WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.
# See the License for the specific language governing permissions and
# limitations under the License.
#
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
#

##############################################################################
#
#   gradlew start up script for POSIX generated by Gradle.
#
#   Important for running:
#
#   (1) You need a POSIX-compliant shell to run this script. If your /bin/sh is
#       noncompliant, but you have some other compliant shell such as ksh or
#       bash, then to run this script, type that shell name before the whole
#       command line, like:
#
#           ksh gradlew
#
#       Busybox and similar reduced shells will NOT work, because this script
#       requires all of these POSIX shell features:
#         * functions;
#         * expansions «$var», «${var}», «${var:-default}», «${var+SET}»,
#           «${var#prefix}», «${var%suffix}», and «$( cmd )»;
#         * compound commands having a testable exit status, especially «case»;
#         * various built-in commands including «command», «set», and «ulimit».
#
#   Important for patching:
#
#   (2) This script targets any POSIX shell, so it avoids extensions provided
#       by Bash, Ksh, etc; in particular arrays are avoided.
#
#       The "traditional" practice of packing multiple parameters into a
#       space-separated string is a well documented source of bugs and security
#       problems, so this is (mostly) avoided, by progressively accumulating
#       options in "$@", and eventually passing that to Java.
#
#       Where the inherited environment variables (DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS, JAVA_OPTS,
#       and GRADLE_OPTS) rely on word-splitting, this is performed explicitly;
#       see the in-line comments for details.
#
#       There are tweaks for specific operating systems such as AIX, CygWin,
#       Darwin, MinGW, and NonStop.
#
#   (3) This script is generated from the Groovy template
#       https://github.com/gradle/gradle/blob/3d91ce3b8caaf77ad09f381f43615b715b53f72c/platforms/jvm/plugins-application/src/main/resources/org/gradle/api/internal/plugins/unixStartScript.txt
#       within the Gradle project.
#
#       You can find Gradle at https://github.com/gradle/gradle/.
#
##############################################################################

# Attempt to set APP_HOME

# Resolve links: $0 may be a link
app_path=$0

# Need this for daisy-chained symlinks.
while
    APP_HOME=${app_path%"${app_path##*/}"}  # leaves a trailing /; empty if no leading path
    [ -h "$app_path" ]
do
    ls=$( ls -ld "$app_path" )
    link=${ls#*' -> '}
    case $link in             #(
      /*)   app_path=$link ;; #(
      *)    app_path=$APP_HOME$link ;;
    esac
done

# This is normally unused
# shellcheck disable=SC2034
APP_BASE_NAME=${0##*/}
# Discard cd standard output in case $CDPATH is set (https://github.com/gradle/gradle/issues/25036)
APP_HOME=$( cd -P "${APP_HOME:-./}" > /dev/null && printf '%s\n' "$PWD" ) || exit

# Use the maximum available, or set MAX_FD != -1 to use that value.
MAX_FD=maximum

warn () {
    echo "$*"
} >&2

die () {
    echo
    echo "$*"
    echo
    exit 1
} >&2

# OS specific support (must be 'true' or 'false').
cygwin=false
msys=false
darwin=false
nonstop=false
case "$( uname )" in                #(
  CYGWIN* )         cygwin=true  ;; #(
  Darwin* )         darwin=true  ;; #(
  MSYS* | MINGW* )  msys=true    ;; #(
  NONSTOP* )        nonstop=true ;;
esac



# Determine the Java command to use to start the JVM.
if [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] ; then
    if [ -x "$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java" ] ; then
        # IBM's JDK on AIX uses strange locations for the executables
        JAVACMD=$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java
    else
        JAVACMD=$JAVA_HOME/bin/java
    fi
    if [ ! -x "$JAVACMD" ] ; then
        die "ERROR: JAVA_HOME is set to an invalid directory: $JAVA_HOME

Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the
location of your Java installation."
    fi
else
    JAVACMD=java
    if ! command -v java >/dev/null 2>&1
    then
        die "ERROR: JAVA_HOME is not set and no 'java' command could be found in your PATH.

Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the
location of your Java installation."
    fi
fi

# Increase the maximum file descriptors if we can.
if ! "$cygwin" && ! "$darwin" && ! "$nonstop" ; then
    case $MAX_FD in #(
      max*)
        # In POSIX sh, ulimit -H is undefined. That's why the result is checked to see if it worked.
        # shellcheck disable=SC2039,SC3045
        MAX_FD=$( ulimit -H -n ) ||
            warn "Could not query maximum file descriptor limit"
    esac
    case $MAX_FD in  #(
      '' | soft) :;; #(
      *)
        # In POSIX sh, ulimit -n is undefined. That's why the result is checked to see if it worked.
        # shellcheck disable=SC2039,SC3045
        ulimit -n "$MAX_FD" ||
            warn "Could not set maximum file descriptor limit to $MAX_FD"
    esac
fi

# Collect all arguments for the java command, stacking in reverse order:
#   * args from the command line
#   * the main class name
#   * -classpath
#   * -D...appname settings
#   * --module-path (only if needed)
#   * DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS, JAVA_OPTS, and GRADLE_OPTS environment variables.

# For Cygwin or MSYS, switch paths to Windows format before running java
if "$cygwin" || "$msys" ; then
    APP_HOME=$( cygpath --path --mixed "$APP_HOME" )

    JAVACMD=$( cygpath --unix "$JAVACMD" )

    # Now convert the arguments - kludge to limit ourselves to /bin/sh
    for arg do
        if
            case $arg in                                #(
              -*)   false ;;                            # don't mess with options #(
              /?*)  t=${arg#/} t=/${t%%/*}              # looks like a POSIX filepath
                    [ -e "$t" ] ;;                      #(
              *)    false ;;
            esac
        then
            arg=$( cygpath --path --ignore --mixed "$arg" )
        fi
        # Roll the args list around exactly as many times as the number of
        # args, so each arg winds up back in the position where it started, but
        # possibly modified.
        #
        # NB: a `for` loop captures its iteration list before it begins, so
        # changing the positional parameters here affects neither the number of
        # iterations, nor the values presented in `arg`.
        shift                   # remove old arg
        set -- "$@" "$arg"      # push replacement arg
    done
fi


# Add default JVM options here. You can also use JAVA_OPTS and GRADLE_OPTS to pass JVM options to this script.
DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS='"-Xmx64m" "-Xms64m"'

# Collect all arguments for the java command:
#   * DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS, JAVA_OPTS, and optsEnvironmentVar are not allowed to contain shell fragments,
#     and any embedded shellness will be escaped.
#   * For example: A user cannot expect ${Hostname} to be expanded, as it is an environment variable and will be
#     treated as '${Hostname}' itself on the command line.

set -- \
        "-Dorg.gradle.appname=$APP_BASE_NAME" \
        -jar "$APP_HOME/gradle/wrapper/gradle-wrapper.jar" \
        "$@"

# Stop when "xargs" is not available.
if ! command -v xargs >/dev/null 2>&1
then
    die "xargs is not available"
fi

# Use "xargs" to parse quoted args.
#
# With -n1 it outputs one arg per line, with the quotes and backslashes removed.
#
# In Bash we could simply go:
#
#   readarray ARGS < <( xargs -n1 <<<"$var" ) &&
#   set -- "${ARGS[@]}" "$@"
#
# but POSIX shell has neither arrays nor command substitution, so instead we
# post-process each arg (as a line of input to sed) to backslash-escape any
# character that might be a shell metacharacter, then use eval to reverse
# that process (while maintaining the separation between arguments), and wrap
# the whole thing up as a single "set" statement.
#
# This will of course break if any of these variables contains a newline or
# an unmatched quote.
#

eval "set -- $(
        printf '%s\n' "$DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS $JAVA_OPTS $GRADLE_OPTS" |
        xargs -n1 |
        sed ' s~[^-[:alnum:]+,./:=@_]~\\&~g; ' |
        tr '\n' ' '
    )" '"$@"'

exec "$JAVACMD" "$@"

```

```bat
@rem
@rem Copyright 2015 the original author or authors.
@rem
@rem Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the "License");
@rem you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.
@rem You may obtain a copy of the License at
@rem
@rem      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
@rem
@rem Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software
@rem distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS,
@rem WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.
@rem See the License for the specific language governing permissions and
@rem limitations under the License.
@rem
@rem SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
@rem

@if "%DEBUG%"=="" @echo off
@rem ##########################################################################
@rem
@rem  gradlew startup script for Windows
@rem
@rem ##########################################################################

@rem Set local scope for the variables, and ensure extensions are enabled
setlocal EnableExtensions

@rem Catch executions from older scripts and ensure they exit cleanly.
@rem This can be removed once we can be reasonably confident that few people
@rem will be migrating directly to this new wrapper.
goto afterSafetyNet
::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::
::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::
::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::
::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::
::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::
::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::
::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::
::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::
::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::
::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::
::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::
::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::
::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::
::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::
::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::
::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::
::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::
::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::
::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::
::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::
goto exitWithErrorLevel
:afterSafetyNet

set DIRNAME=%~dp0
if "%DIRNAME%"=="" set DIRNAME=.
@rem This is normally unused
set APP_BASE_NAME=%~n0
set APP_HOME=%DIRNAME%

@rem Resolve any "." and ".." in APP_HOME to make it shorter.
for %%i in ("%APP_HOME%") do set APP_HOME=%%~fi

@rem Add default JVM options here. You can also use JAVA_OPTS and GRADLE_OPTS to pass JVM options to this script.
set DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS="-Xmx64m" "-Xms64m"

@rem Find java.exe
if defined JAVA_HOME goto findJavaFromJavaHome

set JAVA_EXE=java.exe
%JAVA_EXE% -version >NUL 2>&1
if %ERRORLEVEL% equ 0 goto execute

1>&2 echo.
1>&2 echo ERROR: JAVA_HOME is not set and no 'java' command could be found in your PATH.
1>&2 echo.
1>&2 echo Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the
1>&2 echo location of your Java installation.

"%COMSPEC%" /c exit 1
goto exitWithErrorLevel

:findJavaFromJavaHome
set JAVA_HOME=%JAVA_HOME:"=%
set JAVA_EXE=%JAVA_HOME%/bin/java.exe

if exist "%JAVA_EXE%" goto execute

1>&2 echo.
1>&2 echo ERROR: JAVA_HOME is set to an invalid directory: %JAVA_HOME%
1>&2 echo.
1>&2 echo Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the
1>&2 echo location of your Java installation.

"%COMSPEC%" /c exit 1
goto exitWithErrorLevel

:execute
@rem Setup the command line



@rem Execute gradlew
@rem endlocal doesn't take effect until after the line is parsed and variables are expanded
@rem which allows us to clear the local environment before executing the java command
endlocal & "%JAVA_EXE%" %DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS% %JAVA_OPTS% %GRADLE_OPTS% "-Dorg.gradle.appname=%APP_BASE_NAME%" -jar "%APP_HOME%\gradle\wrapper\gradle-wrapper.jar" %* & call :exitWithErrorLevel & goto exitWithErrorLevel

@rem This label must not be changed. We rely on old scripts being able to jump to this point.
:exitWithErrorLevel
@rem Use "%COMSPEC%" /c exit to allow operators to work properly in scripts
"%COMSPEC%" /c exit %ERRORLEVEL%
```
