`platforms/forge-1.12.2/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/PlatformImpl.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform;

import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiScreen;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.ScaledResolution;
import net.minecraftforge.common.MinecraftForge;
import net.minecraftforge.fml.common.Loader;
import net.minecraftforge.fml.common.ModContainer;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.forge1122.ForgeHooks;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.forge1122.IcarusScreen;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.forge1122.LegacyRenderer;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.MenuController;

import java.io.File;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.Collections;

/** Forge 1.12.2. */
@SuppressWarnings("unused")
public class PlatformImpl implements Platform {
    private final Renderer renderer = new LegacyRenderer();

    @Override
    public void install() {
        MinecraftForge.EVENT_BUS.register(new ForgeHooks()); //the FML bus is the same bus since 1.8
    }

    @Override
    public Renderer getRenderer() {
        return renderer;
    }

    @Override
    public double getGuiScale() {
        return new ScaledResolution(Minecraft.getMinecraft()).getScaleFactor();
    }

    @Override
    public int getFramebufferWidth() {
        return Minecraft.getMinecraft().displayWidth;
    }

    @Override
    public int getFramebufferHeight() {
        return Minecraft.getMinecraft().displayHeight;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isInGame() {
        return Minecraft.getMinecraft().world != null && Minecraft.getMinecraft().player != null;
    }

    @Override
    public void openScreen(MenuController menu) {
        Minecraft.getMinecraft().displayGuiScreen(new IcarusScreen(menu));
    }

    @Override
    public File getGameDir() {
        return Minecraft.getMinecraft().gameDir;
    }

    @Override
    public Collection<Path> getModRoots(String modid) {
        return Collections.singletonList(container(modid).getSource().toPath());
    }

    @Override
    public ClassLoader getModClassLoader(String modid) {
        return container(modid).getMod().getClass().getClassLoader();
    }

    @Override
    public String getClipboard() {
        return GuiScreen.getClipboardString();
    }

    @Override
    public void setClipboard(String text) {
        GuiScreen.setClipboardString(text);
    }

    private static ModContainer container(String modid) {
        ModContainer container = Loader.instance().getIndexedModList().get(modid);
        if (container == null) throw new IllegalArgumentException("No mod with id " + modid);
        return container;
    }
}

```

`platforms/forge-1.12.2/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/forge1122/ForgeHooks.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.forge1122;

import net.minecraftforge.client.event.RenderGameOverlayEvent;
import net.minecraftforge.fml.common.eventhandler.SubscribeEvent;
import net.minecraftforge.fml.common.gameevent.InputEvent;
import net.minecraftforge.fml.common.gameevent.TickEvent;
import org.lwjgl.input.Keyboard;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.Icarus;

public class ForgeHooks {
    @SubscribeEvent
    public void onKeyInput(InputEvent.KeyInputEvent e) {
        if (Keyboard.getEventKeyState()) { //if the key is down
            Icarus.onKeyPressed(Keyboard.getEventKey()); //LWJGL2 codes are Icarus' codes
        } else {
            Icarus.onKeyReleased(Keyboard.getEventKey());
        }
    }

    @SubscribeEvent
    public void onOverlayText(RenderGameOverlayEvent.Text e) {
        Icarus.renderHud(e.getPartialTicks());
    }

    @SubscribeEvent
    public void onClientTick(TickEvent.ClientTickEvent e) {
        if (e.phase == TickEvent.Phase.START) Icarus.clientTick();
    }
}

```

`platforms/forge-1.12.2/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/forge1122/IcarusScreen.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.forge1122;

import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiScreen;
import org.lwjgl.input.Keyboard;
import org.lwjgl.input.Mouse;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.input.Keys;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.MenuController;

import java.io.IOException;

/** The Minecraft screen showing an Icarus menu; forwards everything to the {@link MenuController}. */
public class IcarusScreen extends GuiScreen {
    private final MenuController menu;

    public IcarusScreen(MenuController menu) {
        this.menu = menu;
    }

    @Override
    public void initGui() {
        Keyboard.enableRepeatEvents(true);
        menu.opened();
    }

    /** Mouse position in (fractional) GUI units. */
    private double mouseX() {
        return Mouse.getX() * (double) width / mc.displayWidth;
    }

    private double mouseY() {
        return height - Mouse.getY() * (double) height / mc.displayHeight - 1;
    }

    @Override
    public void drawScreen(int mouseX, int mouseY, float partialTicks) {
        drawDefaultBackground();
        menu.render(mouseX(), mouseY(), partialTicks);
        if (menu.isFadedOut()) mc.displayGuiScreen(null);
    }

    @Override
    protected void mouseClicked(int mouseX, int mouseY, int mouseButton) {
        menu.mousePressed(mouseX(), mouseY(), mouseButton);
    }

    @Override
    protected void mouseReleased(int mouseX, int mouseY, int state) {
        menu.mouseReleased(mouseX(), mouseY(), state);
    }

    @Override
    public void handleMouseInput() throws IOException {
        super.handleMouseInput();
        int wheel = Mouse.getEventDWheel();
        if (wheel != 0) menu.mouseScrolled(mouseX(), mouseY(), Math.signum(wheel));
    }

    @Override
    protected void keyTyped(char typedChar, int keyCode) throws IOException {
        if (keyCode == Keys.KEY_ESCAPE) {
            if (!menu.keyPressed(keyCode)) menu.requestClose(); //fades out, then closes in drawScreen
            return;
        }
        menu.keyPressed(keyCode);
        if (typedChar != 0 && !Character.isISOControl(typedChar)) menu.charTyped(typedChar);
    }

    @Override
    public void onGuiClosed() {
        Keyboard.enableRepeatEvents(false);
        menu.closed();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean doesGuiPauseGame() {
        return false;
    }
}

```

`platforms/forge-1.12.2/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/forge1122/LegacyRenderer.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.forge1122;

import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.ScaledResolution;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.GlStateManager;
import org.lwjgl.BufferUtils;
import org.lwjgl.opengl.GL11;
import org.lwjgl.opengl.GL12;
import org.lwjgl.opengl.GL15;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Renderer;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Texture;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.DrawList;

import java.nio.ByteBuffer;
import java.nio.ByteOrder;
import java.nio.IntBuffer;

/**
 * Draws Icarus' draw lists with client-side vertex arrays (one glDrawElements per texture), going through
 * GlStateManager for state it caches.
 */
public class LegacyRenderer implements Renderer {
    private static final int STRIDE = 20; //x, y, u, v floats + RGBA bytes

    private ByteBuffer vertices = BufferUtils.createByteBuffer(STRIDE * 4096);
    private IntBuffer indices = BufferUtils.createIntBuffer(6144);
    private IntBuffer pixels = BufferUtils.createIntBuffer(64 * 64);

    @Override
    public Texture createTexture(int width, int height) {
        int id = GL11.glGenTextures();
        GlStateManager.bindTexture(id);
        GL11.glTexParameteri(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL11.GL_TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER, GL11.GL_NEAREST);
        GL11.glTexParameteri(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL11.GL_TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER, GL11.GL_NEAREST);
        GL11.glTexParameteri(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL11.GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_S, GL12.GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
        GL11.glTexParameteri(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL11.GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_T, GL12.GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
        GL11.glTexImage2D(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, 0, GL11.GL_RGBA8, width, height, 0, GL12.GL_BGRA, GL12.GL_UNSIGNED_INT_8_8_8_8_REV, (IntBuffer) null);
        return new GlTexture(id, width, height);
    }

    @Override
    public void upload(Texture texture, int x, int y, int width, int height, int[] argb, int offset, int stride) {
        int count = width * height;
        if (pixels.capacity() < count) pixels = BufferUtils.createIntBuffer(count);
        pixels.clear();
        for (int row = 0; row < height; row++) pixels.put(argb, offset + row * stride, width);
        pixels.flip();
        GlStateManager.bindTexture(((GlTexture) texture).id);
        GL11.glPixelStorei(GL11.GL_UNPACK_ALIGNMENT, 4);
        GL11.glTexSubImage2D(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, 0, x, y, width, height, GL12.GL_BGRA, GL12.GL_UNSIGNED_INT_8_8_8_8_REV, pixels);
    }

    @Override
    public void deleteTexture(Texture texture) {
        GlStateManager.deleteTexture(((GlTexture) texture).id);
    }

    @Override
    public void render(DrawList list) {
        int vertexCount = list.getVertexCount();
        if (vertices.capacity() < vertexCount * STRIDE) vertices = BufferUtils.createByteBuffer(vertexCount * STRIDE * 2).order(ByteOrder.nativeOrder());
        vertices.clear();
        float[] xy = list.getPositions(), uv = list.getUvs();
        int[] colors = list.getColors();
        for (int i = 0; i < vertexCount; i++) {
            int c = colors[i];
            vertices.putFloat(xy[i * 2]).putFloat(xy[i * 2 + 1]).putFloat(uv[i * 2]).putFloat(uv[i * 2 + 1])
                    .put((byte) (c >> 16)).put((byte) (c >> 8)).put((byte) c).put((byte) (c >>> 24));
        }
        int indexCount = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < list.getCommandCount(); i++) indexCount = Math.max(indexCount, list.getCommandIndexStart(i) + list.getCommandIndexCount(i));
        if (indices.capacity() < indexCount) indices = BufferUtils.createIntBuffer(indexCount * 2);
        indices.clear();
        indices.put(list.getIndices(), 0, indexCount);

        double scale = new ScaledResolution(Minecraft.getMinecraft()).getScaleFactor();
        GlStateManager.pushMatrix();
        GlStateManager.scale(1 / scale, 1 / scale, 1);
        GlStateManager.enableBlend();
        GlStateManager.tryBlendFuncSeparate(GL11.GL_SRC_ALPHA, GL11.GL_ONE_MINUS_SRC_ALPHA, GL11.GL_ONE, GL11.GL_ONE_MINUS_SRC_ALPHA);
        GlStateManager.disableAlpha();
        GlStateManager.disableLighting();
        GlStateManager.disableDepth();
        GlStateManager.disableCull();
        GlStateManager.enableTexture2D();
        GlStateManager.shadeModel(GL11.GL_SMOOTH);
        GlStateManager.color(1, 1, 1, 1);
        GL15.glBindBuffer(GL15.GL_ARRAY_BUFFER, 0);

        GL11.glEnableClientState(GL11.GL_VERTEX_ARRAY);
        GL11.glEnableClientState(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_COORD_ARRAY);
        GL11.glEnableClientState(GL11.GL_COLOR_ARRAY);
        vertices.position(0);
        GL11.glVertexPointer(2, GL11.GL_FLOAT, STRIDE, vertices);
        vertices.position(8);
        GL11.glTexCoordPointer(2, GL11.GL_FLOAT, STRIDE, vertices);
        vertices.position(16);
        GL11.glColorPointer(4, true, STRIDE, vertices);

        for (int i = 0; i < list.getCommandCount(); i++) {
            int start = list.getCommandIndexStart(i), count = list.getCommandIndexCount(i);
            if (count == 0) continue;
            GlStateManager.bindTexture(((GlTexture) list.getCommandTexture(i)).id);
            indices.limit(start + count).position(start);
            GL11.glDrawElements(GL11.GL_TRIANGLES, indices);
        }

        GL11.glDisableClientState(GL11.GL_COLOR_ARRAY);
        GL11.glDisableClientState(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_COORD_ARRAY);
        GL11.glDisableClientState(GL11.GL_VERTEX_ARRAY);
        GlStateManager.shadeModel(GL11.GL_FLAT);
        GlStateManager.enableAlpha();
        GlStateManager.enableDepth();
        GlStateManager.enableCull();
        GlStateManager.color(1, 1, 1, 1);
        GlStateManager.popMatrix();
    }

    private static final class GlTexture implements Texture {
        final int id, width, height;

        GlTexture(int id, int width, int height) {
            this.id = id;
            this.width = width;
            this.height = height;
        }

        @Override
        public int getWidth() {
            return width;
        }

        @Override
        public int getHeight() {
            return height;
        }
    }
}
```

`platforms/forge-1.7.10/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/PlatformImpl.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform;

import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiScreen;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.ScaledResolution;
import net.minecraftforge.common.MinecraftForge;
import cpw.mods.fml.common.Loader;
import cpw.mods.fml.common.ModContainer;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.forge1710.ForgeHooks;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.forge1710.IcarusScreen;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.forge1710.LegacyRenderer;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.MenuController;

import java.io.File;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.Collections;

/** Forge 1.7.10. */
@SuppressWarnings("unused")
public class PlatformImpl implements Platform {
    private final Renderer renderer = new LegacyRenderer();

    @Override
    public void install() {
        ForgeHooks hooks = new ForgeHooks();
        MinecraftForge.EVENT_BUS.register(hooks); //render overlay events
        cpw.mods.fml.common.FMLCommonHandler.instance().bus().register(hooks); //input and tick events
    }

    @Override
    public Renderer getRenderer() {
        return renderer;
    }

    @Override
    public double getGuiScale() {
        return new ScaledResolution(Minecraft.getMinecraft(), Minecraft.getMinecraft().displayWidth, Minecraft.getMinecraft().displayHeight).getScaleFactor();
    }

    @Override
    public int getFramebufferWidth() {
        return Minecraft.getMinecraft().displayWidth;
    }

    @Override
    public int getFramebufferHeight() {
        return Minecraft.getMinecraft().displayHeight;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isInGame() {
        return Minecraft.getMinecraft().theWorld != null && Minecraft.getMinecraft().thePlayer != null;
    }

    @Override
    public void openScreen(MenuController menu) {
        Minecraft.getMinecraft().displayGuiScreen(new IcarusScreen(menu));
    }

    @Override
    public File getGameDir() {
        return Minecraft.getMinecraft().mcDataDir;
    }

    @Override
    public Collection<Path> getModRoots(String modid) {
        return Collections.singletonList(container(modid).getSource().toPath());
    }

    @Override
    public ClassLoader getModClassLoader(String modid) {
        return container(modid).getMod().getClass().getClassLoader();
    }

    @Override
    public String getClipboard() {
        return GuiScreen.getClipboardString();
    }

    @Override
    public void setClipboard(String text) {
        GuiScreen.setClipboardString(text);
    }

    private static ModContainer container(String modid) {
        ModContainer container = Loader.instance().getIndexedModList().get(modid);
        if (container == null) throw new IllegalArgumentException("No mod with id " + modid);
        return container;
    }
}
```

`platforms/forge-1.7.10/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/forge1710/ForgeHooks.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.forge1710;

import net.minecraftforge.client.event.RenderGameOverlayEvent;
import cpw.mods.fml.common.eventhandler.SubscribeEvent;
import cpw.mods.fml.common.gameevent.InputEvent;
import cpw.mods.fml.common.gameevent.TickEvent;
import org.lwjgl.input.Keyboard;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.Icarus;

public class ForgeHooks {
    @SubscribeEvent
    public void onKeyInput(InputEvent.KeyInputEvent e) {
        if (Keyboard.getEventKeyState()) { //if the key is down
            Icarus.onKeyPressed(Keyboard.getEventKey()); //LWJGL2 codes are Icarus' codes
        } else {
            Icarus.onKeyReleased(Keyboard.getEventKey());
        }
    }

    @SubscribeEvent
    public void onOverlayText(RenderGameOverlayEvent.Text e) {
        Icarus.renderHud(e.partialTicks);
    }

    @SubscribeEvent
    public void onClientTick(TickEvent.ClientTickEvent e) {
        if (e.phase == TickEvent.Phase.START) Icarus.clientTick();
    }
}

```

`platforms/forge-1.7.10/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/forge1710/IcarusScreen.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.forge1710;

import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiScreen;
import org.lwjgl.input.Keyboard;
import org.lwjgl.input.Mouse;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.input.Keys;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.MenuController;


/** The Minecraft screen showing an Icarus menu; forwards everything to the {@link MenuController}. */
public class IcarusScreen extends GuiScreen {
    private final MenuController menu;

    public IcarusScreen(MenuController menu) {
        this.menu = menu;
    }

    @Override
    public void initGui() {
        Keyboard.enableRepeatEvents(true);
        menu.opened();
    }

    /** Mouse position in (fractional) GUI units. */
    private double mouseX() {
        return Mouse.getX() * (double) width / mc.displayWidth;
    }

    private double mouseY() {
        return height - Mouse.getY() * (double) height / mc.displayHeight - 1;
    }

    @Override
    public void drawScreen(int mouseX, int mouseY, float partialTicks) {
        drawDefaultBackground();
        menu.render(mouseX(), mouseY(), partialTicks);
        if (menu.isFadedOut()) mc.displayGuiScreen(null);
    }

    @Override
    protected void mouseClicked(int mouseX, int mouseY, int mouseButton) {
        menu.mousePressed(mouseX(), mouseY(), mouseButton);
    }

    @Override
    protected void mouseReleased(int mouseX, int mouseY, int state) {
        if (state != -1) menu.mouseReleased(mouseX(), mouseY(), state); //1.7.10 also reports plain movement as -1
    }

    @Override
    public void handleMouseInput() {
        super.handleMouseInput();
        int wheel = Mouse.getEventDWheel();
        if (wheel != 0) menu.mouseScrolled(mouseX(), mouseY(), Math.signum(wheel));
    }

    @Override
    protected void keyTyped(char typedChar, int keyCode) {
        if (keyCode == Keys.KEY_ESCAPE) {
            if (!menu.keyPressed(keyCode)) menu.requestClose(); //fades out, then closes in drawScreen
            return;
        }
        menu.keyPressed(keyCode);
        if (typedChar != 0 && !Character.isISOControl(typedChar)) menu.charTyped(typedChar);
    }

    @Override
    public void onGuiClosed() {
        Keyboard.enableRepeatEvents(false);
        menu.closed();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean doesGuiPauseGame() {
        return false;
    }
}

```

`platforms/forge-1.7.10/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/forge1710/LegacyRenderer.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.forge1710;

import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.ScaledResolution;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.OpenGlHelper;
import org.lwjgl.BufferUtils;
import org.lwjgl.opengl.GL11;
import org.lwjgl.opengl.GL12;
import org.lwjgl.opengl.GL15;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Renderer;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Texture;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.DrawList;

import java.nio.ByteBuffer;
import java.nio.ByteOrder;
import java.nio.IntBuffer;

/**
 * Draws Icarus' draw lists with client-side vertex arrays (one glDrawElements per texture), with raw GL11 state calls
 * (1.7.10 has no GlStateManager).
 */
public class LegacyRenderer implements Renderer {
    private static final int STRIDE = 20; //x, y, u, v floats + RGBA bytes

    private ByteBuffer vertices = BufferUtils.createByteBuffer(STRIDE * 4096);
    private IntBuffer indices = BufferUtils.createIntBuffer(6144);
    private IntBuffer pixels = BufferUtils.createIntBuffer(64 * 64);

    @Override
    public Texture createTexture(int width, int height) {
        int id = GL11.glGenTextures();
        GL11.glBindTexture(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, id);
        GL11.glTexParameteri(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL11.GL_TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER, GL11.GL_NEAREST);
        GL11.glTexParameteri(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL11.GL_TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER, GL11.GL_NEAREST);
        GL11.glTexParameteri(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL11.GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_S, GL12.GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
        GL11.glTexParameteri(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL11.GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_T, GL12.GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
        GL11.glTexImage2D(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, 0, GL11.GL_RGBA8, width, height, 0, GL12.GL_BGRA, GL12.GL_UNSIGNED_INT_8_8_8_8_REV, (IntBuffer) null);
        return new GlTexture(id, width, height);
    }

    @Override
    public void upload(Texture texture, int x, int y, int width, int height, int[] argb, int offset, int stride) {
        int count = width * height;
        if (pixels.capacity() < count) pixels = BufferUtils.createIntBuffer(count);
        pixels.clear();
        for (int row = 0; row < height; row++) pixels.put(argb, offset + row * stride, width);
        pixels.flip();
        GL11.glBindTexture(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, ((GlTexture) texture).id);
        GL11.glPixelStorei(GL11.GL_UNPACK_ALIGNMENT, 4);
        GL11.glTexSubImage2D(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, 0, x, y, width, height, GL12.GL_BGRA, GL12.GL_UNSIGNED_INT_8_8_8_8_REV, pixels);
    }

    @Override
    public void deleteTexture(Texture texture) {
        GL11.glDeleteTextures(((GlTexture) texture).id);
    }

    @Override
    public void render(DrawList list) {
        int vertexCount = list.getVertexCount();
        if (vertices.capacity() < vertexCount * STRIDE) vertices = BufferUtils.createByteBuffer(vertexCount * STRIDE * 2).order(ByteOrder.nativeOrder());
        vertices.clear();
        float[] xy = list.getPositions(), uv = list.getUvs();
        int[] colors = list.getColors();
        for (int i = 0; i < vertexCount; i++) {
            int c = colors[i];
            vertices.putFloat(xy[i * 2]).putFloat(xy[i * 2 + 1]).putFloat(uv[i * 2]).putFloat(uv[i * 2 + 1])
                    .put((byte) (c >> 16)).put((byte) (c >> 8)).put((byte) c).put((byte) (c >>> 24));
        }
        int indexCount = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < list.getCommandCount(); i++) indexCount = Math.max(indexCount, list.getCommandIndexStart(i) + list.getCommandIndexCount(i));
        if (indices.capacity() < indexCount) indices = BufferUtils.createIntBuffer(indexCount * 2);
        indices.clear();
        indices.put(list.getIndices(), 0, indexCount);

        double scale = new ScaledResolution(Minecraft.getMinecraft(), Minecraft.getMinecraft().displayWidth, Minecraft.getMinecraft().displayHeight).getScaleFactor();
        GL11.glPushMatrix();
        GL11.glScaled(1 / scale, 1 / scale, 1);
        GL11.glEnable(GL11.GL_BLEND);
        OpenGlHelper.glBlendFunc(GL11.GL_SRC_ALPHA, GL11.GL_ONE_MINUS_SRC_ALPHA, GL11.GL_ONE, GL11.GL_ONE_MINUS_SRC_ALPHA);
        GL11.glDisable(GL11.GL_ALPHA_TEST);
        GL11.glDisable(GL11.GL_LIGHTING);
        GL11.glDisable(GL11.GL_DEPTH_TEST);
        GL11.glDisable(GL11.GL_CULL_FACE);
        GL11.glEnable(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D);
        GL11.glShadeModel(GL11.GL_SMOOTH);
        GL11.glColor4f(1, 1, 1, 1);
        GL15.glBindBuffer(GL15.GL_ARRAY_BUFFER, 0);

        GL11.glEnableClientState(GL11.GL_VERTEX_ARRAY);
        GL11.glEnableClientState(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_COORD_ARRAY);
        GL11.glEnableClientState(GL11.GL_COLOR_ARRAY);
        vertices.position(0);
        GL11.glVertexPointer(2, GL11.GL_FLOAT, STRIDE, vertices);
        vertices.position(8);
        GL11.glTexCoordPointer(2, GL11.GL_FLOAT, STRIDE, vertices);
        vertices.position(16);
        GL11.glColorPointer(4, true, STRIDE, vertices);

        for (int i = 0; i < list.getCommandCount(); i++) {
            int start = list.getCommandIndexStart(i), count = list.getCommandIndexCount(i);
            if (count == 0) continue;
            GL11.glBindTexture(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, ((GlTexture) list.getCommandTexture(i)).id);
            indices.limit(start + count).position(start);
            GL11.glDrawElements(GL11.GL_TRIANGLES, indices);
        }

        GL11.glDisableClientState(GL11.GL_COLOR_ARRAY);
        GL11.glDisableClientState(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_COORD_ARRAY);
        GL11.glDisableClientState(GL11.GL_VERTEX_ARRAY);
        GL11.glShadeModel(GL11.GL_FLAT);
        GL11.glEnable(GL11.GL_ALPHA_TEST);
        GL11.glEnable(GL11.GL_DEPTH_TEST);
        GL11.glEnable(GL11.GL_CULL_FACE);
        GL11.glColor4f(1, 1, 1, 1);
        GL11.glPopMatrix();
    }

    private static final class GlTexture implements Texture {
        final int id, width, height;

        GlTexture(int id, int width, int height) {
            this.id = id;
            this.width = width;
            this.height = height;
        }

        @Override
        public int getWidth() {
            return width;
        }

        @Override
        public int getHeight() {
            return height;
        }
    }
}
```

`platforms/forge-1.8.9/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/PlatformImpl.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform;

import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiScreen;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.ScaledResolution;
import net.minecraftforge.common.MinecraftForge;
import net.minecraftforge.fml.common.Loader;
import net.minecraftforge.fml.common.ModContainer;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.forge189.ForgeHooks;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.forge189.IcarusScreen;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.forge189.LegacyRenderer;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.MenuController;

import java.io.File;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.Collections;

/** Forge 1.8.9. */
@SuppressWarnings("unused")
public class PlatformImpl implements Platform {
    private final Renderer renderer = new LegacyRenderer();

    @Override
    public void install() {
        MinecraftForge.EVENT_BUS.register(new ForgeHooks()); //1.8.9's FML bus is the same bus
    }

    @Override
    public Renderer getRenderer() {
        return renderer;
    }

    @Override
    public double getGuiScale() {
        return new ScaledResolution(Minecraft.getMinecraft()).getScaleFactor();
    }

    @Override
    public int getFramebufferWidth() {
        return Minecraft.getMinecraft().displayWidth;
    }

    @Override
    public int getFramebufferHeight() {
        return Minecraft.getMinecraft().displayHeight;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isInGame() {
        return Minecraft.getMinecraft().theWorld != null && Minecraft.getMinecraft().thePlayer != null;
    }

    @Override
    public void openScreen(MenuController menu) {
        Minecraft.getMinecraft().displayGuiScreen(new IcarusScreen(menu));
    }

    @Override
    public File getGameDir() {
        return Minecraft.getMinecraft().mcDataDir;
    }

    @Override
    public Collection<Path> getModRoots(String modid) {
        return Collections.singletonList(container(modid).getSource().toPath());
    }

    @Override
    public ClassLoader getModClassLoader(String modid) {
        return container(modid).getMod().getClass().getClassLoader();
    }

    @Override
    public String getClipboard() {
        return GuiScreen.getClipboardString();
    }

    @Override
    public void setClipboard(String text) {
        GuiScreen.setClipboardString(text);
    }

    private static ModContainer container(String modid) {
        ModContainer container = Loader.instance().getIndexedModList().get(modid);
        if (container == null) throw new IllegalArgumentException("No mod with id " + modid);
        return container;
    }
}
```

`platforms/forge-1.8.9/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/forge189/ForgeHooks.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.forge189;

import net.minecraftforge.client.event.RenderGameOverlayEvent;
import net.minecraftforge.fml.common.eventhandler.SubscribeEvent;
import net.minecraftforge.fml.common.gameevent.InputEvent;
import net.minecraftforge.fml.common.gameevent.TickEvent;
import org.lwjgl.input.Keyboard;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.Icarus;

public class ForgeHooks {
    @SubscribeEvent
    public void onKeyInput(InputEvent.KeyInputEvent e) {
        if (Keyboard.getEventKeyState()) { //if the key is down
            Icarus.onKeyPressed(Keyboard.getEventKey()); //LWJGL2 codes are Icarus' codes
        } else {
            Icarus.onKeyReleased(Keyboard.getEventKey());
        }
    }

    @SubscribeEvent
    public void onOverlayText(RenderGameOverlayEvent.Text e) {
        Icarus.renderHud(e.partialTicks);
    }

    @SubscribeEvent
    public void onClientTick(TickEvent.ClientTickEvent e) {
        if (e.phase == TickEvent.Phase.START) Icarus.clientTick();
    }
}
```

`platforms/forge-1.8.9/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/forge189/IcarusScreen.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.forge189;

import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiScreen;
import org.lwjgl.input.Keyboard;
import org.lwjgl.input.Mouse;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.input.Keys;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.MenuController;

import java.io.IOException;

/** The Minecraft screen showing an Icarus menu; forwards everything to the {@link MenuController}. */
public class IcarusScreen extends GuiScreen {
    private final MenuController menu;

    public IcarusScreen(MenuController menu) {
        this.menu = menu;
    }

    @Override
    public void initGui() {
        Keyboard.enableRepeatEvents(true);
        menu.opened();
    }

    /** Mouse position in (fractional) GUI units. */
    private double mouseX() {
        return Mouse.getX() * (double) width / mc.displayWidth;
    }

    private double mouseY() {
        return height - Mouse.getY() * (double) height / mc.displayHeight - 1;
    }

    @Override
    public void drawScreen(int mouseX, int mouseY, float partialTicks) {
        drawDefaultBackground();
        menu.render(mouseX(), mouseY(), partialTicks);
        if (menu.isFadedOut()) mc.displayGuiScreen(null);
    }

    @Override
    protected void mouseClicked(int mouseX, int mouseY, int mouseButton) {
        menu.mousePressed(mouseX(), mouseY(), mouseButton);
    }

    @Override
    protected void mouseReleased(int mouseX, int mouseY, int state) {
        menu.mouseReleased(mouseX(), mouseY(), state);
    }

    @Override
    public void handleMouseInput() throws IOException {
        super.handleMouseInput();
        int wheel = Mouse.getEventDWheel();
        if (wheel != 0) menu.mouseScrolled(mouseX(), mouseY(), Math.signum(wheel));
    }

    @Override
    protected void keyTyped(char typedChar, int keyCode) throws IOException {
        if (keyCode == Keys.KEY_ESCAPE) {
            if (!menu.keyPressed(keyCode)) menu.requestClose(); //fades out, then closes in drawScreen
            return;
        }
        menu.keyPressed(keyCode);
        if (typedChar != 0 && !Character.isISOControl(typedChar)) menu.charTyped(typedChar);
    }

    @Override
    public void onGuiClosed() {
        Keyboard.enableRepeatEvents(false);
        menu.closed();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean doesGuiPauseGame() {
        return false;
    }
}
```

`platforms/forge-1.8.9/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/forge189/LegacyRenderer.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.forge189;

import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.ScaledResolution;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.GlStateManager;
import org.lwjgl.BufferUtils;
import org.lwjgl.opengl.GL11;
import org.lwjgl.opengl.GL12;
import org.lwjgl.opengl.GL15;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Renderer;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Texture;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.DrawList;

import java.nio.ByteBuffer;
import java.nio.ByteOrder;
import java.nio.IntBuffer;

/**
 * Draws Icarus' draw lists with client-side vertex arrays (one glDrawElements per texture), going through
 * GlStateManager for state it caches.
 */
public class LegacyRenderer implements Renderer {
    private static final int STRIDE = 20; //x, y, u, v floats + RGBA bytes

    private ByteBuffer vertices = BufferUtils.createByteBuffer(STRIDE * 4096);
    private IntBuffer indices = BufferUtils.createIntBuffer(6144);
    private IntBuffer pixels = BufferUtils.createIntBuffer(64 * 64);

    @Override
    public Texture createTexture(int width, int height) {
        int id = GL11.glGenTextures();
        GlStateManager.bindTexture(id);
        GL11.glTexParameteri(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL11.GL_TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER, GL11.GL_NEAREST);
        GL11.glTexParameteri(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL11.GL_TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER, GL11.GL_NEAREST);
        GL11.glTexParameteri(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL11.GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_S, GL12.GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
        GL11.glTexParameteri(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL11.GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_T, GL12.GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
        GL11.glTexImage2D(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, 0, GL11.GL_RGBA8, width, height, 0, GL12.GL_BGRA, GL12.GL_UNSIGNED_INT_8_8_8_8_REV, (IntBuffer) null);
        return new GlTexture(id, width, height);
    }

    @Override
    public void upload(Texture texture, int x, int y, int width, int height, int[] argb, int offset, int stride) {
        int count = width * height;
        if (pixels.capacity() < count) pixels = BufferUtils.createIntBuffer(count);
        pixels.clear();
        for (int row = 0; row < height; row++) pixels.put(argb, offset + row * stride, width);
        pixels.flip();
        GlStateManager.bindTexture(((GlTexture) texture).id);
        GL11.glPixelStorei(GL11.GL_UNPACK_ALIGNMENT, 4);
        GL11.glTexSubImage2D(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, 0, x, y, width, height, GL12.GL_BGRA, GL12.GL_UNSIGNED_INT_8_8_8_8_REV, pixels);
    }

    @Override
    public void deleteTexture(Texture texture) {
        GlStateManager.deleteTexture(((GlTexture) texture).id);
    }

    @Override
    public void render(DrawList list) {
        int vertexCount = list.getVertexCount();
        if (vertices.capacity() < vertexCount * STRIDE) vertices = BufferUtils.createByteBuffer(vertexCount * STRIDE * 2).order(ByteOrder.nativeOrder());
        vertices.clear();
        float[] xy = list.getPositions(), uv = list.getUvs();
        int[] colors = list.getColors();
        for (int i = 0; i < vertexCount; i++) {
            int c = colors[i];
            vertices.putFloat(xy[i * 2]).putFloat(xy[i * 2 + 1]).putFloat(uv[i * 2]).putFloat(uv[i * 2 + 1])
                    .put((byte) (c >> 16)).put((byte) (c >> 8)).put((byte) c).put((byte) (c >>> 24));
        }
        int indexCount = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < list.getCommandCount(); i++) indexCount = Math.max(indexCount, list.getCommandIndexStart(i) + list.getCommandIndexCount(i));
        if (indices.capacity() < indexCount) indices = BufferUtils.createIntBuffer(indexCount * 2);
        indices.clear();
        indices.put(list.getIndices(), 0, indexCount);

        double scale = new ScaledResolution(Minecraft.getMinecraft()).getScaleFactor();
        GlStateManager.pushMatrix();
        GlStateManager.scale(1 / scale, 1 / scale, 1);
        GlStateManager.enableBlend();
        GlStateManager.tryBlendFuncSeparate(GL11.GL_SRC_ALPHA, GL11.GL_ONE_MINUS_SRC_ALPHA, GL11.GL_ONE, GL11.GL_ONE_MINUS_SRC_ALPHA);
        GlStateManager.disableAlpha();
        GlStateManager.disableLighting();
        GlStateManager.disableDepth();
        GlStateManager.disableCull();
        GlStateManager.enableTexture2D();
        GlStateManager.shadeModel(GL11.GL_SMOOTH);
        GlStateManager.color(1, 1, 1, 1);
        GL15.glBindBuffer(GL15.GL_ARRAY_BUFFER, 0);

        GL11.glEnableClientState(GL11.GL_VERTEX_ARRAY);
        GL11.glEnableClientState(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_COORD_ARRAY);
        GL11.glEnableClientState(GL11.GL_COLOR_ARRAY);
        vertices.position(0);
        GL11.glVertexPointer(2, GL11.GL_FLOAT, STRIDE, vertices);
        vertices.position(8);
        GL11.glTexCoordPointer(2, GL11.GL_FLOAT, STRIDE, vertices);
        vertices.position(16);
        GL11.glColorPointer(4, true, STRIDE, vertices);

        for (int i = 0; i < list.getCommandCount(); i++) {
            int start = list.getCommandIndexStart(i), count = list.getCommandIndexCount(i);
            if (count == 0) continue;
            GlStateManager.bindTexture(((GlTexture) list.getCommandTexture(i)).id);
            indices.limit(start + count).position(start);
            GL11.glDrawElements(GL11.GL_TRIANGLES, indices);
        }

        GL11.glDisableClientState(GL11.GL_COLOR_ARRAY);
        GL11.glDisableClientState(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_COORD_ARRAY);
        GL11.glDisableClientState(GL11.GL_VERTEX_ARRAY);
        GlStateManager.shadeModel(GL11.GL_FLAT);
        GlStateManager.enableAlpha();
        GlStateManager.enableDepth();
        GlStateManager.enableCull();
        GlStateManager.color(1, 1, 1, 1);
        GlStateManager.popMatrix();
    }

    private static final class GlTexture implements Texture {
        final int id, width, height;

        GlTexture(int id, int width, int height) {
            this.id = id;
            this.width = width;
            this.height = height;
        }

        @Override
        public int getWidth() {
            return width;
        }

        @Override
        public int getHeight() {
            return height;
        }
    }
}
```

`platforms/mc-1.16.5/common/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/mc1165/IcarusScreen.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.mc1165;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.screens.Screen;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.TextComponent;
import org.lwjgl.glfw.GLFW;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.input.Keys;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.MenuController;

/** The Minecraft screen showing an Icarus menu; forwards everything to the {@link MenuController}. */
public class IcarusScreen extends Screen {
    private final MenuController menu;
    private final ModernRenderer renderer;
    private char highSurrogate;

    public IcarusScreen(MenuController menu, ModernRenderer renderer) {
        super(new TextComponent(""));
        this.menu = menu;
        this.renderer = renderer;
    }

    @Override
    protected void init() {
        menu.opened();
    }

    /** Mouse position in fractional GUI units (the int arguments are rounded). */
    private double mouseX() {
        return minecraft.mouseHandler.xpos() * width / minecraft.getWindow().getScreenWidth();
    }

    private double mouseY() {
        return minecraft.mouseHandler.ypos() * height / minecraft.getWindow().getScreenHeight();
    }

    @Override
    public void render(PoseStack poseStack, int mouseX, int mouseY, float partialTick) {
        renderBackground(poseStack); //1.16.5's Screen#render doesn't draw the background itself
        super.render(poseStack, mouseX, mouseY, partialTick);
        renderer.begin(poseStack);
        menu.render(mouseX(), mouseY(), partialTick);
        if (menu.isFadedOut()) onClose();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean mouseClicked(double mouseX, double mouseY, int button) {
        menu.mousePressed(mouseX, mouseY, button);
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean mouseReleased(double mouseX, double mouseY, int button) {
        menu.mouseReleased(mouseX, mouseY, button);
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean mouseScrolled(double mouseX, double mouseY, double delta) {
        menu.mouseScrolled(mouseX, mouseY, delta);
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean keyPressed(int keyCode, int scanCode, int modifiers) {
        if (keyCode == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE) {
            if (!menu.keyPressed(Keys.KEY_ESCAPE)) menu.requestClose(); //fades out, then closes below
            return true;
        }
        menu.keyPressed(Keys.fromGlfw(keyCode));
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean charTyped(char c, int modifiers) {
        if (Character.isHighSurrogate(c)) {
            highSurrogate = c;
            return true;
        }
        if (Character.isLowSurrogate(c) && highSurrogate != 0) {
            menu.charTyped(Character.toCodePoint(highSurrogate, c));
        } else {
            menu.charTyped(c);
        }
        highSurrogate = 0;
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public void removed() {
        menu.closed();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isPauseScreen() {
        return false;
    }
}

```

`platforms/mc-1.16.5/common/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/mc1165/ModernPlatform.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.mc1165;

import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Platform;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Renderer;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.MenuController;

import java.io.File;

/** The loader-independent half of the 1.16.5 platforms. */
public abstract class ModernPlatform implements Platform {
    protected final ModernRenderer renderer = new ModernRenderer();

    @Override
    public Renderer getRenderer() {
        return renderer;
    }

    @Override
    public double getGuiScale() {
        return Minecraft.getInstance().getWindow().getGuiScale();
    }

    @Override
    public int getFramebufferWidth() {
        return Minecraft.getInstance().getWindow().getWidth();
    }

    @Override
    public int getFramebufferHeight() {
        return Minecraft.getInstance().getWindow().getHeight();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isInGame() {
        return Minecraft.getInstance().level != null && Minecraft.getInstance().player != null;
    }

    @Override
    public void openScreen(MenuController menu) {
        Minecraft.getInstance().setScreen(new IcarusScreen(menu, renderer));
    }

    @Override
    public File getGameDir() {
        return Minecraft.getInstance().gameDirectory;
    }

    @Override
    public String getClipboard() {
        return Minecraft.getInstance().keyboardHandler.getClipboard();
    }

    @Override
    public void setClipboard(String text) {
        Minecraft.getInstance().keyboardHandler.setClipboard(text);
    }
}
```

`platforms/mc-1.16.5/common/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/mc1165/ModernRenderer.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.mc1165;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.platform.GlStateManager;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.platform.TextureUtil;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.systems.RenderSystem;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.BufferBuilder;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.BufferUploader;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.DefaultVertexFormat;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.Tesselator;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack;
import com.mojang.math.Matrix4f;
import org.lwjgl.opengl.GL11;
import org.lwjgl.opengl.GL12;
import org.lwjgl.system.MemoryUtil;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Renderer;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Texture;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.DrawList;

import java.nio.IntBuffer;

/** 1.16.5: draw lists as fixed-function triangles (no shaders yet), transformed by the frame's PoseStack. */
public class ModernRenderer implements Renderer {
    private PoseStack poseStack;
    private IntBuffer pixels = MemoryUtil.memAllocInt(64 * 64);

    /** Binds the renderer to the frame's PoseStack; must be called before Icarus draws anything. */
    public void begin(PoseStack poseStack) {
        this.poseStack = poseStack;
    }

    @Override
    public Texture createTexture(int width, int height) {
        int id = TextureUtil.generateTextureId();
        GlStateManager._bindTexture(id);
        GL11.glTexParameteri(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL11.GL_TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER, GL11.GL_NEAREST);
        GL11.glTexParameteri(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL11.GL_TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER, GL11.GL_NEAREST);
        GL11.glTexParameteri(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL11.GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_S, GL12.GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
        GL11.glTexParameteri(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL11.GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_T, GL12.GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
        GL11.glTexImage2D(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, 0, GL11.GL_RGBA8, width, height, 0, GL12.GL_BGRA, GL12.GL_UNSIGNED_INT_8_8_8_8_REV, (IntBuffer) null);
        return new GlTexture(id, width, height);
    }

    @Override
    public void upload(Texture texture, int x, int y, int width, int height, int[] argb, int offset, int stride) {
        int count = width * height;
        if (pixels.capacity() < count) {
            MemoryUtil.memFree(pixels);
            pixels = MemoryUtil.memAllocInt(count);
        }
        pixels.clear();
        for (int row = 0; row < height; row++) pixels.put(argb, offset + row * stride, width);
        pixels.flip();
        GlStateManager._bindTexture(((GlTexture) texture).id);
        GlStateManager._pixelStore(GL11.GL_UNPACK_ROW_LENGTH, 0);
        GlStateManager._pixelStore(GL11.GL_UNPACK_SKIP_PIXELS, 0);
        GlStateManager._pixelStore(GL11.GL_UNPACK_SKIP_ROWS, 0);
        GlStateManager._pixelStore(GL11.GL_UNPACK_ALIGNMENT, 4);
        GL11.glTexSubImage2D(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, 0, x, y, width, height, GL12.GL_BGRA, GL12.GL_UNSIGNED_INT_8_8_8_8_REV, pixels);
    }

    @Override
    public void deleteTexture(Texture texture) {
        TextureUtil.releaseTextureId(((GlTexture) texture).id);
    }

    @Override
    public void render(DrawList list) {
        float scale = (float) Minecraft.getInstance().getWindow().getGuiScale();
        poseStack.pushPose();
        poseStack.scale(1 / scale, 1 / scale, 1);
        Matrix4f pose = poseStack.last().pose();

        RenderSystem.enableBlend();
        RenderSystem.defaultBlendFunc();
        RenderSystem.disableAlphaTest();
        RenderSystem.disableCull();
        RenderSystem.enableTexture();
        RenderSystem.shadeModel(GL11.GL_SMOOTH);

        float[] xy = list.getPositions(), uv = list.getUvs();
        int[] colors = list.getColors(), indices = list.getIndices();
        for (int command = 0; command < list.getCommandCount(); command++) {
            int start = list.getCommandIndexStart(command), count = list.getCommandIndexCount(command);
            if (count == 0) continue;
            RenderSystem.bindTexture(((GlTexture) list.getCommandTexture(command)).id);
            BufferBuilder buffer = Tesselator.getInstance().getBuilder();
            buffer.begin(GL11.GL_TRIANGLES, DefaultVertexFormat.POSITION_COLOR_TEX);
            for (int i = start; i < start + count; i++) {
                int v = indices[i], c = colors[v];
                buffer.vertex(pose, xy[v * 2], xy[v * 2 + 1], 0).color(c >> 16 & 255, c >> 8 & 255, c & 255, c >>> 24).uv(uv[v * 2], uv[v * 2 + 1]).endVertex();
            }
            buffer.end();
            BufferUploader.end(buffer);
        }

        RenderSystem.shadeModel(GL11.GL_FLAT);
        RenderSystem.enableAlphaTest();
        RenderSystem.enableCull();
        RenderSystem.disableBlend();
        poseStack.popPose();
    }

    private static final class GlTexture implements Texture {
        final int id, width, height;

        GlTexture(int id, int width, int height) {
            this.id = id;
            this.width = width;
            this.height = height;
        }

        @Override
        public int getWidth() {
            return width;
        }

        @Override
        public int getHeight() {
            return height;
        }
    }
}

```

```kts
plugins {
    java
    id("xyz.wagyourtail.unimined")

base {
    archivesName.set("icarus-fabric-1.16.5")
}

java {
    toolchain.languageVersion.set(JavaLanguageVersion.of(8))
}

tasks.withType<JavaCompile>().configureEach {
    options.encoding = "UTF-8"
}

// dev-only example mod, launched next to the library by runTestmodClient
val testmod: SourceSet by sourceSets.creating

sourceSets {
    main {
        // Minecraft-free core + the code shared by every 1.16.5 loader
        java.srcDir(rootProject.file("core/src/main/java"))
        resources.srcDir(rootProject.file("core/src/main/resources"))
        java.srcDir(file("../common/java"))
    }
    testmod.java.srcDir(rootProject.file("testmod/src/main/java"))
}

unimined.minecraft {
    version("1.16.5")

    mappings {
        mojmap()
    }

    fabric {
        loader("0.17.3")
    }
}

// not combineWith(main): that would put both in one fabric classpath group, i.e. one mod, and hide testmod
unimined.minecraft(testmod) {
    version("1.16.5")

    mappings {
        mojmap()
    }

    fabric {
        loader("0.17.3")
    }

    defaultRemapJar = false
}

testmod.compileClasspath += sourceSets.main.get().output
testmod.runtimeClasspath += sourceSets.main.get().output

tasks.processResources {
    inputs.property("version", project.version)
    filesMatching("fabric.mod.json") {
        expand("version" to project.version)
    }
}

dependencies {
    val lombok = "org.projectlombok:lombok:${providers.gradleProperty("lombok_version").get()}"
    compileOnly(lombok)
    annotationProcessor(lombok)
    "testmodCompileOnly"(lombok)
    "testmodAnnotationProcessor"(lombok)
}

// unimined 1.x adds the source set output to the jar a second time
tasks.jar { duplicatesStrategy = DuplicatesStrategy.EXCLUDE }
```

`platforms/mc-1.16.5/fabric/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/PlatformImpl.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform;

import net.fabricmc.loader.api.FabricLoader;
import net.fabricmc.loader.api.ModContainer;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.PoseStack;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.Icarus;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.mc1165.ModernPlatform;

import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.Collection;

/**
 * Fabric 1.16.5. Hooks are mixins (studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.mc1165.fabric.mixin) rather than fabric-api
 * events, so Icarus works without fabric-api; they only forward once {@link #install()} ran.
 */
@SuppressWarnings("unused")
public class PlatformImpl extends ModernPlatform {
    private static PlatformImpl installed;

    @Override
    public void install() {
        installed = this;
    }

    /** Called by GuiMixin at the end of the HUD render. */
    public static void renderHud(PoseStack poseStack, float partialTick) {
        if (installed == null) return;
        installed.renderer.begin(poseStack);
        Icarus.renderHud(partialTick);
    }

    /** Called by MinecraftMixin at the start of every client tick. */
    public static void clientTick() {
        if (installed != null) Icarus.clientTick();
    }

    /** Called by KeyboardHandlerMixin for key presses while no screen is open. */
    public static void keyPressed(int key) {
        if (installed != null) Icarus.onKeyPressed(key);
    }

    /** Called by KeyboardHandlerMixin for key releases. */
    public static void keyReleased(int key) {
        if (installed != null) Icarus.onKeyReleased(key);
    }

    @Override
    public Collection<Path> getModRoots(String modid) {
        return container(modid).getRootPaths();
    }

    @Override
    public ClassLoader getModClassLoader(String modid) {
        return PlatformImpl.class.getClassLoader(); //Knot loads every mod with the same class loader
    }

    private static ModContainer container(String modid) {
        return FabricLoader.getInstance().getModContainer(modid).orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("No mod with id " + modid));
    }
}
```

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.mc1165.fabric.mixin;
```

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.mc1165.fabric.mixin;

import net.minecraft.client.KeyboardHandler;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import org.lwjgl.glfw.GLFW;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.PlatformImpl;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.input.Keys;

/** Fabric has no raw key event; this mirrors Forge's KeyInputEvent: key presses while no screen is open. */
@Mixin(KeyboardHandler.class)
public class KeyboardHandlerMixin {
    @Inject(method = "keyPress", at = @At("HEAD"))
    private void icarus$keyPress(long window, int key, int scancode, int action, int modifiers, CallbackInfo ci) {
        if (action == GLFW.GLFW_PRESS && window == Minecraft.getInstance().getWindow().getWindow() && Minecraft.getInstance().screen == null) {
            PlatformImpl.keyPressed(Keys.fromGlfw(key));
        } else if (action == GLFW.GLFW_RELEASE) {
            PlatformImpl.keyReleased(Keys.fromGlfw(key));
        }
    }
}
```

`platforms/mc-1.16.5/forge/build.gradle.kts`:

```kts
plugins {
    java
    id("xyz.wagyourtail.unimined")
}

base {
    archivesName.set("icarus-forge-1.16.5")
}

java {
    toolchain.languageVersion.set(JavaLanguageVersion.of(8))
}

tasks.withType<JavaCompile>().configureEach {
    options.encoding = "UTF-8"
}

repositories {
    maven("https://maven.minecraftforge.net/")
}

sourceSets {
    main {
        // Minecraft-free core + the code shared by every 1.16.5 loader
        java.srcDir(rootProject.file("core/src/main/java"))
        resources.srcDir(rootProject.file("core/src/main/resources"))
        java.srcDir(file("../common/java"))
        // the example mod is compiled in for dev runs and stripped from the published jar below
        java.srcDir(rootProject.file("testmod/src/main/java"))
    }
}

unimined.minecraft {
    version("1.16.5")

    mappings {
        mojmap()
    }

    minecraftForge {
        loader("36.2.42")
    }
}

tasks.jar {
    exclude("studio/dreamys/test/**")
    filesMatching("META-INF/mods.toml") {
        var inTestmod = false
        filter { line ->
            when {
                line.startsWith("# testmod-begin") -> { inTestmod = true; null }
                line.startsWith("# testmod-end") -> { inTestmod = false; null }
                inTestmod -> null
                else -> line
            }
        }
    }
}

tasks.processResources {
    inputs.property("version", project.version)
    filesMatching("META-INF/mods.toml") {
        expand("version" to project.version)
    }
}

dependencies {
    val lombok = "org.projectlombok:lombok:${providers.gradleProperty("lombok_version").get()}"
    compileOnly(lombok)
    annotationProcessor(lombok)
}
```

`platforms/mc-1.16.5/forge/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/PlatformImpl.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform;

import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraftforge.client.event.InputEvent;
import net.minecraftforge.client.event.RenderGameOverlayEvent;
import net.minecraftforge.common.MinecraftForge;
import net.minecraftforge.event.TickEvent;
import net.minecraftforge.fml.ModList;
import net.minecraftforge.fml.loading.moddiscovery.ModFileInfo;
import org.lwjgl.glfw.GLFW;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.Icarus;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.input.Keys;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.mc1165.ModernPlatform;

import java.io.File;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.nio.file.Paths;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;

/** Forge 1.16.5. */
@SuppressWarnings("unused")
public class PlatformImpl extends ModernPlatform {
    @Override
    public void install() {
        MinecraftForge.EVENT_BUS.addListener((InputEvent.KeyInputEvent e) -> {
            if (e.getAction() == GLFW.GLFW_PRESS && Minecraft.getInstance().screen == null) {
                Icarus.onKeyPressed(Keys.fromGlfw(e.getKey()));
            } else if (e.getAction() == GLFW.GLFW_RELEASE) {
                Icarus.onKeyReleased(Keys.fromGlfw(e.getKey()));
            }
        });
        MinecraftForge.EVENT_BUS.addListener((RenderGameOverlayEvent.Text e) -> {
            renderer.begin(e.getMatrixStack());
            Icarus.renderHud(e.getPartialTicks());
        });
        MinecraftForge.EVENT_BUS.addListener((TickEvent.ClientTickEvent e) -> {
            if (e.phase == TickEvent.Phase.START) Icarus.clientTick();
        });
    }

    @Override
    public Collection<Path> getModRoots(String modid) {
        ModFileInfo file = ModList.get().getModFileById(modid);
        if (file == null) throw new IllegalArgumentException("No mod with id " + modid);

        Path path = file.getFile().getFilePath();
        if (!Files.isDirectory(path)) return Collections.singletonList(path); //a mod jar

        //dev: an exploded mod spans several directories (classes, resources), listed in MOD_CLASSES as
        //"group%%path" entries; the mod file only reports the first one
        String modClasses = System.getenv("MOD_CLASSES");
        if (modClasses == null) return Collections.singletonList(path);

        List<String[]> entries = new ArrayList<>();
        String group = null;
        for (String entry : modClasses.split(File.pathSeparator)) {
            int sep = entry.indexOf("%%");
            String[] parsed = sep < 0 ? new String[]{"", entry} : new String[]{entry.substring(0, sep), entry.substring(sep + 2)};
            entries.add(parsed);
            if (Paths.get(parsed[1]).toAbsolutePath().normalize().equals(path.toAbsolutePath().normalize())) group = parsed[0];
        }
        if (group == null) return Collections.singletonList(path);

        List<Path> roots = new ArrayList<>();
        for (String[] entry : entries) {
            if (entry[0].equals(group)) roots.add(Paths.get(entry[1]));
        }
        return roots;
    }

    @Override
    public ClassLoader getModClassLoader(String modid) {
        return PlatformImpl.class.getClassLoader(); //every mod shares FML's transforming class loader
    }
}
```

`platforms/mc-1.20.1/common/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/mc1201/IcarusScreen.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.mc1201;

import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphics;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.screens.Screen;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import org.lwjgl.glfw.GLFW;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.input.Keys;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.MenuController;

/** The Minecraft screen showing an Icarus menu; forwards everything to the {@link MenuController}. */
public class IcarusScreen extends Screen {
    private final MenuController menu;
    private final ModernRenderer renderer;
    private char highSurrogate;

    public IcarusScreen(MenuController menu, ModernRenderer renderer) {
        super(Component.empty());
        this.menu = menu;
        this.renderer = renderer;
    }

    @Override
    public void init() { //public: fabric-api widens Screen#init, and widening is legal for the protected original too
        menu.opened();
    }

    /** Mouse position in fractional GUI units (the int arguments are rounded). */
    private double mouseX() {
        return minecraft.mouseHandler.xpos() * width / minecraft.getWindow().getScreenWidth();
    }

    private double mouseY() {
        return minecraft.mouseHandler.ypos() * height / minecraft.getWindow().getScreenHeight();
    }

    @Override
    public void render(GuiGraphics graphics, int mouseX, int mouseY, float partialTick) {
        renderBackground(graphics); //1.20.1's Screen#render doesn't draw the background itself
        super.render(graphics, mouseX, mouseY, partialTick);
        renderer.begin(graphics);
        menu.render(mouseX(), mouseY(), partialTick);
        if (menu.isFadedOut()) onClose();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean mouseClicked(double mouseX, double mouseY, int button) {
        menu.mousePressed(mouseX, mouseY, button);
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean mouseReleased(double mouseX, double mouseY, int button) {
        menu.mouseReleased(mouseX, mouseY, button);
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean mouseScrolled(double mouseX, double mouseY, double delta) {
        menu.mouseScrolled(mouseX, mouseY, delta);
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean keyPressed(int keyCode, int scanCode, int modifiers) {
        if (keyCode == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE) {
            if (!menu.keyPressed(Keys.KEY_ESCAPE)) menu.requestClose(); //fades out, then closes below
            return true;
        }
        menu.keyPressed(Keys.fromGlfw(keyCode));
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean charTyped(char c, int modifiers) {
        if (Character.isHighSurrogate(c)) {
            highSurrogate = c;
            return true;
        }
        if (Character.isLowSurrogate(c) && highSurrogate != 0) {
            menu.charTyped(Character.toCodePoint(highSurrogate, c));
        } else {
            menu.charTyped(c);
        }
        highSurrogate = 0;
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public void removed() {
        menu.closed();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isPauseScreen() {
        return false;
    }
}

```

`platforms/mc-1.20.1/common/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/mc1201/ModernPlatform.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.mc1201;

import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Platform;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Renderer;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.MenuController;

import java.io.File;

/** The loader-independent half of the 1.20.1 platforms. */
public abstract class ModernPlatform implements Platform {
    protected final ModernRenderer renderer = new ModernRenderer();

    @Override
    public Renderer getRenderer() {
        return renderer;
    }

    @Override
    public double getGuiScale() {
        return Minecraft.getInstance().getWindow().getGuiScale();
    }

    @Override
    public int getFramebufferWidth() {
        return Minecraft.getInstance().getWindow().getWidth();
    }

    @Override
    public int getFramebufferHeight() {
        return Minecraft.getInstance().getWindow().getHeight();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isInGame() {
        return Minecraft.getInstance().level != null && Minecraft.getInstance().player != null;
    }

    @Override
    public void openScreen(MenuController menu) {
        Minecraft.getInstance().setScreen(new IcarusScreen(menu, renderer));
    }

    @Override
    public File getGameDir() {
        return Minecraft.getInstance().gameDirectory;
    }

    @Override
    public String getClipboard() {
        return Minecraft.getInstance().keyboardHandler.getClipboard();
    }

    @Override
    public void setClipboard(String text) {
        Minecraft.getInstance().keyboardHandler.setClipboard(text);
    }
}

```

`platforms/mc-1.20.1/common/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/mc1201/ModernRenderer.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.mc1201;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.platform.GlStateManager;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.platform.TextureUtil;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.systems.RenderSystem;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.BufferBuilder;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.BufferUploader;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.DefaultVertexFormat;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.Tesselator;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.VertexFormat;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphics;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.GameRenderer;
import org.joml.Matrix4f;
import org.lwjgl.opengl.GL11;
import org.lwjgl.opengl.GL12;
import org.lwjgl.system.MemoryUtil;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Renderer;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Texture;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.DrawList;

import java.nio.IntBuffer;

/** 1.20.1: draw lists as triangles through the position_tex_color shader, in the frame's GuiGraphics pass. */
public class ModernRenderer implements Renderer {
    private GuiGraphics graphics;
    private IntBuffer pixels = MemoryUtil.memAllocInt(64 * 64);

    /** Binds the renderer to the frame's GuiGraphics; must be called before Icarus draws anything. */
    public void begin(GuiGraphics graphics) {
        this.graphics = graphics;
    }

    @Override
    public Texture createTexture(int width, int height) {
        int id = TextureUtil.generateTextureId();
        GlStateManager._bindTexture(id);
        GL11.glTexParameteri(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL11.GL_TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER, GL11.GL_NEAREST);
        GL11.glTexParameteri(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL11.GL_TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER, GL11.GL_NEAREST);
        GL11.glTexParameteri(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL11.GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_S, GL12.GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
        GL11.glTexParameteri(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL11.GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_T, GL12.GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
        GL11.glTexImage2D(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, 0, GL11.GL_RGBA8, width, height, 0, GL12.GL_BGRA, GL12.GL_UNSIGNED_INT_8_8_8_8_REV, (IntBuffer) null);
        return new GlTexture(id, width, height);
    }

    @Override
    public void upload(Texture texture, int x, int y, int width, int height, int[] argb, int offset, int stride) {
        int count = width * height;
        if (pixels.capacity() < count) {
            MemoryUtil.memFree(pixels);
            pixels = MemoryUtil.memAllocInt(count);
        }
        pixels.clear();
        for (int row = 0; row < height; row++) pixels.put(argb, offset + row * stride, width);
        pixels.flip();
        GlStateManager._bindTexture(((GlTexture) texture).id);
        GlStateManager._pixelStore(GL11.GL_UNPACK_ROW_LENGTH, 0);
        GlStateManager._pixelStore(GL11.GL_UNPACK_SKIP_PIXELS, 0);
        GlStateManager._pixelStore(GL11.GL_UNPACK_SKIP_ROWS, 0);
        GlStateManager._pixelStore(GL11.GL_UNPACK_ALIGNMENT, 4);
        GL11.glTexSubImage2D(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, 0, x, y, width, height, GL12.GL_BGRA, GL12.GL_UNSIGNED_INT_8_8_8_8_REV, pixels);
    }

    @Override
    public void deleteTexture(Texture texture) {
        TextureUtil.releaseTextureId(((GlTexture) texture).id);
    }

    @Override
    public void render(DrawList list) {
        graphics.flush(); //draw whatever vanilla batched so far underneath us
        float scale = (float) Minecraft.getInstance().getWindow().getGuiScale();
        graphics.pose().pushPose();
        graphics.pose().scale(1 / scale, 1 / scale, 1);
        Matrix4f pose = graphics.pose().last().pose();

        RenderSystem.enableBlend();
        RenderSystem.defaultBlendFunc();
        RenderSystem.disableCull();
        RenderSystem.setShader(GameRenderer::getPositionTexColorShader);

        float[] xy = list.getPositions(), uv = list.getUvs();
        int[] colors = list.getColors(), indices = list.getIndices();
        for (int command = 0; command < list.getCommandCount(); command++) {
            int start = list.getCommandIndexStart(command), count = list.getCommandIndexCount(command);
            if (count == 0) continue;
            RenderSystem.setShaderTexture(0, ((GlTexture) list.getCommandTexture(command)).id);
            BufferBuilder buffer = Tesselator.getInstance().getBuilder();
            buffer.begin(VertexFormat.Mode.TRIANGLES, DefaultVertexFormat.POSITION_TEX_COLOR);
            for (int i = start; i < start + count; i++) {
                int v = indices[i], c = colors[v];
                buffer.vertex(pose, xy[v * 2], xy[v * 2 + 1], 0).uv(uv[v * 2], uv[v * 2 + 1]).color(c >> 16 & 255, c >> 8 & 255, c & 255, c >>> 24).endVertex();
            }
            BufferUploader.drawWithShader(buffer.end());
        }

        RenderSystem.enableCull();
        RenderSystem.disableBlend();
        graphics.pose().popPose();
    }

    private static final class GlTexture implements Texture {
        final int id, width, height;

        GlTexture(int id, int width, int height) {
            this.id = id;
            this.width = width;
            this.height = height;
        }

        @Override
        public int getWidth() {
            return width;
        }

        @Override
        public int getHeight() {
            return height;
        }
    }
}

```

`platforms/mc-1.20.1/fabric/build.gradle.kts`:

```kts
plugins {
    java
    id("xyz.wagyourtail.unimined")
}

base {
    archivesName.set("icarus-fabric-1.20.1")
}

java {
    toolchain.languageVersion.set(JavaLanguageVersion.of(17))
}

tasks.withType<JavaCompile>().configureEach {
    options.encoding = "UTF-8"
}

// dev-only example mod, launched next to the library by runTestmodClient
val testmod: SourceSet by sourceSets.creating

sourceSets {
    main {
        // Minecraft-free core + the code shared by every 1.20.1 loader
        java.srcDir(rootProject.file("core/src/main/java"))
        resources.srcDir(rootProject.file("core/src/main/resources"))
        java.srcDir(file("../common/java"))
    }
    testmod.java.srcDir(rootProject.file("testmod/src/main/java"))
}

unimined.minecraft {
    version("1.20.1")

    mappings {
        mojmap()
    }

    fabric {
        loader("0.17.3")
    }
}

// not combineWith(main): that would put both in one fabric classpath group, i.e. one mod, and hide testmod
unimined.minecraft(testmod) {
    version("1.20.1")

    mappings {
        mojmap()
    }

    fabric {
        loader("0.17.3")
    }

    defaultRemapJar = false
}

testmod.compileClasspath += sourceSets.main.get().output
testmod.runtimeClasspath += sourceSets.main.get().output

tasks.processResources {
    inputs.property("version", project.version)
    filesMatching("fabric.mod.json") {
        expand("version" to project.version)
    }
}

dependencies {
    val lombok = "org.projectlombok:lombok:${providers.gradleProperty("lombok_version").get()}"
    compileOnly(lombok)
    annotationProcessor(lombok)
    "testmodCompileOnly"(lombok)
    "testmodAnnotationProcessor"(lombok)
}

// unimined 1.x adds the source set output to the jar a second time
tasks.jar { duplicatesStrategy = DuplicatesStrategy.EXCLUDE }
```

`platforms/mc-1.20.1/fabric/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/PlatformImpl.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform;

import net.fabricmc.loader.api.FabricLoader;
import net.fabricmc.loader.api.ModContainer;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphics;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.Icarus;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.mc1201.ModernPlatform;

import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.Collection;

/**
 * Fabric 1.20.1. Hooks are mixins (studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.mc1201.fabric.mixin) rather than fabric-api
 * events, so Icarus works without fabric-api; they only forward once {@link #install()} ran.
 */
@SuppressWarnings("unused")
public class PlatformImpl extends ModernPlatform {
    private static PlatformImpl installed;

    @Override
    public void install() {
        installed = this;
    }

    /** Called by GuiMixin at the end of the HUD render. */
    public static void renderHud(GuiGraphics graphics, float partialTick) {
        if (installed == null) return;
        installed.renderer.begin(graphics);
        Icarus.renderHud(partialTick);
    }

    /** Called by MinecraftMixin at the start of every client tick. */
    public static void clientTick() {
        if (installed != null) Icarus.clientTick();
    }

    /** Called by KeyboardHandlerMixin for key presses while no screen is open. */
    public static void keyPressed(int key) {
        if (installed != null) Icarus.onKeyPressed(key);
    }

    /** Called by KeyboardHandlerMixin for key releases. */
    public static void keyReleased(int key) {
        if (installed != null) Icarus.onKeyReleased(key);
    }

    @Override
    public Collection<Path> getModRoots(String modid) {
        return container(modid).getRootPaths();
    }

    @Override
    public ClassLoader getModClassLoader(String modid) {
        return PlatformImpl.class.getClassLoader(); //Knot loads every mod with the same class loader
    }

    private static ModContainer container(String modid) {
        return FabricLoader.getInstance().getModContainer(modid).orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("No mod with id " + modid));
    }
}

```

`platforms/mc-1.20.1/fabric/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/mc1201/fabric/mixin/GuiMixin.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.mc1201.fabric.mixin;

import net.minecraft.client.gui.Gui;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphics;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.PlatformImpl;

/** HUD hook (what fabric-api's HudRenderCallback does), kept here so Icarus doesn't need fabric-api. */
@Mixin(Gui.class)
public class GuiMixin {
    @Inject(method = "render", at = @At("TAIL"))
    private void icarus$render(GuiGraphics graphics, float partialTick, CallbackInfo ci) {
        PlatformImpl.renderHud(graphics, partialTick);
    }
}
```

`platforms/mc-1.20.1/fabric/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/mc1201/fabric/mixin/KeyboardHandlerMixin.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.mc1201.fabric.mixin;

import net.minecraft.client.KeyboardHandler;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import org.lwjgl.glfw.GLFW;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.PlatformImpl;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.input.Keys;

/** Fabric has no raw key event; this mirrors Forge's KeyInputEvent: key presses while no screen is open. */
@Mixin(KeyboardHandler.class)
public class KeyboardHandlerMixin {
    @Inject(method = "keyPress", at = @At("HEAD"))
    private void icarus$keyPress(long window, int key, int scancode, int action, int modifiers, CallbackInfo ci) {
        if (action == GLFW.GLFW_PRESS && window == Minecraft.getInstance().getWindow().getWindow() && Minecraft.getInstance().screen == null) {
            PlatformImpl.keyPressed(Keys.fromGlfw(key));
        } else if (action == GLFW.GLFW_RELEASE) {
            PlatformImpl.keyReleased(Keys.fromGlfw(key));
        }
    }
}
```

```java
@Mixin(Minecraft.class)
public class MinecraftMixin {
    @Inject(method = "tick", at = @At("HEAD"))
    private void icarus$tick(CallbackInfo ci) {
```

```kts
plugins {
    java
    id("xyz.wagyourtail.unimined")
}

base {
    archivesName.set("icarus-forge-1.20.1")
}

java {
    toolchain.languageVersion.set(JavaLanguageVersion.of(17))
}

tasks.withType<JavaCompile>().configureEach {
    options.encoding = "UTF-8"
}

repositories {
    maven("https://maven.minecraftforge.net/")
}

sourceSets {
    main {
        // Minecraft-free core + the code shared by every 1.20.1 loader
        java.srcDir(rootProject.file("core/src/main/java"))
        resources.srcDir(rootProject.file("core/src/main/resources"))
        java.srcDir(file("../common/java"))
        // the example mod is compiled in for dev runs and stripped from the published jar below
        java.srcDir(rootProject.file("testmod/src/main/java"))
    }
}

unimined.minecraft {
    version("1.20.1")

    mappings {
        mojmap()
    }

    minecraftForge {
        loader("47.4.29")
    }
}

tasks.jar {
    exclude("studio/dreamys/test/**")
    filesMatching("META-INF/mods.toml") {
        var inTestmod = false
        filter { line ->
            when {
                line.startsWith("# testmod-begin") -> { inTestmod = true; null }
                line.startsWith("# testmod-end") -> { inTestmod = false; null }
                inTestmod -> null
                else -> line
            }
        }
    }
}

tasks.processResources {
    inputs.property("version", project.version)
    filesMatching("META-INF/mods.toml") {
        expand("version" to project.version)
    }
}

dependencies {
    val lombok = "org.projectlombok:lombok:${providers.gradleProperty("lombok_version").get()}"
    compileOnly(lombok)
    annotationProcessor(lombok)
}
```

`platforms/mc-1.20.1/forge/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/PlatformImpl.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform;

import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraftforge.client.event.InputEvent;
import net.minecraftforge.client.event.RenderGuiEvent;
import net.minecraftforge.common.MinecraftForge;
import net.minecraftforge.event.TickEvent;
import net.minecraftforge.fml.ModList;
import net.minecraftforge.forgespi.language.IModFileInfo;
import org.lwjgl.glfw.GLFW;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.Icarus;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.input.Keys;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.mc1201.ModernPlatform;

import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.Collections;

/** Forge 1.20.1. */
@SuppressWarnings("unused")
public class PlatformImpl extends ModernPlatform {
    @Override
    public void install() {
        MinecraftForge.EVENT_BUS.addListener((InputEvent.Key e) -> {
            if (e.getAction() == GLFW.GLFW_PRESS && Minecraft.getInstance().screen == null) {
                Icarus.onKeyPressed(Keys.fromGlfw(e.getKey()));
            } else if (e.getAction() == GLFW.GLFW_RELEASE) {
                Icarus.onKeyReleased(Keys.fromGlfw(e.getKey()));
            }
        });
        MinecraftForge.EVENT_BUS.addListener((RenderGuiEvent.Post e) -> {
            renderer.begin(e.getGuiGraphics());
            Icarus.renderHud(e.getPartialTick());
        });
        MinecraftForge.EVENT_BUS.addListener((TickEvent.ClientTickEvent e) -> {
            if (e.phase == TickEvent.Phase.START) Icarus.clientTick();
        });
    }

    @Override
    public Collection<Path> getModRoots(String modid) {
        IModFileInfo file = ModList.get().getModFileById(modid);
        if (file == null) throw new IllegalArgumentException("No mod with id " + modid);
        return Collections.singletonList(file.getFile().getSecureJar().getRootPath());
    }

    @Override
    public ClassLoader getModClassLoader(String modid) {
        return PlatformImpl.class.getClassLoader(); //every game-layer mod shares FML's transforming class loader
    }
}

```

```java
package studio.dreamys.test;

import net.minecraftforge.fml.common.Mod;
import net.minecraftforge.fml.event.lifecycle.FMLClientSetupEvent;
import net.minecraftforge.fml.javafmlmod.FMLJavaModLoadingContext;

@Mod("testmod")
public class TestModForge {
    public TestModForge() {
        //client setup runs in parallel; hop onto the main thread like Forge's preInit did
        FMLJavaModLoadingContext.get().getModEventBus().addListener((FMLClientSetupEvent e) -> e.enqueueWork(() -> TestModCommon.init("testmod")));
    }
}
```
