`platforms/mc-1.21.1/common/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/mc1211/IcarusScreen.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.mc1211;

import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphics;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.screens.Screen;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import org.lwjgl.glfw.GLFW;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.input.Keys;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.MenuController;

/** The Minecraft screen showing an Icarus menu; forwards everything to the {@link MenuController}. */
public class IcarusScreen extends Screen {
    private final MenuController menu;
    private final ModernRenderer renderer;
    private char highSurrogate;

    public IcarusScreen(MenuController menu, ModernRenderer renderer) {
        super(Component.empty());
        this.menu = menu;
        this.renderer = renderer;
    }

    @Override
    public void init() { //public: fabric-api widens Screen#init, and widening is legal for the protected original too
        menu.opened();
    }

    /** Mouse position in fractional GUI units (the int arguments are rounded). */
    private double mouseX() {
        return minecraft.mouseHandler.xpos() * width / minecraft.getWindow().getScreenWidth();
    }

    private double mouseY() {
        return minecraft.mouseHandler.ypos() * height / minecraft.getWindow().getScreenHeight();
    }

    @Override
    public void render(GuiGraphics graphics, int mouseX, int mouseY, float partialTick) {
        super.render(graphics, mouseX, mouseY, partialTick); //default background
        renderer.begin(graphics);
        menu.render(mouseX(), mouseY(), partialTick);
        if (menu.isFadedOut()) onClose();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean mouseClicked(double mouseX, double mouseY, int button) {
        menu.mousePressed(mouseX, mouseY, button);
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean mouseReleased(double mouseX, double mouseY, int button) {
        menu.mouseReleased(mouseX, mouseY, button);
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean mouseScrolled(double mouseX, double mouseY, double scrollX, double scrollY) {
        menu.mouseScrolled(mouseX, mouseY, scrollY);
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean keyPressed(int keyCode, int scanCode, int modifiers) {
        if (keyCode == GLFW.GLFW_KEY_ESCAPE) {
            if (!menu.keyPressed(Keys.KEY_ESCAPE)) menu.requestClose(); //fades out, then closes below
            return true;
        }
        menu.keyPressed(Keys.fromGlfw(keyCode));
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean charTyped(char c, int modifiers) {
        if (Character.isHighSurrogate(c)) {
            highSurrogate = c;
            return true;
        }
        if (Character.isLowSurrogate(c) && highSurrogate != 0) {
            menu.charTyped(Character.toCodePoint(highSurrogate, c));
        } else {
            menu.charTyped(c);
        }
        highSurrogate = 0;
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public void removed() {
        menu.closed();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isPauseScreen() {
        return false;
    }
}

```

`platforms/mc-1.21.1/common/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/mc1211/ModernPlatform.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.mc1211;

import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Platform;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Renderer;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.MenuController;

import java.io.File;

/** The loader-independent half of the 1.21.1 platforms. */
public abstract class ModernPlatform implements Platform {
    protected final ModernRenderer renderer = new ModernRenderer();

    @Override
    public Renderer getRenderer() {
        return renderer;
    }

    @Override
    public double getGuiScale() {
        return Minecraft.getInstance().getWindow().getGuiScale();
    }

    @Override
    public int getFramebufferWidth() {
        return Minecraft.getInstance().getWindow().getWidth();
    }

    @Override
    public int getFramebufferHeight() {
        return Minecraft.getInstance().getWindow().getHeight();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isInGame() {
        return Minecraft.getInstance().level != null && Minecraft.getInstance().player != null;
    }

    @Override
    public void openScreen(MenuController menu) {
        Minecraft.getInstance().setScreen(new IcarusScreen(menu, renderer));
    }

    @Override
    public File getGameDir() {
        return Minecraft.getInstance().gameDirectory;
    }

    @Override
    public String getClipboard() {
        return Minecraft.getInstance().keyboardHandler.getClipboard();
    }

    @Override
    public void setClipboard(String text) {
        Minecraft.getInstance().keyboardHandler.setClipboard(text);
    }
}

```

`platforms/mc-1.21.1/common/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/mc1211/ModernRenderer.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.mc1211;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.platform.GlStateManager;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.platform.TextureUtil;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.systems.RenderSystem;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.BufferBuilder;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.BufferUploader;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.DefaultVertexFormat;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.Tesselator;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.VertexFormat;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphics;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.GameRenderer;
import org.joml.Matrix4f;
import org.lwjgl.opengl.GL11;
import org.lwjgl.opengl.GL12;
import org.lwjgl.system.MemoryUtil;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Renderer;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Texture;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.DrawList;

import java.nio.IntBuffer;

/** 1.21.1: draw lists as triangles through the position_tex_color shader, in the frame's GuiGraphics pass. */
public class ModernRenderer implements Renderer {
    private GuiGraphics graphics;
    private IntBuffer pixels = MemoryUtil.memAllocInt(64 * 64);

    /** Binds the renderer to the frame's GuiGraphics; must be called before Icarus draws anything. */
    public void begin(GuiGraphics graphics) {
        this.graphics = graphics;
    }

    @Override
    public Texture createTexture(int width, int height) {
        int id = TextureUtil.generateTextureId();
        GlStateManager._bindTexture(id);
        GL11.glTexParameteri(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL11.GL_TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER, GL11.GL_NEAREST);
        GL11.glTexParameteri(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL11.GL_TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER, GL11.GL_NEAREST);
        GL11.glTexParameteri(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL11.GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_S, GL12.GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
        GL11.glTexParameteri(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL11.GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_T, GL12.GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
        GL11.glTexImage2D(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, 0, GL11.GL_RGBA8, width, height, 0, GL12.GL_BGRA, GL12.GL_UNSIGNED_INT_8_8_8_8_REV, (IntBuffer) null);
        return new GlTexture(id, width, height);
    }

    @Override
    public void upload(Texture texture, int x, int y, int width, int height, int[] argb, int offset, int stride) {
        int count = width * height;
        if (pixels.capacity() < count) {
            MemoryUtil.memFree(pixels);
            pixels = MemoryUtil.memAllocInt(count);
        }
        pixels.clear();
        for (int row = 0; row < height; row++) pixels.put(argb, offset + row * stride, width);
        pixels.flip();
        GlStateManager._bindTexture(((GlTexture) texture).id);
        GlStateManager._pixelStore(GL11.GL_UNPACK_ROW_LENGTH, 0);
        GlStateManager._pixelStore(GL11.GL_UNPACK_SKIP_PIXELS, 0);
        GlStateManager._pixelStore(GL11.GL_UNPACK_SKIP_ROWS, 0);
        GlStateManager._pixelStore(GL11.GL_UNPACK_ALIGNMENT, 4);
        GL11.glTexSubImage2D(GL11.GL_TEXTURE_2D, 0, x, y, width, height, GL12.GL_BGRA, GL12.GL_UNSIGNED_INT_8_8_8_8_REV, pixels);
    }

    @Override
    public void deleteTexture(Texture texture) {
        TextureUtil.releaseTextureId(((GlTexture) texture).id);
    }

    @Override
    public void render(DrawList list) {
        graphics.flush(); //draw whatever vanilla batched so far underneath us
        float scale = (float) Minecraft.getInstance().getWindow().getGuiScale();
        graphics.pose().pushPose();
        graphics.pose().scale(1 / scale, 1 / scale, 1);
        Matrix4f pose = graphics.pose().last().pose();

        RenderSystem.enableBlend();
        RenderSystem.defaultBlendFunc();
        RenderSystem.disableCull();
        RenderSystem.setShader(GameRenderer::getPositionTexColorShader);

        float[] xy = list.getPositions(), uv = list.getUvs();
        int[] colors = list.getColors(), indices = list.getIndices();
        for (int command = 0; command < list.getCommandCount(); command++) {
            int start = list.getCommandIndexStart(command), count = list.getCommandIndexCount(command);
            if (count == 0) continue;
            RenderSystem.setShaderTexture(0, ((GlTexture) list.getCommandTexture(command)).id);
            BufferBuilder buffer = Tesselator.getInstance().begin(VertexFormat.Mode.TRIANGLES, DefaultVertexFormat.POSITION_TEX_COLOR);
            for (int i = start; i < start + count; i++) {
                int v = indices[i];
                buffer.addVertex(pose, xy[v * 2], xy[v * 2 + 1], 0).setUv(uv[v * 2], uv[v * 2 + 1]).setColor(colors[v]);
            }
            BufferUploader.drawWithShader(buffer.buildOrThrow());
        }

        RenderSystem.enableCull();
        RenderSystem.disableBlend();
        graphics.pose().popPose();
    }

    private static final class GlTexture implements Texture {
        final int id, width, height;

        GlTexture(int id, int width, int height) {
            this.id = id;
            this.width = width;
            this.height = height;
        }

        @Override
        public int getWidth() {
            return width;
        }

        @Override
        public int getHeight() {
            return height;
        }
    }
}

```

```kts
// dev-only example mod, launched next to the library by runTestmodClient
val testmod: SourceSet by sourceSets.creating

    main {
        // Minecraft-free core + the code shared by every 1.21.1 loader
        java.srcDir(rootProject.file("core/src/main/java"))
        resources.srcDir(rootProject.file("core/src/main/resources"))
        java.srcDir(file("../common/java"))
    }
    testmod.java.srcDir(rootProject.file("testmod/src/main/java"))

        mojmap()
    }

        loader("0.17.3")
    }

// not combineWith(main): that would put both in one fabric classpath group, i.e. one mod, and hide testmod

    version("1.21.1")
```

`platforms/mc-1.21.1/fabric/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/PlatformImpl.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform;

import net.fabricmc.loader.api.FabricLoader;
import net.fabricmc.loader.api.ModContainer;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphics;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.Icarus;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.mc1211.ModernPlatform;

import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.Collection;

/**
 * Fabric 1.21.1. Hooks are mixins (studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.mc1211.fabric.mixin) rather than fabric-api
 * events, so Icarus works without fabric-api; they only forward once {@link #install()} ran.
 */
@SuppressWarnings("unused")
public class PlatformImpl extends ModernPlatform {
    private static PlatformImpl installed;

    @Override
    public void install() {
        installed = this;
    }

    /** Called by GuiMixin at the end of the HUD render. */
    public static void renderHud(GuiGraphics graphics, float partialTick) {
        if (installed == null) return;
        installed.renderer.begin(graphics);
        Icarus.renderHud(partialTick);
    }

    /** Called by MinecraftMixin at the start of every client tick. */
    public static void clientTick() {
        if (installed != null) Icarus.clientTick();
    }

    /** Called by KeyboardHandlerMixin for key presses while no screen is open. */
    public static void keyPressed(int key) {
        if (installed != null) Icarus.onKeyPressed(key);
    }

    /** Called by KeyboardHandlerMixin for key releases. */
    public static void keyReleased(int key) {
        if (installed != null) Icarus.onKeyReleased(key);
    }

    @Override
    public Collection<Path> getModRoots(String modid) {
        return container(modid).getRootPaths();
    }

    @Override
    public ClassLoader getModClassLoader(String modid) {
        return PlatformImpl.class.getClassLoader(); //Knot loads every mod with the same class loader
    }

    private static ModContainer container(String modid) {
        return FabricLoader.getInstance().getModContainer(modid).orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("No mod with id " + modid));
    }
}

```

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.mc1211.fabric.mixin;

import net.minecraft.client.DeltaTracker;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.Gui;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphics;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.PlatformImpl;

/** HUD hook (what fabric-api's HudRenderCallback does), kept here so Icarus doesn't need fabric-api. */
@Mixin(Gui.class)
public class GuiMixin {
    @Inject(method = "render", at = @At("TAIL"))
    private void icarus$render(GuiGraphics graphics, DeltaTracker deltaTracker, CallbackInfo ci) {
        PlatformImpl.renderHud(graphics, deltaTracker.getGameTimeDeltaPartialTick(false));
    }
}
```

`platforms/mc-1.21.1/fabric/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/mc1211/fabric/mixin/KeyboardHandlerMixin.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.mc1211.fabric.mixin;

import net.minecraft.client.KeyboardHandler;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import org.lwjgl.glfw.GLFW;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.PlatformImpl;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.input.Keys;

/** Fabric has no raw key event; this mirrors Forge's KeyInputEvent: key presses while no screen is open. */
@Mixin(KeyboardHandler.class)
public class KeyboardHandlerMixin {
    @Inject(method = "keyPress", at = @At("HEAD"))
    private void icarus$keyPress(long window, int key, int scancode, int action, int modifiers, CallbackInfo ci) {
        if (action == GLFW.GLFW_PRESS && window == Minecraft.getInstance().getWindow().getWindow() && Minecraft.getInstance().screen == null) {
            PlatformImpl.keyPressed(Keys.fromGlfw(key));
        } else if (action == GLFW.GLFW_RELEASE) {
            PlatformImpl.keyReleased(Keys.fromGlfw(key));
        }
    }
}
```

`platforms/mc-1.21.1/neoforge/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/PlatformImpl.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform;

import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.neoforged.fml.ModList;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.ClientTickEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RenderGuiEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.InputEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.common.NeoForge;
import net.neoforged.neoforgespi.language.IModFileInfo;
import org.lwjgl.glfw.GLFW;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.Icarus;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.input.Keys;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.mc1211.ModernPlatform;

import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.Collections;

/** NeoForge 1.21.1. */
@SuppressWarnings("unused")
public class PlatformImpl extends ModernPlatform {
    @Override
    public void install() {
        NeoForge.EVENT_BUS.addListener((InputEvent.Key e) -> {
            if (e.getAction() == GLFW.GLFW_PRESS && Minecraft.getInstance().screen == null) {
                Icarus.onKeyPressed(Keys.fromGlfw(e.getKey()));
            } else if (e.getAction() == GLFW.GLFW_RELEASE) {
                Icarus.onKeyReleased(Keys.fromGlfw(e.getKey()));
            }
        });
        NeoForge.EVENT_BUS.addListener((RenderGuiEvent.Post e) -> {
            renderer.begin(e.getGuiGraphics());
            Icarus.renderHud(e.getPartialTick().getGameTimeDeltaPartialTick(false));
        });
        NeoForge.EVENT_BUS.addListener((ClientTickEvent.Pre e) -> Icarus.clientTick());
    }

    @Override
    public Collection<Path> getModRoots(String modid) {
        IModFileInfo file = ModList.get().getModFileById(modid);
        if (file == null) throw new IllegalArgumentException("No mod with id " + modid);
        return Collections.singletonList(file.getFile().getSecureJar().getRootPath());
    }

    @Override
    public ClassLoader getModClassLoader(String modid) {
        return PlatformImpl.class.getClassLoader(); //every game-layer mod shares FML's transforming class loader
    }
}

```

`platforms/mc-26.3/buildSrc/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/gradle/ArgFileClasspathPlugin.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.gradle;

import org.gradle.api.Plugin;
import org.gradle.api.Project;
import org.gradle.api.file.ConfigurableFileCollection;
import xyz.wagyourtail.unimined.runs.MinecraftRunTask;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.UncheckedIOException;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

/**
 * Passes unimined run classpaths through a java {@code @argfile} on Windows.
 * <p>
 * A modern Minecraft classpath is longer than Windows allows on a command line, so Gradle's javaexec swaps it for
 * a "pathing jar" whose manifest lists the real entries. The JVM can load classes through that, but
 * {@code java.class.path} then only contains the pathing jar, and Fabric's Knot (which discovers the game and dev
 * mods from {@code java.class.path}) fails with "couldn't locate the game". An argfile keeps the command line short
 * and {@code java.class.path} complete.
 */
public class ArgFileClasspathPlugin implements Plugin<Project> {
    @Override
    public void apply(Project project) {
        if (!System.getProperty("os.name", "").toLowerCase().contains("win")) return;

        // unimined (re)sets the run task's classpath and jvm args in its own configuration action, so take them over
        // only once the task graph is final, right before execution
        project.getGradle().getTaskGraph().whenReady(graph -> graph.getAllTasks().stream()
                .filter(t -> t instanceof MinecraftRunTask && t.getProject() == project)
                .forEach(t -> takeOver(project, (MinecraftRunTask) t)));
    }

    private static void takeOver(Project project, MinecraftRunTask task) {
        File argFile = project.getLayout().getBuildDirectory().file("tmp/" + task.getName() + "/classpath.args").get().getAsFile();

        //hand the JVM only the argfile; an empty task classpath also keeps javaexec from adding its pathing jar
        ConfigurableFileCollection classpath = project.getObjects().fileCollection();
        classpath.from(new java.util.ArrayList<>(task.getRuntimeClasspath().getFrom())); //snapshot: getFrom() is live
        task.getRuntimeClasspath().setFrom();
        task.getJvmArgs().add("@" + argFile.getAbsolutePath());

        task.doFirst(t -> {
            String joined = classpath.getFiles().stream()
                    .map(f -> f.getAbsolutePath().replace('\\', '/'))
                    .collect(Collectors.joining(File.pathSeparator));
            try {
                Files.createDirectories(argFile.toPath().getParent());
                Files.write(argFile.toPath(), ("-cp\n\"" + joined + "\"\n").getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
            } catch (IOException e) {
                throw new UncheckedIOException(e);
            }
        });
    }
}

```

`platforms/mc-26.3/buildSrc/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/gradle/WindowsSafeAutomaticModuleNameProcessor.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.gradle;

import xyz.wagyourtail.unimined.transform.processor.JarProcessor;

import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.io.UncheckedIOException;
import java.nio.file.FileSystem;
import java.nio.file.FileSystems;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.nio.file.StandardOpenOption;
import java.nio.file.attribute.FileTime;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.jar.Attributes;
import java.util.jar.JarFile;
import java.util.jar.Manifest;
import java.util.zip.ZipEntry;

/**
 * Drop-in replacement for unimined 2.0.2's {@code automatic-module-name} jar processor.
 * <p>
 * The builtin one rewrites the jar through a zip filesystem while its own {@link JarFile}
 * handle on that jar is still open, which fails on Windows ("The process cannot access the
 * file because it is being used by another process") for every forge environment.
 * This copy reads the manifest(s) first, closes the handles, then rewrites.
 * It is registered via META-INF/services and found before the builtin because buildSrc's
 * classes precede the plugin jars on the classpath.
 * <p>
 * Delete this (and the services file) once unimined ships a fix.
 */
public class WindowsSafeAutomaticModuleNameProcessor implements JarProcessor {
    private static final String NAME = "automatic-module-name";

    static final class Spec implements JarProcessorSpec {
        private static final long serialVersionUID = 1L;
        final String moduleName;
        final String manifestDonor;

        Spec(String moduleName, String manifestDonor) {
            this.moduleName = moduleName;
            this.manifestDonor = manifestDonor;
        }
    }

    @Override
    public String getName() {
        return NAME;
    }

    @Override
    public JarProcessorSpec buildSpec(SpecContext context) {
        return null;
    }

    @Override
    public String serializeSpec(JarProcessorSpec spec) {
        Spec s = (Spec) spec;
        return s.manifestDonor == null ? s.moduleName : s.moduleName + " " + s.manifestDonor;
    }

    @Override
    public JarProcessorSpec deserializeSpec(String payload) {
        String[] parts = payload.trim().split(" ", 2);
        return new Spec(parts[0], parts.length > 1 ? parts[1] : null);
    }

    @Override
    public void processJar(Path jar, JarProcessorSpec spec, ProcessorContext context) {
        Spec s = (Spec) spec;
        try {
            Manifest manifest = readManifest(jar);
            if (manifest == null) manifest = new Manifest();
            if (s.moduleName.equals(manifest.getMainAttributes().getValue("Automatic-Module-Name"))) return;

            manifest.getMainAttributes().putIfAbsent(Attributes.Name.MANIFEST_VERSION, "1.0");
            manifest.getMainAttributes().putValue("Automatic-Module-Name", s.moduleName);

            if (s.manifestDonor != null) {
                Manifest donor = readManifest(Path.of(s.manifestDonor));
                if (donor != null) {
                    for (Map.Entry<String, Attributes> e : donor.getEntries().entrySet()) {
                        boolean wants = e.getValue().keySet().stream().anyMatch(k ->
                                k.toString().equals("Specification-Version") || k.toString().equals("Implementation-Version"));
                        if (!wants) continue;
                        Attributes out = new Attributes();
                        e.getValue().forEach((k, v) -> {
                            if (!k.toString().contains("Digest")) out.put(k, v);
                        });
                        manifest.getEntries().put(e.getKey(), out);
                    }
                }
            }

            ByteArrayOutputStream bytes = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
            manifest.write(bytes);

            // every JarFile handle is closed by now, so the zipfs rewrite can replace the file
            try (FileSystem fs = FileSystems.newFileSystem(jar, Collections.<String, Object>emptyMap())) {
                Path p = fs.getPath("META-INF/MANIFEST.MF");
                Files.deleteIfExists(p);
                Files.createDirectories(p.getParent());
                Files.write(p, bytes.toByteArray(), StandardOpenOption.CREATE, StandardOpenOption.TRUNCATE_EXISTING);
                Files.setLastModifiedTime(p, FileTime.fromMillis(0));
            }
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new UncheckedIOException(e);
        }
    }

    private static Manifest readManifest(Path jar) throws IOException {
        try (JarFile jf = new JarFile(jar.toFile())) {
            ZipEntry entry = jf.getEntry("META-INF/MANIFEST.MF");
            if (entry == null) return null;
            try (InputStream in = jf.getInputStream(entry)) {
                return new Manifest(in);
            }
        }
    }
}

```

`platforms/mc-26.3/buildSrc/src/main/java/xyz/wagyourtail/unimined/minecraft/localrepo/LocalMavenPublisher.java`:

```java
package xyz.wagyourtail.unimined.minecraft.localrepo;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.UncheckedIOException;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.AccessDeniedException;
import java.nio.file.FileSystemException;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.nio.file.StandardCopyOption;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Shadows unimined 2.0.2's {@code LocalMavenPublisher} (a Kotlin {@code object}, hence {@link #INSTANCE}).
 * <p>
 * The original re-copies every artifact into {@code .gradle/unimined/local-repo} on every configuration with an
 * atomic move over the existing file. On Windows that fails with AccessDeniedException as soon as a Gradle daemon
 * still has the previous copy open, i.e. on practically every build after the first.
 * This copy skips byte-identical files and only replaces changed ones.
 * <p>
 * It wins because buildSrc's classes precede the plugin jars on the shared class path.
 * Delete once unimined ships a fix.
 */
@SuppressWarnings("unused")
public final class LocalMavenPublisher {
    public static final LocalMavenPublisher INSTANCE = new LocalMavenPublisher();

    private LocalMavenPublisher() {
    }

    public Path moduleDir(Path repoDir, String group, String artifactId, String version) {
        return repoDir.resolve(group.replace('.', '/')).resolve(artifactId).resolve(version);
    }

    public Path publish(Path repoDir, String group, String artifactId, String version, Map<String, Path> files) {
        try {
            Path moduleDir = Files.createDirectories(moduleDir(repoDir, group, artifactId, version));

            String pom = "<project xmlns=\"http://maven.apache.org/POM/4.0.0\">\n" +
                    "    <modelVersion>4.0.0</modelVersion>\n" +
                    "    <groupId>" + group + "</groupId>\n" +
                    "    <artifactId>" + artifactId + "</artifactId>\n" +
                    "    <version>" + version + "</version>\n" +
                    "    <packaging>jar</packaging>\n" +
                    "</project>";
            Path pomFile = moduleDir.resolve(artifactId + "-" + version + ".pom");
            if (!Files.exists(pomFile) || !new String(Files.readAllBytes(pomFile), StandardCharsets.UTF_8).equals(pom)) {
                Files.write(pomFile, pom.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
            }

            for (Map.Entry<String, Path> e : files.entrySet()) {
                String name = artifactId + "-" + version + (e.getKey().isEmpty() ? "" : "-" + e.getKey()) + ".jar";
                copyIfChanged(e.getValue(), moduleDir.resolve(name));
            }
            return moduleDir;
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new UncheckedIOException(e);
        }
    }

    private static void copyIfChanged(Path source, Path target) throws IOException {
        if (Files.exists(target) && Files.size(target) == Files.size(source) && Files.mismatch(source, target) == -1) {
            return;
        }

        Path tmp = target.resolveSibling("." + target.getFileName() + "." + System.nanoTime() + ".tmp");
        Files.copy(source, tmp, StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING);
        try {
            Files.move(tmp, target, StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING, StandardCopyOption.ATOMIC_MOVE);
        } catch (AccessDeniedException | java.nio.file.AtomicMoveNotSupportedException ex) {
            try {
                Files.move(tmp, target, StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING);
            } catch (FileSystemException locked) {
                Files.deleteIfExists(tmp);
                throw new IOException("Cannot replace " + target + " (still open by another Gradle daemon? run ./gradlew --stop)", locked);
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`platforms/mc-26.3/buildSrc/src/main/java/xyz/wagyourtail/unimined/transform/forge/internal/mcpconfig/NoOpFunction.java`:

```java
package xyz.wagyourtail.unimined.transform.forge.internal.mcpconfig;

import xyz.wagyourtail.unimined.transform.step.subpipeline.PipelineFunction;
import xyz.wagyourtail.unimined.transform.step.subpipeline.SubStep;
import xyz.wagyourtail.unimined.transform.step.subpipeline.SubpipelineContext;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.UncheckedIOException;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;

/**
 * Shadows unimined 2.0.2's {@code private object NoOpFunction} (McpConfigSubpipeline.kt), which runs NeoForm's
 * {@code downloadManifest} / {@code downloadJson} steps and returns no output. NeoForm 26.x's config passes
 * {@code {downloadManifestOutput}} / {@code {downloadJsonOutput}} to the following steps, and interpolating those
 * fails with "No output from step 'downloadManifest'". Unimined downloads the game itself, so the referenced files
 * are never read; an empty placeholder output is enough.
 * <p>
 * Delete this once unimined handles it upstream.
 */
@SuppressWarnings("unused")
final class NoOpFunction implements PipelineFunction {
    public static final NoOpFunction INSTANCE = new NoOpFunction();

    private NoOpFunction() {
    }

    @Override
    public String getName() {
        return "mcpconfig:noop";
    }

    @Override
    public Path run(SubStep step, SubpipelineContext ctx) {
        Path placeholder = ctx.getWorkDir().resolve(step.getName() + "Output.jar");
        try {
            if (!Files.exists(placeholder)) Files.createFile(placeholder);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new UncheckedIOException(e);
        }
        return placeholder;
    }
}
```

`platforms/mc-26.3/common/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/mc263/IcarusScreen.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.mc263;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.platform.InputConstants;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphicsExtractor;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.screens.Screen;
import net.minecraft.client.input.CharacterEvent;
import net.minecraft.client.input.KeyEvent;
import net.minecraft.client.input.MouseButtonEvent;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.input.Keys;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.MenuController;

/** The Minecraft screen showing an Icarus menu; forwards everything to the {@link MenuController}. */
public class IcarusScreen extends Screen {
    private final MenuController menu;
    private final ModernRenderer renderer;
    private boolean textInput;

    public IcarusScreen(MenuController menu, ModernRenderer renderer) {
        super(Component.empty());
        this.menu = menu;
        this.renderer = renderer;
    }

    @Override
    public void init() { //public: the dev jar widens Screen#init, and widening is legal for the protected original too
        menu.opened();
    }

    /** Mouse position in fractional GUI units. */
    private double mouseX() {
        return minecraft.mouseHandler.xpos() * width / minecraft.getWindow().getScreenWidth();
    }

    private double mouseY() {
        return minecraft.mouseHandler.ypos() * height / minecraft.getWindow().getScreenHeight();
    }

    @Override
    public void extractRenderState(GuiGraphicsExtractor graphics, int mouseX, int mouseY, float partialTick) {
        super.extractRenderState(graphics, mouseX, mouseY, partialTick);
        renderer.begin(graphics);
        menu.render(mouseX(), mouseY(), partialTick);
        updateTextInput();
        if (menu.isFadedOut()) onClose();
    }

    /** SDL only sends typed characters while text input is on, which vanilla's EditBox does on focus; mirror that. */
    private void updateTextInput() {
        boolean typing = menu.isTyping();
        if (typing == textInput) return;
        textInput = typing;
        if (typing) minecraft.textInputManager().startTextInput(this);
        else minecraft.textInputManager().stopTextInput(this);
    }

    /** 26.x numbers mouse buttons like SDL (left 1, middle 2, right 3); Icarus uses LWJGL's (left 0, right 1, middle 2). */
    private static int button(MouseButtonEvent event) {
        switch (event.button()) {
            case InputConstants.MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT: return 0;
            case InputConstants.MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT: return 1;
            case InputConstants.MOUSE_BUTTON_MIDDLE: return 2;
            default: return event.button() - 1;
        }
    }

    @Override
    public boolean mouseClicked(MouseButtonEvent event, boolean doubleClick) {
        menu.mousePressed(event.x(), event.y(), button(event));
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean mouseReleased(MouseButtonEvent event) {
        menu.mouseReleased(event.x(), event.y(), button(event));
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean mouseScrolled(double mouseX, double mouseY, double scrollX, double scrollY) {
        menu.mouseScrolled(mouseX, mouseY, scrollY);
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean keyPressed(KeyEvent event) {
        if (event.key() == InputConstants.KEY_ESCAPE) {
            if (!menu.keyPressed(Keys.KEY_ESCAPE)) menu.requestClose(); //fades out, then closes below
            return true;
        }
        menu.keyPressed(Keys.fromSdl(event.key()));
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean charTyped(CharacterEvent event) {
        menu.charTyped(event.codepoint());
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public void removed() {
        if (textInput) {
            textInput = false;
            minecraft.textInputManager().stopTextInput(this);
        }
        menu.closed();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isPauseScreen() {
        return false;
    }
}

```

`platforms/mc-26.3/common/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/mc263/ModernPlatform.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.mc263;

import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Platform;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Renderer;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.MenuController;

import java.io.File;

/** The loader-independent half of the 26.3 platforms. */
public abstract class ModernPlatform implements Platform {
    protected final ModernRenderer renderer = new ModernRenderer();

    @Override
    public Renderer getRenderer() {
        return renderer;
    }

    @Override
    public double getGuiScale() {
        return Minecraft.getInstance().getWindow().getGuiScale();
    }

    @Override
    public int getFramebufferWidth() {
        return Minecraft.getInstance().getWindow().getWidth();
    }

    @Override
    public int getFramebufferHeight() {
        return Minecraft.getInstance().getWindow().getHeight();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isInGame() {
        return Minecraft.getInstance().level != null && Minecraft.getInstance().player != null;
    }

    @Override
    public void openScreen(MenuController menu) {
        Minecraft.getInstance().gui.setScreen(new IcarusScreen(menu, renderer));
    }

    @Override
    public File getGameDir() {
        return Minecraft.getInstance().gameDirectory;
    }

    @Override
    public String getClipboard() {
        return Minecraft.getInstance().keyboardHandler.getClipboard();
    }

    @Override
    public void setClipboard(String text) {
        Minecraft.getInstance().keyboardHandler.setClipboard(text);
    }
}
```

`platforms/mc-26.3/common/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/mc263/ModernRenderer.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.mc263;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.platform.NativeImage;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.systems.RenderSystem;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.vertex.VertexConsumer;
import com.mojang.renderpearl.api.pipeline.RenderPipeline;
import com.mojang.renderpearl.api.textures.FilterMode;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphicsExtractor;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.navigation.ScreenRectangle;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.render.TextureSetup;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.RenderPipelines;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.state.gui.GuiElementRenderState;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.state.gui.GuiRenderState;
import net.minecraft.client.renderer.texture.DynamicTexture;
import org.joml.Matrix3x2f;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Renderer;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Texture;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.DrawList;

import java.lang.reflect.Field;

/**
 * 26.3: the GUI is retained-mode, so a draw list is submitted as GUI elements (one per texture) whose vertices are
 * built when Minecraft renders the frame. The frame's {@link GuiRenderState} is private in
 * {@link GuiGraphicsExtractor}; 26.x is unobfuscated, so it's read by name.
 */
public class ModernRenderer implements Renderer {
    private static final Field GUI_RENDER_STATE;

    static {
        try {
            GUI_RENDER_STATE = GuiGraphicsExtractor.class.getDeclaredField("guiRenderState");
            GUI_RENDER_STATE.setAccessible(true);
        } catch (NoSuchFieldException e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("GuiGraphicsExtractor.guiRenderState not found", e);
        }
    }

    private GuiGraphicsExtractor graphics;

    /** Binds the renderer to the frame's extractor; must be called before Icarus draws anything. */
    public void begin(GuiGraphicsExtractor graphics) {
        this.graphics = graphics;
    }

    @Override
    public Texture createTexture(int width, int height) {
        NativeImage image = new NativeImage(NativeImage.Format.RGBA, width, height, false);
        DynamicTexture texture = new DynamicTexture(() -> "icarus atlas", image);
        return new AtlasTexture(texture, image, width, height);
    }

    @Override
    public void upload(Texture texture, int x, int y, int width, int height, int[] argb, int offset, int stride) {
        AtlasTexture t = (AtlasTexture) texture;
        for (int row = 0; row < height; row++) {
            int base = offset + row * stride;
            for (int column = 0; column < width; column++) t.image.setPixel(x + column, y + row, argb[base + column]); //ARGB
        }
        t.texture.upload();
    }

    @Override
    public void deleteTexture(Texture texture) {
        ((AtlasTexture) texture).texture.close();
    }

    @Override
    public void render(DrawList list) {
        GuiRenderState state;
        try {
            state = (GuiRenderState) GUI_RENDER_STATE.get(graphics);
        } catch (IllegalAccessException e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(e);
        }
        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        float inverseScale = 1f / mc.getWindow().getGuiScale();
        ScreenRectangle bounds = new ScreenRectangle(0, 0, mc.getWindow().getGuiScaledWidth(), mc.getWindow().getGuiScaledHeight());
        Matrix3x2f pose = new Matrix3x2f(graphics.pose());

        for (int command = 0; command < list.getCommandCount(); command++) {
            if (list.getCommandIndexCount(command) == 0) continue;
            //a fresh element per submit: the state keeps them until the frame is drawn, and several draw lists
            //(HUD, menu, toasts over the menu) are submitted in the same frame
            DrawListElement element = new DrawListElement();
            AtlasTexture texture = (AtlasTexture) list.getCommandTexture(command);
            element.set(list, list.getCommandIndexStart(command), list.getCommandIndexCount(command), inverseScale, pose, bounds,
                    TextureSetup.singleTexture(texture.texture.getTextureView(), RenderSystem.getSamplerCache().getClampToEdge(FilterMode.NEAREST)));
            state.addGuiElement(element);
        }
    }

    /** Emits a range of the draw list's triangles; the GUI pipelines draw quads, so each triangle is a degenerate quad. */
    private static final class DrawListElement implements GuiElementRenderState {
        private DrawList list;
        private int start, count;
        private float inverseScale;
        private Matrix3x2f pose;
        private ScreenRectangle bounds;
        private TextureSetup textureSetup;

        void set(DrawList list, int start, int count, float inverseScale, Matrix3x2f pose, ScreenRectangle bounds, TextureSetup textureSetup) {
            this.list = list;
            this.start = start;
            this.count = count;
            this.inverseScale = inverseScale;
            this.pose = pose;
            this.bounds = bounds;
            this.textureSetup = textureSetup;
        }

        @Override
        public void buildVertices(VertexConsumer consumer) {
            float[] xy = list.getPositions(), uv = list.getUvs();
            int[] colors = list.getColors(), indices = list.getIndices();
            for (int i = start; i < start + count; i += 3) {
                vertex(consumer, indices[i], xy, uv, colors);
                vertex(consumer, indices[i + 1], xy, uv, colors);
                vertex(consumer, indices[i + 2], xy, uv, colors);
                vertex(consumer, indices[i + 2], xy, uv, colors);
            }
        }

        private void vertex(VertexConsumer consumer, int v, float[] xy, float[] uv, int[] colors) {
            consumer.addVertexWith2DPose(pose, xy[v * 2] * inverseScale, xy[v * 2 + 1] * inverseScale)
                    .setUv(uv[v * 2], uv[v * 2 + 1]).setColor(colors[v]);
        }

        @Override
        public RenderPipeline pipeline() {
            return RenderPipelines.GUI_TEXTURED;
        }

        @Override
        public TextureSetup textureSetup() {
            return textureSetup;
        }

        @Override
        public ScreenRectangle scissorArea() {
            return null;
        }

        @Override
        public ScreenRectangle bounds() {
            return bounds;
        }
    }

    private static final class AtlasTexture implements Texture {
        final DynamicTexture texture;
        final NativeImage image;
        final int width, height;

        AtlasTexture(DynamicTexture texture, NativeImage image, int width, int height) {
            this.texture = texture;
            this.image = image;
            this.width = width;
            this.height = height;
        }

        @Override
        public int getWidth() {
            return width;
        }

        @Override
        public int getHeight() {
            return height;
        }
    }
}

```

`platforms/mc-26.3/fabric/build.gradle.kts`:

```kts
plugins {
    java
    id("xyz.wagyourtail.unimined")
    id("icarus.argfile-classpath")
}

base {
    archivesName.set("icarus-fabric-26.3")
}

java {
    toolchain.languageVersion.set(JavaLanguageVersion.of(25))
}

tasks.withType<JavaCompile>().configureEach {
    options.encoding = "UTF-8"
    options.release.set(25) //unimined may compile some envs with the daemon's JDK; pin the bytecode level
}

unimined {
    // compile and launch with the java toolchain above instead of the JDK running gradle
    useToolchains = true

    minecraft {
        version = "26.3"
        loader("fabric", "0.20.1")

        // dev-only example mod, launched next to the library by runTestmodClient
        sourceSet("testmod") {
            version = "26.3"
            loader("fabric", "0.20.1")
            combineWith("main")
        }
    }
}

sourceSets {
    main {
        // Minecraft-free core + the code shared by every 26.3 loader
        java.srcDir(rootProject.file("../../core/src/main/java"))
        resources.srcDir(rootProject.file("../../core/src/main/resources"))
        java.srcDir(file("../common/java"))
    }
    named("testmod") {
        java.srcDir(rootProject.file("../../testmod/src/main/java"))
    }
}

tasks.processResources {
    inputs.property("version", project.version)
    filesMatching("fabric.mod.json") {
        expand("version" to project.version)
    }
}

dependencies {
    val lombok = "org.projectlombok:lombok:${providers.gradleProperty("lombok_version").get()}"
    compileOnly(lombok)
    annotationProcessor(lombok)
    "testmodCompileOnly"(lombok)
    "testmodAnnotationProcessor"(lombok)
}

// the example mod only ever runs from its dev classes; never package it
tasks.matching { it.name.contains("testmod", ignoreCase = true) && it.name.endsWith("Jar") }.configureEach {
    enabled = false
}

// Fabric treats the classpath directory holding fabric.mod.json as the mod's root. Keep each source set's
// classes and resources in one directory so the mod root contains its classes (Icarus scans it for @IPage),
// like it would inside a jar. (Loom does the same job with fabric.classPathGroups; unimined does not.)
for (name in listOf("main", "testmod")) {
    sourceSets.named(name) {
        output.setResourcesDir(java.destinationDirectory)
    }
}
tasks.named("compileTestmodJava") {
    dependsOn("processResources") //main's resources now share main's classes directory
}

// launch with this project's toolchain rather than whatever unimined picks
val runLauncher = javaToolchains.launcherFor { languageVersion.set(java.toolchain.languageVersion) }
gradle.taskGraph.whenReady {
    allTasks.filterIsInstance<xyz.wagyourtail.unimined.runs.MinecraftRunTask>().filter { it.project == project }.forEach {
        it.javaExecutable.set(runLauncher.map { l -> l.executablePath.asFile.absolutePath })
    }
}
```

`platforms/mc-26.3/fabric/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/PlatformImpl.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform;

import net.fabricmc.loader.api.FabricLoader;
import net.fabricmc.loader.api.ModContainer;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphicsExtractor;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.Icarus;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.mc263.ModernPlatform;

import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.Collection;

/**
 * Fabric 26.3. Hooks are mixins (studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.mc263.fabric.mixin) rather than fabric-api
 * events, so Icarus works without fabric-api; they only forward once {@link #install()} ran.
 */
@SuppressWarnings("unused")
public class PlatformImpl extends ModernPlatform {
    private static PlatformImpl installed;

    @Override
    public void install() {
        installed = this;
    }

    /** Called by HudMixin at the end of the HUD render. */
    public static void renderHud(GuiGraphicsExtractor graphics, float partialTick) {
        if (installed == null) return;
        installed.renderer.begin(graphics);
        Icarus.renderHud(partialTick);
    }

    /** Called by MinecraftMixin at the start of every client tick. */
    public static void clientTick() {
        if (installed != null) Icarus.clientTick();
    }

    /** Called by KeyboardHandlerMixin for key presses while no screen is open. */
    public static void keyPressed(int key) {
        if (installed != null) Icarus.onKeyPressed(key);
    }

    /** Called by KeyboardHandlerMixin for key releases. */
    public static void keyReleased(int key) {
        if (installed != null) Icarus.onKeyReleased(key);
    }

    @Override
    public Collection<Path> getModRoots(String modid) {
        return container(modid).getRootPaths();
    }

    @Override
    public ClassLoader getModClassLoader(String modid) {
        return PlatformImpl.class.getClassLoader(); //Knot loads every mod with the same class loader
    }

    private static ModContainer container(String modid) {
        return FabricLoader.getInstance().getModContainer(modid).orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("No mod with id " + modid));
    }
}
```

`platforms/mc-26.3/fabric/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/mc263/fabric/mixin/HudMixin.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.mc263.fabric.mixin;

import net.minecraft.client.DeltaTracker;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.GuiGraphicsExtractor;
import net.minecraft.client.gui.Hud;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.PlatformImpl;

/** HUD hook (what fabric-api's HudRenderCallback does), kept here so Icarus doesn't need fabric-api. */
@Mixin(Hud.class)
public class HudMixin {
    @Inject(method = "extractRenderState", at = @At("TAIL"))
    private void icarus$extractRenderState(GuiGraphicsExtractor graphics, DeltaTracker deltaTracker, CallbackInfo ci) {
        PlatformImpl.renderHud(graphics, deltaTracker.getGameTimeDeltaPartialTick(false));
    }
}
```

`platforms/mc-26.3/fabric/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/mc263/fabric/mixin/KeyboardHandlerMixin.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.mc263.fabric.mixin;

import com.mojang.blaze3d.platform.InputConstants;
import net.minecraft.client.KeyboardHandler;
import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.input.KeyEvent;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.input.Keys;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.PlatformImpl;

/** Fabric has no raw key event; this mirrors Forge's KeyInputEvent: key presses while no screen is open. */
@Mixin(KeyboardHandler.class)
public class KeyboardHandlerMixin {
    @Inject(method = "keyPress", at = @At("HEAD"))
    private void icarus$keyPress(long window, int action, KeyEvent event, CallbackInfo ci) {
        Minecraft mc = Minecraft.getInstance();
        if (action == InputConstants.PRESS && window == mc.getWindow().handle() && mc.gui.screen() == null) {
            PlatformImpl.keyPressed(Keys.fromSdl(event.key()));
        } else if (action == InputConstants.RELEASE) {
            PlatformImpl.keyReleased(Keys.fromSdl(event.key()));
        }
    }
}

```

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.mc263.fabric.mixin;

import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.PlatformImpl;

/** Start-of-tick hook (what fabric-api's ClientTickEvents.START_CLIENT_TICK does). */
@Mixin(Minecraft.class)
public class MinecraftMixin {
    @Inject(method = "tick", at = @At("HEAD"))
    private void icarus$tick(CallbackInfo ci) {
        PlatformImpl.clientTick();
    }
}
```

```json
{
  "required": true,
```

```java
package studio.dreamys.test.mixin;

import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.minecraft.client.main.GameConfig;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.Mixin;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.At;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.Inject;
import org.spongepowered.asm.mixin.injection.callback.CallbackInfo;
import studio.dreamys.test.TestModCommon;

/** Fabric client entrypoints run before the game window exists; initialise once Minecraft is constructed. */
@Mixin(Minecraft.class)
public class MinecraftMixin {
    @Inject(method = "<init>", at = @At("TAIL"))
    private void testmod$init(GameConfig gameConfig, CallbackInfo ci) {
        TestModCommon.init("testmod");
    }
}
```

`platforms/mc-26.3/neoforge/build.gradle.kts`:

```kts
plugins {
    java
    id("xyz.wagyourtail.unimined")
    id("icarus.argfile-classpath")
}

base {
    archivesName.set("icarus-neoforge-26.3")
}

java {
    toolchain.languageVersion.set(JavaLanguageVersion.of(25))
}

tasks.withType<JavaCompile>().configureEach {
    options.encoding = "UTF-8"
    options.release.set(25) //unimined may compile some envs with the daemon's JDK; pin the bytecode level
}

unimined {
    // compile and launch with the java toolchain above instead of the JDK running gradle
    useToolchains = true

    minecraft {
        version = "26.3"
        loader("neoforge", "26.3.0.103")
    }
}

sourceSets {
    main {
        // Minecraft-free core + the code shared by every 26.3 loader
        java.srcDir(rootProject.file("../../core/src/main/java"))
        resources.srcDir(rootProject.file("../../core/src/main/resources"))
        java.srcDir(file("../common/java"))
        // the example mod is compiled in for dev runs and stripped from the published jar below: neoforge's
        // userdev launch setup only applies to runs named exactly "client"/"server", so a separate testmod
        // env/project can't be launched properly
        java.srcDir(rootProject.file("../../testmod/src/main/java"))
    }
}

tasks.jar {
    exclude("studio/dreamys/test/**")
    filesMatching("META-INF/neoforge.mods.toml") {
        var inTestmod = false
        filter { line ->
            when {
                line.startsWith("# testmod-begin") -> { inTestmod = true; null }
                line.startsWith("# testmod-end") -> { inTestmod = false; null }
                inTestmod -> null
                else -> line
            }
        }
    }
}

tasks.processResources {
    inputs.property("version", project.version)
    filesMatching("META-INF/neoforge.mods.toml") {
        expand("version" to project.version)
    }
}

dependencies {
    val lombok = "org.projectlombok:lombok:${providers.gradleProperty("lombok_version").get()}"
    compileOnly(lombok)
    annotationProcessor(lombok)
}

// launch with this project's toolchain rather than whatever unimined picks
val runLauncher = javaToolchains.launcherFor { languageVersion.set(java.toolchain.languageVersion) }
gradle.taskGraph.whenReady {
    allTasks.filterIsInstance<xyz.wagyourtail.unimined.runs.MinecraftRunTask>().filter { it.project == project }.forEach {
        it.javaExecutable.set(runLauncher.map { l -> l.executablePath.asFile.absolutePath })
    }
}


```

`platforms/mc-26.3/neoforge/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/icarus/platform/PlatformImpl.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.icarus.platform;

import net.minecraft.client.Minecraft;
import net.neoforged.fml.ModList;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.ClientTickEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.RenderGuiEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.client.event.InputEvent;
import net.neoforged.neoforge.common.NeoForge;
import net.neoforged.neoforgespi.language.IModFileInfo;
import com.mojang.blaze3d.platform.InputConstants;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.Icarus;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.input.Keys;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.mc263.ModernPlatform;

import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.Collection;

/** NeoForge 26.3. */
@SuppressWarnings("unused")
public class PlatformImpl extends ModernPlatform {
    @Override
    public void install() {
        NeoForge.EVENT_BUS.addListener((InputEvent.Key e) -> {
            if (e.getAction() == InputConstants.PRESS && Minecraft.getInstance().gui.screen() == null) {
                Icarus.onKeyPressed(Keys.fromSdl(e.getKey()));
            } else if (e.getAction() == InputConstants.RELEASE) {
                Icarus.onKeyReleased(Keys.fromSdl(e.getKey()));
            }
        });
        NeoForge.EVENT_BUS.addListener((RenderGuiEvent.Post e) -> {
            renderer.begin(e.getGuiGraphics());
            Icarus.renderHud(e.getPartialTick().getGameTimeDeltaPartialTick(false));
        });
        NeoForge.EVENT_BUS.addListener((ClientTickEvent.Pre e) -> Icarus.clientTick());
    }

    @Override
    public Collection<Path> getModRoots(String modid) {
        IModFileInfo file = ModList.get().getModFileById(modid);
        if (file == null) throw new IllegalArgumentException("No mod with id " + modid);
        return file.getFile().getContents().getContentRoots(); //jar files or, in dev, class/resource directories
    }

    @Override
    public ClassLoader getModClassLoader(String modid) {
        return PlatformImpl.class.getClassLoader(); //every game-layer mod shares FML's transforming class loader
    }
}

```

```java
package studio.dreamys.test;

import net.neoforged.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.neoforged.bus.api.IEventBus;
import net.neoforged.fml.common.Mod;
import net.neoforged.fml.event.lifecycle.FMLClientSetupEvent;

@Mod(value = "testmod", dist = Dist.CLIENT)
public class TestModNeoForge {
    public TestModNeoForge(IEventBus modBus) {
        //client setup runs in parallel; hop onto the main thread like Forge's preInit did
        modBus.addListener((FMLClientSetupEvent e) -> e.enqueueWork(() -> TestModCommon.init("testmod")));
    }
}
```

`testmod/build.gradle.kts`:

```kts
// The example mod's shared code. Platforms compile these sources into their own dev-only `testmod`
// source set (rather than depending on this project) so the pages live in the test mod's own output,
// exactly like a real mod's pages would, which is where Icarus scans for them.
plugins {
    java
}

java {
    toolchain.languageVersion.set(JavaLanguageVersion.of(8))
}

tasks.withType<JavaCompile>().configureEach {
    options.encoding = "UTF-8"
}

dependencies {
    implementation(project(":core"))
    testRuntimeOnly("com.google.code.gson:gson:2.2.4")
}

// Renders a theme's menu headlessly for comparison with the reference footage:
// ./gradlew :testmod:snapshot -Pargs="skeet 1.25 4 build/snap.png"
tasks.register<JavaExec>("snapshot") {
    classpath = sourceSets["test"].runtimeClasspath
    mainClass.set("studio.dreamys.test.snapshot.Snapshot")
    args = (project.findProperty("args") as String? ?: "skeet 1.25 0 build/snapshot.png").split(" ")
    workingDir = projectDir
    systemProperty("java.awt.headless", "true")
}

// src/test only holds the snapshot tool above, no tests; Gradle 9 fails a test task that finds none
tasks.test { enabled = false }

```

`testmod/src/main/java/studio/dreamys/test/TestModCommon.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.test;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.Icarus;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.IcarusMod;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.IcarusOptions;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.hud.HudContext;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.hud.HudElement;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.hud.Toast;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.hud.Watermark;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.input.Keys;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.Canvas;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.theme.Theme;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.theme.Themes;

/**
 * The example mod, independent of Minecraft version and mod loader: a recreation of the skeet menu
 * ({@code studio.dreamys.test.skeet}) or the fatality menu ({@code studio.dreamys.test.fatality}). The two themes
 * offer different controls, so each has its own pages; {@code ICARUS_TEST_THEME=fatality} picks the second.
 * Each platform has a tiny entrypoint (@Mod class, mixin, ...) that calls {@link #init(String)}.
 */
public class TestModCommon {
    private static IcarusMod mod;

    public static void init(String modid) {
        String themeId = System.getenv("ICARUS_TEST_THEME");
        Theme theme = Themes.byId(themeId == null ? "skeet" : themeId);
        if (theme == null) theme = Themes.SKEET;
        mod = Icarus.create(modid, theme, IcarusOptions.defaults()
                .themeSelector(false)
                .pagesPackage("studio.dreamys.test." + theme.getId()));
        //the fatality header shows the product name
        if (theme == Themes.FATALITY) mod.getMenu().setTitle("FATALITY");

        if (theme == Themes.SKEET) {
            //like the original: DEL opens the menu, "Menu color" is the accent and "DPI scale" resizes the menu
            mod.setMenuKey(Keys.KEY_DELETE);
            mod.setAccent(studio.dreamys.test.skeet.Misc.Settings.Menu_color.getRGB());
            mod.onChange(studio.dreamys.test.skeet.Misc.Settings.class, "Menu_color", (java.awt.Color c) -> mod.setAccent(c.getRGB()));
            applyDpi(studio.dreamys.test.skeet.Misc.Settings.DPI_scale);
            mod.onChange(studio.dreamys.test.skeet.Misc.Settings.class, "DPI_scale", TestModCommon::applyDpi);
        }

        //HUD: a watermark in the theme's style, toasts below it (K, J and L send test ones)
        mod.addHud(new Watermark("icarus | " + modid));
        mod.toasts().setOffsetY(32);
        mod.addKeyListener(TestModCommon::key);

        //dev aid: open the menu on the title screen so every platform can be checked without a world
        if (System.getenv("ICARUS_TEST_OPEN") != null) {
            mod.addHud(new HudElement() {
                private int ticks;

                @Override
                public void render(Canvas canvas, HudContext hud) {
                }

                @Override
                public void tick() {
                    if (++ticks == 100) {
                        mod.openMenu();
                        //a long toast, to check toasts over the menu without a world
                        mod.toasts().send(new Toast("Icarus", "Menu opened by ICARUS_TEST_OPEN.", 600));
                    }
                }
            });
        }
    }

    public static void notify(String title, String message) {
        if (mod != null) mod.toasts().send(new Toast(title, message));
    }

    /** "125%" is the size the skeet theme was measured at. */
    private static void applyDpi(String percent) {
        try {
            mod.setMenuScale(Integer.parseInt(percent.replace("%", "").trim()) / 125.0);
        } catch (NumberFormatException ignored) {
        }
    }

    private static void key(int keyCode) {
        if (keyCode == Keys.KEY_K) notify("Test", "Test");
        if (keyCode == Keys.KEY_J) notify("Test", "This is a very cool informational toast which is much larger in size.");
        if (keyCode == Keys.KEY_L) {
            notify("Hello", "Lorem ipsum dolor sit amet, consectetur adipiscing elit. Donec congue neque libero, a posuere erat pharetra sed. "
                    + "Phasellus convallis nisi vel tortor malesuada commodo eu in nunc.");
        }
    }
}

```

```java
package studio.dreamys.test.fatality;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.annotation.ILabel;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.annotation.field.DropdownOptions;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.annotation.field.IAttach;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.annotation.field.IKeybind;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.annotation.field.SliderOptions;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.annotation.type.IGroup;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.annotation.type.IPage;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.annotation.type.ISubPage;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.input.Keys;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.ListModel;
import studio.dreamys.test.TestModCommon;

import java.awt.Color;

/** The RAGE tab: AIMBOT (one sub-page per weapon, shown as the weapon row) and ANTI-AIM. */
@IPage(order = 0)
public class Rage {
    @ILabel("Aimbot")
    @ISubPage(order = 0, icon = 'Y')
    public static class Aimbot_Auto {
        @ILabel("Auto")
        @IGroup(column = 0)
        public static class Weapon {
            @SliderOptions(min = 0, max = 100, onlyInt = true, units = " %")
            public static int Hitchance = 97;

            @ILabel("Min-damage")
            @SliderOptions(min = 0, max = 120, onlyInt = true, units = " dmg")
            public static int Min_damage = 73;

            @SliderOptions(min = 0, max = 100, onlyInt = true, units = " %", minText = "Auto")
```

```java
/** The config tab: Icarus' built-in Presets group goes first; this adds the Lua group next to it. */
@IPage(icon = "config", order = 7)
public class Config {
    @IGroup(column = 1)
    public static class Lua {
        public static Runnable Reload_active_scripts = () -> TestModCommon.notify("Lua", "Reloaded active scripts.");

        @ILabel("")
        public static final ListModel Scripts = new ListModel("havoc_vision", "model_changer", "dt_speed", "havoc_illusion")
                .searchable(true).height(298 / 1.25);

        @ILabel("Updated 18 days ago")
        public static Label Updated;

        public static boolean Load_on_startup = false;

        public static Runnable Load_script = () -> TestModCommon.notify("Lua", "Loaded " + Scripts.getSelected() + ".");
    }
}
```

```java
package studio.dreamys.test.skeet;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.annotation.ILabel;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.annotation.field.DropdownOptions;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.annotation.field.IKeybind;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.annotation.field.IWarning;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.input.Keys;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.KeybindMode;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.settings.Label;
import studio.dreamys.test.TestModCommon;

import java.awt.Color;

/** The MISC tab, as in the reference. */
@IPage(icon = "misc", order = 4)
public class Misc {
    @IGroup(column = 0, order = 0)
    public static class Miscellaneous {
        @ILabel("Override FOV")
        @SliderOptions(min = 1, max = 135, onlyInt = true, units = "°")
        public static int Override_FOV = 90;

        @ILabel("Override zoom FOV")
        @SliderOptions(min = 0, max = 100, onlyInt = true, units = "%")
        public static int Override_zoom_FOV = 100;

        public static boolean Knifebot = false;
        public static boolean Zeusbot = false;
        public static boolean Automatic_weapons = false;
```

`testmod/src/test/java/studio/dreamys/test/snapshot/RasterRenderer.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.test.snapshot;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Renderer;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Texture;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.render.DrawList;

import java.awt.image.BufferedImage;

/**
 * A software {@link Renderer}: keeps texture pixels and rasterises each {@link DrawList} into an image (pixel-centre
 * sampling, nearest texture filtering, per-vertex colour × texel, source-over blending), like the GPU path does.
 * Used to compare themes against reference screenshots without Minecraft.
 */
public final class RasterRenderer implements Renderer {
    public BufferedImage image;
    private int[] target;
    private int width, height;

    public static final class RasterTexture implements Texture {
        final int w, h;
        final int[] pixels;

        RasterTexture(int w, int h) {
            this.w = w;
            this.h = h;
            this.pixels = new int[w * h];
        }

        @Override
        public int getWidth() {
            return w;
        }

        @Override
        public int getHeight() {
            return h;
        }
    }

    /** Starts a new image filled with {@code background} (ARGB, opaque). */
    public void clear(int width, int height, int background) {
        this.width = width;
        this.height = height;
        image = new BufferedImage(width, height, BufferedImage.TYPE_INT_RGB);
        target = new int[width * height];
        java.util.Arrays.fill(target, background);
    }

    /** Copies the rendered pixels into {@link #image}. */
    public BufferedImage finish() {
        image.setRGB(0, 0, width, height, target, 0, width);
        return image;
    }

    @Override
    public Texture createTexture(int width, int height) {
        return new RasterTexture(width, height);
    }

    @Override
    public void upload(Texture texture, int x, int y, int w, int h, int[] argb, int offset, int stride) {
        RasterTexture t = (RasterTexture) texture;
        for (int row = 0; row < h; row++) System.arraycopy(argb, offset + row * stride, t.pixels, (y + row) * t.w + x, w);
    }

    @Override
    public void deleteTexture(Texture texture) {
    }

    @Override
    public void render(DrawList list) {
        float[] xy = list.getPositions(), uv = list.getUvs();
        int[] colors = list.getColors(), indices = list.getIndices();
        for (int c = 0; c < list.getCommandCount(); c++) {
            RasterTexture tex = (RasterTexture) list.getCommandTexture(c);
            int start = list.getCommandIndexStart(c), end = start + list.getCommandIndexCount(c);
            for (int i = start; i < end; i += 3) triangle(xy, uv, colors, indices[i], indices[i + 1], indices[i + 2], tex);
        }
    }

    private void triangle(float[] xy, float[] uv, int[] colors, int a, int b, int c, RasterTexture tex) {
        float x0 = xy[a * 2], y0 = xy[a * 2 + 1], x1 = xy[b * 2], y1 = xy[b * 2 + 1], x2 = xy[c * 2], y2 = xy[c * 2 + 1];
        float area = (x1 - x0) * (y2 - y0) - (y1 - y0) * (x2 - x0);
        if (area == 0) return;
        int minX = Math.max(0, (int) Math.floor(Math.min(x0, Math.min(x1, x2))));
        int maxX = Math.min(width - 1, (int) Math.ceil(Math.max(x0, Math.max(x1, x2))));
        int minY = Math.max(0, (int) Math.floor(Math.min(y0, Math.min(y1, y2))));
        int maxY = Math.min(height - 1, (int) Math.ceil(Math.max(y0, Math.max(y1, y2))));
        for (int py = minY; py <= maxY; py++) {
            float sy = py + 0.5f;
            for (int px = minX; px <= maxX; px++) {
                float sx = px + 0.5f;
                float w0 = ((x1 - sx) * (y2 - sy) - (y1 - sy) * (x2 - sx)) / area;
                float w1 = ((x2 - sx) * (y0 - sy) - (y2 - sy) * (x0 - sx)) / area;
                float w2 = 1 - w0 - w1;
                //half-open edges so shared quad diagonals don't draw twice
                if (w0 < 0 || w1 < 0 || w2 < 0) continue;
                if ((w0 == 0 && !topLeft(x1, y1, x2, y2, area)) || (w1 == 0 && !topLeft(x2, y2, x0, y0, area))
                        || (w2 == 0 && !topLeft(x0, y0, x1, y1, area))) continue;
                float u = w0 * uv[a * 2] + w1 * uv[b * 2] + w2 * uv[c * 2];
                float v = w0 * uv[a * 2 + 1] + w1 * uv[b * 2 + 1] + w2 * uv[c * 2 + 1];
                int texel = tex.pixels[clamp((int) Math.floor(v * tex.h), tex.h) * tex.w + clamp((int) Math.floor(u * tex.w), tex.w)];
                int ca = colors[a], cb = colors[b], cc = colors[c];
                float alpha = mix(ca >>> 24, cb >>> 24, cc >>> 24, w0, w1, w2) * (texel >>> 24) / (255f * 255f);
                if (alpha <= 0) continue;
                float r = mix(ca >> 16 & 255, cb >> 16 & 255, cc >> 16 & 255, w0, w1, w2) * (texel >> 16 & 255) / 255f;
                float g = mix(ca >> 8 & 255, cb >> 8 & 255, cc >> 8 & 255, w0, w1, w2) * (texel >> 8 & 255) / 255f;
                float bl = mix(ca & 255, cb & 255, cc & 255, w0, w1, w2) * (texel & 255) / 255f;
                int index = py * width + px, dst = target[index];
                int nr = Math.round(r * alpha + (dst >> 16 & 255) * (1 - alpha));
                int ng = Math.round(g * alpha + (dst >> 8 & 255) * (1 - alpha));
                int nb = Math.round(bl * alpha + (dst & 255) * (1 - alpha));
                target[index] = 0xFF000000 | nr << 16 | ng << 8 | nb;
            }
        }
    }

    private static boolean topLeft(float ax, float ay, float bx, float by, float area) {
        float dx = bx - ax, dy = by - ay;
        if (area < 0) {
            dx = -dx;
            dy = -dy;
        }
        return (dy == 0 && dx > 0) || dy < 0;
    }

    private static float mix(int a, int b, int c, float w0, float w1, float w2) {
        return a * w0 + b * w1 + c * w2;
    }

    private static int clamp(int v, int size) {
        return v < 0 ? 0 : v >= size ? size - 1 : v;
    }
}

```

`testmod/src/test/java/studio/dreamys/test/snapshot/Snapshot.java`:

```java
package studio.dreamys.test.snapshot;

import studio.dreamys.icarus.Icarus;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.IcarusMod;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.IcarusOptions;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Platform;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.platform.Renderer;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.theme.Theme;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.theme.Themes;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.Menu;
import studio.dreamys.icarus.ui.MenuController;

import javax.imageio.ImageIO;
import java.io.File;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * Renders a theme's menu without Minecraft, for pixel comparison with the reference footage.
 * <p>
 * {@code Snapshot <theme> <scale> <tab> <out.png> [mouseX mouseY] [click x y]...}: renders the testmod pages of that
 * theme at {@code scale} screen pixels per design unit (the skeet reference is 1.25, fatality 1), with the window
 * at the top-left (1 pixel margin), on tab index {@code tab}.
 */
public final class Snapshot {
    public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {
        String themeId = args[0];
        double scale = Double.parseDouble(args[1]);
        int tab = Integer.parseInt(args[2]);
        File out = new File(args[3]);
        double mouseX = args.length > 5 ? Double.parseDouble(args[4]) : -100, mouseY = args.length > 5 ? Double.parseDouble(args[5]) : -100;

        Theme theme = Themes.byId(themeId);
        RasterRenderer renderer = new RasterRenderer();
        Path game = Files.createTempDirectory("icarus-snapshot");
        Headless platform = new Headless(renderer, scale * 2, game.toFile());
        Icarus.setPlatform(platform);

        String pkg = "studio.dreamys.test." + themeId;
        List<Class<?>> pages = new ArrayList<>();
        for (String name : PAGES.get(themeId)) {
            try {
                pages.add(Class.forName(pkg + "." + name));
            } catch (ClassNotFoundException ignored) {
                //not written yet
            }
        }
        IcarusMod mod = Icarus.createDetached("snapshot", theme, IcarusOptions.defaults().themeSelector(false), pages,
                game.resolve("config"), game.resolve("legacy"));
        Menu menu = mod.getMenu();
        if (themeId.equals("fatality")) menu.setTitle("FATALITY");
        menu.opened();
        menu.skipFade();

        //first frame positions the window; then pin it near the origin
        renderer.clear(platform.width, platform.height, 0xFF404040);
        menu.render(0, 0, 0);
        menu.window.x = 1 / scale;
        menu.window.y = 1 / scale;
        menu.selectTab(tab);

        //optional clicks (design units), e.g. to open a dropdown
        for (int i = 6; i + 1 < args.length; i += 2) {
            double cx = Double.parseDouble(args[i]), cy = Double.parseDouble(args[i + 1]);
            menu.render(cx * scale / platform.guiScale, cy * scale / platform.guiScale, 0);
            menu.mousePressed(cx * scale / platform.guiScale, cy * scale / platform.guiScale, args[i].startsWith("-") ? 1 : 0);
            menu.mouseReleased(cx * scale / platform.guiScale, cy * scale / platform.guiScale, 0);
        }

        //optional toast (title|message), slid fully in
        String toast = System.getenv("SNAPSHOT_TOAST");
        if (toast != null) {
            String[] parts = toast.split("[|]", 2);
            mod.toasts().send(parts[0], parts.length > 1 ? parts[1] : "");
            for (int i = 0; i < 20; i++) mod.toasts().tick();
        }

        //optional typed text into whatever the clicks focused
        String typed = System.getenv("SNAPSHOT_TYPE");
        if (typed != null) typed.codePoints().forEach(menu::charTyped);

        double gx = mouseX * scale / platform.guiScale, gy = mouseY * scale / platform.guiScale;
        for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
            renderer.clear(platform.width, platform.height, 0xFF404040);
            menu.render(gx, gy, 0);
        }
        int w = (int) Math.ceil((menu.window.w + 2 / scale) * scale) + 1, h = (int) Math.ceil((menu.window.h + 2 / scale) * scale) + 1;
        if (System.getenv("SNAPSHOT_FULL") != null) { //popups may reach past the window
            w = platform.width;
            h = platform.height;
        }
        ImageIO.write(renderer.finish().getSubimage(0, 0, Math.min(w, platform.width), Math.min(h, platform.height)), "png", out);
        System.out.println("wrote " + out + " window " + menu.window.w * scale + "x" + menu.window.h * scale);
    }

    private static final java.util.Map<String, String[]> PAGES = new java.util.HashMap<>();

    static {
        PAGES.put("skeet", new String[]{"Rage", "AntiAim", "Legit", "Visuals", "Misc", "Skins", "Players", "Config"});
        PAGES.put("fatality", new String[]{"Rage", "Visuals", "Misc", "Inventory", "Legit"});
    }

    static final class Headless implements Platform {
        final Renderer renderer;
        final double guiScale;
        final File gameDir;
        final int width = 1400, height = 1100;
        String clipboard = "";

        Headless(Renderer renderer, double guiScale, File gameDir) {
            this.renderer = renderer;
            this.guiScale = guiScale;
            this.gameDir = gameDir;
        }

        @Override
        public void install() {
        }

        @Override
        public Renderer getRenderer() {
            return renderer;
        }

        @Override
        public double getGuiScale() {
            return guiScale;
        }

        @Override
        public int getFramebufferWidth() {
            return width;
        }

        @Override
        public int getFramebufferHeight() {
            return height;
        }

        @Override
        public boolean isInGame() {
            return false;
        }

        @Override
        public void openScreen(MenuController menu) {
        }

        @Override
        public File getGameDir() {
            return gameDir;
        }

        @Override
        public Collection<Path> getModRoots(String modid) {
            return Collections.emptyList();
        }

        @Override
        public ClassLoader getModClassLoader(String modid) {
            return Snapshot.class.getClassLoader();
        }

        @Override
        public String getClipboard() {
            return clipboard;
        }

        @Override
        public void setClipboard(String text) {
            clipboard = text;
        }
    }
}

```
